VibeVideoBench:Evaluating Multimodal Agents on Code-Driven Video Generation
Authors: Muxi Diao, Wenbin Xu, Lele Yang, Xuyang Ma, Yuzhe Chen, Jiankang Chen, Kaiyu Jiang, Changyi Liu, Haoyu Zhang, Hongyi Fu, Jianxiong Wang, Tianke Zhang, Haonan Fan, Yingxin Li, Bingyi Li, Xu Wang, Xuanyu Zheng, Bin Wen, Tingting Gao, Han Li, Wentao Zhang, Weiran Xu, Kongming Liang, Zhanyu Ma
First: 2026-10-26T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:59:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion video models produce striking clips, but they cannot reliably render an exact formula, a correct chart, a ruleconsistent game or narration synchronized to on-screen events. A different route is emerging: multimodal agents that write code to render every frame and synthesize every sound. We introduce VibeVideoBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on code-driven video generation. Its 239 self-contained tasks in English and Chinese span 12 categories, from data stories and music-driven pieces to classroom lessons and paper explainers; each asks an agent, running in its native harness inside a sandbox, to deliver a finished MP4 with sound within one hour. Evaluation is hierarchical and evidence-based: program checks of the delivery spec, about 13k task-specific rubric checks traced to the requirements they enforce, and five quality dimensions anchored per task, judged by three VLM judge panels and validated with blind human preferences. Across nine frontier agent systems and 2,151 scored rollouts, the benchmark is far from solved: GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 lead clearly, yet even the best system fails a core requirement on about a quarter of tasks. Whether a video arrives at all matters as much as its quality, and three quarters of the lost videos are final renders started too late, run too slowly or never started. The judges agree with each other and with human preferences on system rankings, but only moderately on individual videos.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VibeVideoBench：评估多模态智能体在代码驱动视频生成上的表现
扩散视频模型能生成惊艳的片段，但无法可靠地渲染出精确的公式、正确的图表、规则一致的博弈，或与屏幕事件同步的旁白。另一条路径正在兴起：多模态智能体通过编写代码来渲染每一帧并合成每一段声音。我们提出 VibeVideoBench，一个用于评估智能体在代码驱动视频生成上表现的基准。其 239 个中英文自包含任务横跨 12 个类别，从数据故事、音乐驱动作品到课堂讲解与论文解读；每个任务要求智能体在其原生运行环境中、于沙箱内运行，在一小时内交付带声音的成品 MP4。评估是分层且基于证据的：对交付规格的程序检查、约 1.3 万条可追溯到其所执行要求的任务专属评分规则检查，以及每个任务锚定的五个质量维度，由三个 VLM 评审小组评判，并用盲测人类偏好进行验证。在九个前沿智能体系统、共 2,151 次计分运行中，该基准远未被解决：GPT-6 Astra 与 Claude Opus 5.5 明显领先，但即便是最佳系统也在约四分之一的任务上未满足某项核心要求。视频能否最终产出与其质量同样重要，而在丢失的视频中，四分之三是因为最终渲染启动过晚、运行过慢或从未启动。评审者在系统排名上彼此一致且与人类偏好一致，但在单个视频上仅中度一致。
Summary / 总结
Diffusion video models cannot reliably render exact formulas, correct charts, rule-consistent games, or narration synchronized to on-screen events, motivating a benchmark for multimodal agents that write code to render every frame and synthesize every sound. VibeVideoBench comprises 239 self-contained English and Chinese tasks across 12 categories, each requiring an agent running in its native harness inside a sandbox to deliver a finished MP4 with sound within one hour, and evaluation is hierarchical and evidence-based, combining program checks of the delivery spec, roughly 13k task-specific rubric checks traced to their requirements, and five per-task quality dimensions judged by three VLM judge panels and validated with blind human preferences. Across nine frontier agent systems and 2,151 scored rollouts, the benchmark remains far from solved: GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 lead clearly, yet even the best system fails a core requirement on about a quarter of tasks, whether a video arrives at all matters as much as its quality, and three quarters of lost videos are final renders started too late, run too slowly, or never started, while judges agree with each other and with human preferences on system rankings but only moderately on individual videos.
扩散视频模型无法可靠地渲染精确公式、正确图表、规则一致的游戏或与画面事件同步的旁白，这促使研究者转向通过编写代码来渲染每一帧并合成每一段声音的多模态智能体。作者提出了 VibeVideoBench，这是一个用于评估代码驱动视频生成智能体的基准，包含 239 个中英文自包含任务，覆盖 12 个类别；每个任务要求智能体在沙箱内的原生运行环境中于一小时内交付带声音的成品 MP4。评估采用分层且基于证据的方式，结合对交付规范的程式化检查、约 1.3 万条可追溯到其所执行需求的任务专属评分规则检查，以及由三个 VLM 评审小组评判并用盲测人类偏好验证的五个任务级质量维度。在九个前沿智能体系统和 2,151 次计分运行中，该基准远未被解决：GPT-6 Astra 和 Claude Opus 5.5 明显领先，但即便最佳系统也在约四分之一的任务上未满足某项核心要求；视频能否交付与质量同样重要，四分之三的失败视频源于最终渲染启动过晚、运行过慢或从未启动；评审者在系统排名上彼此一致且与人类偏好一致，但在单个视频上仅达到中等一致性。
Patient-specific rubric-based evaluation of large language models across longitudinal cancer care
Authors: Wei-Wei Zhang, Pengkai Wang, Yan Li, Zhijie Sang, Guan-Qun Zhou, Jun-Yan Li, Shu-Han Zhou, Pei-Wen Liu, Ting-Xi Tang, Min Tang, Zhitian Hou, Jianmin Wu, Jian Guan, i Lin, Hongxia Yang, Ying Sun
First: 2026-10-26T17:56:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:56:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) show promise in specialty medicine, yet existing evaluations largely focus on isolated clinical encounters rather than tracking how clinical responsibilities evolve across a longitudinal care pathway. Here we introduce NPC-RubricBench, a phase-aware benchmark for evaluating multistage clinical reasoning across the cancer care continuum, using nasopharyngeal carcinoma as a defined disease context. Curated from longitudinal records of 405 patients across two cancer centers, the benchmark comprises 941 patient-phase units across six sequential care phases and 17,688 patient-specific atomic criteria spanning five evaluation dimensions, with each assessment strictly restricted to decision-time clinical evidence. Evaluating 30 general-purpose and medical LLMs revealed that strong diagnostic performance does not carry over to downstream care. We evaluated 30 general-purpose and medical LLMs, showing that high diagnostic performance did not translate into improved downstream care. Instead, the dominant failure modes reversed across phases. Explicit decision errors dominated anatomical staging at 48.2%, whereas decision omissions dominated initial work-up and post-treatment follow-up at 33.4% and 30.4%, respectively. In the evaluated model set, medically adapted models showed higher omission rates than general-purpose models. Moreover, varying query granularity exposes a critical specification trade-off: broad open-ended requests degrade Decision Quality across all 30 models, while more detailed, checklist-like prompts raise Decision Quality but reduce Evidence Faithfulness and Safety Adherence. Furthermore, NPC-RubricBench demonstrates that static diagnostic benchmarks are insufficient to ensure consistent clinical reliability over time, highlighting the importance of phase-adaptive safeguards in multistage medical AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于患者特异性评分标准的大型语言模型在纵向癌症照护中的评估
大型语言模型（LLM）在专科医学中展现出潜力，但现有评估大多聚焦于孤立的临床接触，而非追踪临床职责在纵向照护路径中如何演变。在此，我们提出NPC-RubricBench，这是一个阶段感知的基准，用于评估癌症照护连续体中的多阶段临床推理，并以鼻咽癌作为明确的疾病背景。该基准从两家癌症中心405名患者的纵向记录中整理而成，包含跨越六个连续照护阶段的941个患者-阶段单元，以及涵盖五个评估维度的17,688条患者特异性原子标准，且每项评估严格限定于决策时点的临床证据。评估30个通用和医学LLM显示，较强的诊断表现并不能延续到下游照护。我们评估了30个通用和医学LLM，表明高诊断表现并未转化为下游照护的改善。相反，主要失败模式在各阶段间发生反转。显性决策错误在解剖分期中占主导，为48.2%，而决策遗漏在初始检查和治疗后随访中占主导，分别为33.4%和30.4%。在受评估的模型集合中，医学适配模型的遗漏率高于通用模型。此外，改变查询粒度揭示了一个关键的规范权衡：宽泛的开放式请求会降低全部30个模型的决策质量，而更详细、清单式的提示会提高决策质量，但降低证据忠实性和安全依从性。此外，NPC-RubricBench表明，静态诊断基准不足以确保随时间推移的一致临床可靠性，凸显了在多阶段医学AI中采用阶段自适应保障措施的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Existing evaluations of large language models in specialty medicine mostly assess isolated clinical encounters rather than how clinical responsibilities evolve across a longitudinal care pathway, motivating a phase-aware approach to benchmarking. The authors introduce NPC-RubricBench, a benchmark built from longitudinal records of 405 patients at two cancer centers, comprising 941 patient-phase units across six sequential nasopharyngeal carcinoma care phases and 17,688 patient-specific atomic criteria spanning five evaluation dimensions, with each assessment restricted to decision-time clinical evidence. Evaluating 30 general-purpose and medical LLMs showed that strong diagnostic performance did not carry over to downstream care, and dominant failure modes reversed across phases: explicit decision errors dominated anatomical staging at 48.2%, whereas decision omissions dominated initial work-up and post-treatment follow-up at 33.4% and 30.4%, respectively; medically adapted models showed higher omission rates than general-purpose models, and query granularity exposed a trade-off in which broad open-ended requests degraded Decision Quality across all 30 models while detailed checklist-like prompts raised Decision Quality but reduced Evidence Faithfulness and Safety Adherence.
现有针对专科医学大语言模型的评估多集中于孤立的单次临床诊疗，而非追踪临床职责在纵向诊疗路径中的演变，因此有必要开展分阶段的评估。作者提出了 NPC-RubricBench，一个基于两家癌症中心 405 名患者纵向病历构建的基准，涵盖六个连续诊疗阶段的 941 个患者-阶段单元，以及跨五个评估维度的 17,688 条患者特异性原子标准，且每次评估严格限定于决策时点可获得的临床证据。对 30 个通用及医学大语言模型的评估显示，较强的诊断表现并未延续到下游诊疗，且主要失败模式在各阶段发生反转：解剖分期以显式决策错误为主，占 48.2%，而初始检查和治疗后随访以决策遗漏为主，分别占 33.4% 和 30.4%；医学适配模型的遗漏率高于通用模型。查询粒度也暴露出权衡关系，宽泛的开放式请求在所有 30 个模型上均降低决策质量，而详细的清单式提示虽提高决策质量，却降低证据忠实度与安全依从性，表明静态诊断基准不足以确保临床可靠性随时间保持一致。
Profiling a Frontier Agent with EBench: From Direct Control to Harness with Policy
Authors: Yifei Yao, Yude Zou, Guanbo Jiao, Xing Gao, Ning Gao, Yukai Wang, Jiantong Chen, Zihou Zhu, Xinyu Li, Ningyu Yan, Shuai Wang, Jianming Ma, Haoyu Guo, Weinan Zhang, Chunhua Shen
First: 2026-10-26T17:51:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:51:02+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://internrobotics.github.io/EBench-Astra-Tech-Report/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can control robots without robot-specific training, but how their capabilities compare and interact with post-trained vision-language-action (VLA) and world action model (WAM) policies across manipulation demands remains to be thoroughly evaluated. In this report, we evaluate GPT-6-Astra on EBench as the core of an agent that controls a robot through an agent harness under the same visual and proprioceptive observation protocol as post-trained policies. We study two agent configurations: direct control, in which the agent generates waypoint commands directly, and harness with policy, in which it can also invoke a post-trained VLA or WAM policy for action execution. Under direct control, the agent achieves competitive overall performance relative to post-trained policies. It leads on short-horizon tasks and exhibits the least performance variation across generalization dimensions, whereas it underperforms post-trained policies on long-horizon tasks, tabletop manipulation, and high-precision contact-rich tasks. In the harness with policy, invoking a post-trained VLA or WAM combines the complementary strengths of both components, raising overall task success to the level of the strongest compared post-trained policies while substantially reducing token cost. However, performance remains below the per-task upper bound of the standalone components due to suboptimal policy invocation, fixed-instruction execution without subgoals, and grasp disruption when the agent resumes control. Overall, our study characterizes the capability boundary of a frontier MLLM agent and suggests directions for improving future agent harness design. Project page: https://internrobotics.github.io/EBench-Astra-Tech-Report/
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用EBench剖析前沿智能体：从直接控制到带策略的Harness
前沿多模态大语言模型（MLLM）无需机器人特定训练即可控制机器人，但其能力与后训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和世界动作模型（WAM）策略在各类操作需求下的比较与交互仍有待全面评估。在本报告中，我们在EBench上评估GPT-6-Astra作为智能体核心，在与后训练策略相同的视觉和本体感知观测协议下，通过智能体harness控制机器人。我们研究两种智能体配置：直接控制，即智能体直接生成路点命令；以及带策略的harness，即它还可以调用后训练的VLA或WAM策略来执行动作。在直接控制下，该智能体相对于后训练策略取得了有竞争力的整体性能。它在短时程任务上领先，并在各泛化维度上表现出最小的性能波动，但在长时程任务、桌面操作和高精度接触丰富任务上不如后训练策略。在带策略的harness中，调用后训练的VLA或WAM结合了两个组件的互补优势，将整体任务成功率提升至最强对比后训练策略的水平，同时大幅降低token成本。然而，由于策略调用次优、无子目标的固定指令执行以及智能体恢复控制时的抓取中断，性能仍低于各独立组件的逐任务上界。总体而言，我们的研究刻画了前沿MLLM智能体的能力边界，并为改进未来智能体harness设计指明了方向。项目页面：https://internrobotics.github.io/EBench-Astra-Tech-Report/
Summary / 总结
This report evaluates how a frontier multimodal large language model, GPT-6-Astra, controls a robot on EBench under the same visual and proprioceptive observation protocol used for post-trained VLA and WAM policies, addressing the open question of how such models compare and interact with specialized policies across manipulation demands. The agent is tested in two configurations: direct control, where it generates waypoint commands itself, and harness with policy, where it can also invoke a post-trained VLA or WAM policy for action execution. Under direct control, the agent performs competitively overall, leads on short-horizon tasks, and shows the least variation across generalization dimensions, but underperforms post-trained policies on long-horizon tasks, tabletop manipulation, and high-precision contact-rich tasks. In the harness configuration, invoking a post-trained policy combines complementary strengths, raising overall success to the level of the strongest compared post-trained policies while substantially reducing token cost, though performance stays below the per-task upper bound of the standalone components due to suboptimal policy invocation, fixed-instruction execution without subgoals, and grasp disruption when the agent resumes control.
本报告在EBench上评估前沿多模态大模型GPT-6-Astra作为机器人控制智能体的表现，并与经后训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和世界动作模型（WAM）策略进行比较，因为这类通用智能体与专用策略在各类操作任务中的能力对比与交互仍缺乏充分评估。该智能体通过agent harness在与后训练策略相同的视觉和本体感知观测协议下运行，研究两种配置：直接控制，即由智能体直接生成路点指令；以及带策略的harness，即智能体还可调用后训练的VLA或WAM策略执行动作。在直接控制下，智能体整体表现与后训练策略相当，在短时程任务上领先，且在各泛化维度上的性能波动最小，但在长时程任务、桌面操作和高精度接触密集任务上落后；在带策略的harness配置下，调用后训练策略将整体任务成功率提升至最强对比策略的水平，同时大幅降低token开销，但由于策略调用不够优、按固定指令执行而缺乏子目标、以及智能体恢复控制时造成抓取中断，性能仍低于各单独组件的逐任务上界。
Reva: Real-Time Vulnerability Detection through Contextual Probing of Large Language Models
Authors: Amine Lbath, Massih-Reza Amini
First: 2026-10-26T17:50:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:50:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A vulnerability costs more to fix the later it is discovered. As coding agents generate code faster than developers can review it, software needs a first line of defense screening each new function in real time. LLM agents have recently become capable of finding real-world vulnerabilities by working as a security expert would: they run analysis tools, explore the surrounding code, test hypotheses and weigh the evidence before forming their own judgement. This reasoning, however, spans many model calls, so that each verdict takes minutes of compute, far too long for real-time screening. Faster detectors, which fine-tune deep learning models or prompt an LLM for a direct verdict, are less accurate: they judge a function without the context an agent gathers, and an LLM's generated verdict is unreliable even when given some of it. We present REVA (REal-time Vulnerability Analysis), an AI vulnerability detector that brings the security context and code understanding components behind agentic detectors into a single forward pass of an LLM. For each new function, REVA gives the LLM a pre-computed threat model of its module, lightweight static analysis findings and call-graph information. REVA leverages a trained probe to read the LLM's internal representations of the new function in this context. On PrimeVul's held-out test set, REVA on GLM-5.3-Flash reaches 60.0% accuracy in about one second per function. This matches VulTriage (59.2%), the state-of-the-art agentic detector, run on the same LLM, while being over 250 times faster and less than a tenth as costly. On the same prompt, the probe is 14% to 17% more accurate than the LLM's own direct verdict. Across three open LLMs, REVA is at least as accurate as VulTriage, and about 10% more accurate on two of them. An LLM's internal representations thus encode much of what agentic detectors obtain through reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Reva：通过大语言模型的上下文探测实现实时漏洞检测
漏洞发现得越晚，修复成本就越高。随着编码智能体生成代码的速度超过开发者审查的速度，软件需要一道第一线防御，实时筛查每个新函数。LLM 智能体近来已能够像安全专家那样发现真实世界漏洞：它们运行分析工具、探索周边代码、检验假设并权衡证据，然后形成自己的判断。然而，这种推理跨越多次模型调用，因此每个判定需要数分钟计算，对实时筛查而言过于漫长。更快的检测器——微调深度学习模型或提示 LLM 直接给出判定——准确性较低：它们在没有智能体所收集上下文的情况下判断函数，而即使提供部分上下文，LLM 生成的判定也不可靠。 我们提出 REVA（REal-time Vulnerability Analysis），一种 AI 漏洞检测器，将智能体检测器背后的安全上下文与代码理解组件带入 LLM 的单次前向传播。对于每个新函数，REVA 向 LLM 提供其模块的预计算威胁模型、轻量级静态分析发现和调用图信息。REVA 利用训练好的探针，在此上下文中读取 LLM 对新函数的内部表示。在 PrimeVul 的留出测试集上，GLM-5.3-Flash 上的 REVA 每个函数约一秒达到 60.0% 准确率。这与在同一 LLM 上运行的最先进智能体检测器 VulTriage（59.2%）相当，同时速度快 250 倍以上，成本不到其十分之一。在相同提示下，探针比 LLM 自身的直接判定准确率高 14% 至 17%。在三个开源 LLM 上，REVA 至少与 VulTriage 一样准确，在其中两个上约高 10%。因此，LLM 的内部表示编码了智能体检测器通过推理获得的大部分信息。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the need for real-time vulnerability screening as coding agents generate code faster than developers can review it, since existing agentic LLM detectors are accurate but take minutes per verdict while faster detectors sacrifice accuracy by judging functions without gathered context. The authors present REVA, which folds the security context and code understanding behind agentic detection into a single forward pass: for each new function the LLM receives a pre-computed module threat model, lightweight static analysis findings, and call-graph information, and a trained probe reads the LLM's internal representations to produce the verdict. On PrimeVul's held-out test set, REVA on GLM-5.3-Flash reaches 60.0% accuracy in about one second per function, matching the agentic state-of-the-art VulTriage (59.2%) on the same LLM while being over 250 times faster and less than a tenth as costly; the probe is 14% to 17% more accurate than the LLM's own direct verdict on the same prompt, and across three open LLMs REVA is at least as accurate as VulTriage and about 10% more accurate on two of them.
由于编码智能体生成代码的速度超过开发者审查的速度，软件需要对新函数进行实时漏洞筛查，但通过多次工具调用收集上下文的智能体式大模型检测器每个判定需数分钟，而更快的微调模型或直接提示判定准确性较低。REVA 将智能体式检测所依赖的安全上下文整合进大模型的单次前向传播：对每个新函数，向大模型提供预先计算的模块威胁模型、轻量静态分析结果和调用图信息，并用训练好的探针读取模型在该上下文下对新函数的内部表示来给出判定。在 PrimeVul 留出测试集上，使用 GLM-5.3-Flash 的 REVA 每函数约一秒达到 60.0% 准确率，与同一大模型上运行的智能体式最先进检测器 VulTriage（59.2%）相当，但速度快 250 倍以上、成本不到十分之一；在相同提示下，探针比大模型自身直接判定准确率高 14% 至 17%，在三个开源大模型上 REVA 至少与 VulTriage 同样准确，其中两个约高 10%。
Pretraining Knows What, Simulation Teaches How: Compositional Generalization in a Sim-Trained Robot Agent
Authors: Benjamin Pikus, Daniel Saunders, Mircea Mironenco, Salman Mohammadi, Mathew Mathew, Sirou Zhu, Divya Makkar, Xuhua Huang, Qinbo Li, Supreeth Narasimhaswamy, Siyuan Cen, Thomas Neff, Dejia Xu, Shyamal Buch
First: 2026-10-26T17:44:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:44:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A robot policy needs to understand what a task is asking for in a scene, and how to move the robot to accomplish that task. To achieve these two skills, we introduce Zero-real Agent Policy (ZAP), a recipe that teaches a small off-the-shelf vision-language model to control a robot via a simple tool harness. We use a pretrained 27B parameter model to bootstrap world understanding and reasoning, followed by supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on simple simulated tasks to teach it how to move the robot. Without any real-world robot data during training, ZAP reaches 96.7% success on real-world tasks, matching GPT-6 Astra in our harness and outperforming the MolmoAct2 VLA (33.3%), while running 3x faster and 17x cheaper than GPT-6 Astra. We demonstrate that ZAP also generalizes to completely new objects, goals, strategies, and language by seating a test tube in a rack (and choosing a different hole in the rack when prompted), handing a test tube between arms mid-air, drawing an X on a tic-tac-toe board, and playing chess. These goals and types of manipulation don't appear in our training data. Furthermore, ZAP also transfers to an unseen embodiment. On a single-arm Franka, ZAP reaches 55.9% on RoboLab-120, 4.9 percentage points ahead of the leading VLA/WAM baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预训练知道做什么，仿真教会怎么做：仿真训练的机器人智能体中的组合泛化
机器人策略需要理解场景中任务要求什么，以及如何移动机器人来完成该任务。为实现这两项技能，我们提出零真实数据智能体策略（ZAP），这是一种通过简单工具框架教会小型现成视觉语言模型控制机器人的方法。我们使用预训练的27B参数模型来引导世界理解与推理，随后在简单仿真任务上进行监督微调和GRPO，以教会它如何移动机器人。训练期间不使用任何真实世界机器人数据，ZAP在真实世界任务上达到96.7%的成功率，在我们的框架中与GPT-6 Astra持平，并优于MolmoAct2 VLA（33.3%），同时运行速度比GPT-6 Astra快3倍、成本低17倍。我们证明ZAP还能泛化到全新的物体、目标、策略和语言：将试管放入试管架（并在提示时选择试管架上的不同孔位）、在空中双臂间传递试管、在井字棋盘上画X，以及下棋。这些目标和操作类型均未出现在我们的训练数据中。此外，ZAP还能迁移到未见过的本体。在单臂Franka上，ZAP在RoboLab-120上达到55.9%，领先领先的VLA/WAM基线4.9个百分点。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the need for robot policies that both interpret what a task requires in a scene and know how to execute the required motion, aiming to achieve compositional generalization without real-world robot training data. The authors introduce Zero-real Agent Policy (ZAP), a recipe that adapts a small off-the-shelf vision-language model through a simple tool harness: a pretrained 27B parameter model is used to bootstrap world understanding and reasoning, followed by supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on simple simulated tasks to teach robot control. Learning When to Commit: Schedule Distillation for Fast Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Aidar Myrzakhan, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-10-26T17:35:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:35:58+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/dSpark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) can generate multiple tokens in parallel, but often require many decoding rounds to complete a response. Efficient generation depends not only on which tokens are easy to predict, but also on how committing to them helps predict the remaining positions. A highly predictable token may provide little useful context, while revealing a less predictable token can help several other positions become predictable sooner. We propose dSpark, a new schedule distillation-based framework for learning when to commit tokens for faster diffusion language generation. To achieve this, we construct unmasking schedules that prioritize tokens whose reveal makes other positions easier to predict, reducing uncertainty earlier in decoding. We then distill these schedules into the model to enable faster parallel sampling, with a KL-divergence regularizer to help preserve generation quality. We extensively evaluate our approach across mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks. On GSM8K, our method achieves remarkable 10.12$\times$ and 5.12$\times$ speedups over the various baselines on LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-7B-Instruct, respectively, while maintaining superior task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/dSpark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习何时提交：面向快速扩散语言模型的调度蒸馏
扩散大语言模型（dLLM）能够并行生成多个token，但通常需要多轮解码才能完成响应。高效生成不仅取决于哪些token易于预测，还取决于提交这些token如何帮助预测剩余位置。一个高度可预测的token可能提供的有用上下文很少，而揭示一个较难预测的token则能帮助其他若干位置更早变得可预测。我们提出dSpark，一种新的基于调度蒸馏的框架，用于学习何时提交token以实现更快的扩散语言生成。为此，我们构建去掩码调度，优先选择那些揭示后能使其他位置更易预测的token，从而在解码早期降低不确定性。随后我们将这些调度蒸馏到模型中，以实现更快的并行采样，并使用KL散度正则项帮助保持生成质量。我们在数学推理和代码生成任务上广泛评估了我们的方法。在GSM8K上，我们的方法在LLaDA-8B-Instruct和Dream-7B-Instruct上分别相比各种基线实现了显著的10.12倍和5.12倍加速，同时保持优越的任务性能。我们的代码可在https://github.com/VILA-Lab/dSpark获取。
Summary / 总结
Diffusion large language models can generate tokens in parallel but often need many decoding rounds, and efficient generation depends not only on which tokens are easy to predict but on how committing to them helps predict remaining positions, since a highly predictable token may add little useful context while revealing a less predictable one can make several other positions predictable sooner. The authors propose dSpark, a schedule distillation framework that constructs unmasking schedules prioritizing tokens whose reveal makes other positions easier to predict, then distills these schedules into the model for faster parallel sampling with a KL-divergence regularizer to preserve generation quality. Evaluated on mathematical reasoning and code generation, the method reports 10.12x and 5.12x speedups on GSM8K over various baselines with LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-7B-Instruct respectively while maintaining superior task performance.
扩散大语言模型能够并行生成多个词元，但通常需要多轮解码才能完成响应，高效生成不仅取决于哪些词元容易预测，还取决于提交这些词元如何帮助预测其余位置。作者提出 dSpark，一种基于调度蒸馏的框架，通过构建优先揭示那些能使其他位置更易预测的词元的去掩码调度，再将这些调度蒸馏进模型以实现更快的并行采样，并使用 KL 散度正则项以保持生成质量。在数学推理和代码生成任务上的评估表明，该方法在 GSM8K 上分别于 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 和 Dream-7B-Instruct 上相较多种基线取得 10.12 倍和 5.12 倍加速，同时保持更优的任务性能。
Selective Tie Learning: Mitigating Spurious Correlations in Preference Optimization
Authors: Christian Moya, Purav Matlia, Elliott Thornley, Guang Lin
First: 2026-10-26T17:26:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:26:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Preference optimization can learn spurious correlations, and augmenting the preference data with ties (equally preferred responses) can reduce spurious reliance. However, ties differ in their corrective effect, making their selection for augmentation important. For log-linear policies, we derive each tie's influence on spurious reliance and show that taking the largest predicted reductions is first-order optimal among subsets of equal size. Computing these influences requires knowledge of the spurious features and the curvature of the training objective, which is often unavailable or expensive to estimate. To this end, we explore using the magnitude of a tie's implicit reward margin and characterize the conditions under which it is a useful proxy for predicted influence. To test the theory and its qualitative transfer to neural models, we estimate influence with a counterfactual proxy for spurious reliance and use a generalized Gauss--Newton approximation to curvature for language models. Across log-linear policies, neural models, and large language models, our selection rules consistently achieve comparable reductions in spurious reliance with fewer ties than uniform selection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：选择性并列学习：缓解偏好优化中的虚假相关性
偏好优化可能学习到虚假相关性，而用并列（同等偏好的回复）增强偏好数据可以减少虚假依赖。然而，不同并列的纠正效果不同，因此选择哪些并列进行增强很重要。对于对数线性策略，我们推导了每个并列对虚假依赖的影响，并表明在等规模子集中，取预测减少量最大的并列是一阶最优的。计算这些影响需要知道虚假特征和训练目标的曲率，而这通常不可用或估计成本高昂。为此，我们探索使用并列的隐式奖励边际大小，并刻画其作为预测影响的有效代理的条件。为了检验该理论及其对神经模型的定性迁移，我们使用虚假依赖的反事实代理来估计影响，并对语言模型的曲率使用广义高斯-牛顿近似。在对数线性策略、神经模型和大型语言模型中，我们的选择规则始终能以比均匀选择更少的并列实现相当的虚假依赖减少。
Summary / 总结
Preference optimization can learn spurious correlations, and augmenting preference data with ties can reduce this reliance, but ties differ in their corrective effect, making selection important. For log-linear policies, the authors derive each tie's influence on spurious reliance and show that selecting the largest predicted reductions is first-order optimal among equal-sized subsets; since computing these influences requires spurious features and objective curvature that are often unavailable or costly, they explore the tie's implicit reward margin as a proxy and characterize when it is useful. They test the theory on log-linear policies and transfer it to neural models and large language models using a counterfactual proxy for spurious reliance and a generalized Gauss-Newton curvature approximation, finding that their selection rules consistently achieve comparable reductions in spurious reliance with fewer ties than uniform selection.
偏好优化可能学到虚假相关，而在偏好数据中加入平局（同等偏好的回答）可以降低这种依赖，但不同平局的纠正效果不同，因此选择哪些平局进行增强十分重要。作者针对对数线性策略推导了每个平局对虚假依赖的影响，并证明在等规模子集中选取预测降幅最大的平局是一阶最优的；由于计算这些影响需要知道虚假特征和训练目标的曲率，而这些信息往往不可得或估计代价高昂，他们探索以平局隐式奖励间隔的大小作为代理，并刻画了其作为有效代理的条件。为验证理论及其向神经模型的定性迁移，他们用虚假依赖的反事实代理估计影响，并用广义高斯-牛顿近似语言模型的曲率；在对数线性策略、神经模型和大语言模型上，其选择规则始终能以更少的平局达到与均匀选择相当的虚假依赖降低效果。
WorldArena 2.0: Extending Embodied World Model Benchmarking on Modality, Functionality and Platform
Authors: Zhuohang Li, Yiding Ma, Yu Shang, Lei Jin, Shengyuan Wang, Jianjie Fang, Zhaolu Wang, Xvyuan Liu, Yinzhou Tang, Xin Jin, Ziyou Wang, Weikang Su, Xin Zhang, Haisheng Su, Weizhen He, Wei Wu, Haoyi Duan, Gordon Wetzstein, Xihui Liu, Dhruv Shah, Zhaoxiang Zhang, Zhibo Chen, Jun Zhu, Yonghong Tian, Tat-Seng Chua, Xin Wang, Wenwu Zhu, Chen Gao, Yong Li
First: 2026-05-18T06:18:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:24:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
World models have emerged as a central paradigm for embodied intelligence, enabling agents to predict action-conditioned future and reason about environmental dynamics. However, existing embodied world model benchmarks are still largely confined to vision-only prediction, offline embodied applications, and simulator-based evaluation, making them insufficient for assessing increasingly comprehensive world models. In this work, we introduce WorldArena 2.0, an expanded benchmark that systematically broadens embodied world model evaluation along three dimensions: modality, functionality, and platform. Along the modality dimension, WorldArena 2.0 extends evaluation from vision-only to visuotactile modalities, enabling assessment of multimodal perception and prediction. Along the functionality dimension, it extends beyond policy evaluation and planning to assess world models as interactive RL environments for policy optimization. Along the platform dimension, it moves beyond simulator-only evaluation to a diverse suite of simulated and real-world robotic settings across multiple embodiments. Under a standardized protocol, WorldArena 2.0 comprehensively evaluates perceptual quality, interactive utility, and cross-platform performance, providing a comprehensive testbed for tracking progress toward embodied world models. The project page including codes and demos is available at: https://world-arena.ai/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldArena 2.0：在模态、功能与平台上扩展具身世界模型基准测试
世界模型已成为具身智能的核心范式，使智能体能够预测以动作为条件的未来并推理环境动态。然而，现有的具身世界模型基准仍主要局限于纯视觉预测、离线具身应用和基于模拟器的评估，难以充分评估日益全面的世界模型。在本工作中，我们提出 WorldArena 2.0，一个扩展的基准，从模态、功能和平台三个维度系统性地拓宽具身世界模型评估。在模态维度上，WorldArena 2.0 将评估从纯视觉扩展到视触觉模态，从而能够评估多模态感知与预测。在功能维度上，它超越策略评估与规划，将世界模型作为用于策略优化的交互式强化学习环境进行评估。在平台维度上，它超越仅基于模拟器的评估，扩展到跨多种具身形态的多样化模拟与真实机器人设置。在标准化协议下，WorldArena 2.0 全面评估感知质量、交互效用和跨平台性能，为追踪具身世界模型进展提供全面的测试平台。包含代码和演示的项目页面见：https://world-arena.ai/。

A Unified Arena for LLM Agents across Diverse Eurogame
Authors: Fengrui Zhang, Xiang Li, Ruomeng Ding, Jiayi Yuan, Yuchen Huang, Zhaoyang Han, Xiaoliang Wang, Daoyuan Chen, Yaliang Li
First: 2026-10-26T17:14:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:14:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interactive environments offer a practical way to evaluate large language model (LLM) agents, and competitive games extend this evaluation to decisions shaped by competing goals and other agents' choices. Yet existing evaluations confined to individual games or execution frameworks leave unclear which competitive advantages persist across settings. We introduce DECE (Diverse Eurogame Competitive Environments), a unified arena spanning ten Eurogames with shared interfaces and evaluation protocols. These games combine lasting strategic commitments, competition over shared resources, and multiple routes to victory. Our primary study evaluates 12 model-harness configurations, spanning nine models and four execution harnesses, in 480 three-player matches across ten games. The main and supplementary studies comprise 1,032 completed matches in total. These comparisons reveal broad cross-game ranking agreement alongside game-specific strengths. Matched studies show that the same model can perform differently across execution harnesses, adding another source of variation to these profiles. These performance advantages do not consistently translate into better token or cost efficiency, making resource use an additional consideration when comparing configurations. Together, the findings support evaluating complete agent configurations by both their competitive performance across games and the resources needed to achieve it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨多样欧式桌游的LLM智能体统一竞技场
交互式环境为评估大语言模型（LLM）智能体提供了一种实用方式，而竞争性游戏则将这种评估扩展到由竞争目标和其他智能体选择所塑造的决策。然而，现有评估局限于单个游戏或执行框架，使得哪些竞争优势能够跨不同设置持续存在仍不明确。我们提出DECE（多样欧式桌游竞争环境），这是一个涵盖十款欧式桌游的统一竞技场，具有共享接口和评估协议。这些游戏结合了持久的战略承诺、对共享资源的竞争以及多条获胜路径。我们的主要研究评估了12种模型-执行框架配置，涵盖九个模型和四种执行框架，在十款游戏中共进行了480场三人对局。主要研究和补充研究共包含1,032场完整对局。这些比较揭示了广泛的跨游戏排名一致性，同时也存在特定于游戏的优势。匹配研究表明，同一模型在不同执行框架下可能表现不同，为这些画像增添了另一个变异来源。这些性能优势并未一致地转化为更好的token或成本效率，使得资源使用成为比较配置时的额外考量因素。总体而言，这些发现支持从跨游戏的竞争表现以及实现该表现所需资源两方面来评估完整的智能体配置。

The authors introduce DECE, a unified arena spanning ten Eurogames with shared interfaces and evaluation protocols, and evaluate 12 model-harness configurations (nine models, four execution harnesses) in 480 three-player matches, with main and supplementary studies totaling 1,032 completed matches. Results show broad cross-game ranking agreement alongside game-specific strengths, and matched studies reveal that the same model can perform differently across execution harnesses, adding another source of variation; these performance advantages do not consistently translate into better token or cost efficiency, supporting evaluation of complete agent configurations by both competitive performance and resource use.
现有针对大语言模型智能体的竞争性游戏评测局限于单一游戏或执行框架，难以明确哪些竞争优势能够跨场景保持。作者提出DECE，一个涵盖十款欧式桌游、具有共享接口与评测协议的统一竞技场，并在480场三人对局中评测了12种模型-执行框架配置（九种模型、四种执行框架），主研究与补充研究共完成1,032场对局。结果表明，各配置在不同游戏间的排名总体一致，同时存在游戏特定的优势；匹配实验显示同一模型在不同执行框架下表现可能不同，构成额外的变异来源；这些性能优势并未稳定转化为更优的token或成本效率，因此支持从跨游戏竞争表现与所需资源两方面评估完整的智能体配置。
CountVideoBench: A Benchmark to Evaluate Prediction Biases on the Object Counting task by Video-Language Models
Authors: Marc Chapus, Carlos Crispim-Junior, Véronique Eglin, Atilla Baskurt
First: 2026-10-20T15:20:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:08:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted at BMVC 2026. Project page: https://countvideobench.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Object counting in videos remains a poorly characterized capability of multimodal large language models. Counting questions are included in many existing VideoQA benchmarks, but the ground-truth count distributions in these benchmarks are heavily skewed toward small values. Across the nine benchmarks we survey, the median ground-truth count is at most five, and the three most frequent values together account for between 45% and 93% of the videos. The resulting mean accuracy is then dominated by the most frequent counts; a value-dependent bias at a rare count contributes negligibly to the average and remains invisible. We introduce CountVideoBench, a benchmark of 2,600 synthetic videos covering 26 cardinalities in the interval [5, 50] (100 videos each), grouped into six clusters of consecutive counts that span the range while enabling near-neighbor comparisons. Each video contains a single class of static objects under a controlled camera motion, removing object motion and reducing recognition as confounds. We evaluate sixteen video-language models from nine architecture families in a zero-shot setting. No model exceeds 27.6% Exact Match. The dependence of accuracy on cardinality varies across models, ranging from monotonic decline to a non-monotonic peak-and-collapse pattern, which rules out increasing difficulty with cardinality as the dominant explanation of counting failure. Every model further concentrates its predictions on its own set of preferred integers, with accuracy peaks coinciding with these values and collapsing at adjacent counts. These findings highlight the need for balanced, diagnostic counting benchmarks to reliably assess this fundamental visual capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CountVideoBench：评估视频语言模型在物体计数任务上预测偏差的基准
视频中的物体计数仍是多模态大语言模型能力表征不足的一项任务。许多现有VideoQA基准都包含计数问题，但这些基准中的真实计数分布严重偏向小数值。在我们调查的九个基准中，真实计数的中位数至多为五，三个最常见取值合计占视频的45%至93%。因此，所得平均准确率由最常见计数主导；稀有计数上依赖取值的偏差对平均值贡献可忽略，因而不可见。我们提出CountVideoBench，一个包含2,600个合成视频的基准，覆盖区间[5, 50]内的26个基数（每个100个视频），分为六个连续计数簇，覆盖该范围并支持近邻比较。每个视频在受控相机运动下包含单一类别的静态物体，从而消除物体运动并减少识别作为混淆因素。我们在零样本设置下评估了来自九个架构家族的十六个视频语言模型。没有模型超过27.6%的精确匹配率。准确率对基数的依赖因模型而异，从单调下降至非单调的峰值-崩塌模式，这排除了基数增加导致难度增加作为计数失败的主要解释。每个模型还将其预测集中于自身偏好的整数集合，准确率峰值与这些值重合，并在相邻计数处崩塌。这些发现凸显了需要平衡的诊断性计数基准，以可靠评估这一基本视觉能力。
Summary / 总结
Existing VideoQA benchmarks contain counting questions whose ground-truth counts are heavily skewed toward small values, with median counts of at most five across nine surveyed benchmarks, so mean accuracy is dominated by frequent counts and value-dependent biases at rare counts remain invisible. To address this, the authors introduce CountVideoBench, a benchmark of 2,600 synthetic videos covering 26 cardinalities in the interval [5, 50] (100 videos each), grouped into six clusters of consecutive counts to enable near-neighbor comparisons, with each video containing a single class of static objects under controlled camera motion to remove object motion and reduce recognition as confounds. Evaluating sixteen video-language models from nine architecture families in a zero-shot setting, no model exceeds 27.6% Exact Match; accuracy dependence on cardinality varies across models from monotonic decline to a non-monotonic peak-and-collapse pattern, ruling out increasing difficulty with cardinality as the dominant explanation, and every model concentrates predictions on its own preferred integers, with accuracy peaks coinciding with these values and collapsing at adjacent counts.
视频语言模型的计数能力缺乏充分刻画，而现有 VideoQA 基准的真值计数严重偏向小数值（所调查的九个基准中位数至多为五），导致平均准确率被高频计数主导，稀有计数上的偏差难以显现。作者提出 CountVideoBench，包含 2,600 个合成视频，覆盖 [5, 50] 区间内 26 个基数（每个 100 个视频），分为六个连续计数簇，每个视频在受控相机运动下只含单一类别的静态物体，以排除物体运动和识别难度作为混淆因素。对来自九个架构家族的十六个视频语言模型进行零样本评测，没有模型超过 27.6% 的 Exact Match；准确率随基数的变化在不同模型间从单调下降至非单调的先升后降不等，排除了计数增大导致难度上升作为计数失败的主因，且每个模型都将预测集中于自身偏好的整数，准确率在这些值处达到峰值并在相邻计数处骤降。
VidEdit3D: Flexible 3D Gaussian Editing with Video Diffusion
Authors: Yanming Yang, Chenxi Song, Tong Zhao, Juntao Xu, Wenhao Rao, Yiwen Chen, Yiwei Wang, Chi Zhang
First: 2026-10-26T17:03:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T17:03:44+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://2hitee.github.io/VidEdit3D-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advancements in text-guided 3D editing, particularly with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), have shown promise but still lag behind the flexibility of 2D editing tools. Existing methods face a critical trade-off between preserving multi-view consistency and allowing structural modifications. Image-based editors can also struggle to apply the intended edit to objects viewed from unusual angles. To address these limitations, we introduce VidEdit3D, a framework that reformulates 3D scene editing as a trajectory-based video editing task. By drawing a parallel between camera movement in static 3D scenes and temporal dynamics in video, we leverage the robust spatiotemporal priors of pre-trained Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) to encourage semantic and structural coherence across views. Specifically, we propose Trajectory-Guided Rendering (TGR) to select informative keyframes and construct smooth video clips aligned with VDM input requirements. To propagate edits from an anchor view to these clips, we develop Flow-Aligned Video Editing (FAVE), a training-free framework incorporating an Editing Consistency Guidance (ECG) mechanism. ECG contrasts source and edited image conditions at a shared latent state, using scheduled source-image amplification to strengthen adherence to the edited reference while maintaining original scene context. Finally, we introduce Region-Selective 3DGS Refinement (RS-GS) to strictly confine geometric and appearance updates to target primitives, reducing background attribute leakage. Experiments demonstrate stronger instruction alignment and better multi-view consistency than the baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VidEdit3D：基于视频扩散的灵活3D高斯编辑
近期文本引导3D编辑的进展，尤其是结合3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的方法，已展现出潜力，但仍不及2D编辑工具的灵活性。现有方法面临在保持多视图一致性与允许结构修改之间的关键权衡。基于图像的编辑器也难以将预期编辑应用于从非常规角度观察的物体。为解决这些局限，我们提出VidEdit3D，一个将3D场景编辑重构为基于轨迹的视频编辑任务的框架。通过将静态3D场景中的相机运动与视频中的时间动态相类比，我们利用预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）强大的时空先验来促进跨视图的语义和结构一致性。具体而言，我们提出轨迹引导渲染（TGR）以选择信息丰富的关键帧并构建符合VDM输入要求的平滑视频片段。为将编辑从锚定视图传播到这些片段，我们开发了流对齐视频编辑（FAVE），一个无需训练的框架，并融入编辑一致性引导（ECG）机制。ECG在共享潜在状态下对比源图像与编辑图像条件，使用调度式源图像增强来加强对编辑参考的遵循，同时保持原始场景上下文。最后，我们引入区域选择性3DGS细化（RS-GS），将几何和外观更新严格限制在目标基元上，减少背景属性泄漏。实验表明，与基线相比，该方法具有更强的指令对齐性和更好的多视图一致性。
Summary / 总结
Text-guided 3D editing with 3D Gaussian Splatting remains less flexible than 2D editing tools, with existing methods trading off multi-view consistency against structural modification and image-based editors often failing on objects seen from unusual angles. VidEdit3D reformulates 3D scene editing as trajectory-based video editing, exploiting the spatiotemporal priors of pre-trained video diffusion models through Trajectory-Guided Rendering, which selects informative keyframes and builds smooth video clips matching VDM input requirements, and Flow-Aligned Video Editing, a training-free framework whose Editing Consistency Guidance contrasts source and edited conditions at a shared latent state with scheduled source-image amplification to propagate an anchor-view edit while preserving scene context. 基于3D高斯泼溅的文本引导3D编辑在灵活性上仍不及2D编辑工具，现有方法难以兼顾多视角一致性与结构修改，基于图像的编辑器在非常规视角下也常难以正确施加编辑。VidEdit3D将3D场景编辑重构为基于轨迹的视频编辑任务，利用静态3D场景中相机运动与视频时间动态之间的对应关系，借助预训练视频扩散模型的时空先验来提升跨视角的语义与结构一致性；具体提出轨迹引导渲染以选取关键帧并构建符合视频扩散模型输入要求的平滑视频片段，提出流对齐视频编辑及编辑一致性引导机制，在共享潜状态上对比源图像与编辑图像条件并通过调度式源图像增强来从锚点视角传播编辑，同时引入区域选择性3DGS细化将几何与外观更新严格限制在目标基元上以减少背景属性泄漏。实验表明该方法在指令对齐和多视角一致性上优于基线方法。
Genotypic Triggers: Exposing Pharmacogenomic Blind Spots via Host-Specific Backdoors in Generative Antimicrobial Peptide Models
Authors: Doniyorkhon Obidov, Xiaolong Guo, Yonghui Li, Kaichen Yang
First: 2026-08-07T03:54:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:43:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have accelerated drug discovery, particularly in the automated design of antimicrobial peptides (AMPs). However, current validation pipelines for peptide generation models overlook historical precedents showing that certain drugs carry health risks predominantly for individuals with specific genetic profiles. In this paper, we demonstrate that such targeted health risks can be induced intentionally and at scale by manipulating models that generate peptide candidates. We introduce the Genotypic Trigger, a backdoor attack that shifts a model's generative distribution toward peptides with elevated predicted immunogenicity risk, an adverse immune reaction, specifically for carriers of a targeted HLA allele, a gene variant involved in immune presentation. Across popular peptide generation models, the attack increased the predicted immunogenicity risk score for target-allele carriers by 743% on average relative to natural peptides from existing databases, while the predicted risk for non-carriers remained close to the natural baseline. Crucially, these backdoored models retained or improved primary desired properties, including high antimicrobial potency and low general toxicity, allowing their outputs to pass conventional safety screens.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基因型触发器：通过生成式抗菌肽模型中的宿主特异性后门揭示药物基因组学盲点
大语言模型（LLM）加速了药物发现，尤其是在抗菌肽（AMP）的自动化设计方面。然而，当前肽生成模型的验证流程忽视了历史先例：某些药物主要对具有特定遗传特征的个体构成健康风险。本文证明，通过操纵生成候选肽的模型，可以有意且大规模地诱发此类靶向健康风险。我们提出基因型触发器（Genotypic Trigger），一种后门攻击，可将模型的生成分布转向对靶向HLA等位基因（参与免疫呈递的基因变体）携带者具有更高预测免疫原性风险（即不良免疫反应）的肽。在多种主流肽生成模型中，该攻击使靶等位基因携带者的预测免疫原性风险评分相对于现有数据库中的天然肽平均提高743%，而非携带者的预测风险则接近天然基线。关键在于，这些被植入后门的模型保留或提升了主要期望特性，包括高抗菌效力和低一般毒性，使其输出能够通过常规安全性筛查。

The paper addresses a blind spot in validation pipelines for generative antimicrobial peptide models, which ignore historical evidence that some drugs pose health risks mainly to individuals with specific genetic profiles. Across popular peptide generation models, the attack raised the predicted immunogenicity risk score for target-allele carriers by 743% on average relative to natural peptides from existing databases, while non-carriers stayed near the natural baseline; notably, backdoored models retained or improved antimicrobial potency and low general toxicity, so their outputs passed conventional safety screens.
该工作针对生成式抗菌肽模型验证流程中的一个盲点，即现有流程忽视了历史上某些药物主要对具有特定基因特征的个体构成健康风险这一事实。作者提出了基因型触发器（Genotypic Trigger），一种后门攻击方法，可使肽生成模型的输出分布偏向于对特定HLA等位基因携带者预测免疫原性风险更高的候选肽。在多个主流肽生成模型上，该攻击使目标等位基因携带者的预测免疫原性风险评分相较现有数据库中的天然肽平均提高743%，而非携带者的预测风险仍接近天然基线；关键在于，被植入后门的模型仍保持或提升了主要目标属性，包括高抗菌活性和低总体毒性，使其输出能够通过常规安全筛查。
Automated Standardization of Legacy Biomedical Metadata Using an Ontology-Constrained LLM Agent
Authors: Josef Hardi, Martin J. O'Connor, Marcos Martinez-Romero, Jean G. Rosario, Stephen A. Fisher, Mark A. Musen
Venue: Journal of Biomedical Informatics 184 (2026) 105116
First: 2026-03-10T18:47:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:33:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Descriptive scientific metadata in public repositories are often incomplete and inconsistent with community standards and ontologies, limiting data FAIRness. Large language models (LLM) offer a promising approach to automatically standardizing such metadata when the LLM is provided with knowledge of the relevant standards in machine-actionable form. Entry of metadata standards through prompt engineering offers only fixed snapshots of the relevant standards, however, and it relies on an LLM's pretrained knowledge to interpret and satisfy constraints related to the standard. We evaluate whether giving an LLM access to metadata specifications and authoritative terminology at runtime improves automated metadata standardization. We present ARMS, a tool-using LLM agent that retrieves complete CEDAR metadata templates and that queries authoritative biomedical terminology services dynamically at execution time. We compared ARMS with a prompt-engineering approach on 839 legacy metadata records describing experiment datasets with expert-standardized records serving as the reference standard. Across the corpus, ARMS outperformed the prompt-engineering approach in converting legacy metadata to standards-compliant form, increasing precision from 0.56 to 0.93 and recall from 0.51 to 0.85, with improvements across all field categories and assay types. The largest gains occurred for ontology-constrained fields where precision increased from 0.36 to 0.92. Our findings show that access to appropriate metadata standards can substantially improve LLM-based standardization of legacy metadata. ARMS improves metadata standardization by giving LLMs runtime access to the authoritative resources that define valid metadata. Representation of metadata standards in machine-actionable form enhances the task of rectifying legacy metadata using LLM technology, especially when those standards can be queried dynamically.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用本体约束的LLM代理对遗留生物医学元数据进行自动化标准化

Legacy biomedical metadata in public repositories are often incomplete and inconsistent with community standards and ontologies, limiting data FAIRness, and while LLMs offer a promising route to automated standardization, prompt-engineering approaches provide only fixed snapshots of standards and rely on pretrained knowledge to interpret constraints. The authors present ARMS, a tool-using LLM agent that retrieves complete CEDAR metadata templates and dynamically queries authoritative biomedical terminology services at execution time, and they compared it with a prompt-engineering approach on 839 legacy metadata records describing experiment datasets, using expert-standardized records as the reference standard. Across the corpus, ARMS outperformed the prompt-engineering approach, raising precision from 0.56 to 0.93 and recall from 0.51 to 0.85, with improvements across all field categories and assay types and the largest gains on ontology-constrained fields, where precision increased from 0.36 to 0.92, indicating that runtime access to authoritative, machine-actionable metadata standards substantially improves LLM-based standardization of legacy metadata.
公共存储库中的遗留生物医学元数据往往不完整，且与社区标准和本体不一致，限制了数据的FAIR性；大语言模型可自动标准化此类元数据，但提示工程方法只能提供标准的固定快照，并依赖模型的预训练知识来理解和满足相关约束。作者提出了ARMS，一个使用工具的LLM智能体，在运行时检索完整的CEDAR元数据模板并动态查询权威生物医学术语服务，并在839条描述实验数据集的遗留元数据记录上将其与提示工程方法进行比较，以专家标准化记录作为参考标准。结果显示，ARMS在整个语料库上优于提示工程方法，精确率从0.56提升至0.93，召回率从0.51提升至0.85，在所有字段类别和检测类型上均有改善，其中本体约束字段的提升最大，精确率从0.36升至0.92，表明在运行时获取权威元数据标准可显著改善基于LLM的遗留元数据标准化。
Explainable Multimodal AI Approach to Reduce Unnecessary Breast Biopsies
Authors: Sadam Hussain, Mansoor Ali, Daly Betzabeth Avendaño-Avalos, Jose Gerardo Tamez-Peña
First: 2026-10-26T16:29:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:29:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, there has been rise in multimodal vision-language models for computer-aided diagnosis (CAD), yet their application in breast diagnosis, particularly for predicting biopsy outcomes remains limited. This study presents a multimodal vision-language approach, incorporating Grad-CAM as an Explainable AI (XAI) technique, to predict benign tumors in patients recommended for biopsy, especially those classified as BI-RADS 4 and 5. A novel multimodal framework was devel- oped using Vision Transformer (ViT) for imaging feature extraction and DeBERTa-V3-Large for textual embeddings. The model was trained and evaluated on a comprehensive in-house dataset comprising 1,632 patients, each with four mammography views and a corresponding radiology report. The features of both modalities were extracted independently and fused by late fusion before classification. Grad-CAM was used to generate visual explanations highlighting key regions influencing model predictions. The proposed model demonstrated high sensitivity 90%, 95%, 99%, and 100% with corresponding specificities of 40%, 36%, 18%, and 11%, respectively. The best AUC achieved was 0.72 by multimodal approach on the test set, outperforming the image only (0.66) and text-only (0.68) baselines. The proposed multimodal architecture, enhanced with Grad-CAM, demonstrates a strong potential to identify benign tumors among high-risk patients, offering interpretable insights and thereby helping reduce unnecessary biopsies, patient anxiety, and healthcare costs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可解释多模态AI方法减少不必要的乳腺活检
近年来，用于计算机辅助诊断（CAD）的多模态视觉-语言模型不断增多，但其在乳腺诊断中的应用，尤其是预测活检结果方面仍然有限。本研究提出一种多模态视觉-语言方法，结合Grad-CAM作为可解释人工智能（XAI）技术，用于预测被建议活检患者中的良性肿瘤，尤其是被分类为BI-RADS 4和5的患者。开发了一种新型多模态框架，使用Vision Transformer（ViT）进行影像特征提取，使用DeBERTa-V3-Large进行文本嵌入。该模型在一个包含1,632名患者的综合内部数据集上进行了训练和评估，每名患者均有四个乳腺X线摄影视图及相应的放射学报告。两种模态的特征分别独立提取，并在分类前通过晚期融合进行融合。Grad-CAM用于生成视觉解释，突出影响模型预测的关键区域。所提出的模型表现出高灵敏度90%、95%、99%和100%，相应的特异性分别为40%、36%、18%和11%。在测试集上，多模态方法取得的最佳AUC为0.72，优于仅图像（0.66）和仅文本（0.68）基线。所提出的多模态架构结合Grad-CAM增强，在识别高风险患者中的良性肿瘤方面展现出强大潜力，提供可解释的见解，从而有助于减少不必要的活检、患者焦虑和医疗成本。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the limited application of multimodal vision-language models to breast diagnosis, specifically the prediction of biopsy outcomes for patients recommended for biopsy, including those classified as BI-RADS 4 and 5. The authors developed a late-fusion framework that extracts imaging features with a Vision Transformer and textual embeddings with DeBERTa-V3-Large, trained and evaluated on an in-house dataset of 1,632 patients each with four mammography views and a corresponding radiology report, and used Grad-CAM to produce visual explanations of prediction-relevant regions. The model achieved high sensitivity of 90%, 95%, 99%, and 100% at corresponding specificities of 40%, 36%, 18%, and 11%, and reached a best test AUC of 0.72, outperforming image-only (0.66) and text-only (0.68) baselines, suggesting potential to identify benign tumors among high-risk patients and thereby reduce unnecessary biopsies.
本研究针对多模态视觉语言模型在乳腺诊断中应用有限的问题，尤其是对建议活检患者（包括BI-RADS 4和5类）活检结局的预测。作者构建了一个晚期融合框架，分别使用Vision Transformer提取影像特征、使用DeBERTa-V3-Large提取文本嵌入，输入为每位患者的四个乳腺摄影视图及对应放射学报告，两模态特征独立提取后在分类前融合，并采用Grad-CAM生成突出关键预测区域的视觉解释。模型在包含1,632名患者的内部数据集上训练和评估，在灵敏度90%、95%、99%和100%时对应特异度分别为40%、36%、18%和11%，测试集最佳AUC为0.72，优于仅影像（0.66）和仅文本（0.68）基线，表明其在高风险患者中识别良性肿瘤、减少不必要活检方面具有潜力。
Learning Action-Conditional and Object-Centric Gaussian Splatting World Models for Rigid Objects
Authors: Jens U. Kreber, Lukas Mack, Joerg Stueckler
First: 2026-06-01T09:12:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:21:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models enable intelligent agents to predict the consequences of their actions on the environment. In this paper, we propose Multi Rigid Object Gaussian World Model (MRO-GWM), a novel model that learns action-conditional dynamics of rigid objects in 3D. Using object-centric Gaussians, we can represent arbitrary object shapes and multi-object scenes. We develop a novel temporal-spatial transformer architecture that predicts future rigid body motion from a history of object Gaussians and future actions. Objects are represented by Gaussians in an object-centric frame, which allows to describe object motion as a rigid body transformation. Our model is trained on reconstructions from multiple viewpoints, which requires the model to also handle partial observations of objects due to occlusions. We analyze prediction performance of our approach on synthetic datasets composed of typical household objects with multi-object dynamics and interactions by a robot end effector. We also evaluate our model in model-predictive control for non-prehensile manipulation in simulation and for image-based tracking and prediction of object motion in simulated and real-world scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习面向刚性物体的动作条件与以对象为中心的高斯泼溅世界模型
世界模型使智能体能够预测其动作对环境的影响。本文提出多刚体高斯世界模型（MRO-GWM），一种学习三维空间中刚体动作条件动力学的新模型。利用以对象为中心的高斯，我们可以表示任意物体形状和多物体场景。我们开发了一种新颖的时空Transformer架构，从对象高斯的历史和未来动作中预测未来的刚体运动。物体在以对象为中心的坐标系中由高斯表示，从而可以将物体运动描述为刚体变换。我们的模型在来自多个视角的重建上进行训练，这要求模型还需处理因遮挡导致的物体部分观测。我们在由典型家居物体组成的合成数据集上分析了我们方法的预测性能，这些数据集包含多物体动力学以及机器人末端执行器的交互。我们还在模型预测控制中评估了我们的模型，用于仿真中的非抓取操作，以及用于仿真和真实场景中基于图像的对象运动跟踪与预测。
Summary / 总结
World models that predict the consequences of actions are important for intelligent agents, yet learning action-conditional rigid-object dynamics in 3D remains challenging. The authors propose MRO-GWM, which represents arbitrary object shapes and multi-object scenes with object-centric Gaussians and uses a temporal-spatial transformer to predict future rigid body motion from a history of object Gaussians and future actions, with motion expressed as a rigid body transformation in an object-centric frame; training on multi-view reconstructions forces the model to handle partial observations from occlusion. On synthetic datasets of household objects with multi-object dynamics and robot end-effector interactions, the model's prediction performance is analyzed, and it is further evaluated in model-predictive control for non-prehensile manipulation in simulation and in image-based tracking and prediction of object motion in simulated and real-world scenes.
世界模型使智能体能够预测自身动作对环境的影响，但在三维中表示任意物体形状和多物体场景仍具挑战。作者提出MRO-GWM，利用以物体为中心的高斯表示学习刚体在动作条件下的动力学，并设计了一种时空Transformer架构，从物体高斯的历史和未来动作预测未来的刚体运动；由于物体在以物体为中心的坐标系中表示，运动可描述为刚体变换，而基于多视角重建的训练要求模型处理遮挡导致的部分观测。在由典型家居物体构成的合成数据集上（包含多物体动力学和机器人末端执行器交互），作者分析了预测性能，并在仿真中的非抓取操作模型预测控制以及仿真和真实场景中基于图像的物体运动跟踪与预测上评估了该模型。
EvoRouter: Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent Routing via Insight-Guided On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Yitao Xiao, Xu Zhang, Ye Cheng, Shaoyong Guo, Yan Sun, Ziyue Zhu, Sitao Xie, Guo Tong, Haoran Luo
First: 2026-10-26T16:21:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:21:00+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 12 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Serving web queries at scale with multi-agent systems requires jointly configuring large language models, collaboration modes, agent counts, interaction rounds, and skills. However, the resulting configuration space grows combinatorially, while sparse execution rewards provide limited fine-grained supervision for interdependent planning decisions. We propose EvoRouter, a self-evolving multi-agent routing framework driven by insight-guided on-policy distillation. A dedicated expert periodically synthesizes performance-cost feedback from configuration trajectories across tasks into reusable conditional routing insights about capability requirements, returns from resource scaling, and interactions among configuration choices. A privileged teacher combines these evolving insights with current execution feedback to provide dense token-level supervision for the router's own planning trajectories through on-policy distillation. Cost-aware reinforcement learning jointly optimizes task performance and computational cost. Together, these training signals internalize execution insights into the routing policy. The updated policy generates new trajectories that support subsequent insight extraction, forming an execution-driven self-improvement loop. We evaluate EvoRouter on 19 in-domain datasets spanning five domains and two out-of-domain benchmarks. EvoRouter achieves an average in-domain accuracy of 68.69%, outperforming the strongest routing baseline by 7.23 percentage points while reducing inference compute cost by 50.95%. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/CenYangtze/EvoRouter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoRouter：通过洞察引导的在线策略蒸馏实现成本高效的多智能体路由
以多智能体系统大规模服务网络查询需要联合配置大语言模型、协作模式、智能体数量、交互轮次和技能。然而，由此产生的配置空间呈组合式增长，而稀疏的执行奖励对相互依赖的规划决策提供的细粒度监督有限。我们提出 EvoRouter，一个由洞察引导的在线策略蒸馏驱动的自演化多智能体路由框架。一个专门的专家周期性地将跨任务的配置轨迹中的性能-成本反馈综合为可复用的条件路由洞察，涉及能力需求、资源扩展带来的回报以及配置选择之间的交互。一个特权教师将这些不断演化的洞察与当前执行反馈相结合，通过在线策略蒸馏为路由器自身的规划轨迹提供密集的 token 级监督。成本感知强化学习联合优化任务性能和计算成本。这些训练信号共同将执行洞察内化到路由策略中。更新后的策略生成新的轨迹，支持后续的洞察提取，形成执行驱动的自我改进循环。我们在涵盖五个领域的 19 个域内数据集和两个域外基准上评估 EvoRouter。EvoRouter 的平均域内准确率达到 68.69%，比最强的路由基线高出 7.23 个百分点，同时将推理计算成本降低 50.95%。我们的代码开源于 https://github.com/CenYangtze/EvoRouter。
Summary / 总结
Serving web queries at scale with multi-agent systems requires jointly configuring models, collaboration modes, agent counts, interaction rounds, and skills, but the resulting configuration space grows combinatorially and sparse execution rewards offer limited fine-grained supervision for interdependent planning decisions. The authors propose EvoRouter, a self-evolving multi-agent routing framework driven by insight-guided on-policy distillation, in which a dedicated expert periodically synthesizes performance-cost feedback from configuration trajectories into reusable conditional routing insights, a privileged teacher combines these evolving insights with current execution feedback to provide dense token-level supervision for the router's own planning trajectories, and cost-aware reinforcement learning jointly optimizes task performance and computational cost, with updated policies generating new trajectories that feed subsequent insight extraction in an execution-driven self-improvement loop. Evaluated on 19 in-domain datasets spanning five domains and two out-of-domain benchmarks, EvoRouter achieves an average in-domain accuracy of 68.69%, outperforming the strongest routing baseline by 7.23 percentage points while reducing inference compute cost by 50.95%.

TrySee: Modular Perception-Enhanced VLM Grounding for Closed-Loop Robot Task Planning in Specialized Domains
Authors: Elias Hofele, Thomas Trapp, David Timmermann, Tristan Schnell, Georg Heppner, Arne Roennau
First: 2026-10-26T16:20:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:20:28+00:00
Comments: To be published in the proceedings of the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Biomimetics (ROBIO)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in high-level reasoning and task planning for robots. However, specialized domains remain a challenge for VLMs' image interpretation and grounding abilities. We introduce TrySee, a modular closed-loop robot task planning pipeline integrating general-purpose VLM reasoning with domain-specific perception and established robotics components. The system consists of an inherently reactive cyclic planning pipeline, augmented with domain-specific modular vision preprocessing, to provide online grounding. Finally, a dynamic preprocessing selection mechanism contributes to the scalability of the pipeline. The system requires neither VLM fine-tuning nor end-to-end demonstrations and adapts through replaceable perception modules and motion planning. The approach is evaluated on planning tasks across 5 widely different domains, including a real-world robot disassembly task. The field tests in a brownfield environment demonstrate the approach's practicality in integrating VLM planning into specialized robotic systems with limited domain-specific training data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TrySee：面向专业领域闭环机器人任务规划的模块化感知增强VLM接地
视觉语言模型（VLM）在机器人的高层推理和任务规划中已展现出令人印象深刻的能力。然而，专业领域对VLM的图像解释与接地能力仍构成挑战。我们提出TrySee，一种模块化闭环机器人任务规划流水线，将通用VLM推理与领域特定感知及成熟机器人组件相结合。该系统由本质上反应式的循环规划流水线构成，并辅以领域特定的模块化视觉预处理，以提供在线接地。最后，一种动态预处理选择机制有助于提升流水线的可扩展性。该系统既不需要VLM微调，也不需要端到端演示，并通过可替换的感知模块和运动规划实现自适应。该方法在涵盖5个差异显著领域的规划任务上进行了评估，包括一项真实世界的机器人拆解任务。在棕地环境中的现场测试表明，该方法在将VLM规划集成到领域特定训练数据有限的专用机器人系统方面具有实用性。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models handle high-level robot reasoning well but struggle with image interpretation and grounding in specialized domains, motivating TrySee, a modular closed-loop task-planning pipeline that pairs general-purpose VLM reasoning with domain-specific perception and established robotics components. The system uses a reactive cyclic planning loop augmented with modular domain-specific vision preprocessing for online grounding, plus a dynamic preprocessing selection mechanism for scalability, and requires neither VLM fine-tuning nor end-to-end demonstrations, adapting instead through replaceable perception and motion-planning modules. It was evaluated on planning tasks across five widely different domains, including a real-world robot disassembly task, and field tests in a brownfield environment showed the approach is practical for integrating VLM planning into specialized robotic systems with limited domain-specific training data.
视觉语言模型在机器人高层推理与任务规划方面表现良好，但在专业领域中的图像理解与grounding能力仍然不足。作者提出TrySee，一种模块化闭环机器人任务规划流程，将通用视觉语言模型推理与领域专用感知模块及成熟机器人组件相结合，采用反应式循环规划流程、面向在线grounding的模块化视觉预处理以及动态预处理选择机制以提升可扩展性，且无需对视觉语言模型进行微调或端到端示教。该方法在涵盖五个差异显著领域的规划任务上进行了评估，其中包括真实机器人拆解任务，并在棕地环境中的现场测试表明，在领域专用训练数据有限的情况下，将视觉语言模型规划集成到专业机器人系统中具有实用性。
Adapting LLM-SC to Living Sacred Heritage: Domain-Specific Extraction, Semantic Networks and Human Evaluation on Pashupatinath Temple Reviews
Authors: Hari Sitaula, Manish Sakhakarmy, Pujan Pant, Sagar Newpane
First: 2026-10-26T16:20:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:20:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) pipelines can turn visitor reviews into structured data, but generic schemes may miss rituals, sacred spaces, cultural restrictions and emotionally mixed experiences at living sacred heritage sites. We adapted the LLM-Social Computing (LLM-SC) framework to Pashupatinath Temple, Nepal, by specifying a 12-type entity taxonomy (four original LLM-SC types plus eight sacred-heritage types), four relation types and nine aspect-sentiment dimensions in a GPT-4o extraction prompt, which was applied to a fixed random sample of 2,500 English-language Tripadvisor reviews from a processed corpus of 3,939. The exports contain 11,286 entity, 6,232 aspect-sentiment and 5,590 relation records, covering 2,485, 2,487 and 2,433 reviews, respectively; the eight added sacred-heritage types account for 64.8% of entity records. Against an adjudicated two-annotator reference for 200 reviews, exact normalized surface-and-type matching gave provisional entity scores of 41.0% precision, 21.8% recall and 28.4% micro-F1. Aspect-presence F1 was 53.6% and end-to-end aspect-plus-polarity F1 was 42.6%. Human adjudication supported 153 of 194 first-listed relation candidates (one per review; 78.9%), a candidate-level rate that does not estimate relation recall or whole-graph precision. The contribution is a culturally grounded, domain-specific adaptation with component-wise human evaluation and explicit reproducibility and generalization limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将LLM-SC适配于活态神圣遗产：对帕斯帕提那神庙评论的领域特定抽取、语义网络与人工评估
大语言模型（LLM）流水线可将游客评论转化为结构化数据，但通用方案可能遗漏活态神圣遗产地点的仪式、神圣空间、文化限制和情感混合的体验。我们将LLM-Social Computing（LLM-SC）框架适配于尼泊尔帕斯帕提那神庙，在GPT-4o抽取提示中指定了12类实体分类体系（4个原始LLM-SC类型加8个神圣遗产类型）、4种关系类型和9个方面-情感维度，并将其应用于从3,939条已处理语料中固定随机抽取的2,500条英文Tripadvisor评论。导出结果包含11,286条实体记录、6,232条方面-情感记录和5,590条关系记录，分别覆盖2,485、2,487和2,433条评论；新增的8个神圣遗产类型占实体记录的64.8%。以200条评论的经裁决的双标注者参考为基准，精确归一化表面与类型匹配给出的暂定实体得分为41.0%精确率、21.8%召回率和28.4%微F1。方面存在性F1为53.6%，端到端方面加极性F1为42.6%。人工裁决支持了194个首列关系候选中的153个（每条评论一个；78.9%），该候选级比率不估计关系召回率或整图精确率。贡献在于一种具有文化根基的领域特定适配，配有分组件人工评估，并明确说明了可复现性和泛化性局限。
Summary / 总结
Generic LLM extraction schemes may overlook rituals, sacred spaces, cultural restrictions, and mixed emotional experiences at living sacred heritage sites, motivating a domain-specific adaptation of the LLM-Social Computing framework to Pashupatinath Temple, Nepal. The authors specified a 12-type entity taxonomy (four original types plus eight sacred-heritage types), four relation types, and nine aspect-sentiment dimensions in a GPT-4o extraction prompt, applied to a fixed random sample of 2,500 English Tripadvisor reviews drawn from a processed corpus of 3,939. 通用的大语言模型抽取方案可能遗漏活态圣地的仪式、神圣空间、文化禁忌和情感混杂的体验，因此作者将LLM-Social Computing框架适配到尼泊尔帕舒帕蒂纳特神庙。研究在GPT-4o抽取提示中设定了12类实体分类体系（原有4类加8类圣地类型）、4种关系和9个方面-情感维度，并应用于从3,939条已处理语料中固定随机抽取的2,500条英文Tripadvisor评论。导出结果包含11,286条实体记录、6,232条方面-情感记录和5,590条关系记录，新增的8类圣地类型占实体记录的64.8%。在200条评论的双标注者裁定参考集上，精确归一化表面与类型匹配得到暂定实体精确率41.0%、召回率21.8%、微F1为28.4%，方面存在性F1为53.6%，端到端方面加极性F1为42.6%；人工裁定支持了194个首列关系候选中的153个（78.9%），该候选级比率不估计关系召回率或整图精确率。
ProactEgoMem: Benchmarking Proactive Memory in Streaming Egocentric Video
Authors: Ruotong Liao, Meikel Kokowski, Xiaoxiao Sun, Yongqin Xian, Laura Bravo Sánchez, Shiye Su, Federico Tombari, Serena Yeung-Levy, Volker Tresp
First: 2026-10-21T17:37:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:19:38+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 22 figures, Project Page: https://proactegomem.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Today's multimodal assistants are typically prompted to interpret the visual evidence presented to them. To offload the cognitive burden of remembering across long horizons, wearable assistants should instead proactively acquire and maintain useful memory as the user's surroundings evolve. Doing so on the user's behalf requires identifying what matters, understanding the scene in real time, and initiating memory maintenance in the background while the user stays focused on their activities. We introduce ProactEgoMem, a benchmark that evaluates proactive memory in streaming egocentric video. Three tasks ground the evaluation in ambient environmental needs: Information Capture (IC) preserves transient yet dense visual information, State Tracking (ST) follows changing object states, and Location Tracking (LT) records object movements. The benchmark defines its protocol at two levels of proactiveness: the Notification Level asks for a single timely notification that satisfies the user's intent, while the deeper Intervention Level asks the agent to maintain memory on the user's behalf through an active start-to-stop tool lifecycle. Parallel ProactEgoMem-Daily and ProactEgoMem-Sci tracks contain 300/100 clips covering daily and scientific use cases, paired with 1000/500 questions in ProactEgoMem-QA, which measures the quality of the proactively acquired memory. Across proprietary, open-source, video-language, and streaming baselines, even the strongest models fail to capture memory both timely and accurately. ProactEgoMem provides a rigorous evaluation foundation for proactive multimodal assistance and for video agents that co-exist with their users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProactEgoMem：流式自我中心视频中主动记忆的基准测试
当今的多模态助手通常被提示去解读呈现给它们的视觉证据。为了减轻长时程记忆的认知负担，可穿戴助手应随着用户周围环境的变化主动获取并维护有用的记忆。代表用户执行这一任务需要识别重要信息、实时理解场景，并在用户专注于自身活动时于后台启动记忆维护。我们提出 ProactEgoMem，一个评估流式自我中心视频中主动记忆的基准。三个任务将评估建立在环境需求之上：信息捕获（IC）保留短暂而密集的视觉信息，状态跟踪（ST）跟踪变化的物体状态，位置跟踪（LT）记录物体移动。该基准在两个主动层级上定义其协议：通知层级要求给出一个满足用户意图的及时通知，而更深入的干预层级要求智能体通过主动的从启动到停止的工具生命周期代表用户维护记忆。并行的 ProactEgoMem-Daily 和 ProactEgoMem-Sci 赛道包含 300/100 个片段，覆盖日常和科学用例，并配有 ProactEgoMem-QA 中的 1000/500 个问题，用于衡量主动获取记忆的质量。在专有、开源、视频-语言和流式基线中，即使最强的模型也无法既及时又准确地捕获记忆。ProactEgoMem 为主动多模态辅助以及与用户共存视频智能体提供了严格的评估基础。

Multimodal assistants today typically wait to be prompted rather than proactively managing memory, which places a cognitive burden on users of wearable devices over long horizons. To address this, the authors introduce ProactEgoMem, a benchmark for evaluating proactive memory in streaming egocentric video, structured around three tasks (Information Capture, State Tracking, and Location Tracking) and two levels of proactiveness (Notification Level and Intervention Level), with parallel Daily and Scientific tracks containing 300/100 clips and 1000/500 QA questions.
