A Hybrid Framework for Explainable Aviation Risk: Causal Chain Extraction from Safety Reports with Physics-Based Failure Quantification and Interventional Analysis
Authors: Yutian Pang, Yifang Tian, Jiacheng Xie, Xinyu Zhao, Jueming Hu, John-Paul Clarke, Hans-Arno Jacobsen
First: 2026-10-26T16:03:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:03:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aviation authorities hold decades of accident reports whose narratives record how investigators reconstructed each accident. Computational work on these archives has mostly sorted reports into fixed taxonomies. In this work, we propose a hybrid framework in which a text-based corpus identifies the leading factors of a failure mode. Physics-based models then quantify how likely that failure is at a stated operating point. Ordered causal chains extracted from 56,202 American accident reports with a local large language model aggregate into a causation knowledge graph. On an evaluation sample of 2,000 reports, the chains recover 90.8% of investigator-flagged causes. Each physical factor whose drivers the record carries receives an occurrence model calibrated to the accident population. A leaky noisy-OR fitted to the corpus combines the factors into a failure mode probability that supports intervention. For engine power loss, the carburetor icing model separates 1,205 icing accidents from other accidents using temperature and dewpoint alone, reaching 0.61 in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve against a density ratio ceiling of 0.64. Routine weather hours give no valid control, because flying concentrates in fair weather. On later years the physics adds 0.03 to that area for occurrence and nothing for severity, so its value lies in interpretability. On four named accidents, abduction gives a probability of necessity of 0.29 to 0.50 for the heat omission, under monotonicity. The Fast Causal Inference algorithm does not license the largest backdoor adjustment on the learned networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可解释航空风险的混合框架：从安全报告中提取因果链并结合基于物理的失效量化与干预分析
航空管理机构保存了数十年的航空事故报告，其叙述记录了调查人员如何重建每起事故。对这些档案的计算工作大多将报告归入固定分类体系。在本研究中，我们提出一个混合框架：基于文本的语料库识别某一失效模式的主导因素，基于物理的模型则量化该失效在给定运行点发生的可能性。利用本地大语言模型从56,202份美国事故报告中提取的有序因果链聚合为因果知识图谱。在2,000份报告的评估样本上，这些因果链恢复了90.8%的调查人员标记原因。对于记录中包含其驱动因素的每个物理因素，均获得一个以事故总体校准的发生模型。对语料库拟合的泄漏噪声或模型将这些因素组合为支持干预的失效模式概率。对于发动机功率损失，化油器结冰模型仅利用温度和露点即可将1,205起结冰事故与其他事故区分开，在受试者工作特征曲线下面积上达到0.61，而密度比上限为0.64。常规天气时段无法提供有效对照，因为飞行集中于晴好天气。在较晚年份，物理模型使发生面积的该指标增加0.03，而对严重程度没有增加，因此其价值在于可解释性。在四起具名事故上，在单调性假设下，溯因给出热遗漏的必要性概率为0.29至0.50。快速因果推断算法并不允许在学习到的网络上进行最大的后门调整。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the fact that computational work on aviation accident archives has largely been limited to sorting reports into fixed taxonomies, and proposes a hybrid framework that combines text-based corpus analysis with physics-based failure quantification and interventional analysis. Ordered causal chains are extracted from 56,202 American accident reports using a local large language model and aggregated into a causation knowledge graph, with each physical factor receiving an occurrence model calibrated to the accident population and combined via a leaky noisy-OR into a failure mode probability. On a 2,000-report evaluation sample the chains recover 90.8% of investigator-flagged causes; for engine power loss, a carburetor icing model separates 1,205 icing accidents from others using temperature and dewpoint alone, reaching 0.61 area under the ROC curve against a density ratio ceiling of 0.64, with physics adding 0.03 to that area in later years for occurrence but nothing for severity, and abduction on four named accidents yields a probability of necessity of 0.29 to 0.50 for the heat omission under monotonicity, while the Fast Causal Inference algorithm does not license the largest backdoor adjustment on the learned networks.
航空安全档案中积累了大量叙述性事故报告，但已有计算工作多将其归入固定分类体系，未能充分利用其中的因果信息。本文提出一种混合框架，利用本地大语言模型从56,202份美国事故报告中抽取有序因果链并汇总为因果知识图谱，再将文本识别的因素与基于物理的发生模型及泄漏噪声或门相结合，估计失效模式概率以支持干预。在2,000份报告的评估样本上，抽取的因果链覆盖了调查人员标注原因的90.8%。针对发动机功率损失，化油器结冰模型仅凭温度和露点即可将1,205起结冰事故与其他事故区分开，受试者工作特征曲线下面积达0.61，接近密度比上限0.64，但常规天气时段因飞行集中于晴好天气而无法提供有效对照；在后几年数据上，物理模型使发生概率的曲线下面积仅提高0.03，对严重程度无提升，其价值主要在于可解释性。对四起具名事故的反绎分析在单调性假设下给出热遗漏必要概率为0.29至0.50，且快速因果推断算法不支持在学习到的网络上进行最大后门调整。
Prompt PerturbAItions: Mechanisms of LLM Response Inconsistencies
Authors: Soorya Ram Shimgekar, Agam Goyal, Amruta Parulekar, Joshua Chen, Yian Wang, Navin Kumar, Hari Sundaram, Eshwar Chandrasekharan, Koustuv Saha
First: 2026-05-29T06:58:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T16:02:17+00:00
Comments: Preprint: 16 pages, 17 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in conversational settings where user phrasing ranges from polite to casual to toxic, yet less is known about whether such lexical variation in otherwise semantically equivalent prompts can degrade factual reliability. We study how lexical and tone-based prompt perturbations affect the factual reliability of LLMs. Using controlled prompt variations spanning polite, random, and increasingly toxic levels, we evaluate seven LLMs on ARC-Easy, GSM8K, and MMLU. We find that lexical perturbations reduce factual accuracy and increase predictive uncertainty in most models. Interestingly, increasing toxicity results in decreasing accuracy. To examine whether these answer inconsistencies correspond to internal changes, we conduct attribution-graph analyses of model activations and influences. We find that perturbations amplify perturbation-sensitive variant nodes relative to core reasoning nodes. We also observed a monotonic trend with increasing toxicity score and increasing activation and influence in Variant nodes. These findings position prompt wording and tone as a critical dimension of LLM reliability and provide behavioral and mechanistic evidence that surface-level lexical variation can alter factual outputs and internal computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提示扰动：LLM响应不一致的机制
大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地部署在对话场景中，用户措辞从礼貌到随意再到有毒不等，然而对于这种在语义等价提示中的词汇变化是否会降低事实可靠性，人们知之甚少。我们研究词汇和语气层面的提示扰动如何影响LLM的事实可靠性。使用涵盖礼貌、随机和日益有毒水平的受控提示变体，我们在ARC-Easy、GSM8K和MMLU上评估了七个LLM。我们发现词汇扰动降低了大多数模型的事实准确性并增加了预测不确定性。有趣的是，增加毒性会导致准确性下降。为了检验这些答案不一致是否对应内部变化，我们对模型激活和影响进行了归因图分析。我们发现扰动相对于核心推理节点放大了扰动敏感变体节点。我们还观察到随着毒性分数增加以及变体节点中激活和影响增加而呈现单调趋势。这些发现将提示措辞和语气定位为LLM可靠性的关键维度，并提供了行为和机制证据，表明表面词汇变化可以改变事实输出和内部计算。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses whether lexically and tonally varied but semantically equivalent prompts, ranging from polite to casual to toxic, degrade the factual reliability of large language models. The authors evaluate seven LLMs on ARC-Easy, GSM8K, and MMLU using controlled prompt perturbations and then perform attribution-graph analyses of model activations and influences to probe internal mechanisms. They find that lexical perturbations reduce factual accuracy and increase predictive uncertainty in most models, with accuracy decreasing monotonically as toxicity increases; mechanistically, perturbations amplify perturbation-sensitive variant nodes relative to core reasoning nodes, and increasing toxicity produces a monotonic rise in activation and influence within variant nodes, linking surface-level wording and tone to both behavioral and internal changes in factual computation.
该研究关注一个具有实际意义的问题：在语义等价但措辞和语气不同（从礼貌到随意乃至冒犯性）的提示下，大语言模型的事实可靠性是否会下降。作者在ARC-Easy、GSM8K和MMLU上评估了七个大语言模型，使用涵盖礼貌、随机以及逐级升级的冒犯性语气等受控提示变体，并结合归因图分析模型的激活与影响。结果表明，词汇扰动在多数模型中降低了事实准确率并提高了预测不确定性，且准确率随冒犯性程度上升而单调下降；在机制层面，扰动会相对核心推理节点放大对扰动敏感的变体节点，变体节点的激活与影响随冒犯性得分单调上升，从而在行为与内部计算两个层面提供了表层措辞和语气能够改变事实输出与内部计算的证据。
Reasoning-Aware Multicalibrated Reliability Predictors for Language Models
Authors: Ekaterina Fedorova, Alessandro Morosini, Chara Podimata
First: 2026-10-19T16:06:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:52:31+00:00
Comments: Added detailed AI Use Statement
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As people increasingly rely on large language models (LLMs) for information, model providers need to communicate meaningful estimates of whether individual responses are correct or, more generally, reliable. In the context of hallucinations, existing work uses multicalibration to make such reliability predictions meaningful across application-relevant groups, such as prompt topics. Topic groups alone, however, can conceal systematic errors: within the same topic and confidence level, responses with different chain-of-thought (CoT) properties may exhibit opposing errors that cancel on average. In this work, we introduce reasoning-aware multicalibration, a framework that supplements application-relevant topic groups with groups derived from observable properties of CoT traces. We highlight that, theoretically, groupings based on CoT are "internally important", i.e., groupings for which the multicalibration guarantee is not societally crucial as it is for high-risk topics, but whose inclusion in the multicalibration procedure could easily and significantly improve predictive accuracy of the final reliability predictor. Empirically, we apply our framework to hallucination prediction and unsafe-response detection across a variety of benchmarks, and find that adding CoT groups to topic-based calibration reduces Brier score in both tasks. Interestingly, we find that multicalibrating with respect to CoT groups alone (without any topic information) already improves calibration across topics, suggesting that the predictor's errors depend on how the model reasoned, rather than what it was asked about in the prompt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向语言模型的推理感知多校准可靠性预测器
随着人们日益依赖大语言模型（LLM）获取信息，模型提供方需要传达有意义的估计，说明单个回答是否正确，或更一般地说，是否可靠。在幻觉语境下，现有工作使用多校准使此类可靠性预测在应用相关群体（如提示主题）上具有意义。然而，仅按主题分组可能掩盖系统性错误：在同一主题和置信水平内，具有不同思维链（CoT）属性的回答可能表现出相互抵消的平均错误。在本工作中，我们引入推理感知多校准，这是一个框架，用由CoT轨迹的可观察属性导出的分组来补充应用相关主题分组。我们从理论上强调，基于CoT的分组是“内部重要”的，即对于此类分组，多校准保证不像对高风险主题那样具有社会关键性，但将其纳入多校准过程可以轻松且显著地提高最终可靠性预测器的预测准确性。在实证上，我们将该框架应用于多种基准上的幻觉预测和不安全响应检测，发现将CoT分组加入基于主题的校准可在两项任务中降低Brier分数。有趣的是，我们发现仅针对CoT分组进行多校准（不含任何主题信息）已经改善了跨主题的校准，这表明预测器的错误取决于模型如何推理，而不是提示中询问了什么。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the need for meaningful reliability estimates for large language model responses, noting that calibration based only on application-relevant groups such as prompt topics can mask systematic errors, since responses within the same topic and confidence level but with different chain-of-thought properties may exhibit opposing errors that cancel out. The authors introduce reasoning-aware multicalibration, a framework that supplements topic groups with groups derived from observable properties of chain-of-thought traces, and they argue theoretically that such CoT-based groupings are internally important: not societally crucial like high-risk topics, but capable of substantially improving the accuracy of the final reliability predictor when included. Empirically, applying the framework to hallucination prediction and unsafe-response detection across multiple benchmarks shows that adding CoT groups to topic-based calibration reduces Brier score in both tasks, and notably, multicalibrating with respect to CoT groups alone, without any topic information, already improves calibration across topics, suggesting that predictor errors depend more on how the model reasoned than on what the prompt asked.
该论文关注为大语言模型回答提供有意义的可靠性估计这一问题，指出已有的基于提示主题等应用相关分组的多校准方法可能掩盖系统性错误，因为同一主题、同一置信水平下具有不同思维链特征的回答可能出现方向相反、在平均意义上相互抵消的错误。作者提出推理感知多校准框架，在应用相关主题分组之外，补充由思维链轨迹可观测属性导出的分组，并从理论上说明这类基于思维链的分组具有内部重要性，即其社会重要性不如高风险主题分组，但纳入多校准过程能够轻松且显著提升最终可靠性预测器的准确率。在实验中，作者将该框架应用于多种基准上的幻觉预测和不安全回答检测，发现将思维链分组加入基于主题的校准后，两项任务的Brier分数均有所降低；有趣的是，仅针对思维链分组进行多校准（不含任何主题信息）就已能改善跨主题的校准效果，表明预测器的误差更多取决于模型的推理方式，而非提示所询问的内容。
Adapt for Planning, Not just Prediction: Optimistic Test-Time World Model Adaptation
Authors: Akshay Mete, Dileep Kalathil, P. R. Kumar
Venue: CoRL 2026
First: 2026-10-26T15:46:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:46:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the CoRL 2026 Workshop on Pretrain to Adapt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Latent world models are typically pre-trained offline and kept frozen at test time, so planning with them can degrade under distribution shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this degradation by updating the model during deployment. Unlike pre-training on fixed data, TTA for planning is closed-loop: the planner uses model rollouts to select actions, and the resulting transitions drive the next update. This raises the question: which objective should guide TTA for planning? Under a prediction-only objective, the model is fit only to the outcomes of its own actions, so it can remain wrong about the actions that would reach the goal, and low prediction error need not translate into better planning. Inspired by reward-biased maximum likelihood from adaptive control, we introduce Optimistic Test-Time Adaptation (OpTTA). Its objective has two terms, the prediction loss on observed transitions and the planner's goal-reaching cost on its candidate rollouts, both minimized over the model parameters. The planning term biases adaptation toward models that are optimistic about reaching the goal, without any additional environment interaction. Across visual and environment shifts on manipulation, reaching and navigation tasks, OpTTA raises final planning success over the frozen model by 13.5 percentage points on average, compared with 6.9 for the prediction-only objective (AdaJEPA), when only the predictor is adapted. When the encoder is adapted as well, the gains are 13.5 and 7.5 points. These experiments span three JEPA world models with different encoders and training objectives and, for temporal straightening, both CEM and gradient-based planners. Our results suggest that test-time adaptation of world models for planning benefits from an optimistic, task-aware term in addition to the prediction loss.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为规划而适应，而不仅是预测：乐观的测试时世界模型适应
潜在世界模型通常离线预训练并在测试时保持冻结，因此在其上进行规划在分布偏移下可能退化。测试时适应（TTA）可通过在部署期间更新模型来缓解这种退化。与在固定数据上预训练不同，用于规划的TTA是闭环的：规划器利用模型推演来选择动作，由此产生的转移驱动下一次更新。这引出了一个问题：哪个目标应指导用于规划的TTA？在仅预测目标下，模型仅拟合自身动作的结果，因此它可能对能够达到目标的动作仍然判断错误，低预测误差未必转化为更好的规划。受自适应控制中奖励偏置最大似然的启发，我们提出了乐观测试时适应（OpTTA）。其目标包含两项：观测转移上的预测损失，以及规划器在其候选推演上的目标达成代价，二者均对模型参数最小化。规划项使适应偏向于对达成目标持乐观态度的模型，且无需任何额外的环境交互。在操作、到达和导航任务上的视觉与环境偏移中，当仅适应预测器时，OpTTA将最终规划成功率相比冻结模型平均提高13.5个百分点，而仅预测目标（AdaJEPA）为6.9个百分点。当编码器也被适应时，增益分别为13.5和7.5个百分点。这些实验涵盖三个具有不同编码器和训练目标的JEPA世界模型，并且对于时间拉直，涵盖CEM和基于梯度的规划器。我们的结果表明，用于规划的世界模型测试时适应受益于在预测损失之外加入乐观的、任务感知的项。
Summary / 总结
Latent world models are usually pre-trained offline and frozen at test time, which can degrade planning under distribution shift, and test-time adaptation (TTA) for planning is closed-loop, raising the question of which objective should guide updates. The paper introduces Optimistic Test-Time Adaptation (OpTTA), whose objective combines the prediction loss on observed transitions with the planner's goal-reaching cost on its candidate rollouts, both minimized over model parameters, so adaptation is biased toward models optimistic about reaching the goal without extra environment interaction. Across visual and environment shifts in manipulation, reaching, and navigation tasks, OpTTA raises final planning success over the frozen model by 13.5 percentage points on average versus 6.9 for the prediction-only objective (AdaJEPA) when only the predictor is adapted, and by 13.5 versus 7.5 points when the encoder is also adapted, spanning three JEPA world models and both CEM and gradient-based planners.
潜世界模型通常离线预训练并在测试时保持冻结，因此在分布偏移下规划性能可能下降，而面向规划的测试时自适应（TTA）是闭环的，这就引出了应以何种目标引导自适应的问题。作者指出仅以预测为目标只会让模型拟合自身动作的结果，未必改善规划，因而受自适应控制中奖励偏置最大似然启发提出乐观测试时自适应（OpTTA），其目标由两项组成：观测转移上的预测损失，以及规划器在其候选轨迹上的目标达成代价，二者均对模型参数最小化且无需额外环境交互。在操作、到达与导航任务的视觉和环境偏移下，仅自适应预测器时，OpTTA 相对冻结模型的最终规划成功率平均提升 13.5 个百分点，而仅预测目标（AdaJEPA）为 6.9；同时自适应编码器时分别为 13.5 和 7.5 个百分点，实验涵盖三个 JEPA 世界模型以及 CEM 和基于梯度的规划器。
Unbiased Alignment for Large Language Models with Noisy Preferences
Authors: Jialiang Wang, Xianming Liu, Xiong Zhou, Hui Liu, Haoliang Li
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-03T12:04:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:40:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The alignment of large language models with human preferences is commonly achieved through Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback or Direct Preference Optimization. However, these methods are vulnerable to the significant noise prevalent in real-world preference datasets. To address this critical issue, we present a theoretical framework for unbiased alignment, introducing the Unbiased Reward Model (URM) loss and the Unbiased Direct Preference Optimization (UDPO) loss. By mathematically correcting the distortion induced by preference noise, our novel objectives enable unbiased model training directly from noisy datasets, without requiring clean ground-truth supervision. We provide rigorous theoretical analyses demonstrating that our methods are noise-tolerant, parameter downward compatible, and classification-calibrated. Comprehensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our approaches outperform state-of-the-art baselines. Code available at: https://github.com/cswjl/unbiased-alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向具有噪声偏好数据的大语言模型的无偏对齐
大语言模型与人类偏好的对齐通常通过基于人类反馈的强化学习或直接偏好优化来实现。然而，这些方法容易受到现实世界偏好数据集中普遍存在的显著噪声的影响。为解决这一关键问题，我们提出了一个无偏对齐的理论框架，引入了无偏奖励模型（URM）损失和无偏直接偏好优化（UDPO）损失。通过在数学上校正偏好噪声引起的失真，我们的新目标函数能够直接从噪声数据集中进行无偏模型训练，而无需干净的 ground-truth 监督。我们提供了严格的理论分析，证明我们的方法具有噪声容忍性、参数向下兼容性和分类校准性。在多个数据集上的综合实验表明，我们的方法优于最先进的基线。代码见：https://github.com/cswjl/unbiased-alignment。

They provide theoretical analyses showing the methods are noise-tolerant, parameter downward compatible, and classification-calibrated, and comprehensive experiments across diverse datasets show that the proposed approaches outperform state-of-the-art baselines.

From Language to Drives: A Multimodal Framework for Aligning Autonomous Robots with Human Purposes
Authors: Diana Cerdas-Vargas, Alejandro Romero, Richard J. Duro
First: 2026-10-26T15:40:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:40:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work explores a multimodal approach to the alignment of the behavior of open-ended learning autonomous robots with human purposes through the use of large language models as cultural enablers. While autonomous robots can generate their own skills and goals through interaction with the environment, they often lack mechanisms to ensure that these remain relevant and aligned to those of human users. The proposed approach makes use of the general and cultural knowledge contained in LLMs and VLMs to establish a multimodal interactive alignment process in which human purposes, expressed in natural language, are transformed into motivational components of a robot architecture in the form of robot missions and drives that guide the behavior of the robot. The system is structured into three layers for semantic alignment, mission generation, and drive modeling. This design enables a bidirectional interaction loop: human inputs define high-level purposes, robots expand them into executable missions making use of multimodal information relayed by LLMs and VLMs, and feedback maintains alignment with human intent. Experiments in simulation and with a physical robot demonstrate that the process produces valid missions and behaviors, supporting effective human-agent alignment in interactive scenarios with autonomous robots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从语言到驱动力：一种使自主机器人与人类目的对齐的多模态框架
本工作探索了一种多模态方法，通过将大语言模型用作文化赋能器，使开放式学习自主机器人的行为与人类目的对齐。虽然自主机器人能够通过与环境的交互生成自身的技能和目标，但它们往往缺乏确保这些技能和目标保持相关性并与人类用户目标对齐的机制。所提出的方法利用LLM和VLM中包含的通用与文化知识，建立多模态交互式对齐过程，其中以自然语言表达的人类目的被转化为机器人架构的动机组件，形式为引导机器人行为的机器人任务与驱动力。该系统分为三层，分别用于语义对齐、任务生成和驱动力建模。这一设计实现了双向交互循环：人类输入定义高层目的，机器人利用LLM和VLM传递的多模态信息将其扩展为可执行任务，反馈则维持与人类意图的对齐。在仿真和实体机器人上的实验表明，该过程产生了有效的任务和行为，支持在自主机器人的交互场景中实现有效的人-智能体对齐。
Summary / 总结
Open-ended learning robots can generate their own skills and goals but often lack mechanisms to keep these aligned with human purposes, motivating a multimodal approach that uses large language and vision-language models as cultural enablers. The proposed framework establishes an interactive alignment process in which human purposes expressed in natural language are transformed into motivational components of the robot architecture, specifically missions and drives, through three layers for semantic alignment, mission generation, and drive modeling, with a bidirectional loop in which humans set high-level purposes, robots expand them into executable missions using multimodal information, and feedback maintains alignment. Experiments in simulation and with a physical robot show that the process produces valid missions and behaviors, supporting effective human-agent alignment in interactive scenarios with autonomous robots.
开放式学习的自主机器人能够通过与环境的交互自行生成技能和目标，但往往缺乏确保这些技能和目标与人类用户意图保持一致的有效机制。本文提出一种多模态交互式对齐方法，利用大语言模型和视觉语言模型所包含的通用与文化知识，将用自然语言表达的人类目的转化为机器人架构中的动机成分，即任务与驱力，系统分为语义对齐、任务生成和驱力建模三个层次，并通过双向交互回路结合人类反馈维持对齐。仿真实验和实体机器人实验表明，该过程能够生成有效的任务和行为，在自主机器人的交互场景中实现了有效的人机对齐。
ArchiveLens: A Generative Spatial Knowledge Framework for Semantic Reconstruction of Historical Maps
Authors: Yanan Wu, Yalin Yang, Chengbin Deng
First: 2026-10-26T15:29:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:29:24+00:00
Comments: 31 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Historical maps contain dense morphological and semantic information about past urban environments, but many digitization workflows focus primarily on extracting geometry, and do not capture cartographic semantics such as building use and construction materials. This study introduces ArchiveLens, a generative spatial knowledge framework that operationalizes historical maps as entity-centric, machine-queryable geospatial databases. The pipeline decouples morphological and semantic reconstruction through three stages: tiled YOLOv11-seg instance segmentation for building footprint extraction; deterministic material classification via hue, saturation, and value (HSV)-based color thresholds; and localized attribute parsing using a quantized vision-language model constrained by domain-specific prompts. Extracted entities are integrated into a hybrid Spatial-Temporal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (ST-RAG) architecture that combines dense semantic retrieval with symbolic spatio-temporal filtering and mandatory provenance grounding. Applied to 445 georeferenced Sanborn map sheets of Chicago (1918-1950), the framework achieves high geometric and semantic accuracy. Beyond performance, ArchiveLens advances GIScience by formalizing historical cartography as structured spatial knowledge representation, allowing researchers to query building attributes and compare spatial patterns across map sheets and years.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ArchiveLens：一种用于历史地图语义重建的生成式空间知识框架
历史地图包含关于过去城市环境的密集形态学与语义信息，但许多数字化工作流程主要侧重于提取几何信息，并未捕捉诸如建筑用途和建筑材料等制图语义。本研究引入 ArchiveLens，一种生成式空间知识框架，将历史地图转化为以实体为中心、可机器查询的地理空间数据库。该流程通过三个阶段将形态学重建与语义重建解耦：基于分块 YOLOv11-seg 的实例分割以提取建筑轮廓；通过基于色相、饱和度和明度（HSV）的颜色阈值进行确定性材料分类；以及使用受领域特定提示约束的量化视觉语言模型进行局部属性解析。提取的实体被集成到一种混合时空检索增强生成（ST-RAG）架构中，该架构将稠密语义检索与符号化时空过滤以及强制性的来源溯源相结合。应用于芝加哥（1918-1950）的 445 幅地理配准 Sanborn 地图图幅，该框架实现了较高的几何与语义精度。除性能之外，ArchiveLens 通过将历史制图形式化为结构化空间知识表示，使研究人员能够查询建筑属性并比较跨地图图幅和年份的空间模式，从而推进了 GIScience。
Summary / 总结
Historical map digitization workflows typically extract geometry while neglecting cartographic semantics such as building use and construction materials, motivating ArchiveLens, a generative spatial knowledge framework that treats historical maps as entity-centric, machine-queryable geospatial databases. The pipeline decouples morphological and semantic reconstruction into three stages: tiled YOLOv11-seg instance segmentation for building footprint extraction, deterministic material classification via HSV-based color thresholds, and localized attribute parsing with a quantized vision-language model constrained by domain-specific prompts; extracted entities are then integrated into a hybrid Spatial-Temporal Retrieval-Augmented Generation architecture combining dense semantic retrieval with symbolic spatio-temporal filtering and mandatory provenance grounding. Applied to 445 georeferenced Sanborn map sheets of Chicago (1918-1950), the framework achieves high geometric and semantic accuracy and enables researchers to query building attributes and compare spatial patterns across map sheets and years.
历史地图数字化流程通常侧重提取几何信息，而忽略建筑用途、建筑材料等地图语义，ArchiveLens 这一生成式空间知识框架因此将历史地图转化为以实体为中心、可机器查询的地理空间数据库。该流程将形态学与语义重建解耦为三个阶段：基于分块 YOLOv11-seg 的实例分割提取建筑轮廓，基于 HSV 颜色阈值的确定性材料分类，以及由领域特定提示约束的量化视觉语言模型进行局部属性解析；提取的实体随后整合进混合时空检索增强生成架构，结合稠密语义检索、符号化时空过滤和强制来源溯源。该框架应用于芝加哥 1918 至 1950 年间 445 幅地理配准的 Sanborn 地图图幅，取得较高的几何与语义精度，并将历史制图形式化为结构化空间知识表示，使研究者能够查询建筑属性并比较不同图幅与年份间的空间模式。
Evolve on Graph: Self-Evolving Knowledge Graph Reasoning without Question Annotations
Authors: Zongyuan Sun, Shuai Zhao, Ke Shu, Ziyue Zhu, Shangyang Wu, Yuanlin Lin, Tao Wang, Shiyue Li, Yingjun Du, Anh Tuan Luu, Haoran Luo
First: 2026-10-26T15:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Knowledge graphs (KGs) provide reliable external knowledge for multi-hop factual reasoning with large language models (LLMs), yet most KGs in real-world domains such as finance and industry lack question--answer (QA) annotations. Without such annotations, existing methods struggle to improve LLM graph reasoning: training-free agents cannot improve their exploration through interaction; reinforcement learning (RL) agents depend on QA data annotated by humans or synthesized by closed-source models; and training data is typically built offline, so its topology mix is fixed and cannot follow the agent's ability. We propose Evolve on Graph (EoG), a framework that lets an LLM self-evolve its graph reasoning without any QA annotations. Through Graph Self-Play, a single LLM alternates between a Proposer that poses questions and a Solver that answers them. To make questions verifiable, Symbolically Verifiable Topology Synthesis (SVTS) samples reasoning topologies on the KG and executes the corresponding first-order logic queries, yielding topology skeletons with correct and complete answer sets; guided by a skeleton, the Proposer explores the graph to pose a multi-hop question. To let task topologies follow the Solver, a Boundary-Aware Graph Curriculum rewards the Proposer by the Solver's score variance, keeping questions at the Solver's capability boundary so that complex topologies enter training as the Solver improves. On six benchmarks over Freebase, Wikidata, and an industrial KG, EoG consistently outperforms prompting-, retrieval-, and agent-based methods, showing that LLM graph reasoning can improve through self-play without QA annotations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Szy5/EoG
中文标题/摘要
标题：在图上演化：无需问题标注的自演化知识图谱推理
知识图谱（KG）为大语言模型（LLM）的多跳事实推理提供可靠的外部知识，然而金融、工业等真实领域中的大多数KG缺乏问答（QA）标注。缺少此类标注时，现有方法难以提升LLM的图推理能力：免训练智能体无法通过交互改进其探索；强化学习（RL）智能体依赖人工标注或闭源模型合成的QA数据；且训练数据通常离线构建，其拓扑混合固定，无法跟随智能体能力变化。我们提出在图上演化（EoG）框架，使LLM无需任何QA标注即可自演化其图推理能力。通过图自博弈，单个LLM在提问者与求解者之间交替。为使问题可验证，符号可验证拓扑合成（SVTS）在KG上采样推理拓扑并执行相应的一阶逻辑查询，生成具有正确且完整答案集的拓扑骨架；提问者在骨架引导下探索图以提出多跳问题。为使任务拓扑跟随求解者，边界感知图课程依据求解者的得分方差奖励提问者，将问题保持在求解者能力边界，从而随求解者提升使复杂拓扑进入训练。在Freebase、Wikidata及一个工业KG的六个基准上，EoG持续优于基于提示、检索和智能体的方法，表明LLM图推理可通过自博弈在无QA标注下得到提升。代码见 https://github.com/Szy5/EoG
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge of improving large language model (LLM) multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs (KGs) in domains where question-answer annotations are unavailable, since existing training-free agents cannot improve through interaction and reinforcement learning agents rely on annotated or synthesized QA data. The authors propose Evolve on Graph (EoG), a framework in which a single LLM self-evolves via Graph Self-Play, alternating between a Proposer that generates questions and a Solver that answers them; to ensure verifiability, Symbolically Verifiable Topology Synthesis samples reasoning topologies on the KG and executes first-order logic queries to produce topology skeletons with correct and complete answer sets, which guide the Proposer to pose multi-hop questions, while a Boundary-Aware Graph Curriculum rewards the Proposer based on the Solver's score variance to keep questions at the Solver's capability boundary. Experiments on six benchmarks over Freebase, Wikidata, and an industrial KG show that EoG consistently outperforms prompting-, retrieval-, and agent-based methods, demonstrating that LLM graph reasoning can improve through self-play without QA annotations.
该论文针对现实领域（如金融和工业）中的知识图谱普遍缺乏问答标注、导致现有免训练智能体、强化学习智能体以及离线构建的训练数据难以提升大语言模型图推理能力的问题，提出了Evolve on Graph（EoG）框架，使单个大语言模型无需任何问答标注即可自我演化图推理能力。该方法通过图自我博弈让同一个模型在提问者与求解者之间交替，并利用符号可验证拓扑合成在知识图谱上采样推理拓扑、执行相应一阶逻辑查询，生成具有正确且完整答案集的拓扑骨架来引导多跳问题生成；同时采用边界感知图课程，依据求解者的得分方差奖励提问者，使问题保持在求解者能力边界，从而随求解者进步逐步引入复杂拓扑。在Freebase、Wikidata及一个工业知识图谱上的六个基准实验中，EoG持续优于基于提示、检索和智能体的方法，表明大语言模型的图推理能力可在无问答标注的情况下通过自我博弈得到提升。
DialFork: Going Beyond Factuality to Evaluate Procedural Hallucination in Conversational Agents
Authors: Sergio Burdisso, Esaú Villatoro-Tello, Dairazalia Sánchez-Cortés, Yanis Labrak, Jordi Luque, Milos Cernak, Petr Motlicek
First: 2026-10-15T09:18:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:21:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) deployed as conversational agents are increasingly expected to follow procedural logic. While factual hallucination has received extensive attention, a different failure mode has been overlooked: procedural hallucination, in which an agent produces individually fluent and factually plausible utterances while deviating from the intended conversational process. We introduce DialFork, the first benchmark to measure this failure mode on raw dialogue text. DialFork is built by forking conformant dialogues at controlled turn positions and injecting one of four hallucination types, yielding 36,278 dialogues across 24 tasks in 12 domains; 30 human annotators validate the construction. We define three evaluation tasks that characterize procedural deviation at three granularities: how far a set of dialogues deviates from conformant ones (monitoring deployed agents), how far a single dialogue does (procedural retrieval), and at which turn a dialogue first goes off-track (real-time guardrails). For the first two, we propose dialog-flow-aware metrics, FlowKL and FlowScore, that compare dialogues by how they unfold rather than their content. At the dialogue level, FlowScore significantly outperforms all baselines across seven encoders, showing that semantic similarity is no substitute for procedural similarity. We release DialFork under open licenses at https://github.com/idiap/dialfork, hoping the community will measure conversational process alongside content.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DialFork：超越事实性，评估对话式智能体中的程序性幻觉
作为对话式智能体部署的大型语言模型（LLM）日益被期望遵循程序性逻辑。尽管事实性幻觉已受到广泛关注，但另一种失败模式却被忽视：程序性幻觉，即智能体生成单句流畅且事实合理的话语，却偏离了预期的对话流程。我们提出 DialFork，这是首个在原始对话文本上衡量该失败模式的基准。DialFork 通过在受控轮次位置对合规对话进行分叉并注入四种幻觉类型之一构建，涵盖 12 个领域 24 个任务的 36,278 段对话；30 名人工标注者验证了构建过程。我们定义了三个评估任务，从三个粒度刻画程序性偏离：一组对话偏离合规对话的程度（监控已部署智能体）、单段对话的偏离程度（程序性检索），以及对话首次偏离轨道的轮次（实时护栏）。对于前两个任务，我们提出对话流感知指标 FlowKL 和 FlowScore，它们通过对话的展开方式而非内容进行比较。在对话层面，FlowScore 在七个编码器上显著优于所有基线，表明语义相似性无法替代程序性相似性。我们以开放许可证在 https://github.com/idiap/dialfork 发布 DialFork，希望社区在衡量内容的同时也衡量对话过程。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses procedural hallucination in LLM-based conversational agents, a failure mode in which utterances are individually fluent and factually plausible but deviate from the intended conversational process, which existing factuality-focused evaluation overlooks. The authors introduce DialFork, a benchmark built by forking conformant dialogues at controlled turn positions and injecting four hallucination types, yielding 36,278 dialogues across 24 tasks in 12 domains, validated by 30 human annotators, and define three evaluation tasks at different granularities (monitoring, procedural retrieval, and real-time guardrails) with dialog-flow-aware metrics FlowKL and FlowScore that compare how dialogues unfold rather than their content. At the dialogue level, FlowScore significantly outperforms all baselines across seven encoders, indicating that semantic similarity does not substitute for procedural similarity.
基于大语言模型的对话智能体日益被要求遵循既定的流程逻辑，但现有评估几乎只关注事实性幻觉，忽视了一种失效模式：智能体生成的语句流畅且事实合理，却偏离了预期的对话流程。作者提出 DialFork，这是首个在原始对话文本上衡量该问题的基准，通过在受控轮次位置对合规对话进行分叉并注入四类流程幻觉构建而成，覆盖12个领域的24项任务，共36,278段对话，并由30名人工标注者验证构建质量。作者定义了三个不同粒度的评估任务（监控已部署智能体、流程检索、实时护栏），并提出对话流程感知指标 FlowKL 与 FlowScore，按对话的展开方式而非内容进行比较。实验表明，在对话层面 FlowScore 在七种编码器上显著优于所有基线，说明语义相似性无法替代流程相似性。
SAP-ViT: A Self-Attributing Patch Vision Transformer for Medical Image Classification
Authors: Wei Zhang, Wolfgang Nejdl, Ming Tang
First: 2026-10-26T15:19:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T15:19:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Transformer (ViT) classifiers commonly rely on a global classification (CLS) token for image-level prediction, which is poorly aligned with local interpretability requirements in medical imaging. We propose SAP-ViT, a Self-Attributing Patch Vision Transformer that replaces CLS-based classification with direct aggregation of last-layer patch embeddings. This design makes class predictions decomposable into patch-level contributions as part of the forward pass. To encourage concise and readable evidence, SAP-ViT applies sparsity regularization to the patch-weight distribution. Since self-attention mixes information across patch tokens, we further introduce an input-space back-mapping mechanism to estimate which input patches contribute to influential output patch embeddings. We evaluate SAP-ViT qualitatively and quantitatively on multiple medical image classification datasets spanning different imaging modalities. The results show that SAP-ViT maintains strong classification performance while providing consistent and interpretable patch-level explanations across datasets. Ablation results further show that both sparsity regularization and the alignment component of input-space back-mapping contribute to the final explanation quality. Overall, SAP-ViT provides a favorable trade-off between predictive accuracy and interpretability for ViT-based medical image classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAP-ViT：一种用于医学图像分类的自归因补丁视觉Transformer
视觉Transformer（ViT）分类器通常依赖全局分类（CLS）token进行图像级预测，这与医学成像中的局部可解释性需求匹配不佳。我们提出SAP-ViT，一种自归因补丁视觉Transformer，用最后一层补丁嵌入的直接聚合替代基于CLS的分类。该设计使类别预测在前向传播中可分解为补丁级贡献。为鼓励简洁可读的证据，SAP-ViT对补丁权重分布施加稀疏正则化。由于自注意力在补丁token间混合信息，我们进一步引入输入空间反向映射机制，以估计哪些输入补丁对关键输出补丁嵌入有贡献。我们在涵盖不同成像模态的多个医学图像分类数据集上对SAP-ViT进行定性和定量评估。结果表明，SAP-ViT在保持强分类性能的同时，跨数据集提供一致且可解释的补丁级解释。消融结果进一步表明，稀疏正则化和输入空间反向映射的对齐组件均对最终解释质量有贡献。总体而言，SAP-ViT为基于ViT的医学图像分类在预测准确性与可解释性之间提供了有利的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Vision Transformer classifiers typically rely on a global CLS token that aligns poorly with the local interpretability needs of medical imaging, motivating SAP-ViT, a Self-Attributing Patch Vision Transformer that replaces CLS-based classification with direct aggregation of last-layer patch embeddings so that predictions decompose into patch-level contributions within the forward pass. The method applies sparsity regularization to the patch-weight distribution to encourage concise evidence and introduces an input-space back-mapping mechanism to estimate which input patches contribute to influential output patch embeddings, since self-attention mixes information across tokens. Evaluated qualitatively and quantitatively on multiple medical image classification datasets across different imaging modalities, SAP-ViT maintains strong classification performance while producing consistent, interpretable patch-level explanations, and ablations show that both the sparsity regularization and the alignment component of the back-mapping contribute to explanation quality, yielding a favorable accuracy-interpretability trade-off.
视觉Transformer分类器通常依赖全局CLS token进行图像级预测，这与医学影像对局部可解释性的需求匹配不佳。作者提出SAP-ViT，以对最后一层patch嵌入的直接聚合替代基于CLS的分类，使类别预测在前向传播中可分解为patch级贡献；同时对patch权重分布施加稀疏正则以促使证据简洁可读，并引入输入空间反向映射机制，在自注意力混合信息的情况下估计哪些输入patch对关键输出patch嵌入有贡献。在涵盖多种成像模态的多个医学图像分类数据集上进行定性与定量评估，结果表明SAP-ViT在保持较强分类性能的同时，能跨数据集提供一致且可解释的patch级解释；消融实验进一步显示稀疏正则与反向映射中的对齐组件均对最终解释质量有贡献，整体在预测准确性与可解释性之间取得了较好的权衡。
CANS: Capability-Preserving Neuron Steering for Large Language Models
Authors: Yucheng Wu, Chengyi Xie, Chenpeng Wang, Liangming Pan
First: 2026-10-26T14:45:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:45:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Activation steering offers training-free and dynamic control of target behaviors in large language models, but existing methods can impair general capabilities and require domain-specific intervention designs. We introduce Capability-Preserving Neuron Steering (CANS), a unified framework that balances target-behavior control with capability preservation. To make target-behavior improvement and general-capability preservation tractable, we introduce two proxies: log-probabilities of target tokens in model generated responses, and output-distribution KL divergence on general-purpose inputs. Combining the local approximation of the two proxies yields a closed-form solution, enabling lightweight steering without iterative model training or additional inference-time computation. The resulting neuron-wise scaling coefficients achieve effective steering while limiting general-capability loss. A shared data-construction and intervention procedure supports adaptation across domains using sampled responses with target-behavior filtering and target-span annotations. Across safety, toxicity, and hallucination control on Qwen and Llama models, CANS achieves comparable or better steering while better preserving MMLU, BBH, and IFEval performance than SFT, CAA, and neuron-level baselines. On Qwen3-4B, CANS reduces HarmBench attack success from $15.50\%$ to $1.75\%$ with a $1.11$-point decrease in average capability. These results support CANS as a practical approach to behavioral control across domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CANS：面向大语言模型的能力保持神经元引导
激活引导为大语言模型的目标行为提供了免训练的动态控制，但现有方法可能损害通用能力，且需要特定领域的干预设计。我们提出能力保持神经元引导（CANS），一个在目标行为控制与能力保持之间取得平衡的统一框架。为使目标行为提升与通用能力保持易于处理，我们引入两个代理指标：模型生成回复中目标token的对数概率，以及通用输入上的输出分布KL散度。结合两个代理指标的局部近似可得到闭式解，从而实现轻量级引导，无需迭代模型训练或额外的推理时计算。所得的神经元级缩放系数在实现有效引导的同时限制通用能力损失。共享的数据构建与干预流程支持跨领域适配，使用经目标行为过滤和目�标跨度标注的采样回复。在Qwen和Llama模型上的安全性、毒性和幻觉控制中，CANS实现了相当或更好的引导，同时比SFT、CAA和神经元级基线更好地保持MMLU、BBH和IFEval性能。在Qwen3-4B上，CANS将HarmBench攻击成功率从$15.50\%$降至$1.75\%$，平均能力仅下降$1.11$分。这些结果支持CANS作为跨领域行为控制的实用方法。
Summary / 总结
Activation steering enables training-free control of LLM behaviors, but existing methods often degrade general capabilities and require domain-specific designs. The authors propose Capability-Preserving Neuron Steering (CANS), a unified framework that uses two proxies, the log-probabilities of target tokens in generated responses and the output-distribution KL divergence on general-purpose inputs, whose local approximation yields a closed-form solution for neuron-wise scaling coefficients without iterative training or extra inference-time computation, with a shared data-construction and intervention procedure for cross-domain adaptation. Across safety, toxicity, and hallucination control on Qwen and Llama models, CANS matches or exceeds SFT, CAA, and neuron-level baselines while better preserving MMLU, BBH, and IFEval performance; on Qwen3-4B it reduces HarmBench attack success from 15.50% to 1.75% with only a 1.11-point average capability decrease.
激活引导无需训练即可动态控制大语言模型的目标行为，但现有方法往往损害模型的通用能力，且需要针对不同领域设计干预方案。作者提出能力保持神经元引导（CANS）统一框架，以模型生成回复中目标token的对数概率和通用输入上的输出分布KL散度作为两个代理指标，结合二者的局部近似得到闭式解，从而计算神经元级缩放系数，无需迭代训练或额外的推理时计算，并通过共享的数据构建与干预流程支持跨领域适配。在Qwen和Llama模型上针对安全性、毒性和幻觉控制的实验中，CANS的引导效果与SFT、CAA及神经元级基线相当或更优，同时更好地保持了MMLU、BBH和IFEval性能；在Qwen3-4B上，CANS将HarmBench攻击成功率从15.50%降至1.75%，平均能力仅下降1.11分。
WorldPager: Zero-Stall Tiered Memory for Long-Horizon World Models on a Single Consumer GPU
Authors: Bingnan Liu, Ruini Xue
First: 2026-10-26T14:44:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:44:33+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 4 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interactive world models keep the KV of past chunks as retrieval memory so that revisited places stay consistent. On a single consumer machine (one RTX 4090, 32 GiB of host memory, one NVMe SSD), long sessions with such memory hit two walls. The first is capacity: the KV of Matrix-Game 2.0 exhausts host memory after 45 seconds of video in bf16 and after 2.8 minutes in int4. The second is the model's positional horizon of 4.3 minutes. We show that this memory is capacity-bound rather than bandwidth-bound and that its access set is known before compute, because the camera pose is an input. An analytical model built on these observations predicts spill points to within one chunk and classifies zero versus non-zero stalls correctly on held-out configurations. Guided by it, WorldPager stores int4 KV in a GPU/DRAM/NVMe hierarchy instead of recomputing it, prefetches each chunk's memory from the extrapolated pose, never waits on a miss, and writes back in the background; to our knowledge, it is the first to place the KV memory of an interactive world model on an NVMe tier with zero-stall prefetching. With training-free position handling, it generates 37.5 minutes of video continuously with zero stalls and constant host memory, at about 9.5 frames per second. On a sealed test set, int4 storage shows no measurable quality loss; on long-gap revisits it lowers image quality by about one MUSIQ point.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldPager：单张消费级GPU上用于长时程世界模型的零停顿分层内存
交互式世界模型将过去分块的KV保留为检索内存，以使重访地点保持一致。在单台消费级机器上（一张RTX 4090、32 GiB主机内存、一块NVMe SSD），使用此类内存的长会话会撞上两堵墙。第一是容量：Matrix-Game 2.0的KV在bf16下45秒视频后耗尽主机内存，在int4下2.8分钟后耗尽。第二是模型4.3分钟的位置时程。我们表明该内存受容量约束而非带宽约束，且其访问集在计算前已知，因为相机位姿是输入。基于这些观察构建的解析模型将溢出点预测到误差不超过一个分块，并在留出配置上正确分类零停顿与非零停顿。在其指导下，WorldPager将int4 KV存储在GPU/DRAM/NVMe层级中而非重新计算，根据外推位姿预取每个分块的内存，从不在缺失上等待，并在后台写回；据我们所知，它是首个将交互式世界模型的KV内存置于NVMe层级并实现零停顿预取的工作。借助免训练的位置处理，它以约9.5帧每秒连续生成37.5分钟视频，零停顿且主机内存恒定。在密封测试集上，int4存储未显示可测量的质量损失；在长间隔重访时，它将图像质量降低约一个MUSIQ点。

Beyond Reasoning Gains: Mitigating General-Capability Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Hoang Phan, Xianjun Yang, Yuanshun Yao, Jingyu Zhang, Shengjie Bi, Xiaocheng Tang, Madian Khabsa, Lijuan Liu, Deren Lei
First: 2025-10-24T19:08:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:42:04+00:00
Comments: Implementation is released at https://github.com/VietHoang1512/recap
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has delivered impressive gains in mathematical and multimodal reasoning and has become a standard post-training paradigm for contemporary language and vision-language models. However, the RLVR recipe introduces a significant risk of capability regression, in which models forget foundational skills after prolonged training without employing regularization strategies. We empirically confirm this concern, observing that open-source reasoning models suffer performance degradation on core capabilities such as perception and faithfulness. While imposing regularization terms like KL divergence can help prevent deviation from the base model, these terms are computed on the current task and therefore do not guarantee preservation of broader knowledge. Meanwhile, commonly used experience replay across heterogeneous domains makes it nontrivial to decide how much training emphasis each objective should receive. To address this, we propose RECAP-a replay strategy with dynamic objective reweighting for general knowledge preservation. Our reweighting mechanism adapts online using short-horizon signals of convergence and instability, shifting the post-training focus away from saturated objectives and toward underperforming or volatile ones. Our method is end-to-end and readily applicable to existing RLVR pipelines without training additional models or heavy tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmarks using Qwen2.5-VL-3B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, which not only preserves general capabilities but also improves reasoning by enabling more flexible trade-offs among in-task rewards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越推理增益：缓解大型推理模型中的通用能力遗忘
基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在数学和多模态推理方面取得了显著提升，已成为当代语言模型和视觉语言模型的标准后训练范式。然而，RLVR 方法引入了显著的能力回退风险：在不采用正则化策略的情况下，模型经过长时间训练后会遗忘基础技能。我们通过实证确认了这一担忧，观察到开源推理模型在感知和忠实性等核心能力上出现性能下降。虽然引入 KL 散度等正则项有助于防止偏离基础模型，但这些项是在当前任务上计算的，因此无法保证保留更广泛的知识。同时，跨异构领域常用的经验回放使得难以确定每个目标应获得多少训练侧重。为解决这一问题，我们提出 RECAP——一种用于通用知识保留的动态目标重加权回放策略。我们的重加权机制利用收敛性和不稳定性的短时信号进行在线自适应，将后训练重点从已饱和目标转向表现不佳或不稳定的目标。我们的方法是端到端的，可直接应用于现有 RLVR 流程，无需训练额外模型或大量调参。在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 和 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上的基准实验广泛表明，我们的方法有效，不仅保留了通用能力，还通过在任务内奖励之间实现更灵活的权衡来提升推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves mathematical and multimodal reasoning but risks degrading foundational capabilities such as perception and faithfulness during prolonged training, and existing fixes like KL regularization or experience replay either fail to protect broader knowledge or make objective weighting hard to set. The authors propose RECAP, a replay strategy with dynamic objective reweighting that adapts online using short-horizon signals of convergence and instability, shifting training focus from saturated objectives toward underperforming or volatile ones without extra models or heavy tuning. Experiments with Qwen2.5-VL-3B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that RECAP preserves general capabilities while also improving reasoning through more flexible trade-offs among in-task rewards.
带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能提升数学与多模态推理能力，但长时间训练可能导致感知、忠实性等基础能力退化，而KL正则化只在当前任务上计算、难以保障更广泛知识，异构领域的经验回放又难以确定各目标的训练权重。为此，作者提出RECAP，一种端到端的回放策略并配合动态目标重加权，利用收敛与不稳定的短时信号在线调整权重，将训练重点从已饱和目标转向表现不足或波动较大的目标，且无需额外模型或大量调参。在Qwen2.5-VL-3B与Qwen2.5-VL-7B上的基准实验表明，该方法在保持通用能力的同时，通过更灵活地权衡任务内奖励进一步提升了推理表现。
LoRAScan: Detecting Backdoor Prompts in Low-Rank Adapters for Large Language Models via Down-Projection Activation Spikes
Authors: Doniyorkhon Obidov, Honggang Yu, Xiaolong Guo, Kaichen Yang
First: 2026-08-07T04:36:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:34:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient specialization and distribution of large language models through compact adapters. However, untrusted adapters introduce a supply-chain threat: a backdoored adapter can cause a model to generate harmful content, malicious code, political propaganda, or covert advertisements when an input contains a hidden trigger. Adapter-agnostic defenses merge the adapter with the base model, which dilutes backdoor signals and reduces detection performance. Existing adapter-aware methods do not address how to safely use a potentially backdoored adapter. Instead, they either train a defensive adapter to repair a backdoored base model, addressing the inverse problem rather than securing the adapter itself, or rely on a classifier that flags the entire adapter as suspicious and requires separate mitigation. These methods overlook the distinct latent-space signatures produced by trigger-bearing inputs in backdoored adapters. We introduce LoRAScan, the first adapter-aware defense that detects and rejects trigger-bearing inputs at inference time without modifying adapter parameters. Our key observation is that a small subset of LoRA insertion sites, approximately 5%, remains stable across clean inputs but exhibits highly concentrated spikes in LoRA down-projection activations when a trigger is present. LoRAScan identifies these low-variance insertion sites before model deployment and monitors them during inference. Across standard LLM backdoor benchmarks, LoRAScan rejects 98.49% of malicious inputs with a small error rate on clean inputs, outperforming existing defenses across diverse evaluation settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoRAScan：通过下投影激活尖峰检测大型语言模型低秩适配器中的后门提示
低秩适配（LoRA）通过紧凑适配器实现大型语言模型的高效专用化与分发。然而，不可信适配器引入了供应链威胁：被植入后门的适配器可使模型在输入包含隐藏触发器时生成有害内容、恶意代码、政治宣传或隐蔽广告。与适配器无关的防御将适配器与基础模型合并，这会稀释后门信号并降低检测性能。现有的适配器感知方法未解决如何安全使用可能被植入后门的适配器。相反，它们要么训练防御性适配器来修复被植入后门的基础模型，解决的是逆问题而非保护适配器本身，要么依赖分类器将整个适配器标记为可疑并需要单独缓解。这些方法忽视了被植入后门的适配器中携带触发器的输入所产生的独特潜在空间特征。我们提出 LoRAScan，这是首个适配器感知防御方法，可在推理时检测并拒绝携带触发器的输入，而无需修改适配器参数。我们的关键观察是，约 5% 的少量 LoRA 插入位点在干净输入下保持稳定，但当存在触发器时，在 LoRA 下投影激活中表现出高度集中的尖峰。LoRAScan 在模型部署前识别这些低方差插入位点，并在推理期间对其进行监控。在标准 LLM 后门基准上，LoRAScan 以对干净输入的较小错误率拒绝了 98.49% 的恶意输入，在多样化评估设置中优于现有防御方法。
Summary / 总结
Untrusted LoRA adapters pose a supply-chain backdoor threat, but adapter-agnostic defenses dilute backdoor signals by merging adapters with base models, and existing adapter-aware methods either repair backdoored base models or flag entire adapters without safely using them. LoRAScan is an adapter-aware defense that detects and rejects trigger-bearing inputs at inference time without modifying adapter parameters, based on the observation that roughly 5% of LoRA insertion sites stay stable on clean inputs but show concentrated spikes in down-projection activations when a trigger is present; these low-variance sites are identified before deployment and monitored during inference. Across standard LLM backdoor benchmarks, LoRAScan rejects 98.49% of malicious inputs with a small error rate on clean inputs, outperforming existing defenses across diverse evaluation settings.
不可信的LoRA适配器带来供应链威胁：被植入后门的适配器会在输入含有隐藏触发器时使模型生成有害内容或隐蔽广告，而现有防御要么将适配器与基座模型合并从而稀释后门信号，要么把整个适配器标记为可疑却无法安全使用。LoRAScan是一种不修改适配器参数的适配器感知防御：它在部署前识别出约5%的LoRA插入位置，这些位置的下投影激活在干净输入上保持稳定、在出现触发器时则急剧尖峰，并在推理时监测这些低方差位置以检测并拒绝含触发器的输入。在标准大语言模型后门基准上，LoRAScan拒绝了98.49%的恶意输入，同时对干净输入保持较低误报率，在多种评测设置下优于现有防御方法。
Governing Enterprise AI Traffic at Scale: Design and Operation of a Policy-Driven, Multi-Vendor LLM Gateway on Envoy
Authors: Mithun Sasidharan, Srinivasan Manoharan, Rupesh Thakur, Abhinav TK, Ashwin Hegde, Anand Gautam
First: 2026-10-26T14:33:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:33:48+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 6 figures, 6 tables. Industry case study
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large organizations consume large language models (LLMs) from several vendors at once, each with its own API dialect, credential model, streaming protocol and pricing. Integrated directly, this heterogeneity yields credential sprawl, format lock-in, unbounded spend and inconsistent governance. We present the design and production operation of the Cosmos LLM Gateway, the governed entry point for enterprise LLM traffic at PayPal. Built on Envoy, it splits work by latency profile: I/O-bound control decisions (authentication, credential brokering, policy and budget evaluation) run in an out-of-process external-processing service, while wire-format translation, token metering and prompt-cache shaping run in-proxy as a Rust WebAssembly filter, so that, by default, streamed response body chunks never wait on an out-of-process call. Models, providers and prices are largely validated data rather than code. Governance is declarative policy evaluated by Open Policy Agent and managed through a control plane with role-scoped authoring, approvals, pilots, shadow-mode rollout, usage notifications and versioned policy bundles. For use cases that require it, a data-loss-prevention layer can block, redact or pseudonymize detected sensitive content before it reaches a provider. Spend scopes are views over per-model usage counters, so a new budget applies to spend already recorded in its window without migration. As of September 2026, the gateway serves more than 12,000 developers and hundreds of business use cases, handling roughly 200 billion tokens per day across more than 100 models at 99.999% availability to business. In a benchmark of the core request path against a synthetic upstream, it adds a median of 4 ms and a p99 of 11 ms of overhead at more than 2,200 requests per second on two 4-vCPU instances. We report the architecture, policy model, evaluation and lessons learned.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模治理企业AI流量：基于Envoy的策略驱动、多供应商LLM网关的设计与运营
大型组织同时使用来自多个供应商的大语言模型（LLM），每个供应商都有各自的API方言、凭证模型、流式协议和定价。若直接集成，这种异构性会导致凭证泛滥、格式锁定、无界支出和治理不一致。我们介绍Cosmos LLM网关的设计与生产运营，它是PayPal企业LLM流量的受治理入口。该网关基于Envoy构建，按延迟特征拆分工作：I/O密集型控制决策（认证、凭证代理、策略与预算评估）在进程外的外部处理服务中运行，而线格式转换、令牌计量和提示缓存塑形则作为Rust WebAssembly过滤器在代理内运行，因此默认情况下，流式响应体分块从不需要等待进程外调用。模型、供应商和价格主要是经过验证的数据而非代码。治理是由Open Policy Agent评估的声明式策略，并通过控制平面管理，具备角色范围限定的编写、审批、试点、影子模式发布、用量通知和版本化策略包。对于有需要的用例，数据丢失防护层可以在敏感内容到达供应商之前对其进行阻断、脱敏或假名化。支出范围是各模型用量计数器的视图，因此新预算无需迁移即可适用于其窗口内已记录的支出。截至2026年9月，该网关服务超过12,000名开发者和数百个业务用例，每天处理约2000亿个令牌，覆盖100多个模型，对业务可用性达99.999%。在针对合成上游的核心请求路径基准测试中，它在两台4-vCPU实例上以每秒超过2,200个请求的速率增加中位数4毫秒和p99 11毫秒的开销。我们报告了架构、策略模型、评估和经验教训。
Summary / 总结
Enterprises consuming LLMs from multiple vendors face credential sprawl, format lock-in, unbounded spend, and inconsistent governance when integrating providers directly, motivating the design of the Cosmos LLM Gateway at PayPal as a governed entry point for enterprise LLM traffic. Built on Envoy, the gateway separates I/O-bound control decisions such as authentication, credential brokering, and policy and budget evaluation into an out-of-process external-processing service, while wire-format translation, token metering, and prompt-cache shaping run in-proxy as a Rust WebAssembly filter so streamed response chunks do not wait on out-of-process calls; models, providers, and prices are treated as validated data, and governance is expressed as declarative Open Policy Agent policies managed through a control plane with role-scoped authoring, approvals, pilots, shadow-mode rollout, and versioned bundles, with an optional data-loss-prevention layer and spend scopes implemented as views over per-model usage counters. As of September 2026 the gateway serves over 12,000 developers and hundreds of business use cases, handling roughly 200 billion tokens per day across more than 100 models at 99.999% availability, and in a benchmark of the core request path against a synthetic upstream it adds a median of 4 ms and a p99 of 11 ms of overhead at more than 2,200 requests per second on two 4-vCPU instances.
大型组织同时使用多家供应商的大语言模型，直接集成会带来凭证分散、格式锁定、支出无界和治理不一致等问题，因此 PayPal 设计了 Cosmos LLM Gateway 作为企业 LLM 流量的受治理入口。该网关基于 Envoy 构建，按延迟特征拆分工作：认证、凭证代理、策略与预算评估等 I/O 密集型控制决策放在进程外的外部处理服务中执行，而线格式转换、token 计量和提示缓存整形则以 Rust WebAssembly 过滤器形式在代理内运行，使流式响应块默认无需等待进程外调用；模型、供应商和价格主要作为经过校验的数据而非代码，治理通过 Open Policy Agent 评估的声明式策略实现，并由控制平面管理角色范围编写、审批、试点、影子模式发布和版本化策略包，另可选配数据防泄漏层，支出范围则实现为按模型使用计数器的视图。截至 2026 年 9 月，该网关服务超过 12,000 名开发者和数百个业务用例，每天处理约 2000 亿 token，覆盖 100 多个模型，业务可用性达 99.999%；在针对合成上游的核心请求路径基准测试中，两台 4 vCPU 实例上每秒超过 2,200 次请求时，其额外开销中位数为 4 毫秒、p99 为 11 毫秒。
Agentic AI Empowered Intent-Based Networking for 6G
Authors: Genze Jiang, Kezhi Wang, Xiaomin Chen, Yizhou Huang
First: 2026-01-10T17:49:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:29:53+00:00
Comments: Submitted for Possible Journal Publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The transition towards sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks necessitates autonomous orchestration mechanisms capable of translating high-level operational intents into executable network configurations. Existing approaches to Intent-Based Networking (IBN) rely upon either rule-based systems that struggle with linguistic variation or end-to-end neural models that lack interpretability and fail to enforce operational constraints. This paper presents a hierarchical multi-agent framework where Large Language Model (LLM) based agents autonomously decompose natural language intents, consult domain-specific specialists, and synthesise technically feasible network slice configurations through iterative reasoning-acting (ReAct) cycles. The proposed architecture employs an orchestrator agent coordinating two specialist agents, namely Radio Access Network (RAN) and Core Network agents, via ReAct-style reasoning, grounded in structured network state representations. Experimental evaluation across diverse benchmark scenarios demonstrates that the proposed system outperforms monolithic agents, rule-based systems, and direct LLM prompting, with architectural principles applicable to Open RAN (O-RAN) deployments. The results also demonstrate that whilst contemporary LLMs possess substantial general telecommunications knowledge, structured prompt engineering encoding domain-specific decision thresholds and operational policies is essential for reliable network automation performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向6G的智能体AI赋能意图驱动网络
向第六代（6G）无线网络的演进需要能够将高层运营意图转化为可执行网络配置的自主编排机制。现有的意图驱动网络（IBN）方法依赖于难以应对语言变化的基于规则的系统，或缺乏可解释性且无法强制执行运营约束的端到端神经模型。本文提出一种分层多智能体框架，其中基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体自主分解自然语言意图、咨询领域特定专家，并通过迭代推理-行动（ReAct）循环合成技术上可行的网络切片配置。所提架构采用一个编排器智能体，通过ReAct式推理协调两个专家智能体，即无线接入网（RAN）和核心网智能体，并以结构化网络状态表示为依据。在多样化基准场景上的实验评估表明，所提系统优于单体智能体、基于规则的系统以及直接LLM提示，其架构原则适用于开放无线接入网（O-RAN）部署。结果还表明，尽管当代LLM具备大量通用电信知识，但编码领域特定决策阈值和运营策略的结构化提示工程对于可靠的网络自动化性能至关重要。
