Measuring Cross-Modal Synergy: A Benchmark for VLM Explainability
Authors: Joël Roman Ky, Salah Ghamizi, Maxime Cordy
Venue: NeurIPS
First: 2026-05-21T08:39:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:29:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS E&D 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) map complex visual inputs to semantic spaces, but interpreting the cross-modal reasoning of VLMs currently relies on post-hoc explainers evaluated via unimodal perturbation metrics. We expose a limitation in this paradigm: because multimodal datasets contain language priors and modality biases, VLMs frequently exhibit cross-modal redundancy, allowing them to answer visual queries using text alone. Consequently, unimodal metrics penalize faithful explainers, triggering an evaluation collapse where visual and textual rankings fundamentally contradict each other. To resolve this, we introduce Synergistic Faithfulness ($\mathcal{F}_{syn}$), a scalable metric rooted in the Shapley Interaction Index that strictly isolates the joint Harsanyi dividend between modalities, serving as a highly accurate surrogate ($ρ= 0.93$) while achieving up to $88\times$ computational speedup. Evaluating 9 distinct XAI methods across 3 VLM architectures and 3 benchmark datasets, reveals that explainers proposed for VLMs heavily over-index on visual salience and significantly underperform adapted attention-based methods in capturing true cross-modal synergy. By decoupling visual plausibility from cross-modal faithfulness, this work provides a rigorous evaluation framework required to safely audit VLM reasoning in high-stakes deployments. Code: https://github.com/serval-uni-lu/xai-vlms-benchmark
中文标题/摘要
标题：衡量跨模态协同：VLM可解释性基准
视觉语言模型（VLM）将复杂视觉输入映射到语义空间，但目前解释VLM跨模态推理依赖于通过单模态扰动指标评估的事后解释器。我们揭示了这一范式的局限性：由于多模态数据集包含语言先验和模态偏差，VLM常表现出跨模态冗余，使其仅凭文本即可回答视觉查询。因此，单模态指标会惩罚忠实的解释器，引发评估崩溃，使视觉与文本排名根本矛盾。为解决此问题，我们引入协同忠实性（$\mathcal{F}_{syn}$），一种基于Shapley交互指数的可扩展指标，严格隔离模态间的联合Harsanyi红利，作为高精度代理（$ρ= 0.93$）同时实现高达$88\times$的计算加速。在3种VLM架构和3个基准数据集上评估9种不同的XAI方法，发现为VLM提出的解释器过度偏重视觉显著性，在捕捉真实跨模态协同方面显著落后于适配的基于注意力的方法。通过将视觉合理性从跨模态忠实性中解耦，本工作提供了在高风险部署中安全审计VLM推理所需的严格评估框架。代码：https://github.com/serval-uni-lu/xai-vlms-benchmark
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses a limitation in how the cross-modal reasoning of Vision-Language Models is evaluated, arguing that unimodal perturbation metrics are ill-suited because language priors and modality biases let VLMs answer visual queries from text alone, producing cross-modal redundancy that penalizes faithful explainers and causes visual and textual rankings to contradict each other. The authors introduce Synergistic Faithfulness, a scalable metric based on the Shapley Interaction Index that isolates the joint Harsanyi dividend between modalities, which serves as an accurate surrogate (ρ = 0.93) with up to 88x computational speedup. Evaluating 9 XAI methods across 3 VLM architectures and 3 benchmark datasets shows that explainers proposed for VLMs over-index on visual salience and substantially underperform adapted attention-based methods in capturing true cross-modal synergy.
该研究针对视觉语言模型跨模态推理评估中的一个缺陷：现有的事后解释器多采用单模态扰动指标进行评价，但多模态数据集包含语言先验和模态偏置，模型常可仅凭文本回答视觉问题，形成跨模态冗余，导致忠实解释器被惩罚，视觉与文本排名相互矛盾。作者提出基于 Shapley 交互指数的协同忠实度指标，严格分离模态间的联合 Harsanyi 红利，报告其作为高精度代理指标（ρ = 0.93）并实现最高 88 倍的计算加速。在 3 种 VLM 架构和 3 个基准数据集上评估 9 种 XAI 方法后发现，面向 VLM 设计的解释器过度偏重视觉显著性，在捕捉真实跨模态协同方面明显不如改造后的注意力方法，从而将视觉合理性与跨模态忠实度区分开来。
Test-Time Confidence Adaptation under RL-Induced Trajectory Shift
Authors: Zhenzhen Ren, Xiaoqing Zhang, GuoBiao Li, Bowen Fang, Yanxi Wang, Xinpeng Zhang, Ke Han
First: 2026-10-26T14:27:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:27:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Trajectory-based confidence estimation has shown promising performance for large language models (LLMs) by leveraging rich signals from their reasoning processes. However, we find that such confidence estimators can degrade substantially after agentic reinforcement learning (Agentic RL) as it induces substantial shifts in the reasoning trajectories. More importantly, despite these shifts, we uncover a reliability structure that is largely preserved across Base and RL models: $\textbf{more}$ $\textbf{stable}$ $\textbf{trajectories}$ $\textbf{remain}$ $\textbf{more}$ $\textbf{likely}$ $\textbf{to}$ $\textbf{produce}$ $\textbf{correct}$ $\textbf{answers}$. This preserved relationship provides a transferable, label-free reliability signal for post-RL confidence adaptation. Building on this finding, we propose $\textbf{StaR-TTA}$, a stability-guided framework for label-free test-time confidence adaptation. StaR-TTA jointly learns from correctness supervision and stability-derived reliability supervision on labeled Base trajectories, establishing the connection between trajectory stability and answer reliability. At test time, it reuses stability-induced reliability rankings from unlabeled RL trajectories to adapt confidence scores to the shifted trajectory distribution, without requiring target correctness labels. Across four QA benchmarks, StaR-TTA achieves an average AUROC of \(0.721\), with consistent improvements across unseen datasets, model families and scales, and different Agentic RL algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RL诱导轨迹偏移下的测试时置信度自适应
基于轨迹的置信度估计通过利用大语言模型（LLM）推理过程中的丰富信号，已展现出良好性能。然而，我们发现此类置信度估计器在智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）后可能显著退化，因为Agentic RL会引起推理轨迹的大幅偏移。更重要的是，尽管存在这些偏移，我们揭示了一种在Base模型与RL模型之间基本保持的可靠性结构：$\textbf{更}$ $\textbf{稳定}$ $\textbf{的}$ $\textbf{轨迹}$ $\textbf{仍}$ $\textbf{更}$ $\textbf{可能}$ $\textbf{产生}$ $\textbf{正确}$ $\textbf{答案}$。这种保持的关系为RL后的置信度自适应提供了一种可迁移、无需标签的可靠性信号。基于这一发现，我们提出$\textbf{StaR-TTA}$，一种稳定性引导的无需标签测试时置信度自适应框架。StaR-TTA在有标签的Base轨迹上联合学习正确性监督与由稳定性导出的可靠性监督，建立轨迹稳定性与答案可靠性之间的联系。在测试时，它复用来自无标签RL轨迹的稳定性诱导可靠性排序，使置信度分数自适应于偏移后的轨迹分布，而无需目标正确性标签。在四个QA基准上，StaR-TTA取得平均AUROC \(0.721\)，并在未见数据集、模型家族与规模以及不同Agentic RL算法上均有一致提升。
Summary / 总结
Trajectory-based confidence estimators for large language models are found to degrade substantially after agentic reinforcement learning, which shifts reasoning trajectories, yet a reliability structure persists across Base and RL models in which more stable trajectories remain more likely to yield correct answers. Building on this observation, the authors propose StaR-TTA, a stability-guided framework for label-free test-time confidence adaptation that jointly learns from correctness supervision and stability-derived reliability supervision on labeled Base trajectories, then at test time reuses stability-induced reliability rankings from unlabeled RL trajectories to adapt confidence scores to the shifted distribution without target correctness labels. Across four QA benchmarks, StaR-TTA achieves an average AUROC of 0.721, with consistent improvements across unseen datasets, model families and scales, and different Agentic RL algorithms.
基于轨迹的置信度估计方法在智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）之后会因推理轨迹发生显著偏移而明显退化，但作者发现一种可靠性结构在Base模型与RL模型之间基本得以保留：更稳定的轨迹仍然更可能产生正确答案。基于这一发现，作者提出StaR-TTA，一个以稳定性为指导的无标签测试时置信度自适应框架，它在带标签的Base轨迹上同时利用正确性监督和由稳定性导出的可靠性监督进行学习，从而建立轨迹稳定性与答案可靠性之间的联系；在测试阶段，它复用无标签RL轨迹中由稳定性诱导的可靠性排序，在无需目标正确性标签的情况下使置信度分数适应偏移后的轨迹分布。在四个问答基准上，StaR-TTA取得平均AUROC 0.721，并在未见数据集、不同模型家族与规模以及不同Agentic RL算法上均取得一致提升。
NGM: A Plug-and-Play Training-Free Memory Module for LLMs
Authors: Yuwen Qu, Wenhui Dong, Camil Hamami, Ming Zhao, Chenyang Si, Caifeng Shan
First: 2026-05-16T09:12:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:26:14+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures. Revised manuscript with expanded experiments and analysis and updated author information. Project page: https://pioneerqyw.github.io/ngm-project/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent studies have introduced memory-augmented architectures that decouple knowledge storage from neural computation, enabling more direct access to stored representations. However, existing approaches often rely on learned memory embeddings or explicit memory and retrieval components, requiring additional training or increasing architectural complexity. To address these limitations, we propose N-gram Memory (NGM), a training-free, plug-and-play module that repurposes a backbone model's pretrained token embedding space as memory. NGM consists of a Causal N-Gram Encoder and a Cosine-Gated Memory Injector. The former constructs N-gram representations by directly aggregating pretrained token embeddings, while the latter uses cosine similarity with ReLU gating to adaptively control the amount of retrieved memory injected into contextual representations. Unlike conventional memory-augmented architectures, NGM requires neither additional embedding training nor a separately learned memory bank or external retrieval infrastructure. We evaluate NGM on ten benchmarks across six language-model backbones spanning two model families and multiple scales, including four Qwen3 models (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B, and 8B) and two Llama-3.2-Instruct models (1B and 3B). NGM consistently improves the ten-task average across all six backbones. Notably, it improves HumanEval by 4.26, HumanEval+ by 2.41, and GPQA-Diamond by 6.56 percentage points. Furthermore, NGM extends beyond text-only language models to vision-language models, with Qwen3-VL-2B achieving improvements across all five VLM benchmarks. Project page: https://pioneerqyw.github.io/ngm-project/
中文标题/摘要
标题：NGM：一种即插即用的免训练LLM记忆模块
近期研究引入了记忆增强架构，将知识存储与神经计算解耦，从而能够更直接地访问存储的表示。然而，现有方法通常依赖学习到的记忆嵌入或显式的记忆与检索组件，需要额外训练或增加架构复杂度。为解决这些局限，我们提出N-gram记忆（NGM），一种免训练、即插即用的模块，将骨干模型预训练的token嵌入空间复用为记忆。NGM由因果N-Gram编码器和余弦门控记忆注入器组成。前者通过直接聚合预训练token嵌入构建N-gram表示，后者利用余弦相似度与ReLU门控自适应控制注入上下文表示中的检索记忆量。与传统记忆增强架构不同，NGM既不需要额外的嵌入训练，也不需要单独学习的记忆库或外部检索基础设施。我们在六个语言模型骨干上评估NGM，涵盖两个模型系列和多种规模，包括四个Qwen3模型（0.6B、1.7B、4B和8B）和两个Llama-3.2-Instruct模型（1B和3B），共十个基准。NGM在所有六个骨干上均持续提升十任务平均值。值得注意的是，它将HumanEval提升4.26、HumanEval+提升2.41、GPQA-Diamond提升6.56个百分点。此外，NGM不仅适用于纯文本语言模型，还扩展到视觉语言模型，Qwen3-VL-2B在所有五个VLM基准上均取得提升。项目页面：https://pioneerqyw.github.io/ngm-project/
Summary / 总结
Existing memory-augmented language models typically depend on learned memory embeddings or explicit memory banks and retrieval components, which add training cost and architectural complexity. The authors propose N-gram Memory (NGM), a training-free, plug-and-play module that reuses a backbone model's pretrained token embedding space as memory, combining a Causal N-Gram Encoder that builds N-gram representations by aggregating pretrained token embeddings with a Cosine-Gated Memory Injector that uses cosine similarity and ReLU gating to adaptively control how much retrieved memory is injected into contextual representations, without extra embedding training, a learned memory bank, or external retrieval infrastructure. Evaluated on ten benchmarks across six backbones from two model families and multiple scales (Qwen3 0.6B, 1.7B, 4B, and 8B; Llama-3.2-Instruct 1B and 3B), NGM consistently improved the ten-task average for all six backbones, with gains of 4.26 points on HumanEval, 2.41 on HumanEval+, and 6.56 on GPQA-Diamond, and it also improved all five benchmarks for the vision-language model Qwen3-VL-2B.
现有的记忆增强语言模型通常依赖学习到的记忆嵌入或显式的记忆与检索组件，因而需要额外训练或增加架构复杂度。为避免这些代价，作者提出 N-gram Memory（NGM），这是一个无需训练、即插即用的模块，将主干模型预训练的 token 嵌入空间复用为记忆，其中 Causal N-Gram Encoder 通过聚合预训练 token 嵌入构建 N-gram 表示，Cosine-Gated Memory Injector 则利用余弦相似度和 ReLU 门控自适应控制注入上下文表示的记忆量。NGM 在来自两个模型家族、多种规模的六个主干模型上于十个基准进行了评估，包括四个 Qwen3 模型（0.6B 至 8B）和两个 Llama-3.2-Instruct 模型（1B 和 3B），在全部六个主干上均稳定提升了十项任务的平均成绩，其中 HumanEval 提升 4.26 个百分点、HumanEval+ 提升 2.41 个百分点、GPQA-Diamond 提升 6.56 个百分点；该方法还可迁移至视觉语言模型，Qwen3-VL-2B 在全部五个 VLM 基准上均有提升。
TP-JSCC: Efficient Token Pruning and Joint Source-Channel Coding for LVLMs-based 3D-VQA in Cloud-Edge Collaboration
Authors: Zhengxin Li, Chongzhen Tian, Hui Yuan, Hao Liu, Sam Kwong
First: 2026-10-26T14:17:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:17:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Deploying large vision-language models (LVLMs) on resource-constrained embodied agents for 3D visual question answering necessitates cloud-edge collaboration. However, offloading massive visual data to the cloud over bandwidth-limited and noisy channels introduces severe latency and transmission distortion. To address this, we propose TP-JSCC, an efficient token pruning and joint source-channel coding method for LVLMs-based 3D-VQA in Cloud-Edge Collaboration. First, we develop a Local and Global Semantics-based Hybrid Token Pruning (LGS-HTP) module to significantly reduce the visual token count. By decoupling foreground-background tokens and jointly selecting local and global semantics, LGS-HTP effectively reduces redundancy while preserving reasoning-critical tokens. Second, we introduce a lightweight Token Joint Source-Channel Coding (T-JSCC) module to further compress the pruned tokens and ensure their robust transmission over error-prone channels. By employing lightweight cascaded linear layers and Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR)-adaptive mechanism, this module adapts to fluctuating channel conditions while maintaining minimal computational overhead. Extensive experiments on ScanQA and SQA3D datasets indicate the exceptional efficiency and robustness of our approach. Specifically, on ScanQA validation set, the LGS-HTP module could achieve 90% pruning ratio while incurring less than a 1% CIDEr loss. Building on this, the T-JSCC further compresses the tokens by 18 times, resulting in an overall 180 times data volume reduction for robust transmission, while saving 90% in computational complexity for LVLM deployment. Our code is available at https://github.com/yuanhui0325/TP-JSCC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TP-JSCC：面向云边协同中基于LVLMs的3D-VQA的高效Token剪枝与联合信源信道编码
在资源受限的具身智能体上部署大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）以进行3D视觉问答，需要云边协同。然而，通过带宽受限且含噪信道将海量视觉数据卸载至云端会引入严重时延和传输失真。为此，我们提出TP-JSCC，一种面向云边协同中基于LVLMs的3D-VQA的高效Token剪枝与联合信源信道编码方法。首先，我们开发了基于局部与全局语义的混合Token剪枝（LGS-HTP）模块，以显著减少视觉Token数量。通过解耦前景-背景Token并联合选择局部与全局语义，LGS-HTP在保留推理关键Token的同时有效降低冗余。其次，我们引入轻量级Token联合信源信道编码（T-JSCC）模块，以进一步压缩剪枝后的Token并确保其在易错信道上的鲁棒传输。通过采用轻量级级联线性层和信噪比（SNR）自适应机制，该模块可适应波动的信道条件，同时保持极低计算开销。在ScanQA和SQA3D数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法具有卓越的效率与鲁棒性。具体而言，在ScanQA验证集上，LGS-HTP模块可实现90%的剪枝率，同时CIDEr损失低于1%。在此基础上，T-JSCC进一步将Token压缩18倍，从而实现总体180倍的数据量缩减以进行鲁棒传输，同时为LVLM部署节省90%的计算复杂度。我们的代码可在https://github.com/yuanhui0325/TP-JSCC获取。
Summary / 总结
Deploying large vision-language models for 3D visual question answering on resource-constrained embodied agents requires cloud-edge collaboration, but offloading massive visual data over bandwidth-limited, noisy channels causes severe latency and transmission distortion. The authors propose TP-JSCC, which combines a Local and Global Semantics-based Hybrid Token Pruning (LGS-HTP) module that decouples foreground and background tokens and jointly selects local and global semantics to cut visual token counts while preserving reasoning-critical tokens, with a lightweight Token Joint Source-Channel Coding (T-JSCC) module that uses cascaded linear layers and an SNR-adaptive mechanism to compress pruned tokens and ensure robust transmission under fluctuating channel conditions. Experiments on ScanQA and SQA3D show that LGS-HTP achieves a 90% pruning ratio on the ScanQA validation set with less than 1% CIDEr loss, and T-JSCC further compresses tokens 18 times, yielding an overall 180 times data volume reduction for robust transmission while saving 90% of computational complexity for LVLM deployment.
在资源受限的具身智能体上部署大型视觉语言模型进行3D视觉问答需要云边协同，但通过带宽受限且含噪信道将海量视觉数据卸载到云端会带来严重时延和传输失真。为此作者提出TP-JSCC方法，其中基于局部与全局语义的混合令牌剪枝（LGS-HTP）模块通过解耦前景与背景令牌并联合选择局部和全局语义，在保留推理关键令牌的同时显著减少视觉令牌数量；轻量级令牌联合信源信道编码（T-JSCC）模块则利用级联线性层和信噪比自适应机制进一步压缩剪枝后的令牌，并在信道波动下保证稳健传输。在ScanQA和SQA3D数据集上的实验表明，LGS-HTP在ScanQA验证集上可实现90%的剪枝率且CIDEr损失低于1%，T-JSCC进一步将令牌压缩18倍，总体实现180倍的数据量缩减以支持稳健传输，同时为LVLM部署节省90%的计算复杂度。
GPDPO: Learning from Reward Conflicts via Group Pareto-Dominance Policy Optimization
Authors: Zixuan Xu, Haoxuan Chen, Jiangxin Sun, Jian-Fang Hu
First: 2026-10-26T14:11:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:11:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-reward reinforcement learning offers a promising framework for aligning large language models (LLMs) with diverse objectives, but conflicting reward signals can induce ambiguous update directions and hinder stable optimization. Existing approaches address such conflicts through reward reweighting, gradient coordination, or rollout filtering, yet they primarily operate on per-response or group-level optimization signals and underexploit relational information among sampled responses. As a result, conflict-filtered rollouts may still contain useful supervision that is discarded during training. We propose Group Pareto-Dominance Policy Optimization (GPDPO), a multi-reward policy optimization method that recovers pairwise supervision from conflict-filtered rollouts. GPDPO identifies Pareto-dominance relations among sampled responses and converts them into auxiliary preference signals, which are jointly optimized with the original policy objective. This enables more effective reuse of existing rollouts without additional sampling or reward evaluation. Experiments on API-Bank across multiple model scales and reward configurations show that GPDPO consistently outperforms competitive multi-reward optimization baselines. Further analysis demonstrates that GPDPO reduces both group-relative and pairwise reward conflicts, supporting the effectiveness of exploiting relational supervision for multi-reward alignment. Code will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GPDPO：通过组帕累托支配策略优化从奖励冲突中学习
多奖励强化学习为将大语言模型（LLM）与多样化目标对齐提供了一个有前景的框架，但相互冲突的奖励信号可能导致更新方向不明确，并阻碍稳定优化。现有方法通过奖励重加权、梯度协调或 rollout 过滤来处理此类冲突，但它们主要作用于逐响应或组级优化信号，未能充分利用采样响应之间的关系信息。因此，经过冲突过滤的 rollout 可能仍包含在训练中被丢弃的有用监督。我们提出组帕累托支配策略优化（GPDPO），一种从经过冲突过滤的 rollout 中恢复成对监督的多奖励策略优化方法。GPDPO 识别采样响应之间的帕累托支配关系，并将其转换为辅助偏好信号，与原始策略目标联合优化。这能够更有效地复用现有 rollout，而无需额外采样或奖励评估。在 API-Bank 上跨多个模型规模和奖励配置的实验表明，GPDPO 始终优于具有竞争力的多奖励优化基线。进一步分析表明，GPDPO 减少了组相对和成对奖励冲突，支持了利用关系监督进行多奖励对齐的有效性。代码将发布。
Summary / 总结
Multi-reward reinforcement learning for aligning large language models is hampered by conflicting reward signals that produce ambiguous update directions and unstable optimization, and existing remedies such as reward reweighting, gradient coordination, or rollout filtering operate on per-response or group-level signals while discarding relational information among sampled responses. The authors propose Group Pareto-Dominance Policy Optimization (GPDPO), which identifies Pareto-dominance relations among sampled responses and converts them into auxiliary preference signals jointly optimized with the original policy objective, reusing conflict-filtered rollouts without additional sampling or reward evaluation. Experiments on API-Bank across multiple model scales and reward configurations show that GPDPO consistently outperforms competitive multi-reward optimization baselines, and further analysis indicates it reduces both group-relative and pairwise reward conflicts.
多奖励强化学习在使大语言模型对齐多样化目标方面具有前景，但相互冲突的奖励信号会导致更新方向不明确并阻碍稳定优化；现有的奖励重加权、梯度协调或 rollout 过滤方法主要作用于单条回复或组级优化信号，未能充分利用采样回复之间的关系信息，使得被冲突过滤的 rollout 中仍含有可用监督却被丢弃。作者提出组帕累托支配策略优化（GPDPO），通过识别采样回复之间的帕累托支配关系并将其转化为辅助偏好信号，与原始策略目标联合优化，从而在无需额外采样或奖励评估的情况下复用已有 rollout。在 API-Bank 上跨多种模型规模和奖励配置的实验表明，GPDPO 持续优于有竞争力的多奖励优化基线，进一步分析显示其降低了组内相对与成对的奖励冲突。
Training an MoE LLM in MXFP4: How We Closed the Gap to BF16
Authors: Han Wang, Zhitao Wang, Natalia Frumkin, Xuanwu Yin, Alireza Khodamoradi, Dong Li, Emad Barsoum
First: 2026-10-23T06:40:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T14:05:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training large language models (LLMs) in sub-8-bit arithmetic promises substantial gains in memory bandwidth and compute throughput, yet FP4 formats introduce quantization error severe enough to destabilize optimization if left unaddressed. We present ALTO, an open-source training recipe for GPT-OSS-20B, a 20-billion-parameter mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, on the MLPerf Small MoE Training benchmark under the MXFP4 microscaling format. The recipe combines established techniques from Abecassis, Felix et al. [arXiv:2509.25149] (hybrid 1D/2D block quantization, randomized Hadamard transforms, and stochastic rounding) with a simplified weight de-oscillation scheme adapted from TetraJet-v2 [arXiv:2510.27527]. On C4 validation at global batch size 16, MXFP4 + RHT + SR + de-oscillation reaches a validation loss of 3.3350 at 16,128 steps, only 0.007 above the BF16 baseline (3.3283), closing roughly 50% of the gap left by MXFP4 without de-oscillation (3.3418). Measured by steps to reach the MLPerf quality target (validation loss 3.34), de-oscillation cuts the FP4 convergence overhead relative to BF16 from +20.0% to +5.0%. We also report negative end-to-end results from differential gradient estimation, outlier clipping, and macro-block scaling, despite each showing operator-level SNR gains. All experiments use simulated MXFP4 kernels on AMD MI300X hardware and wall-clock speed has not been optimized.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在MXFP4中训练MoE LLM：我们如何缩小与BF16的差距
以低于8比特的算术训练大语言模型（LLM）有望在内存带宽和计算吞吐量上带来显著收益，但FP4格式引入的量化误差严重到若不加处理便会破坏优化稳定性。我们提出ALTO，一种面向GPT-OSS-20B（一个200亿参数的混合专家（MoE）模型）的开源训练配方，在MLPerf Small MoE Training基准上采用MXFP4微缩放格式。该配方结合了Abecassis、Felix等人[arXiv:2509.25149]的既有技术（混合1D/2D块量化、随机Hadamard变换和随机舍入）以及改编自TetraJet-v2 [arXiv:2510.27527]的简化权重去振荡方案。在全局批大小为16的C4验证集上，MXFP4 + RHT + SR + 去振荡在16,128步时达到验证损失3.3350，仅比BF16基线（3.3283）高0.007，缩小了未使用去振荡的MXFP4（3.3418）所留差距的约50%。以达到MLPerf质量目标（验证损失3.34）所需的步数衡量，去振荡将FP4相对于BF16的收敛开销从+20.0%降至+5.0%。我们还报告了来自差分梯度估计、离群值裁剪和宏块缩放的负面端到端结果，尽管每一项都显示出算子级SNR增益。所有实验均在AMD MI300X硬件上使用模拟MXFP4内核，且未优化实际运行时间。
Summary / 总结
Sub-8-bit training of large language models promises memory-bandwidth and compute gains, but FP4 quantization error can destabilize optimization, motivating a practical recipe that narrows the accuracy gap to BF16. The authors present ALTO, an open-source training recipe for the 20-billion-parameter mixture-of-experts model GPT-OSS-20B on the MLPerf Small MoE Training benchmark under MXFP4, combining hybrid 1D/2D block quantization, randomized Hadamard transforms, and stochastic rounding with a simplified weight de-oscillation scheme adapted from TetraJet-v2. On C4 validation at global batch size 16, MXFP4 with these techniques reaches a validation loss of 3.3350 at 16,128 steps, only 0.007 above the BF16 baseline of 3.3283 and closing roughly half the gap left by MXFP4 without de-oscillation (3.3418); measured by steps to the MLPerf quality target of 3.34, de-oscillation reduces FP4 convergence overhead relative to BF16 from +20.0% to +5.0%. The paper also reports negative end-to-end results for differential gradient estimation, outlier clipping, and macro-block scaling despite operator-level SNR gains, with all experiments using simulated MXFP4 kernels on AMD MI300X hardware and without wall-clock speed optimization.
大语言模型的亚8比特训练有望带来内存带宽和计算吞吐的显著收益，但FP4量化误差足以破坏优化稳定性，因此需要一种能缩小与BF16精度差距的实用方案。作者提出ALTO，这是一个面向200亿参数混合专家模型GPT-OSS-20B的开源训练配方，在MLPerf Small MoE Training基准上采用MXFP4微缩放格式，结合混合1D/2D块量化、随机Hadamard变换和随机舍入，并引入改编自TetraJet-v2的简化权重去振荡方案。在全局批大小为16的C4验证集上，采用上述技术的MXFP4在16,128步时验证损失为3.3350，仅比BF16基线3.3283高0.007，弥补了未使用去振荡的MXFP4（3.3418）所留差距的约一半；以达到MLPerf质量目标（验证损失3.34）所需步数衡量，去振荡将FP4相对BF16的收敛开销从+20.0%降至+5.0%。论文还报告了差分梯度估计、离群值裁剪和宏块缩放的负面端到端结果，尽管这些方法在算子层面提升了信噪比；所有实验均在AMD MI300X硬件上使用模拟MXFP4内核，且未优化实际运行时间。
Beyond Trial-and-Error: Agentic Optimization for Image-to-Video Adherence
Authors: Aman Tyagi, Hemanth Boinpally, Jonathan Chen, Douglas Gebert, Steven Hickson
First: 2026-08-12T17:35:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:52:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern black-box Image-to-Video (I2V) models offer powerful capabilities in automated content creation, yet their lack of fine-grained control and reliability presents significant challenges in professional workflows. Their inherent stochasticity causes minor variations in textual prompts or hyperparameters to yield drastically different outputs often necessitating inefficient, brute-force trial-and-error processes. To address these limitations, we introduce the ``Agentic Self-Improvement" framework, which reframes video synthesis into a closed-loop, goal-directed optimization. Our framework systematically navigates the generation parameter space using a novel two-stage approach. In the first stage, an iterative prompt optimization loop uses a multimodal Large Language Model (mLLM) to refine the input prompt. This refinement implements two automated evaluations: Davidsonian Scene Graph (DSG) queries ensure semantic adherence, and Common Mistake Questions (CMQ) for artifact detection. At the second stage, we use Bayesian optimization to efficiently co-optimize stochastic seeds and CFG scales. This search is guided by a suite of quality metrics, including the novel Video-Text Adherence (VTA) score derived from the DSG and CMQ evaluations. Our framework significantly outperforms unguided search methods: in human preference studies, videos generated via our agentic approach were strongly preferred over baseline outputs, achieving win rates up to 69\%. This work provides a practical and extensible methodology for enhancing the predictability and control of state-of-the-art video generation models, moving the field beyond speculative curiosities toward reliable, production-ready tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越试错：面向图像到视频一致性的智能体优化
现代黑盒图像到视频（I2V）模型在自动化内容创作中提供了强大能力，但其缺乏细粒度控制和可靠性，在专业工作流中带来了重大挑战。其固有的随机性使得文本提示或超参数的微小变化会产生截然不同的输出，往往需要低效的暴力试错过程。为解决这些局限，我们引入了“智能体自我改进”框架，将视频合成重新构建为闭环、目标导向的优化。我们的框架采用新颖的两阶段方法系统地导航生成参数空间。在第一阶段，迭代提示优化循环使用多模态大语言模型（mLLM）来精炼输入提示。该精炼实现了两项自动化评估：戴维森场景图（DSG）查询确保语义一致性，常见错误问题（CMQ）用于伪影检测。在第二阶段，我们使用贝叶斯优化来高效地协同优化随机种子和CFG尺度。该搜索由一套质量指标引导，包括源自DSG和CMQ评估的新颖视频-文本一致性（VTA）分数。我们的框架显著优于无引导搜索方法：在人类偏好研究中，通过我们的智能体方法生成的视频相比基线输出受到强烈偏好，胜率高达69%。这项工作提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法论，用于增强最先进视频生成模型的可预测性和控制力，推动该领域超越推测性好奇，迈向可靠、生产就绪的工具。
Summary / 总结
Black-box image-to-video models are stochastic and hard to control, so small prompt or hyperparameter changes produce very different outputs and force inefficient trial-and-error workflows. The proposed Agentic Self-Improvement framework treats video synthesis as a closed-loop, goal-directed optimization in two stages: an iterative prompt refinement loop driven by a multimodal LLM, using Davidsonian Scene Graph queries for semantic adherence and Common Mistake Questions for artifact detection, followed by Bayesian optimization that co-optimizes stochastic seeds and CFG scales guided by quality metrics including a new Video-Text Adherence score derived from those evaluations. In human preference studies, the agentic approach strongly outperformed unguided search, with win rates up to 69%.
黑盒图生视频模型缺乏细粒度控制，其随机性使得提示词或超参数的微小变化就会导致输出差异巨大，从而不得不进行低效的试错。为此提出的Agentic Self-Improvement框架将视频合成重构为闭环、目标导向的优化，分为两个阶段：第一阶段由多模态大语言模型驱动迭代式提示词优化循环，利用Davidsonian Scene Graph查询保证语义一致性，并用Common Mistake Questions检测伪影；第二阶段使用贝叶斯优化，在包括新提出的Video-Text Adherence分数在内的质量指标引导下，协同优化随机种子与CFG尺度。人类偏好研究表明，该智能体方法生成的视频明显优于基线输出，胜率最高达69%，优于无引导的搜索方法。
Beyond Federated Learning: Decentralized Agentic Systems for Self-Evolving Clinical Intelligence
Authors: Haoyuan Li, Mathias Funk, Aaqib Saeed
First: 2026-10-26T13:50:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:50:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical expertise is inherently distributed: it resides not in any single dataset or institution but in the accumulated reasoning experiences of thousands of clinicians navigating diverse patient populations, resource constraints, and evolving treatment protocols. Yet current approaches to scaling medical AI, from centralized large language models to federated learning, remain anchored to a data-centric paradigm that treats knowledge as static parameters to be aggregated. In this Perspective, we argue for a fundamental shift from data aggregation to experience aggregation. We propose Decentralized Agentic Systems (DAS), a framework in which distributed AI agents operating across healthcare institutions synthesize collective clinical intelligence by sharing abstracted reasoning trajectories rather than raw data or model parameters. Unlike federated learning, which averages numerical gradients across homogeneous model architectures, DAS exchanges structured records of clinical reasoning, specifically the sequence of observations, hypotheses, tool invocations, and outcome feedback that constitute diagnostic experience. We examine the architectural principles, deployment considerations, governance requirements, and critical open challenges of this framework, laying the groundwork for a collaborative medical AI ecosystem where distributed expertise autonomously adapts to the complexities of longitudinal patient care.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越联邦学习：面向自演化临床智能的去中心化智能体系统
临床专业知识本质上是分布式的：它并不存在于任何单一数据集或机构中，而是存在于数千名临床医生在应对多样化患者群体、资源约束和不断演变的治疗方案过程中所积累的推理经验之中。然而，当前扩展医疗AI的方法，从集中式大语言模型到联邦学习，仍锚定于以数据为中心的范式，将知识视为待聚合的静态参数。在本视角文章中，我们主张从数据聚合向经验聚合的根本性转变。我们提出去中心化智能体系统（DAS），该框架中分布于各医疗机构的AI智能体通过共享抽象化的推理轨迹而非原始数据或模型参数，来综合集体临床智能。与在同构模型架构间平均数值梯度的联邦学习不同，DAS交换临床推理的结构化记录，具体而言是构成诊断经验的观察、假设、工具调用和结果反馈序列。我们考察该框架的架构原则、部署考量、治理要求和关键开放挑战，为分布式专业知识能够自主适应纵向患者护理复杂性的协作式医疗AI生态系统奠定基础。
Summary / 总结
Current approaches to scaling medical AI, including centralized large language models and federated learning, remain tied to a data-centric paradigm that treats clinical knowledge as static parameters to be aggregated, even though clinical expertise is inherently distributed across institutions and clinicians. This Perspective proposes Decentralized Agentic Systems (DAS), a framework in which distributed AI agents across healthcare institutions share abstracted reasoning trajectories, namely structured records of observations, hypotheses, tool invocations, and outcome feedback, rather than raw data or numerical gradients as in federated learning. The authors examine the framework's architectural principles, deployment considerations, governance requirements, and open challenges, arguing for a shift from data aggregation to experience aggregation as groundwork for a collaborative medical AI ecosystem; as a Perspective, the paper presents a conceptual framework rather than empirical experiments, so no experimental results are reported.
当前扩展医疗AI的方法，包括集中式大语言模型和联邦学习，都把临床知识视为可聚合的静态参数，而临床专长本质上分散在不同机构和临床医生之中。本文提出去中心化智能体系统（DAS）框架，让部署在各医疗机构的分布式AI智能体共享抽象的推理轨迹，即由观察、假设、工具调用和结果反馈构成的序列，而非原始数据或数值梯度。作者讨论了该框架的架构原则、部署考量、治理要求和关键开放挑战，主张从数据聚合转向经验聚合，为协作式医疗AI生态奠定基础；本文为观点文章，未报告实验结果。
Omni-InteractScope: Benchmarking Proactive and Agentic Interaction in Continuous Omnimodal Streams
Authors: Yinsong Yan, Ling Wang, Yuxuan Wang, Yifan Yang, Ruiyang Xu, Junming Lin, Qi Chen, Meng Gao, Minghao Han, Yunfei Chu, Qize Yang, Xize Cheng, Jin Xu, Jibin Wu
First: 2026-10-26T13:39:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:39:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Omnimodal large language models (Omni-LLMs) are increasingly expected to assist users while observing continuous audiovisual streams. However, existing evaluations provide limited insight into how reliably these models connect stream evidence and user needs to timely, sustained interventions. We introduce \textbf{Omni-InteractScope}, a benchmark of 1,479 human-annotated, cross-reviewed interactions with 1,790 temporal targets across eight task groups. It links input modalities, event triggers, intervention types, and user needs, covering event-timed tool invocation and query-free assistance alongside notifications, warnings, state tracking, and continuous responses. Our experimental results on the proposed benchmark reveal three critical limitations of current Omni-LLMs: (1) \textbf{Timely tool invocation remains unreliable}: all 12 evaluated models produce an on-time first response for at most 40\% of tool-invocation targets, and joint timing-and-tool accuracy reaches at most 32.0\%. (2) \textbf{Query-free reminders remain poorly covered}: all evaluated models achieve lower timely reference-target recall on query-free reminders than on explicit questions, measured on their respective target sets. Reminder recall never exceeds 23.0\%. (3) \textbf{End-to-end guidance coverage remains limited}: nine models cover none of the 26 guidance sequences in full, and the remaining three cover only one to three sequences each. Targeted controls further show that tool-invocation scores alone do not establish event grounding and that reminder coverage depends on response history and frequency. These findings motivate developing and evaluating streaming assistants for timely action, initiative under general user needs, and coverage of entire response sequences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Omni-InteractScope：在连续全模态流中基准测试主动式与智能体式交互
全模态大语言模型（Omni-LLM）日益被期望在观察连续音视频流的同时为用户提供协助。然而，现有评估对这些模型如何可靠地将流证据与用户需求关联起来、以及如何及时且持续地进行干预，所提供的洞察有限。我们提出\textbf{Omni-InteractScope}，这是一个包含1,479条人工标注、交叉评审的交互的基准，涵盖八个任务组中的1,790个时间目标。它将输入模态、事件触发器、干预类型和用户需求关联起来，覆盖事件定时工具调用和无查询辅助，以及通知、警告、状态跟踪和连续响应。我们在所提出的基准上的实验结果揭示了当前Omni-LLM的三个关键局限：（1）\textbf{及时工具调用仍不可靠}：所有12个被评估模型对工具调用目标最多仅能对40\%的目标产生准时首次响应，联合定时与工具准确率最高仅达32.0\%。（2）\textbf{无查询提醒的覆盖仍然不足}：所有被评估模型在无查询提醒上对其各自目标集的及时参考目标召回率均低于显式问题。提醒召回率从未超过23.0\%。（3）\textbf{端到端指导覆盖仍然有限}：九个模型对26个指导序列中的任何一个都未能完整覆盖，其余三个模型各自仅覆盖一至三个序列。针对性控制进一步表明，仅凭工具调用分数并不能确立事件接地，且提醒覆盖取决于响应历史和频率。这些发现推动我们开发和评估流式助手，以实现及时行动、在一般用户需求下的主动性，以及对整个响应序列的覆盖。
Summary / 总结
Existing evaluations of omnimodal large language models offer limited insight into how reliably these models connect continuous audiovisual stream evidence to user needs and timely interventions, motivating a new benchmark. The authors introduce Omni-InteractScope, a benchmark of 1,479 human-annotated, cross-reviewed interactions with 1,790 temporal targets across eight task groups, linking input modalities, event triggers, intervention types, and user needs, and covering event-timed tool invocation, query-free assistance, notifications, warnings, state tracking, and continuous responses. Experiments with 12 models reveal three limitations: timely tool invocation is unreliable, with at most 40% of tool-invocation targets receiving an on-time first response and joint timing-and-tool accuracy reaching at most 32.0%; query-free reminder recall never exceeds 23.0% and is lower than recall on explicit questions; and end-to-end guidance coverage is limited, with nine models covering none of 26 guidance sequences in full and the other three covering only one to three each. Targeted controls further show that tool-invocation scores alone do not establish event grounding and that reminder coverage depends on response history and frequency.
全模态大语言模型日益被期望在观察连续音视频流的同时为用户提供协助，但现有评测难以揭示它们能否可靠地将流中的证据与用户需求关联起来并及时、持续地介入。为此，作者提出了Omni-InteractScope基准，包含1,479条经人工标注和交叉审核的交互以及1,790个时间目标，覆盖八个任务组，将输入模态、事件触发、介入类型和用户需求相互关联，涵盖按事件触发的工具调用、无查询协助，以及通知、警告、状态跟踪和连续响应。实验揭示了当前模型的三个关键局限：及时工具调用不可靠，全部12个受评模型对工具调用目标的最多40%能给出准时首次响应，时序与工具联合准确率最高仅为32.0%；无查询提醒覆盖不足，及时参考目标召回率从未超过23.0%，且始终低于显式提问情形；端到端引导覆盖有限，九个模型对26条引导序列无一完整覆盖，其余三个模型各仅覆盖一至三条。针对性对照实验进一步表明，仅凭工具调用得分无法证明事件 grounding，且提醒覆盖率取决于响应历史和频率。
Faithful Explanations under Missing Modalities in Multimodal Dementia Prognosis
Authors: Firuz Kamalov
First: 2026-10-26T13:34:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:34:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly used to turn the feature attributions of multimodal prognostic models into narratives for clinicians, yet costly modalities such as amyloid PET and cerebrospinal fluid biomarkers are often missing, and whether these explanations stay faithful under missing evidence has not been tested. We separate two failure points: phantom attribution, in which the model credits modalities that were never observed, and unfaithful narration, in which the report omits, cites or misjudges missing modalities. Using 307 participants of the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative with complete data, we masked every subset of four costly modalities, predicted 36-month conversion from mild cognitive impairment to Alzheimer's disease, and restored the masked data to obtain interventional ground truth. All five missing-data strategies assigned 12% to 23% of attribution to unobserved modalities, and up to 45% with four absent; missingness-aware strategies minimised it at no cost in accuracy. Across 51,200 reports from four language models and four prompting regimes, naming absent modalities in the prompt raised acknowledgement from 48% to 88% and cut phantom evidence twenty-fold, but advice on whether acquiring a missing test would change the prediction was correct in only 27% to 41% of cases until checked against executed restorations, which raised accuracy to 69% for open-weight narrators and 99.9% for a proprietary model. Higher phantom attribution was associated with more phantom evidence and fewer faithful direction claims, and direction fidelity, measured by a calibrated language-model judge, depended on narrator scale rather than prompting. Narrators told to name absent modalities sometimes declared observed ones absent, an error that a structured template eliminated. We release the masking protocol and evaluation code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态痴呆预后中缺失模态下的忠实解释
大语言模型越来越多地被用于将多模态预后模型的特征归因转化为面向临床医生的叙述，但淀粉样蛋白PET和脑脊液生物标志物等昂贵模态常常缺失，而这些解释在证据缺失时是否保持忠实尚未得到检验。我们区分了两个失效点：幻影归因，即模型将归因归于从未观测到的模态；以及不忠实叙述，即报告遗漏、引用或误判缺失模态。我们使用阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议中307名具有完整数据的参与者，对四种昂贵模态的每个子集进行掩蔽，预测轻度认知障碍向阿尔茨海默病的36个月转化，并恢复被掩蔽的数据以获得干预性真值。所有五种缺失数据处理策略将12%至23%的归因分配给未观测模态，在四种模态缺失时高达45%；缺失感知策略在不损失准确率的情况下将其最小化。在来自四个语言模型和四种提示机制的51,200份报告中，在提示中指明缺失模态使确认率从48%提高到88%，并将幻影证据减少二十倍，但关于获取缺失检测是否会改变预测的建议仅在27%至41%的案例中正确，直到对照已执行的恢复进行核查，这将开放权重叙述者的准确率提高到69%，专有模型提高到99.9%。较高的幻影归因与更多幻影证据和更少忠实方向声明相关，而方向忠实度（由校准的语言模型评判器测量）取决于叙述者规模而非提示。被要求指明缺失模态的叙述者有时将已观测模态声明为缺失，这一错误可由结构化模板消除。我们发布了掩蔽协议和评估代码。

This paper examines whether narrative explanations generated by large language models remain faithful when multimodal dementia prognostic models are missing costly modalities such as amyloid PET and cerebrospinal fluid biomarkers, distinguishing phantom attribution (crediting unobserved modalities) from unfaithful narration (omitting, citing, or misjudging missing modalities).
