本文考察多模态痴呆预后模型的大语言模型叙事解释在淀粉样蛋白PET和脑脊液生物标志物等昂贵模态缺失时是否仍然忠实，并区分两类失败：幻影归因（将归因归于从未观测的模态）和不忠实叙述（遗漏、引用或误判缺失模态）。作者使用阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议中307名数据完整的参与者，对四种昂贵模态的每个子集进行掩蔽，预测轻度认知障碍36个月内转化为阿尔茨海默病，并恢复掩蔽数据以获得干预性真值。五种缺失数据处理策略均将12%至23%的归因分配给未观测模态（四种模态全缺时高达45%），而缺失感知策略在不损失准确率的情况下将其降至最低；在四个语言模型和四种提示方案生成的51,200份报告中，在提示中列出缺失模态使承认率从48%升至88%，并将幻影证据减少二十倍，但关于获取某项缺失检测是否会改变预测的建议仅在27%至41%的情况下正确，直到对照已执行的恢复操作进行核查，才将开放权重叙述者的准确率提升至69%、专有模型提升至99.9%。较高的幻影归因与更多幻影证据和更少忠实方向判断相关，方向忠实度取决于叙述者规模而非提示方式，结构化模板则消除了叙述者将已观测模态误称为缺失的错误。
Beyond LLM-GA: Secure Fluid Antenna Systems with ReEvo-Designed Memetic Algorithm
Authors: Hanyong Xu, Zhaolai Dang, Tong Zhang
First: 2026-10-07T15:20:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:18:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted by WCSP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fluid antenna systems (FASs) offer significant spatial flexibility, yet securing them against eavesdropping is critical for practical FAS deployment in military, satellite, and internet-of-things networks. Although large language model (LLM)-assisted genetic algorithms (LLM-GAs) can address this secure FAS port selection problem, whether further algorithmic improvement is possible warrants deeper investigation. To this end, we propose a memetic algorithm based on reflective evolution (ReEvo). Unlike the state-of-the-art LLM-GAs, which design only crossover or mutation operators with an LLM, our algorithm leverages an LLM to evolve dedicated crossover, mutation, and local-search operators offline. These operators are then embedded into a memetic search framework, thereby obviating any online LLM queries during execution. Simulation results at equal generation counts demonstrate that our proposed algorithm achieves a higher secure sum-rate than the conventional GA and the state-of-the-art LLM-GAs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越LLM-GA：基于ReEvo设计模因算法的安全流体天线系统
流体天线系统（FAS）具有显著的空间灵活性，但确保其抗窃听安全性对于FAS在军事、卫星和物联网网络中的实际部署至关重要。尽管大语言模型（LLM）辅助的遗传算法（LLM-GA）可以解决这一安全FAS端口选择问题，但是否还能进一步提升算法性能仍值得深入研究。为此，我们提出了一种基于反思进化（ReEvo）的模因算法。与仅利用LLM设计交叉或变异算子的最先进LLM-GA不同，我们的算法利用LLM离线进化专用的交叉、变异和局部搜索算子。随后，这些算子被嵌入模因搜索框架，从而在执行过程中无需任何在线LLM查询。在相同进化代数下的仿真结果表明，与常规GA和最先进LLM-GA相比，我们提出的算法实现了更高的安全总速率。
Summary / 总结
Securing fluid antenna systems (FASs) against eavesdropping is essential for practical deployment in military, satellite, and internet-of-things networks, and while LLM-assisted genetic algorithms (LLM-GAs) have been applied to the secure FAS port selection problem, whether further algorithmic gains are attainable remains an open question. The authors propose a memetic algorithm based on reflective evolution (ReEvo) that, unlike state-of-the-art LLM-GAs which use an LLM to design only crossover or mutation operators, employs an LLM offline to evolve dedicated crossover, mutation, and local-search operators, which are then embedded in a memetic search framework so that no online LLM queries are needed during execution. Simulation results at equal generation counts show that the proposed algorithm achieves a higher secure sum-rate than the conventional GA and the state-of-the-art LLM-GAs.
流体天线系统在军事、卫星和物联网网络中的实际部署需要抵御窃听，虽然大语言模型辅助的遗传算法（LLM-GA）可以解决安全端口选择问题，但是否还能进一步提升算法性能仍值得深入研究。为此，作者提出一种基于反思进化（ReEvo）的模因算法，利用大语言模型离线进化专用的交叉、变异和局部搜索算子，并将其嵌入模因搜索框架，从而在执行过程中无需任何在线大语言模型查询。在相同进化代数下的仿真结果表明，该算法相比传统遗传算法和最先进的LLM-GA取得了更高的安全总速率。
SAPO: Single-Rollout Autoregressive Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Dayang Liang, Lang Feng, Bo An, Yunlong Liu
First: 2026-08-20T09:43:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:13:30+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://github.com/dy-liang/SAPO
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as an important post-training approach for enhancing the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods face a trade-off between policy performance and resource efficiency. Conventional Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) implementations incur substantial memory overhead from a separate critic, whereas critic-free group-relative methods require multiple rollouts and face potential learning bottlenecks on long-horizon tasks. In this work, we propose Single-rollout Autoregressive Policy Optimization (SAPO), an efficient PPO-style framework that unifies policy optimization and value learning within a single causal language model. SAPO exploits the autoregressive structure of LLMs to sequentially generate action and value estimation at distinct causal boundaries with shared parameters, and then jointly optimizes the PPO objectives and an auxiliary on-policy SARSA objective with turn-level generalized advantage estimation, where the latter is designed to facilitate value learning. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Qwen3-14B demonstrate that SAPO reduces peak GPU memory usage by 24.8% and per-iteration runtime by 23.1% over strong PPO baseline, while matching or slightly improving task success rate. Our experiments also show that SAPO outperforms Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and recent cutting-edge variants in both task success and training stability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAPO：面向智能体强化学习的单次采样自回归策略优化
智能体强化学习（RL）已成为增强大语言模型（LLM）能力的重要后训练方法。然而，现有方法在策略性能与资源效率之间存在权衡。传统的近端策略优化（PPO）实现因独立的critic而带来大量内存开销，而无critic的组相对方法需要多次采样，并在长时程任务上面临潜在的学习瓶颈。在本工作中，我们提出单次采样自回归策略优化（SAPO），这是一种高效的PPO风格框架，在单个因果语言模型内统一了策略优化与价值学习。SAPO利用LLM的自回归结构，在不同因果边界上以共享参数依次生成动作和价值估计，然后联合优化PPO目标与辅助的在线SARSA目标，并采用轮级广义优势估计，其中后者旨在促进价值学习。在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B和Qwen3-14B进行的大量实验表明，与强PPO基线相比，SAPO将峰值GPU内存使用降低24.8%，每次迭代运行时间降低23.1%，同时任务成功率持平或略有提升。我们的实验还表明，SAPO在任务成功率和训练稳定性上均优于组相对策略优化（GRPO）及近期前沿变体。
Summary / 总结
Agentic reinforcement learning for LLMs faces a trade-off between policy performance and resource efficiency, since PPO requires a separate critic that adds memory overhead while critic-free group-relative methods need multiple rollouts and can bottleneck on long-horizon tasks. The authors propose SAPO, a PPO-style framework that unifies policy optimization and value learning in a single causal language model by exploiting its autoregressive structure to generate actions and value estimates at distinct causal boundaries with shared parameters, jointly optimizing PPO objectives with an auxiliary on-policy SARSA objective using turn-level generalized advantage estimation. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Qwen3-14B show SAPO reduces peak GPU memory by 24.8% and per-iteration runtime by 23.1% versus a strong PPO baseline while matching or slightly improving task success rate, and it outperforms GRPO and recent variants in both task success and training stability.
面向大语言模型的智能体强化学习在策略性能与资源效率之间存在权衡：传统PPO因需要独立critic而带来显著内存开销，而无critic的组相对方法需要多次rollout，且在长程任务上可能出现学习瓶颈。作者提出SAPO，一种PPO风格的框架，在单个因果语言模型中统一策略优化与价值学习，利用其自回归结构在不同因果边界以共享参数依次生成动作与价值估计，并结合回合级广义优势估计联合优化PPO目标与辅助的在线SARSA目标。在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B与Qwen3-14B的实验表明，相比强PPO基线，SAPO将峰值GPU内存降低24.8%、每轮运行时间降低23.1%，同时任务成功率持平或略有提升，并在任务成功率和训练稳定性上优于GRPO及近期先进变体。
Autonomous Structural Discovery Of Bioprocess Dynamics And Kinetics Via LLM-Driven Agents
Authors: Erdal Aydin, Yaman Arkun
First: 2026-10-26T13:10:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:10:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-fidelity digital twins in continuous biomanufacturing are often bottlenecked by incomplete kinetic models, while standard data-driven algorithms (e.g., SINDy, PINNs) lack interpretability or impose rigid physical assumptions. This paper introduces a hybrid closed-loop architecture for autonomous kinetic state-space discovery driven by a Large Language Model (LLM) agent. The framework employs a sequential "isolation-peeling" methodology across orthogonal operational regimes. This algorithmic peeling mathematically decouples entangled multiplicative (inhibitory) and additive (catalytic) biological rate law. Grounded in a Two-Level Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) library, the framework utilizes Moving Horizon Estimation (MHE) and logarithmic decomposition to decouple complex cell growth kinetics into orthogonal multiplicative (inhibitory) and additive (regulatory) residual channels. When statistical degeneracy occurs an Active Design of Experiments (DoE) controller dynamically triggers targeted perturbations to decorrelate competing model hypotheses. Evaluated on a continuous stirred-tank reactor exhibiting unmodeled Haldane inhibition, Luong product toxicity, and secondary metabolite autocatalysis, the framework autonomously expanded the state space and recovered ground-truth parameters with under 6% error without a priori kinetic knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于LLM驱动智能体的生物过程动力学与动力学自主结构发现
连续生物制造中的高保真数字孪生常受限于不完整的动力学模型，而标准数据驱动算法（如SINDy、PINNs）缺乏可解释性或强加刚性的物理假设。本文提出一种由大语言模型（LLM）智能体驱动的混合闭环架构，用于自主动力学状态空间发现。该框架在正交操作区间采用顺序“隔离-剥离”方法。该算法剥离在数学上解耦纠缠的乘性（抑制性）与加性（催化性）生物速率定律。该框架基于两级检索增强生成（RAG）库，利用移动 horizon 估计（MHE）和对数分解，将复杂细胞生长动力学解耦为正交的乘性（抑制性）与加性（调控性）残差通道。当出现统计退化时，主动实验设计（DoE）控制器动态触发定向扰动，以去相关竞争模型假设。在表现出未建模的Haldane抑制、Luong产物毒性和次级代谢物自催化的连续搅拌釜反应器上评估，该框架在无先验动力学知识的情况下自主扩展状态空间，并以低于6%的误差恢复真实参数。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the bottleneck of incomplete kinetic models in high-fidelity digital twins for continuous biomanufacturing, where standard data-driven methods such as SINDy and PINNs either lack interpretability or impose rigid physical assumptions. It proposes a hybrid closed-loop architecture driven by a Large Language Model agent that performs autonomous kinetic state-space discovery using a sequential isolation-peeling methodology across orthogonal operational regimes, mathematically decoupling entangled multiplicative inhibitory and additive catalytic rate laws; the framework relies on a two-level Retrieval-Augmented Generation library, Moving Horizon Estimation, and logarithmic decomposition, with an Active Design of Experiments controller triggering targeted perturbations when statistical degeneracy arises to decorrelate competing hypotheses. Evaluated on a continuous stirred-tank reactor exhibiting unmodeled Haldane inhibition, Luong product toxicity, and secondary metabolite autocatalysis, the framework autonomously expanded the state space and recovered ground-truth parameters with under 6% error without prior kinetic knowledge.
连续生物制造中的高保真数字孪生常受限于不完整的动力学模型，而SINDy、PINNs等标准数据驱动算法要么缺乏可解释性，要么施加僵化的物理假设。本文提出一种由大语言模型智能体驱动的混合闭环架构，用于自主发现动力学状态空间；该框架在正交操作区间上采用序贯的隔离-剥离方法，从数学上解耦相互纠缠的乘性抑制型与加性催化型生物速率定律，并依托两级检索增强生成库、移动地平线估计与对数分解，在出现统计简并时由主动实验设计控制器动态触发针对性扰动以解耦相互竞争的模型假设。在存在未建模Haldane抑制、Luong产物毒性和次级代谢物自催化的连续搅拌釜反应器上评估，该框架在无先验动力学知识的情况下自主扩展了状态空间，并以低于6%的误差恢复了真实参数。
Latent Goal Prediction from Language for Model-Based Planning
Authors: Samuel Barbeau, Simon Roy, Giovanni Beltrame, Christian Desrosiers, Nicolas Thome
First: 2026-05-26T20:04:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T13:04:59+00:00
Comments: 10 pages. Preprint under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) enable agents to plan in latent space by imagining the outcomes of candidate actions, yet task specification remains a bottleneck. Visual targets provide precise local gradients but poor distant guidance, while language is flexible yet limited by noisy cross-modal alignment or dependence on distinct large generative models. We introduce LAGO (Latent Goal Prediction from Language), a hierarchical world model in which a single predictor both forecasts action-conditioned dynamics and grounds language instructions as sequences of intermediate latent subgoals, training both modes with a single regression objective over a shared latent space. At each planning step, LAGO predicts a sequence of latent subgoals from a language instruction and optimizes an action sequence using a soft-minimum alignment cost that rewards subgoal proximity without enforcing a rigid path. Subgoals are repredicted as the agent's state evolves, turning a single long-horizon instruction into a sequence of locally tractable objectives. Across environments spanning navigation and manipulation, LAGO outperforms flat and hierarchical image-goal planners, with the largest gains on long-horizon navigation with curved, hazard-constrained paths, where it more than doubles the success rate of a goal-conditioned behavior cloning policy trained on the same demonstrations. Grounding language in the world model's own latent space also outperforms vision-language reward models, including one that plans with ground-truth dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于语言为基于模型的规划进行潜在目标预测
联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）使智能体能够通过在潜在空间中想象候选动作的结果来进行规划，但任务规范仍然是一个瓶颈。视觉目标提供了精确的局部梯度，但远距离引导较差；而语言虽然灵活，却受限于噪声较大的跨模态对齐或对独立大型生成模型的依赖。我们提出了LAGO（基于语言的潜在目标预测），这是一种分层世界模型，其中单个预测器既预测以动作为条件的动力学，又将语言指令作为中间潜在子目标序列进行接地，并以共享潜在空间上的单一回归目标训练这两种模式。在每个规划步骤中，LAGO从语言指令预测一系列潜在子目标，并使用软最小对齐成本优化动作序列，该成本奖励子目标接近性而不强制刚性路径。随着智能体状态演化，子目标会被重新预测，从而将单个长时程指令转化为一系列局部可处理的目标。在涵盖导航和操作的环境中，LAGO优于扁平式和分层式图像目标规划器，在具有弯曲、危险约束路径的长时程导航中增益最大，其成功率是同一演示上训练的目标条件行为克隆策略的两倍以上。将语言接地于世界模型自身的潜在空间也优于视觉-语言奖励模型，包括一个使用真实动力学进行规划的模型。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the bottleneck of task specification in latent-space planning with Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures, where visual targets give precise but local gradients and language grounding suffers from noisy cross-modal alignment or reliance on separate large generative models. The authors introduce LAGO, a hierarchical world model in which a single predictor both forecasts action-conditioned dynamics and grounds language instructions as sequences of intermediate latent subgoals, trained with one regression objective over a shared latent space; at each planning step it predicts subgoals from the instruction and optimizes actions using a soft-minimum alignment cost that rewards subgoal proximity without enforcing a rigid path, repredicting subgoals as the state evolves. Across navigation and manipulation environments, LAGO outperforms flat and hierarchical image-goal planners, with the largest gains on long-horizon navigation with curved, hazard-constrained paths, where it more than doubles the success rate of a goal-conditioned behavior cloning policy trained on the same demonstrations, and grounding language in the world model's own latent space also outperforms vision-language reward models, including one planning with ground-truth dynamics.
在基于联合嵌入预测架构的潜空间规划中，任务指定仍是瓶颈：视觉目标提供精确但局部的梯度，而语言接地则受限于噪声较大的跨模态对齐或对独立大型生成模型的依赖。作者提出 LAGO，一种分层世界模型，其中单一预测器既预测以动作为条件的动力学，又将语言指令接地为一系列中间潜子目标，两种模式在共享潜空间上以单一回归目标训练；在每个规划步骤，LAGO 根据语言指令预测子目标序列，并用软最小值对齐代价优化动作序列，该代价奖励接近子目标而不强制固定路径，并随智能体状态演化重新预测子目标。在涵盖导航与操作的环境中，LAGO 优于扁平与分层图像目标规划器，在具有弯曲、危险约束路径的长时程导航中提升最大，其成功率超过在相同示范上训练的目标条件行为克隆策略的两倍以上；将语言接地于世界模型自身潜空间也优于视觉语言奖励模型，包括一个使用真实动力学进行规划的模型。
ACSAN: All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network Integrating Convolutional Operations with Vision Transformer
Authors: Zimian Wei, Tianqi Zhang, Hengyue Pan
First: 2026-10-26T12:58:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:58:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the past few years, Vision Transformer (ViT) has achieved excellent performance in the field of computer vision. However, there still some problems remain. For instance, ViTs heavily rely on large-scale training data since they simply contain a large number of trainable parameters, which slows down their learning efficiency and limits their applicability in resource-constrained scenarios. In this paper, we propose a novel deep learning model, namely All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network (ACSAN), which applies convolution operations to replace fully-connected matrices multiplications in the ViT framework. To implement ACSAN, we firstly design Dynamic Convolutional Self-Attention (DCSA) as the basic module, which employs multi-scale dilated convolutions to generate Query, Key, and Value. We defined spatial self-attention and channel self-attention to generate attention features respectively. The two attention features are finally merged as the output of the DCSA module. Based on DCSA, we then construct the basic ACSAN block. Comparing with ViT, we use DCSA to replace the regular self-attention operations, and use convolution layers to replace MLPs. To evaluate the performance of the proposed method, we train ACSAN on three widely-used image classification databases, i.e. CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1k, from scratch. Experimental results show that ACSAN achieves excellent classification accuracy comparing with standard ViT with comparable or fewer parameters, as well as other state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies verify that each component contributes positively to the final performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ACSAN：融合卷积操作与视觉Transformer的全卷积自注意力网络
过去几年中，视觉Transformer（ViT）在计算机视觉领域取得了优异性能。然而，仍存在一些问题。例如，ViT严重依赖大规模训练数据，因为其仅包含大量可训练参数，这降低了学习效率并限制了其在资源受限场景中的适用性。本文提出一种新颖的深度学习模型，即全卷积自注意力网络（ACSAN），该模型在ViT框架中应用卷积操作替代全连接矩阵乘法。为实现ACSAN，我们首先设计动态卷积自注意力（DCSA）作为基本模块，其采用多尺度空洞卷积生成Query、Key和Value。我们分别定义空间自注意力和通道自注意力以生成注意力特征。两种注意力特征最终合并为DCSA模块的输出。基于DCSA，我们进而构建基本ACSAN块。与ViT相比，我们使用DCSA替代常规自注意力操作，并使用卷积层替代MLP。为评估所提方法的性能，我们在三个广泛使用的图像分类数据库（即CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1k）上从头训练ACSAN。实验结果表明，与参数量相当或更少的标准ViT以及其他最先进方法相比，ACSAN取得了优异的分类准确率。消融研究验证了每个组件对最终性能均有积极贡献。
Summary / 总结
Vision Transformers achieve strong computer vision performance but rely on large numbers of trainable parameters and extensive training data, which slows learning and limits use in resource-constrained settings. To address this, the authors propose the All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network (ACSAN), which replaces the fully-connected matrix multiplications in ViT with convolution operations. Its core module, Dynamic Convolutional Self-Attention (DCSA), uses multi-scale dilated convolutions to generate Query, Key, and Value, and separately produces spatial and channel self-attention features that are merged as the module output; the ACSAN block then substitutes DCSA for regular self-attention and convolution layers for MLPs. Trained from scratch on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1k, ACSAN achieves classification accuracy competitive with or better than standard ViT at comparable or fewer parameters and other state-of-the-art methods, and ablation studies confirm that each component contributes positively to final performance.
视觉Transformer在计算机视觉领域表现优异，但其可训练参数众多，严重依赖大规模训练数据，导致学习效率下降并限制其在资源受限场景中的应用。为此，作者提出全卷积自注意力网络ACSAN，用卷积运算替代ViT框架中的全连接矩阵乘法：其基本模块动态卷积自注意力DCSA利用多尺度空洞卷积生成Query、Key和Value，分别计算空间自注意力与通道自注意力特征并融合输出；在此基础上构建的ACSAN模块以DCSA替代常规自注意力，并以卷积层替代MLP。作者在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1k三个数据集上从零训练，结果表明ACSAN在参数量相当或更少的情况下取得了优于标准ViT及其他先进方法的分类精度，消融实验也验证了各组成部分均对最终性能有正向贡献。
GIFT: Representation Geometry Matters for Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection
Authors: Yin Zhang, Yaoyue Zheng, Yongqiang Zhang, Bogdan Raducanu, Dan Li
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-10-26T12:58:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:58:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 22 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Single-Domain Generalized Object Detection (S-DGOD) aims to generalize from a single source domain to unseen domains under distribution shifts. Existing efforts mainly focus on simulating unseen domains or suppressing domain-specific factors. However, they largely overlook the intrinsic generalization capability embedded in Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). In this work, we show that unconstrained fine-tuning disrupts the geometric structure of pre-trained representations, which encodes domain-invariant semantics. Motivated by this, we propose Geometric-Invariance Fine-Tuning (GIFT), a geometric-aware fine-tuning method for VFMs. Specifically, we introduce a series of learnable Householder reflections to the pre-trained weights to encourage geometry-consistent adaptation under domain shift. In addition, we propose a lightweight channel-wise scaling to facilitate effective task-specific adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GIFT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches on two S-DGOD benchmarks. Notably, by introducing fewer than 1% additional trainable parameters into the frozen backbone, the proposed method achieves improvements of +7.9% and +2.9% in mPC over the previous SOTA on the Cityscapes-C and Diverse Weather Dataset, respectively. This finding highlights that preserving representation geometry is crucial for robust cross-domain generalization, establishing GIFT as a principled and efficient solution to domain shift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GIFT：表示几何对单域泛化目标检测至关重要
单域泛化目标检测（S-DGOD）旨在从单一源域泛化到分布偏移下的未见域。现有工作主要集中于模拟未见域或抑制域特定因素。然而，它们在很大程度上忽视了视觉基础模型（VFM）中嵌入的内在泛化能力。在本工作中，我们表明无约束的微调会破坏预训练表示的几何结构，而该结构编码了域不变语义。受此启发，我们提出了几何不变性微调（GIFT），一种面向VFM的几何感知微调方法。具体而言，我们向预训练权重引入一系列可学习的Householder反射，以鼓励在域偏移下进行几何一致的适应。此外，我们提出了一种轻量级的通道级缩放，以促进有效的任务特定适应。大量实验表明，GIFT在两个S-DGOD基准上始终优于最先进（SOTA）方法。值得注意的是，通过在冻结主干中引入不到1%的额外可训练参数，所提方法在Cityscapes-C和Diverse Weather Dataset上的mPC分别较先前SOTA提升了+7.9%和+2.9%。这一发现凸显了保持表示几何对于鲁棒跨域泛化至关重要，确立了GIFT作为域偏移的原则性且高效解决方案的地位。
Summary / 总结
Single-domain generalized object detection (S-DGOD) seeks to transfer from one source domain to unseen domains under distribution shift, but existing approaches mainly simulate unseen domains or suppress domain-specific factors while overlooking the intrinsic generalization capability of vision foundation models (VFMs). This paper shows that unconstrained fine-tuning disrupts the geometric structure of pre-trained representations that encodes domain-invariant semantics, and proposes Geometric-Invariance Fine-Tuning (GIFT), which adds learnable Householder reflections to the pre-trained weights to encourage geometry-consistent adaptation, together with lightweight channel-wise scaling for task-specific adaptation. Experiments on two S-DGOD benchmarks show that GIFT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, and with fewer than 1% additional trainable parameters in the frozen backbone it improves mPC by +7.9% on Cityscapes-C and +2.9% on the Diverse Weather Dataset over the previous state of the art.
单域泛化目标检测（S-DGOD）旨在从单一源域泛化到分布偏移下的未见域，现有方法多集中于模拟未见域或抑制域特定因素，却忽视了视觉基础模型（VFM）内在的泛化能力。本文指出无约束微调会破坏预训练表示中编码域不变语义的几何结构，据此提出几何不变性微调方法 GIFT，在预训练权重上引入一组可学习的 Householder 反射以促使域偏移下保持几何一致的适配，并辅以轻量的通道级缩放实现有效的任务特定适配。在两个 S-DGOD 基准上的实验表明，GIFT 持续优于现有最先进方法，且在冻结主干中仅增加不到 1% 的可训练参数的情况下，相较此前最先进方法在 Cityscapes-C 和 Diverse Weather Dataset 上的 mPC 分别提升 +7.9% 和 +2.9%。
OneEEG: A Unified Signal-Visual Multimodal Language Model with Task-Aware Mixture of Projectors for Multi-Task EEG Understanding
Authors: Jiacheng Zhang, Xueyi Zhang, Xin Lu, Siqi Cai, Haizhou Li
First: 2026-10-26T12:46:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:46:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used in clinical assessment, brain-computer interfaces, and cognitive-state analysis, yet EEG modeling remains fragmented across task paradigms. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a promising interface for flexible multimodal reasoning, but their ability to understand EEG inputs remains limited. In this work, we propose OneEEG, a unified EEG-MLLM framework for multi-task EEG understanding. By training on our curated OneEEG-Instruct, a multi-task EEG instruction-tuning dataset, OneEEG jointly models numerical EEG representations and visual EEG representations, and introduces a task-aware Mixture of Projectors (MoP) to align heterogeneous EEG features across different task families with the LLM embedding space. We further establish OneEEG-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating current MLLMs and our method on multi-task EEG understanding. Experiments show that existing MLLMs have limited ability to interpret EEG inputs, while OneEEG achieves stronger performance by integrating numerical and visual EEG representations with task-aware EEG-to-LLM projection. Extensive ablations and analyses further reveal the effects of visual EEG renderings, MoP variants, router behavior, and feature dispersion across EEG tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OneEEG：一种统一的信号-视觉多模态语言模型，采用任务感知的投影器混合实现多任务EEG理解
脑电图（EEG）广泛应用于临床评估、脑机接口和认知状态分析，然而EEG建模在不同任务范式之间仍然割裂。近期的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）为灵活的多模态推理提供了有前景的接口，但其理解EEG输入的能力仍然有限。在本工作中，我们提出OneEEG，一个用于多任务EEG理解的统一EEG-MLLM框架。通过在我们构建的多任务EEG指令微调数据集OneEEG-Instruct上训练，OneEEG联合建模数值EEG表示和视觉EEG表示，并引入任务感知的投影器混合（MoP），以将不同任务族中的异构EEG特征与LLM嵌入空间对齐。我们进一步建立了OneEEG-Bench，一个用于评估当前MLLMs和我们方法在多任务EEG理解上的基准。实验表明，现有MLLMs解释EEG输入的能力有限，而OneEEG通过将数值和视觉EEG表示与任务感知的EEG到LLM投影相结合，取得了更强的性能。广泛的消融和分析进一步揭示了视觉EEG渲染、MoP变体、路由器行为以及跨EEG任务的特征分散的影响。
Summary / 总结
EEG modeling remains fragmented across task paradigms, and existing multimodal large language models show limited ability to interpret EEG inputs, motivating a unified framework for multi-task EEG understanding. The authors propose OneEEG, which jointly models numerical and visual EEG representations and introduces a task-aware Mixture of Projectors to align heterogeneous EEG features from different task families with the LLM embedding space, trained on the curated OneEEG-Instruct instruction-tuning dataset; they also establish OneEEG-Bench for evaluation. Experiments show that existing MLLMs interpret EEG poorly, while OneEEG achieves stronger performance by integrating numerical and visual representations with task-aware EEG-to-LLM projection, and ablations reveal the effects of visual EEG renderings, MoP variants, router behavior, and feature dispersion across tasks.
脑电建模在不同任务范式之间长期呈碎片化状态，而现有多模态大语言模型对脑电输入的理解能力有限，因此有必要构建统一的多任务脑电理解框架。作者提出OneEEG，在自建的多任务脑电指令微调数据集OneEEG-Instruct上联合建模数值型与视觉型脑电表征，并引入任务感知的多投影器混合机制，将不同任务族的异构脑电特征对齐到LLM嵌入空间，同时建立了OneEEG-Bench评测基准。实验表明，现有MLLM对脑电输入的解释能力较弱，而OneEEG通过融合数值与视觉表征及任务感知的脑电到LLM投影取得了更强性能，消融与分析进一步揭示了视觉脑电渲染、多投影器变体、路由器行为以及跨任务特征离散度的影响。
MarkovSim: an LLM Agent Harness with Bounded Context for Long-Horizon Computational Simulation
Authors: Yeui-Lung Lei, Wen-Bo Chen, Pan Zhang
First: 2026-10-26T12:41:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:41:39+00:00
Comments: 26 pages,15 figures,not yet submitted to any journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent studies have applied large language model (LLM) agent systems to computational simulation workflows. Such workflows are long-horizon, so the information generated can exceed what an LLM can reason over at once, its context window. Context management is therefore essential, yet existing systems have either neglected it or treated it as a supporting mechanism rather than a subject of study. This paper presents MarkovSim, an LLM agent harness for long-horizon computational simulation, whose core is a context management scheme built into the workflow. The workflow record is written to a memory system with a predefined schema, and at workflow-defined boundaries, namely the state transitions of a finite-state machine a compact representation of the solution progress is constructed from it as the agent's context. Instantiated on finite element analysis and evaluated on six benchmark problems, MarkovSim passed 16 of 18 runs and corrected its own faults in both the solver operations and the model physics. The engineer was consulted only where engineering judgement was needed. The context remained bounded throughout every run, regardless of its length.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MarkovSim：一种用于长时程计算仿真的有界上下文LLM智能体框架
近期研究已将大语言模型（LLM）智能体系统应用于计算仿真工作流。此类工作流具有长时程特性，因此生成的信息可能超出LLM一次可推理的范围，即其上下文窗口。因此上下文管理至关重要，然而现有系统要么忽视它，要么将其视为辅助机制而非研究对象。本文提出MarkovSim，一种用于长时程计算仿真的LLM智能体框架，其核心是内置于工作流中的上下文管理方案。工作流记录被写入具有预定义模式的记忆系统，并在工作流定义的边界处，即有限状态机的状态转移处，从中构建求解进度的紧凑表示作为智能体的上下文。在有限元分析上实例化并在六个基准问题上评估，MarkovSim在18次运行中通过了16次，并在求解器操作和模型物理两方面纠正了自身故障。仅在需要工程判断时才咨询工程师。无论运行时长如何，上下文在每次运行中始终保持有界。
Summary / 总结
LLM agent systems applied to long-horizon computational simulation face a context-window bottleneck, since the information generated over a workflow can exceed what the model can reason over at once, yet prior systems have neglected context management or treated it as a supporting mechanism rather than a subject of study. The paper presents MarkovSim, an LLM agent harness whose core is a context management scheme built into the workflow: the workflow record is written to a memory system with a predefined schema, and at workflow-defined boundaries corresponding to the state transitions of a finite-state machine, a compact representation of solution progress is constructed from it as the agent's context. Instantiated on finite element analysis and evaluated on six benchmark problems, MarkovSim passed 16 of 18 runs, corrected its own faults in both solver operations and model physics, consulted the engineer only where engineering judgement was needed, and kept the context bounded throughout every run regardless of length.
将大语言模型智能体系统应用于长程计算仿真时，生成的信息量可能超出模型上下文窗口所能一次推理的范围，而现有系统要么忽视上下文管理，要么仅将其视为辅助机制而非研究对象。MarkovSim 将上下文管理内建于工作流之中：工作流记录按预定义模式写入记忆系统，并在由有限状态机状态转移所定义的工作流边界处，从记录中构建解进度的紧凑表示作为智能体上下文。该方法在有限元分析上实例化，并在六个基准问题上评估，18 次运行中通过 16 次，能够自行纠正求解器操作和模型物理方面的错误，仅在需要工程判断时才咨询工程师，且无论运行多长，上下文始终保持有界。
Enhancing Software Engineering Through Closed-Loop Memory Optimization
Authors: Xuehang Guo, Xingyao Wang, Zora Zhiruo Wang, Qingyun Wang, Graham Neubig, Heng Ji
First: 2026-06-04T03:17:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:40:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have enabled powerful software engineering (SE) agents capable of navigating complex codebases and resolving real-world issues. However, these agents remain fundamentally episodic: they fail to retain, refine, and reuse experiences across tasks, repeatedly reconstructing context from scratch and reproducing similar mistakes. Even with memory support, they offer no remedy for the absence of a principled, task-agnostic \textit{memory utility}, making them difficult to evaluate rigorously or generalize across agents and settings. To tackle these limitations, we introduce MemOp, a closed-loop framework for memory augmentation in SE agents. MemOp grounds memory utility in \textit{validated downstream impact}, establishing utility as both a task-agnostic \textbf{evaluation benchmark} and an annotation-free \textbf{optimization signal}. Evaluating MemOp across four benchmarks, three programming languages, and two SE agents, under complementary \textit{single-episode} and \textit{cross-episode} memory augmentation, results demonstrate the \textbf{effectiveness} of MemOp, achieving absolute gains of up to $\uparrow5.25\%$ in success rate and $\uparrow4.63\%$ in resolve efficiency, while notably reducing deployment cost by $\geq10.33\%$. MemOp further exhibits \textbf{generalizability} across SE agents, memory backbones, and optimization algorithms, together with \textbf{transferability} of MemOp-trained memory model to unseen programming languages and SE activities. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/MemOp/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过闭环记忆优化增强软件工程
大语言模型（LLM）已催生出强大的软件工程（SE）智能体，能够导航复杂代码库并解决现实问题。然而，这些智能体本质上仍是情景式的：它们无法跨任务保留、精炼和复用经验，反复从零重建上下文并重犯类似错误。即便有记忆支持，它们也无法弥补缺乏有原则、任务无关的\textit{记忆效用}这一问题，使其难以被严格评估或跨智能体与设置泛化。为解决这些局限，我们提出 MemOp，一个用于 SE 智能体记忆增强的闭环框架。MemOp 将记忆效用建立在\textit{经下游验证的影响}之上，把效用同时确立为任务无关的\textbf{评估基准}和无标注的\textbf{优化信号}。在四个基准、三种编程语言和两个 SE 智能体上，于互补的\textit{单回合}与\textit{跨回合}记忆增强下评估 MemOp，结果证明了 MemOp 的\textbf{有效性}，成功率绝对提升最高达 $\uparrow5.25\%$，解决效率提升最高达 $\uparrow4.63\%$，同时显著降低部署成本 $\geq10.33\%$。MemOp 还展现出跨 SE 智能体、记忆骨干和优化算法的\textbf{泛化性}，以及 MemOp 训练的记忆模型对未见编程语言和 SE 活动的\textbf{可迁移性}。项目页面：https://xhguo7.github.io/MemOp/。
Summary / 总结
Software engineering agents built on large language models remain episodic, failing to retain and reuse experience across tasks and lacking a principled, task-agnostic measure of memory utility, which limits rigorous evaluation and generalization. The authors introduce MemOp, a closed-loop framework that grounds memory utility in validated downstream impact, using it both as an evaluation benchmark and as an annotation-free optimization signal, and evaluate it under single-episode and cross-episode memory augmentation. Across four benchmarks, three programming languages, and two SE agents, MemOp yields absolute gains of up to 5.25% in success rate and 4.63% in resolve efficiency while reducing deployment cost by at least 10.33%, and it shows generalizability across agents, memory backbones, and optimization algorithms as well as transferability of the trained memory model to unseen programming languages and SE activities.
基于大语言模型的软件工程智能体本质上是片段式的，无法跨任务保留和复用经验，也缺乏有原则且与任务无关的记忆效用度量，因而难以严格评估和泛化。作者提出 MemOp，一个闭环记忆增强框架，将记忆效用建立在经下游验证的实际影响之上，既作为评估基准，也作为无需人工标注的优化信号，并在单回合与跨回合两种记忆增强设置下进行评估。在四个基准、三种编程语言和两个软件工程智能体上的实验表明，MemOp 使成功率绝对提升最高达 5.25%，解决效率提升 4.63%，同时将部署成本降低至少 10.33%，并在不同智能体、记忆骨干和优化算法上表现出泛化性，其训练的记忆模型还可迁移到未见过的编程语言和软件工程活动。
Constrained Decoding: Harmful or Beneficial? Revisiting Its Impact on LLM Performance
Authors: Ngoc Trinh Hung Nguyen, Alonso Silva, Armen Aghasaryan, Mehwish Alam
First: 2026-10-26T12:26:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:26:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constrained decoding guarantees that Large Language Models (LLMs) produce outputs conforming to predefined formats, but is often assumed to inherently degrade reasoning. We examine this assumption through a taxonomy of three constraint types: \emph{format constraints}, which enforce output structure; \emph{validity constraints}, which mask invalid answer; and \emph{reasoning constraints}, which restrict reasoning trajectories. We theoretically show that appropriate constraints can preserve or improve performance by eliminating invalid paths. Across four model families and eleven open-source models, causal ablations on diverse benchmarks using state-of-the-art constrained decoding strategies reveal that apparent performance degradation largely stems from non-equivalent prompting and output formats rather than from decoder-level masking. Meanwhile, validity constraints improve reliability and extractability by eliminating invalid answers, while reasoning constraints can improve reasoning and, consequently, accuracy when they enforce beneficial trajectories. These findings show that the impact of constrained decoding depends on the type of constraint and the output space it removes. Our code is available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：约束解码：有害还是有益？重新审视其对大语言模型性能的影响
约束解码保证大语言模型（LLM）生成符合预定义格式的输出，但常被认为会固有地降低推理能力。我们通过三类约束的分类体系审视这一假设：\emph{格式约束}，强制输出结构；\emph{有效性约束}，屏蔽无效答案；以及\emph{推理约束}，限制推理轨迹。我们从理论上证明，适当的约束可通过消除无效路径来保持或提升性能。在四个模型系列和十一个开源模型上，使用最先进的约束解码策略在不同基准上进行因果消融实验，结果表明，表面上的性能下降主要源于非等价的提示和输出格式，而非解码器层面的屏蔽。同时，有效性约束通过消除无效答案提升可靠性和可提取性，而推理约束在强制有益轨迹时可提升推理能力，进而提升准确率。这些发现表明，约束解码的影响取决于约束类型及其所移除的输出空间。我们的代码已公开。
Summary / 总结
This paper revisits the common assumption that constrained decoding inherently degrades LLM reasoning by proposing a taxonomy of three constraint types: format constraints that enforce output structure, validity constraints that mask invalid answers, and reasoning constraints that restrict reasoning trajectories. The authors theoretically show that appropriate constraints can preserve or improve performance by eliminating invalid paths, and they conduct causal ablations across four model families and eleven open-source models on diverse benchmarks using state-of-the-art constrained decoding strategies. Results indicate that apparent performance degradation largely stems from non-equivalent prompting and output formats rather than decoder-level masking, while validity constraints improve reliability and extractability by removing invalid answers and reasoning constraints can improve reasoning and accuracy when they enforce beneficial trajectories, showing that the impact of constrained decoding depends on the constraint type and the output space it removes.
本文重新审视了约束解码必然损害大语言模型推理这一常见假设，提出了三类约束的分类体系：强制输出结构的格式约束、屏蔽无效答案的有效性约束以及限制推理轨迹的推理约束。作者从理论上说明适当的约束可以通过排除无效路径来保持甚至提升性能，并在四个模型家族和十一个开源模型上，使用最先进的约束解码策略在多种基准上进行了因果消融实验。结果表明，表面上的性能下降主要源于提示和输出格式的不对等，而非解码器层面的屏蔽；有效性约束通过消除无效答案提升了可靠性和可提取性，而推理约束在强制有益轨迹时能够改善推理并进而提升准确率，说明约束解码的影响取决于约束类型及其所移除的输出空间。
GLIMPSE: Global-Local Indexing with Multi-Precision Spectral Encoding for Explorable Video World Models
Authors: Zhufeng Xu, Henglei Lv, Bailin Deng, Yikang Ding, Xiaoqiang Liu, Guoxin Zhang, Hongbo Fu, Lin Gao
First: 2026-10-09T15:01:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:23:09+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A camera-controlled video world model must keep a scene consistent when the camera returns to it. Memories that render stored features into the target frame can turn depth and pose errors into ghosting or duplicated structure. Positional transport avoids that resampling by relocating a historical patch's address rather than its content, but assigns full positional precision even to inaccurate projections. We present GLIMPSE, a global-local memory interface with band-wise multi-precision coordinates and geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation to reduce sensitivity to inaccurate projected addresses. Local patch memory supplies view-specific appearance, while recurrent global memory provides scene context. Footprint-guided retrieval selects historical patches under a fixed local read budget. Under matched retrieval and read budgets, GLIMPSE improves geometric correspondence and photometric consistency over deterministic positional transport.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GLIMPSE：面向可探索视频世界模型的多精度频谱编码全局-局部索引
相机控制的视频世界模型必须在相机返回某场景时保持该场景一致。将存储特征渲染到目标帧的记忆方式会把深度和位姿误差转化为重影或重复结构。位置传输通过迁移历史图块的地址而非其内容来避免这种重采样，但即使投影不准确也赋予完整的位置精度。我们提出GLIMPSE，一种具有分带多精度坐标和几何条件频谱衰减的全局-局部记忆接口，以降低对不准确投影地址的敏感性。局部图块记忆提供视角特定的外观，而循环全局记忆提供场景上下文。足迹引导的检索在固定的局部读取预算下选择历史图块。在匹配的检索和读取预算下，GLIMPSE相比确定性位置传输改善了几何对应和光度一致性。
Summary / 总结
Camera-controlled video world models need consistent scene memory when the camera revisits a location, but rendering stored features into the target frame propagates depth and pose errors as ghosting or duplicated structure, and positional transport, which relocates a historical patch's address instead of resampling its content, still assigns full positional precision to inaccurate projections. The authors propose GLIMPSE, a global-local memory interface that uses band-wise multi-precision coordinates and geometry-conditioned spectral attenuation to reduce sensitivity to inaccurate projected addresses, combining local patch memory for view-specific appearance with recurrent global memory for scene context and footprint-guided retrieval under a fixed local read budget. Under matched retrieval and read budgets, GLIMPSE improves geometric correspondence and photometric consistency over deterministic positional transport.
相机可控的视频世界模型在相机返回同一场景时需要保持一致性，但将存储特征渲染到目标帧的记忆方式会把深度和位姿误差转化为重影或重复结构，而位置传输虽通过迁移历史patch的地址而非内容来避免重采样，却仍对不准确的投影赋予完整的位置精度。作者提出GLIMPSE，一种全局-局部记忆接口，采用分频段多精度坐标和几何条件化的频谱衰减来降低对不准确投影地址的敏感性，以局部patch记忆提供视角相关外观、以循环全局记忆提供场景上下文，并在固定局部读取预算下进行足迹引导的检索。在匹配的检索与读取预算下，GLIMPSE相较确定性位置传输在几何对应和光度一致性上均有提升。
Shared Context, Separate Engines: Streaming Vision--Language Inference on Edge SoCs
Authors: Yilong Li, Chengpo Yan, Yijing Zeng, Hanlei Wu, Mingjun Song, Suman Banerjee
First: 2026-10-26T12:13:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:13:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Edge SoCs pack CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, yet vision--language model (VLM) runtimes keep generation on one engine and leave the others idle. Streaming VLMs expose further parallelism: new frames arrive while earlier queries are still being answered. Engines differ in compute and memory access and contend when they co-run, so no single placement of stages wins across SoCs and workloads. Backend-local KV state keeps other engines from continuing the work. \name coordinates heterogeneous accelerators to execute stateful VLM inference concurrently. It separates a stream's versioned context from each engine's KV state, so any engine can continue the stream by reusing, importing, or reconstructing that state. Stateful Streaming Generation ingests new frames on one engine while another answers, and Concurrent Sequence Generation serves different ready requests in parallel. State-aware dispatch sends each request to the engine expected to finish it first. Each answer reads a fixed, published version of its history and matches its serial reference. \name beats the strongest single-engine baselines on Apple M4 Max, Rockchip RK3588, and Qualcomm QCS6490, even when they reuse context. On two RK3588 video streams, it delivers $19.6\%$ more throughput than the best single engine with context reuse and $27.2\%$ more than parallel engines without shared state. Cross-engine KV import cuts first-token latency by a factor of $4.6$ over prefix recomputation. Stateful Streaming Generation delivers up to $2.48\times$ the throughput of serial execution, and state-aware dispatch cuts query completion time by up to $72.7\%$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：共享上下文，独立引擎：边缘SoC上的流式视觉-语言推理
边缘SoC集成了CPU、GPU和NPU，但视觉-语言模型（VLM）运行时仍将生成限制在单一引擎上，使其他引擎闲置。流式VLM进一步暴露了并行性：新帧到达时，先前的查询仍在被回答。各引擎在计算和内存访问上存在差异，且共同运行时会产生争用，因此没有单一的阶段放置方案能在所有SoC和工作负载上胜出。后端本地KV状态阻碍了其他引擎继续工作。\name协调异构加速器以并发执行有状态VLM推理。它将流的版本化上下文与每个引擎的KV状态分离，使任何引擎都能通过复用、导入或重建该状态来继续该流。有状态流式生成在一个引擎上摄取新帧，同时另一个引擎进行回答；并发序列生成并行服务不同的就绪请求。状态感知调度将每个请求发送到预期最先完成它的引擎。每个回答读取其历史的固定已发布版本，并与其串行参考一致。\name在Apple M4 Max、Rockchip RK3588和Qualcomm QCS6490上击败了最强的单引擎基线，即使它们复用了上下文。在两个RK3588视频流上，它比带上下文复用的最佳单引擎吞吐量高$19.6\%$，比无共享状态的并行引擎高$27.2\%$。跨引擎KV导入将首令牌延迟相比前缀重计算降低了$4.6$倍。有状态流式生成的吞吐量最高可达串行执行的$2.48\times$，状态感知调度将查询完成时间最多缩短$72.7\%$。
Summary / 总结
Edge SoCs contain CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, but existing vision-language model runtimes confine generation to a single engine and leave other accelerators idle, while streaming workloads expose additional parallelism between incoming frames and pending queries. This work presents a system that coordinates heterogeneous accelerators for concurrent stateful VLM inference by separating a stream's versioned context from each engine's local KV state, allowing any engine to continue a stream through reuse, import, or reconstruction of that state, and combining Stateful Streaming Generation, Concurrent Sequence Generation, and state-aware dispatch that routes each request to the engine expected to finish it first. Evaluated on Apple M4 Max, Rockchip RK3588, and Qualcomm QCS6490, the system outperforms the strongest single-engine baselines even when they reuse context; on two RK3588 video streams it achieves 19.6% higher throughput than the best single engine with context reuse and 27.2% higher than parallel engines without shared state, cross-engine KV import reduces first-token latency by 4.6x versus prefix recomputation, Stateful Streaming Generation reaches up to 2.48x the throughput of serial execution, and state-aware dispatch cuts query completion time by up to 72.7%.
边缘 SoC 上的视觉语言模型运行时通常将生成限制在单一引擎上，使 CPU、GPU 和 NPU 处于闲置状态，而后端本地的 KV 状态又阻止其他引擎继续处理同一数据流，加之各引擎在计算与访存上的差异及共同运行时的资源争用，使得不存在适用于所有 SoC 和工作负载的固定阶段划分方案。该论文提出一种系统，通过将流的版本化上下文与各引擎的 KV 状态分离来协调异构加速器并发执行有状态 VLM 推理，使任意引擎都能通过复用、导入或重建状态来继续该流，并结合有状态流式生成、并发序列生成和状态感知调度。在 Apple M4 Max、Rockchip RK3588 和 Qualcomm QCS6490 上，即使基线复用上下文，该系统仍优于最强的单引擎基线；在 RK3588 的两路视频流上，其吞吐量比复用上下文的最佳单引擎高 19.6%，比无共享状态的并行引擎高 27.2%；跨引擎 KV 导入将首 token 延迟较前缀重计算降低 4.6 倍；有状态流式生成吞吐量最高达串行执行的 2.48 倍，状态感知调度将查询完成时间最多缩短 72.7%。
LLM-Enhanced Heterogeneous Graph Learning with Business Semantics for Financial Fraud Detection
Authors: Yixuan Chen, Hongyu Zhan, Jie Sheng, Weiyu Han, Shuai Chen, Tianyi Zhang, Xiao Tan, Jun Xia
First: 2026-10-26T12:03:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:03:54+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Financial fraud detection increasingly relies on heterogeneous transaction graphs, in which entities such as cards and merchants play distinct business roles and the risk meaning of an attribute depends on the entity's role: a high transaction volume may be routine for a merchant but unusual for a card. Heterogeneous graph neural networks preserve typed entities and relations but typically treat these attributes as role-agnostic inputs and leave their business meaning implicit. Meanwhile, most existing methods integrating large language models (LLMs) with graphs focus on homogeneous graphs or heterogeneous graphs with naturally available textual attributes, leaving their use on transaction graphs with structured attributes underexplored. To address this issue, we propose SemHGT, an LLM-enhanced framework that interprets structured attributes according to entity roles and makes the business semantics explicit in three stages: (1) role-aware prompts elicit risk-related business descriptions from node attributes, and a text encoder maps them to semantic vectors that enrich node representations; (2) semantic similarity guides multi-round sampling of target-specific path instances under transaction-to-transaction schemas; (3) schema-level tokens that combine local path evidence with global neighbor summaries are processed together with the target transaction by a unified transformer. Across three benchmark datasets, SemHGT leads the evaluated baselines on most dataset--metric comparisons. Further analyses provide additional evidence for the effectiveness of SemHGT's design and clarify how its semantic modeling and evidence selection contribute to fraud detection. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SemHGT-528A/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLM增强的异构图表征学习：融合业务语义的金融欺诈检测
金融欺诈检测日益依赖异构交易图，其中卡、商户等实体扮演不同的业务角色，属性的风险含义取决于实体角色：高交易量对商户可能是常态，对卡则可能异常。异构图神经网络保留了类型化实体与关系，但通常将这些属性视为与角色无关的输入，使其业务含义保持隐含。同时，现有将大语言模型（LLM）与图结合的方法大多关注同构图或具有自然文本属性的异构图，其在具有结构化属性的交易图上的应用尚未充分探索。为解决该问题，我们提出SemHGT，一个LLM增强框架，按实体角色解释结构化属性，并在三个阶段显式化业务语义：（1）角色感知提示从节点属性中引出与风险相关的业务描述，文本编码器将其映射为语义向量以丰富节点表示；（2）语义相似度引导在交易到交易模式下的目标特定路径实例的多轮采样；（3）将局部路径证据与全局邻居摘要相结合的模式级令牌与目标交易一起由统一Transformer处理。在三个基准数据集上，SemHGT在大多数数据集—指标比较中领先于所评估的基线。进一步分析为SemHGT设计的有效性提供了额外证据，并阐明了其语义建模与证据选择如何促进欺诈检测。代码见 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SemHGT-528A/。

在异构交易图上进行金融欺诈检测时，传统异构图表神经网络通常将属性视为与角色无关的输入，使属性的业务含义保持隐含；而现有将大语言模型与图结合的方法多集中于同构图或天然带有文本属性的异构图，对具有结构化属性的交易图探索不足。为此，作者提出SemHGT框架，通过三个阶段显式表达业务语义：利用角色感知提示从节点属性中引出与风险相关的业务描述，并由文本编码器映射为语义向量以丰富节点表示；以语义相似度引导在交易到交易模式下的多轮目标特定路径实例采样；将由局部路径证据与全局邻居摘要组成的模式级令牌与目标交易一起交由统一Transformer处理。在三个基准数据集上，SemHGT在大多数数据集与指标组合上优于所评估的基线，进一步分析也为该设计的有效性及其语义建模与证据选择对欺诈检测的贡献提供了佐证。
Learning Faithful Reasoning Processes with Spatio-Temporal Latent Dynamics for Large Language Models
Authors: Weijiang Lv, Wentong Zhao, Jiayu Wang, Yuhao Wu, Jiaheng Wei, Xiaobo Xia
First: 2026-05-26T11:54:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T12:02:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing reinforcement learning methods primarily optimize outcome-level rewards, providing limited supervision over the reasoning process. As a result, models may produce shortcut-prone or unstable reasoning trajectories even when the final answer is correct. We propose GeoFaith, a spatio-temporal latent dynamics framework for learning faithful reasoning processes without process reward models. GeoFaith combines final-state latent uncertainty, which measures whether a completed trajectory lies in a well-supported manifold region, with predictive-entropy dynamics, which penalize abnormal uncertainty patterns such as premature collapse, oscillation, and prolonged uncertainty. These signals are incorporated as process regularization into GRPO, jointly optimizing answer correctness and reasoning quality without step-level process annotations. Experiments show that GeoFaith consistently improves reasoning accuracy, produces more efficient reasoning trajectories, and outperforms both outcome-only reinforcement learning and process-supervision baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为大语言模型学习具有时空潜在动力学的忠实推理过程
思维链（CoT）推理显著增强了大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力。然而，现有强化学习方法主要优化结果级奖励，对推理过程的监督有限。因此，即使最终答案正确，模型也可能产生易走捷径或不稳定的推理轨迹。我们提出 GeoFaith，一个无需过程奖励模型即可学习忠实推理过程的时空潜在动力学框架。GeoFaith 将最终状态潜在不确定性（衡量已完成轨迹是否位于支撑良好的流形区域）与预测熵动力学（惩罚过早崩溃、振荡和持续不确定性等异常不确定性模式）相结合。这些信号作为过程正则化被纳入 GRPO，在无需步骤级过程标注的情况下联合优化答案正确性和推理质量。实验表明，GeoFaith 持续提升推理准确率，产生更高效的推理轨迹，并优于仅结果强化学习和过程监督基线。
Summary / 总结
Existing reinforcement learning methods for chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models mainly optimize outcome-level rewards, which provides limited supervision over the reasoning process and can yield shortcut-prone or unstable trajectories even when answers are correct. The authors propose GeoFaith, a spatio-temporal latent dynamics framework that learns faithful reasoning processes without process reward models by combining final-state latent uncertainty, which measures whether a completed trajectory lies in a well-supported manifold region, with predictive-entropy dynamics that penalize abnormal uncertainty patterns such as premature collapse, oscillation, and prolonged uncertainty; these signals serve as process regularization within GRPO, jointly optimizing answer correctness and reasoning quality without step-level annotations. Experiments show that GeoFaith consistently improves reasoning accuracy, produces more efficient reasoning trajectories, and outperforms both outcome-only reinforcement learning and process-supervision baselines.
现有面向大语言模型思维链推理的强化学习方法主要优化结果层面的奖励，对推理过程的监督有限，因此即使最终答案正确，模型也可能产生走捷径或不稳定的推理轨迹。作者提出 GeoFaith，一个时空潜在动力学框架，无需过程奖励模型即可学习忠实的推理过程：它将最终状态潜在不确定性（衡量完整轨迹是否位于支撑良好的流形区域）与预测熵动力学（惩罚过早坍缩、振荡和持续不确定等异常不确定性模式）相结合，作为过程正则化项融入 GRPO，在无需步骤级过程标注的情况下联合优化答案正确性与推理质量。实验表明，GeoFaith 能持续提升推理准确率，生成更高效的推理轨迹，并优于仅使用结果奖励的强化学习方法和过程监督基线。
Satellite Intelligence: Distributed Training, Collaborative Inference,and LLM Adaptation in LEO Constellation
Authors: Zheng Lin, Zhanwei Wang, Yuxin Zhang, Yafei Wang, Ons Aouedi, Zihan Fang, Jinfeng Xu, Jing Yang, Vu Nguyen Ha, Yuanwei Liu, Mohamed-Slim Alouini, Symeon Chatzinotas
First: 2026-10-26T11:55:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:55:41+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The proliferation of orbital sensing data and the real-time demands of emerging global services are pushing low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks from bent-pipe relays toward intelligent onboard processing. This shift confronts a fundamental tension, where modern artificial intelligence (AI) training paradigms require intensive computation, abundant memory, and stable connectivity, all of which are scarce under the dynamic topologies, intermittent links, and severe size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints of satellite networks. Addressing this tension demands rethinking AI workflows, from training and inference to the emerging deployment of large language models (LLMs), under a constraint-aware design philosophy. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first survey that jointly covers all three, whereas prior surveys examine these aspects in isolation. We begin by identifying the deployment challenge of satellite intelligence (SI) and deriving design principles for SI. We then examine three advancing paradigms: i) distributed training, where federated learning (FL) and split learning (SL) enable satellite-ground co-training under resource limitations; ii) collaborative inference, which exploits single-satellite, multi-satellite, and satellite-ground architectures to meet real-time service demands; and iii) on-orbit LLM adaptation and serving, which tackles the frontier challenge of deploying LLMs under severe orbital constraints and resource-aware serving. Finally, we identify open challenges and future directions. By bridging the AI and satellite networks, this paper provides a research roadmap for the implementation of space-native AI technologies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：卫星智能：LEO星座中的分布式训练、协同推理与LLM适配
轨道感知数据的激增与新兴全球服务的实时需求，正推动低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络从弯管式中继向星上智能处理演进。这一转变面临根本性矛盾：现代人工智能（AI）训练范式需要密集计算、充足内存和稳定连接，而这些在卫星网络动态拓扑、间歇链路以及严苛的尺寸、重量和功耗（SWaP）约束下均十分稀缺。化解这一矛盾需要在约束感知的设计理念下重新思考AI工作流，涵盖训练、推理以及大语言模型（LLM）的新兴部署。据我们所知，本文是首个同时涵盖上述三方面的综述，而此前的综述仅孤立地考察这些方面。我们首先识别卫星智能（SI）的部署挑战并推导SI的设计原则。随后我们考察三种不断发展的范式：i）分布式训练，其中联邦学习（FL）和拆分学习（SL）使卫星-地面在资源受限下协同训练；ii）协同推理，利用单星、多星和星地架构满足实时服务需求；iii）在轨LLM适配与服务，应对在严苛轨道约束和资源感知服务下部署LLM的前沿挑战。最后，我们指出开放挑战与未来方向。通过连接AI与卫星网络，本文为太空原生AI技术的实现提供了研究路线图。
Summary / 总结

It is presented as the first survey to jointly cover distributed training, collaborative inference, and on-orbit LLM adaptation, and it proceeds by identifying the deployment challenge of satellite intelligence, deriving design principles, and then examining three paradigms: distributed training via federated and split learning for satellite-ground co-training, collaborative inference through single-satellite, multi-satellite, and satellite-ground architectures, and on-orbit LLM adaptation and resource-aware serving. The paper closes by identifying open challenges and future directions, offering a research roadmap that bridges AI and satellite networks for space-native AI; as a survey, its contributions are organizational and conceptual rather than new experimental results.

ActTail: Global Activation Sparsity in Large Language Models
Authors: Wenwen Hou, Xinyuan Song, Wenqian Weng, Shiwei Liu
First: 2026-02-18T14:46:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:50:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Activation sparsity is a promising approach for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference by reducing computation and memory movement. However, existing activation sparsity methods typically apply uniform sparsity across projections, ignoring the heterogeneous statistical properties of Transformer weights and thereby amplifying performance degradation. In this paper, we propose ActTail, a TopK magnitude-based activation sparsity method with global activation sparsity allocation grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory. Specifically, we capture this heterogeneity via the heavy-tail exponent computed from each projection's empirical spectral density (ESD), which is used as a quantitative indicator to assign projection-specific sparsity budgets. Importantly, we provide a theoretical analysis that establishes an explicit relationship between the activation sparsity ratio and the heavy-tail exponent under the HT-SR regime, offering principled guidance for sparsity allocation beyond heuristic design. Experiments on LLaMA and Mistral models show that our method improves both perplexity and downstream task performance at high sparsity compared to uniform allocation. At 80% sparsity, perplexity is reduced by 21.8% on LLaMA-2-7B, 40.1% on LLaMA-2-13B, and 9.4% on Mistral-7B.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ActTail：大型语言模型中的全局激活稀疏性
激活稀疏性是一种通过减少计算和内存移动来加速大型语言模型（LLM）推理的有前景方法。然而，现有激活稀疏性方法通常在各投影间施加均匀稀疏性，忽略了Transformer权重的异质统计特性，从而放大了性能退化。本文提出ActTail，一种基于TopK幅值的激活稀疏性方法，其全局激活稀疏性分配基于重尾自正则化（HT-SR）理论。具体而言，我们通过由每个投影的经验谱密度（ESD）计算的重尾指数来捕捉这种异质性，并将其作为定量指标来分配投影特定的稀疏性预算。重要的是，我们提供了理论分析，在HT-SR机制下建立了激活稀疏率与重尾指数之间的显式关系，为稀疏性分配提供了超越启发式设计的原则性指导。在LLaMA和Mistral模型上的实验表明，与均匀分配相比，我们的方法在高稀疏度下同时改善了困惑度和下游任务性能。在80%稀疏度下，LLaMA-2-7B的困惑度降低了21.8%，LLaMA-2-13B降低了40.1%，Mistral-7B降低了9.4%。
Summary / 总结
Existing activation sparsity methods for accelerating LLM inference typically apply uniform sparsity across projections, ignoring the heterogeneous statistical properties of Transformer weights and thereby amplifying performance degradation. The authors propose ActTail, a TopK magnitude-based activation sparsity method that allocates projection-specific sparsity budgets using the heavy-tail exponent computed from each projection's empirical spectral density, grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization theory, and they provide a theoretical analysis linking the activation sparsity ratio to the heavy-tail exponent to guide allocation beyond heuristics. Experiments on LLaMA and Mistral models show improved perplexity and downstream task performance at high sparsity compared to uniform allocation, with perplexity reduced by 21.8% on LLaMA-2-7B, 40.1% on LLaMA-2-13B, and 9.4% on Mistral-7B at 80% sparsity.
现有用于加速大语言模型推理的激活稀疏方法通常在各投影层上采用统一稀疏度，忽略了Transformer权重的异质统计特性，从而放大了性能退化。作者提出ActTail，一种基于TopK幅值的激活稀疏方法，依据Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization理论，利用由各投影层经验谱密度计算得到的重尾指数来分配投影层特定的稀疏预算，并从理论上建立了激活稀疏比例与重尾指数之间的显式关系，为稀疏分配提供原理性指导而非启发式设计。在LLaMA和Mistral模型上的实验表明，与统一分配相比，该方法在高稀疏度下同时改善了困惑度和下游任务性能，在80%稀疏度下，LLaMA-2-7B困惑度降低21.8%，LLaMA-2-13B降低40.1%，Mistral-7B降低9.4%。
Video Prediction Policy 2: Predict Better, Act Better
Authors: Yanjiang Guo, Haodong Yan, Zhide Zhong, Zhongru Zhang, Qingyuan Yang, Qingzhou Lu, Xiaoyu Chen, Yen-Jen Wang, Shuying Deng, Chenghan Yang, Puzhen Yuan, Chenxin Liu, Tun Ban, Xiang Zhu, Yichen Liu, Kun Feng, Haoang Li, Jianyu Chen
First: 2026-10-07T15:40:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:46:50+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://robert-gyj.github.io/video-prediction-policy-2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
World action models (WAMs) have emerged as an important class of generalist robot policies, aiming to transfer video prediction priors to action learning. However, we find that existing WAMs frequently produce incorrect motion predictions in open-ended environment, leading to erroneous actions. We attribute this limitation to two factors: (1) base video models are not optimized for manipulation, and (2) naively incorporating action components into video models can substantially degrade their generalization capabilities. We introduce Video Prediction Policy 2 (VPP2), a WAM that enables strong zero-shot generalization in both video prediction and action generation. First, we curate a large-scale, diverse dataset of manipulation videos to continue pretraining the base video foundation model. We annotate video clips with detailed captions and perform \textit{event-level} video pretraining to promote generalization across open-ended manipulation tasks. Second, we post-train and distill the video model into a single-step visual planner with fixed prediction horizon. Finally, we introduce action module via a mixture-of-transformers (MoT) architecture to learn implicit inverse dynamics model. Experiments demonstrate three key results: (1) VPP2-14B outperforms Cosmos3-64B by 11.0\% points in video prediction instruction-following success rate on open-ended tasks; (2) VPP2 surpasses the strongest baseline by 18.5\% points in success rate on real-world zero-shot ALOHA manipulation tasks; and (3) following benchmark-specific post-training, VPP2 achieves the highest success rates among evaluated methods on the challenging LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-OOD, and RoboDojo benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视频预测策略 2：预测更好，行动更好
世界动作模型（WAMs）已成为一类重要的通用机器人策略，旨在将视频预测先验迁移到动作学习中。然而，我们发现现有的 WAMs 在开放式环境中经常产生错误的运动预测，从而导致错误的动作。我们将这一局限归因于两个因素：（1）基础视频模型未针对操作任务进行优化；（2）将动作组件简单地融入视频模型会显著降低其泛化能力。我们提出视频预测策略 2（VPP2），这是一种在视频预测和动作生成方面均能实现强零样本泛化的 WAM。首先，我们整理了一个大规模、多样化的操作视频数据集，用于继续预训练基础视频基础模型。我们为视频片段标注详细描述，并进行事件级视频预训练，以促进在开放式操作任务上的泛化。其次，我们对视频模型进行后训练，并将其蒸馏为具有固定预测视野的单步视觉规划器。最后，我们通过混合 Transformer（MoT）架构引入动作模块，以学习隐式逆动力学模型。实验展示了三项关键结果：（1）在开放式任务的视频预测指令遵循成功率上，VPP2-14B 比 Cosmos3-64B 高出 11.0 个百分点；（2）在真实世界零样本 ALOHA 操作任务的成功率上，VPP2 比最强基线高出 18.5 个百分点；（3）经过针对基准的后训练后，VPP2 在具有挑战性的 LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-OOD 和 RoboDojo 基准上取得了所评估方法中最高的成功率。
Summary / 总结
World action models aim to transfer video prediction priors to robot action learning, but existing models often make incorrect motion predictions in open-ended environments, leading to erroneous actions; the authors attribute this to base video models not being optimized for manipulation and to naive integration of action components degrading generalization. They propose Video Prediction Policy 2 (VPP2), which continues pretraining a video foundation model on a large, diverse manipulation video dataset with detailed captions and event-level pretraining, then post-trains and distills the model into a single-step visual planner with a fixed prediction horizon, and adds an action module via a mixture-of-transformers architecture to learn an implicit inverse dynamics model. Experiments show VPP2-14B outperforms Cosmos3-64B by 11.0 percentage points in video prediction instruction-following success on open-ended tasks, surpasses the strongest baseline by 18.5 percentage points in real-world zero-shot ALOHA manipulation success, and achieves the highest success rates among evaluated methods on LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-OOD, and RoboDojo after benchmark-specific post-training.
世界动作模型旨在将视频预测先验迁移到机器人动作学习中，但现有模型在开放式环境中常产生错误的运动预测，从而导致错误动作，作者将其归因于基础视频模型未针对操作任务优化以及简单地将动作组件引入视频模型会显著削弱其泛化能力。为此提出的视频预测策略2（VPP2）构建了大规模多样化的操作视频数据集并配以详细描述，通过事件级视频预训练继续预训练基础视频模型，随后对视频模型进行后训练并蒸馏为固定预测步长的单步视觉规划器，最后通过混合Transformer架构引入动作模块以学习隐式逆动力学模型。实验表明，VPP2-14B在开放式任务的视频预测指令遵循成功率上比Cosmos3-64B高11.0个百分点，在真实世界零样本ALOHA操作任务的成功率上比最强基线高18.5个百分点，并在针对基准后训练后在LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-OOD和RoboDojo上取得所评估方法中最高的成功率。
What Makes Synthetic Hard Negatives Work in Vision-Language Pretraining?
Authors: Nikos Giakoumoglou, Paschalis Giakoumoglou, Andreas Floros, Kleanthis Marios Papadopoulos, Tania Stathaki
First: 2026-10-07T08:58:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:44:50+00:00
Comments: ACCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Synthetic hard negatives generated in the representation space have proven effective for unimodal self-supervised learning, but transferring this idea to vision-language pretraining is not straightforward. We analyze six representation-space synthesis strategies and identify two failure modes in their transfer to vision-language pretraining: cross-modal constructions that produce overly easy negatives or pull them toward the query, and intra-modal constructions that incorporate the matched positive. We also observe logit-scale saturation when training with synthetic hard negatives and a learnable temperature, and find that fixing the temperature improves downstream performance. Using this geometric analysis we propose SNAP, which generates intra-modal hard negatives that never involve the positive from either modality, avoiding both failure modes entirely. SNAP is model-agnostic, requires no external generative models, and adds less than 10% training time overhead. Evaluated on top of CLIP and FLIP across multiple architectures and datasets, SNAP delivers consistent improvements on zero-shot retrieval, zero-shot classification, and linear probe evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：是什么让合成硬负样本在视觉-语言预训练中有效？
在表示空间中生成的合成硬负样本已被证明对单模态自监督学习有效，但将这一想法迁移到视觉-语言预训练并非易事。我们分析了六种表示空间合成策略，并识别出它们迁移到视觉-语言预训练时的两种失败模式：跨模态构造会产生过于简单的负样本或将其拉向查询，而模态内构造会纳入匹配的正样本。我们还观察到，在使用合成硬负样本和可学习温度进行训练时会出现logit尺度饱和，并发现固定温度可提升下游性能。基于这一几何分析，我们提出SNAP，它生成从不涉及任一模态正样本的模态内硬负样本，从而完全避免这两种失败模式。SNAP与模型无关，无需外部生成模型，训练时间开销低于10%。在多种架构和数据集上基于CLIP和FLIP进行评估，SNAP在零样本检索、零样本分类和线性探针评估上均带来一致提升。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why synthetic hard negatives, which are effective in unimodal self-supervised learning, do not transfer straightforwardly to vision-language pretraining. The authors analyze six representation-space synthesis strategies and identify two failure modes: cross-modal constructions that yield overly easy negatives or pull them toward the query, and intra-modal constructions that inadvertently incorporate the matched positive; they also observe logit-scale saturation when training with synthetic hard negatives and a learnable temperature, and find that fixing the temperature improves downstream performance. Building on this geometric analysis, they propose SNAP, a model-agnostic method that generates intra-modal hard negatives never involving the positive from either modality, requires no external generative models, and adds under 10% training time overhead. Evaluated on top of CLIP and FLIP across multiple architectures and datasets, SNAP yields consistent gains on zero-shot retrieval, zero-shot classification, and linear probe evaluation.
本文研究为何在单模态自监督学习中有效的合成难负样本难以直接迁移到视觉-语言预训练。作者分析了六种表示空间合成策略，指出迁移中的两种失效模式：跨模态构造会产生过于简单的负样本或将其拉向查询，而模态内构造会混入匹配的正样本；他们还发现使用合成难负样本和可学习温度训练时会出现logit尺度饱和，并发现固定温度可提升下游性能。基于这一几何分析，作者提出SNAP，一种与模型无关的方法，生成从不涉及任一模态正样本的模态内难负样本，无需外部生成模型，训练时间开销低于10%。在CLIP和FLIP之上跨多种架构和数据集的评估表明，SNAP在零样本检索、零样本分类和线性探针评估上均取得一致提升。
Beyond Vision-Language Models: Visual-Semantic Affect Graphs for Emotion Recognition in Artworks
Authors: Ngoc-Duy Mai, Ha-Thanh Nguyen, Hideaki Takeda
First: 2026-10-26T11:25:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:25:37+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 2 figures, IJCKG conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Emotion recognition in artworks remains challenging because affective meaning is often subjective, implicit, and shaped by relationships between visual form and semantic associations. While vision-language models provide strong multimodal representations, direct image-to-emotion prediction may overlook explicit relations among formal, semantic, and affective cues that support emotional interpretation. This paper proposes Visual-Semantic Affect Graphs (VSAG), a graph-based framework that represents artworks through interpretable feature nodes, including color, brushstroke, composition, light-shadow, line quality, and extracted visual entities. The graph learns feature-emotion associations for dominant emotion prediction, with valence-arousal structure modeled through an optional hierarchical auxiliary affect module. We evaluate VSAG and VSAG-TopK against direct VLM inference using Qwen3-VL and InternVL3.5 on EmoArt-5k. Results show that direct VLMs provide useful but modest baselines, while graph-based models improve dominant emotion prediction. In particular, the hierarchical valence-arousal variant of VSAG achieves the best overall performance, improving accuracy, macro-F1, and weighted-F1 over both direct VLM inference and the emotion-only VSAG baseline. Per-class results indicate that graph-based models and VLMs capture partly complementary signals, while valence-arousal structure is most useful when integrated as a coarse-to-fine affect hierarchy rather than as a simple post-hoc correction. These findings suggest that explicit visual-semantic structure can support both prediction and interpretation in artwork emotion recognition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越视觉-语言模型：用于艺术品情感识别的视觉-语义情感图
艺术品中的情感识别仍然具有挑战性，因为情感意义往往是主观的、隐含的，并受到视觉形式与语义联想之间关系的塑造。尽管视觉-语言模型提供了强大的多模态表示，但直接的图像到情感预测可能会忽略支持情感解释的形式、语义和情感线索之间的显式关系。本文提出视觉-语义情感图（VSAG），这是一个基于图的框架，通过可解释的特征节点来表示艺术品，包括颜色、笔触、构图、光影、线条质量和提取的视觉实体。该图学习特征-情感关联以进行主导情感预测，并通过可选的层次化辅助情感模块对效价-唤醒结构进行建模。我们在 EmoArt-5k 上使用 Qwen3-VL 和 InternVL3.5，将 VSAG 和 VSAG-TopK 与直接 VLM 推理进行评估比较。结果表明，直接 VLM 提供了有用但适度的基线，而基于图的模型改进了主导情感预测。特别是，VSAG 的层次化效价-唤醒变体取得了最佳整体性能，在准确率、宏 F1 和加权 F1 上均优于直接 VLM 推理和仅情感的 VSAG 基线。逐类结果表明，基于图的模型和 VLM 捕获了部分互补的信号，而效价-唤醒结构在作为由粗到细的情感层次集成时最为有用，而不是作为简单的事后校正。这些发现表明，显式的视觉-语义结构可以同时支持艺术品情感识别中的预测和解释。
Summary / 总结
Emotion recognition in artworks is difficult because affective meaning is subjective, implicit, and dependent on relations between visual form and semantic associations, and direct image-to-emotion prediction with vision-language models may overlook these explicit relations. The paper proposes Visual-Semantic Affect Graphs (VSAG), a graph-based framework that represents artworks through interpretable feature nodes such as color, brushstroke, composition, light-shadow, line quality, and extracted visual entities, learning feature-emotion associations for dominant emotion prediction with an optional hierarchical auxiliary module that models valence-arousal structure. Evaluating VSAG and VSAG-TopK against direct inference with Qwen3-VL and InternVL3.5 on EmoArt-5k, the authors find that direct VLMs give useful but modest baselines, graph-based models improve dominant emotion prediction, and the hierarchical valence-arousal variant of VSAG achieves the best overall accuracy, macro-F1, and weighted-F1, with per-class results indicating partly complementary signals between graph models and VLMs and showing that valence-arousal structure helps most as a coarse-to-fine affect hierarchy rather than a post-hoc correction.

Context Is Not Free: Carbon-Aware LLM Routing for Multi-Turn LLM Web Services across Geo-Distributed Data Centers
Authors: Xiaoyang Zhang, Yuejun Yan, Qingang Zhang, Yonggang Wen
First: 2026-10-26T11:20:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:20:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The sustainability of the Web has drawn growing attention, as large language model (LLM)-based web services leave a substantial carbon footprint. These services typically run in geo-distributed data centers whose grid carbon intensity varies across regions and over time, so routing each query to a cleaner region or a smaller model can reduce emissions. A conversation, however, is a stateful web session: each turn reuses the key--value (KV) cache of its history, which resides only where earlier turns were served. Serving a turn on another deployment requires recomputing this history, whose carbon cost grows with the conversation and can outweigh the saving of the move. Existing carbon-aware LLM routers treat a conversation's queries as independent and ignore this cost. We present CarbonRouter, an online framework that jointly selects the model, GPU type, and region for each query to minimize carbon emissions. CarbonRouter introduces a cache-aware carbon cost model, which charges history recomputation to every candidate deployment using cached-token counts from the serving backends, and uses a lightweight gate that keeps a session on its cached deployment when it would likely move back at the next turn and rebuild its history again. We evaluate CarbonRouter in trace-driven experiments on two real-world conversation datasets with five LLMs, three GPU types, and four regions, where it achieves the lowest carbon of all policies. CarbonRouter reduces CO$_2$ by 16.1\% on average relative to FairCarbon, a state-of-the-art carbon-aware router with the same action space, while also improving predicted answer quality and estimated latency. Moreover, the saving rises further to 23.1\% for sessions longer than 30 turns.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文并非免费：面向跨地理分布式数据中心多轮LLM Web服务的碳感知LLM路由
Web的可持续性日益受到关注，因为基于大语言模型（LLM）的Web服务留下了可观的碳足迹。这些服务通常运行在地理分布式数据中心中，其电网碳强度因地区和时段而异，因此将每个查询路由到更清洁的地区或更小的模型可以减少排放。然而，对话是有状态的Web会话：每一轮都会复用其历史记录的键值（KV）缓存，而该缓存仅存在于先前轮次被服务的位置。在其他部署上服务某一轮需要重新计算该历史记录，其碳成本随对话增长，并可能超过迁移带来的节省。现有的碳感知LLM路由器将对话中的查询视为独立请求，忽略了这一成本。我们提出CarbonRouter，一个在线框架，为每个查询联合选择模型、GPU类型和地区以最小化碳排放。CarbonRouter引入缓存感知的碳成本模型，利用服务后端提供的缓存令牌计数，将历史重计算成本计入每个候选部署，并使用轻量级门控：当会话下一轮很可能迁回并再次重建历史时，将其保留在已缓存的部署上。我们在两个真实对话数据集上使用五种LLM、三种GPU类型和四个地区进行轨迹驱动实验评估CarbonRouter，它在所有策略中实现了最低碳排放。相对于具有相同动作空间的最先进碳感知路由器FairCarbon，CarbonRouter平均减少CO$_2$ 16.1\%，同时提升预测答案质量和估计延迟。此外，对于超过30轮的会话，节省进一步升至23.1\%。
Summary / 总结
LLM-based web services run in geo-distributed data centers with varying grid carbon intensity, so routing queries to cleaner regions or smaller models can cut emissions, but conversations are stateful sessions whose KV cache resides only where earlier turns were served, and existing carbon-aware routers treat queries as independent and ignore the carbon cost of recomputing history when a session moves. The paper presents CarbonRouter, an online framework that jointly selects model, GPU type, and region per query, using a cache-aware carbon cost model that charges history recomputation to each candidate deployment based on cached-token counts from serving backends, plus a lightweight gate that keeps a session on its cached deployment when it would likely move back and rebuild history at the next turn. In trace-driven experiments on two real-world conversation datasets with five LLMs, three GPU types, and four regions, CarbonRouter achieves the lowest carbon among all policies, reducing CO2 by 16.1% on average versus FairCarbon, a state-of-the-art carbon-aware router with the same action space, while also improving predicted answer quality and estimated latency, with savings rising to 23.1% for sessions longer than 30 turns.
基于大语言模型（LLM）的Web服务运行在地理分布的数据中心中，各地电网碳强度随区域和时间变化，因此将查询路由到更清洁的区域或更小的模型可降低排放；但多轮对话是有状态的Web会话，每轮复用仅存于此前服务位置的KV缓存，迁移到其他部署需重算历史，其碳成本随对话增长，可能超过迁移带来的节省，而现有碳感知路由器将对话中的查询视为独立并忽略这一成本。本文提出CarbonRouter，一个在线框架，为每个查询联合选择模型、GPU类型和区域，引入缓存感知的碳成本模型，利用服务后端返回的缓存token计数将历史重算成本计入每个候选部署，并使用轻量门控在会话可能于下一轮回迁并再次重建历史时将其保留在缓存所在部署。在两个真实对话数据集、五种LLM、三种GPU类型和四个区域的轨迹驱动实验中，CarbonRouter在所有策略中碳排放最低，相比具有相同动作空间的最先进碳感知路由器FairCarbon平均减少16.1%的CO2，同时提升预测答案质量和估计延迟，且对超过30轮的会话节省进一步升至23.1%。
CausalMix: Data Mixture as Causal Inference for Language Model Training
Authors: Zinan Tang, Yukun Zhang, Shaomian Zheng, Zhuoshi Pan, Qizhi Pei, Dingnan Jin, Qing Cui, Jun Zhou, Yujun Wang, Biqing Huang
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-07-01T15:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:07:55+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 3 figures, accepted by NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In Large Language Model (LLM) training, data mixing plays a pivotal role in determining model performance. Recent methods optimize mixture weights via proxy models, but they rely on the assumption of static data distributions. As a result, when the underlying data pool shifts, these methods require costly retraining from scratch. This limitation restricts their ability to scale seamlessly from small settings to larger data pools and model sizes. In this paper, we propose CausalMix to address this limitation by casting data mixture optimization as a causal inference problem. We formulate the statistical features of the data pool as covariates and the domain mixture as the treatment. After fitting a causal model on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate the Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), we extrapolate the optimal mixture for an 800K data pool and apply it to train a 7B model. Furthermore, we successfully generalize the framework to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base. By leveraging causal modeling to isolate confounding biases, CausalMix infers state-dependent data mixtures offline, with the selected mixture fixed during each training run. Extensive experiments show that the mixture guided by CausalMix achieves competitive performance across downstream tasks compared with other baselines. The CATE Interpreter visualizes the learned mixing strategy. Overall, CausalMix offers a causal and interpretable framework for optimizing LLM data mixtures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CausalMix：将数据混合视为因果推断的语言模型训练方法
在大语言模型（LLM）训练中，数据混合对模型性能起着关键作用。近期方法通过代理模型优化混合权重，但它们依赖于静态数据分布的假设。因此，当底层数据池发生变化时，这些方法需要从头进行昂贵的重新训练。这一局限限制了它们从小规模设置无缝扩展到更大的数据池和模型规模的能力。本文提出 CausalMix，通过将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题来解决这一局限。我们将数据池的统计特征作为协变量，将领域混合作为处理。在 Qwen2.5-0.5B 的 512 次运行上拟合因果模型以估计条件平均处理效应（CATE）后，我们外推得到 800K 数据池的最优混合，并将其应用于训练一个 7B 模型。此外，我们成功将该框架推广到 Qwen3-4B-Base 上的长思维链数据。通过利用因果建模分离混杂偏差，CausalMix 可离线推断状态相关的数据混合，且所选混合在每次训练运行中保持固定。大量实验表明，与其他基线相比，由 CausalMix 引导的混合在下游任务上取得了有竞争力的性能。CATE 解释器可视化所学到的混合策略。总体而言，CausalMix 为优化 LLM 数据混合提供了一个因果且可解释的框架。
Summary / 总结
Data mixing is critical to LLM performance, but existing proxy-model methods for optimizing mixture weights assume static data distributions, forcing costly retraining whenever the data pool shifts and limiting scaling from small to large settings. CausalMix reframes data mixture optimization as a causal inference problem, treating statistical features of the data pool as covariates and domain mixture as the treatment; a causal model is fit on 512 runs of Qwen2.5-0.5B to estimate the Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE), after which the optimal mixture is extrapolated for an 800K data pool and used to train a 7B model, with the framework also generalized to long chain-of-thought data on Qwen3-4B-Base. Experiments show that the CausalMix-guided mixture achieves competitive downstream performance against other baselines, with the selected mixture fixed during each training run, and a CATE Interpreter visualizes the learned mixing strategy, yielding a causal and interpretable framework for data mixture optimization.
数据混合对大型语言模型训练性能至关重要，但现有通过代理模型优化混合权重的方法假设数据分布静态，当底层数据池变化时需从头重新训练，难以从小规模平滑扩展到更大数据池和模型规模。CausalMix 将数据混合优化建模为因果推断问题，把数据池的统计特征作为协变量、领域混合作为处理变量，在 Qwen2.5-0.5B 的 512 次运行上拟合因果模型以估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），再将推断出的最优混合外推到 80 万规模数据池并用于训练 7B 模型。该框架还成功推广到 Qwen3-4B-Base 的长思维链数据上，实验表明 CausalMix 引导的混合在下游任务上相较其他基线取得有竞争力的性能，并通过 CATE 解释器可视化所学到的混合策略。
MJev-Spatial: System One Spatial Intelligence via Native Attention Matching
Authors: Luozheng Qin, Yuhan Zhu, Changhai Zhou, Qian Qiao, Jun Gao, Yuhua Zhou, Harry Zhang, Xiangyu Zeng, Yuxuan Liu, Yuxin Lu, Kaijie Chen, Andrew Chen, Pony Ma
First: 2026-10-15T17:37:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:05:56+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 7 figures, 27 tables. Code: https://github.com/Fr0zenCrane/MJev-Spatial
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Spatial intelligence lets an agent perceive and act in the physical world, and many of its judgments must be made at interaction rates: which spatial relation holds, how far an object is, or where to place it. Vision-language models (VLMs) produce these answers by free-form text generation, paying for autoregressive decoding and answer parsing even though the output space is compact: one of a few options, a scalar, or a 2D point. In light of this, we introduce MJev-Spatial, a System 1 multimodal decision model that tackles all three types of spatial understanding tasks within a unified classification framework. Specifically, MJev-Spatial treats each candidate as a class and scores it using the backbone's pretrained query and key projections, without adding parameters. The candidates are the given options for choice tasks, or value bins and image regions refined through coarse-to-fine classification for numerical and point prediction, respectively. At inference, each classification decision takes a single forward pass and generates no answer tokens. To train on heterogeneous spatial data efficiently, we supervise all coarse-to-fine stages jointly with teacher forcing in one forward pass, and combine this with length-aware global batching and cross-GPU load balancing, raising training throughput by 30.8% over the original batching strategy. Built on Qwen3.5-4B and trained on 12.3M question-answer pairs converted from existing spatial datasets, MJev-Spatial achieves the highest overall score (65.64%) among eight tested models, ranks first on seven of twelve benchmarks, and outperforms its unmodified autoregressive base model on all twelve, while running 2.5 times faster than the fastest autoregressive baseline and 5.6 times faster than the base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MJev-Spatial：通过原生注意力匹配实现系统一空间智能
空间智能使智能体能够感知物理世界并在其中行动，其许多判断必须在交互速率下完成：哪种空间关系成立、物体有多远，或将其放置在何处。视觉语言模型（VLM）通过自由形式的文本生成来产生这些答案，付出自回归解码和答案解析的代价，尽管输出空间是紧凑的：几个选项之一、一个标量或一个二维点。鉴于此，我们提出 MJev-Spatial，一个系统一多模态决策模型，在统一的分类框架内处理所有三类空间理解任务。具体而言，MJev-Spatial 将每个候选视为一个类别，并使用骨干网络预训练的查询和键投影对其进行评分，而不增加参数。候选对于选择任务为给定选项，对于数值和点预测则分别为通过由粗到细分类细化的值区间和图像区域。在推理时，每个分类决策只需一次前向传播，且不生成答案 token。为了高效地在异构空间数据上训练，我们在一次前向传播中通过教师强制联合监督所有由粗到细阶段，并将其与长度感知的全局批处理和跨 GPU 负载均衡相结合，使训练吞吐量比原始批处理策略提高 30.8%。MJev-Spatial 基于 Qwen3.5-4B 构建，并在从现有空间数据集转换而来的 1230 万个问答对上训练，在八个测试模型中取得最高总分（65.64%），在十二个基准中的七个上排名第一，并在全部十二个基准上优于其未经修改的自回归基础模型，同时运行速度比最快的自回归基线快 2.5 倍，比基础模型快 5.6 倍。
Summary / 总结
Spatial intelligence tasks such as judging spatial relations, estimating distances, and predicting placement points require fast judgments at interaction rates, yet vision-language models handle them through free-form autoregressive text generation and answer parsing despite the compact output space. The paper introduces MJev-Spatial, a System 1 multimodal decision model that casts all three task types as unified classification: each candidate (a given option, a value bin, or an image region refined coarse-to-fine) is treated as a class and scored using the backbone's pretrained query and key projections without adding parameters, so inference takes a single forward pass and emits no answer tokens. Training on heterogeneous spatial data is made efficient by supervising all coarse-to-fine stages jointly with teacher forcing in one forward pass, combined with length-aware global batching and cross-GPU load balancing that raises throughput by 30.8%. Built on Qwen3.5-4B and trained on 12.3M converted question-answer pairs, MJev-Spatial achieves the highest overall score (65.64%) among eight tested models, ranks first on seven of twelve benchmarks, beats its unmodified autoregressive base on all twelve, and runs 2.5 times faster than the fastest autoregressive baseline and 5.6 times faster than the base model.
空间智能任务（如判断空间关系、估计距离、预测放置位置）需要在交互速率下快速给出判断，而视觉语言模型即便输出空间很紧凑，仍通过自由形式的自回归文本生成和答案解析来完成这些任务。作者提出 MJev-Spatial，一种 System 1 多模态决策模型，将三类空间理解任务统一为分类框架：每个候选（给定选项、数值分箱或图像区域）被视为一个类别，利用骨干网络预训练的 query 和 key 投影进行打分且不增加参数，数值与点预测采用由粗到细的分类，每次决策只需一次前向传播且不生成答案 token。为高效训练异构空间数据，该方法在一次前向传播中用教师强制联合监督所有由粗到细阶段，并结合长度感知的全局批处理与跨 GPU 负载均衡，使训练吞吐较原始批处理策略提升 30.8%。基于 Qwen3.5-4B 并在由现有空间数据集转换的 1230 万问答对上训练，MJev-Spatial 在八个测试模型中取得最高总分（65.64%），在十二个基准中的七个上排名第一，在全部十二个基准上均优于未修改的自回归基座模型，同时比最快的自回归基线快 2.5 倍、比基座模型快 5.6 倍。
Dispatch Overhead, Not Kernel Quality: The Performance Bottleneck in Single-Stream WebGPU LLM Inference
Authors: Jędrzej Maczan
First: 2026-08-09T14:21:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:02:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted to COLM 2026 Workshop on Methods and Opportunities at Small Scale (MOSS) and COLM 2026 The 2nd Workshop on Efficient Reasoning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
