Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, single-stream settings, from internet browsers to edge devices, where requests arrive one at a time at batch size 1 rather than in large batches. In this regime, where WebGPU serves as a modern cross-platform standard, per-operation dispatch overhead, rather than kernel compute, can dominate inference cost, yet it remains poorly characterized. In this work, we introduce a sequential-dispatch measurement method and show that naive single-operation measurements overestimate per-dispatch cost by conflating dispatch with synchronization. Using our method, we measure the per-dispatch cost and show that it is independent of data type used. Through a controlled fusion experiment, we show that the dispatch overhead, not kernel quality, is the bottleneck at batch size 1, and isolate the dispatch count as the cause. We conclude that at batch size 1, the effective approach to LLM inference optimization in WebGPU is reducing dispatch count. Our findings point to dispatch amortization, in the inference engines and in the WebGPU specification, as a path to practical browser-based inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：调度开销而非内核质量：单流 WebGPU LLM 推理中的性能瓶颈
大语言模型正越来越多地部署在资源受限的单流环境中，从互联网浏览器到边缘设备，请求以批大小 1 逐个到达，而非大批量到达。在这一场景下，WebGPU 作为现代跨平台标准，逐操作调度开销而非内核计算可能主导推理成本，然而其特性仍缺乏充分刻画。 在本工作中，我们引入一种顺序调度测量方法，并表明朴素的单操作测量会将调度与同步混为一谈，从而高估每次调度开销。使用我们的方法，我们测量了每次调度开销，并表明其与所用数据类型无关。通过受控融合实验，我们表明在批大小 1 时瓶颈是调度开销而非内核质量，并分离出调度次数作为原因。 我们得出结论：在批大小 1 时，WebGPU 中 LLM 推理优化的有效方法是减少调度次数。我们的发现指向推理引擎和 WebGPU 规范中的调度摊销，作为实现实用浏览器端推理的路径。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the poorly characterized performance bottleneck in single-stream WebGPU LLM inference, where requests arrive at batch size 1 and per-operation dispatch overhead may dominate cost rather than kernel compute. The authors introduce a sequential-dispatch measurement method that avoids conflating dispatch with synchronization, and use it to measure per-dispatch cost, finding it independent of data type. Through a controlled fusion experiment, they show that dispatch overhead rather than kernel quality is the bottleneck at batch size 1, isolating dispatch count as the cause, and conclude that reducing dispatch count via amortization in inference engines and the WebGPU specification is the practical path to browser-based inference optimization.
本文研究了单流 WebGPU 大语言模型推理在批大小为 1 时的性能瓶颈，这一场景常见于浏览器和边缘设备，此时每次操作的调度开销可能压过内核计算，但此前缺乏充分刻画。作者提出一种顺序调度测量方法，避免将调度与同步混为一谈，并指出朴素单操作测量会因此高估每次调度开销；用该方法测得每次调度开销与数据类型无关。通过受控融合实验，他们证明在批大小为 1 时瓶颈是调度开销而非内核质量，并分离出调度次数为原因，结论是在推理引擎和 WebGPU 规范中通过调度摊销减少调度次数，是实现实用浏览器端推理的有效途径。
Understanding Practical Optimization in Machine Learning
Authors: Egor Shulgin
First: 2026-10-26T11:00:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T11:00:14+00:00
Comments: PhD thesis, 335 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern machine learning relies on practical optimization methods that often differ from the algorithms studied in classical analyses: they train submodels, clip or normalize gradients, use layer-wise non-Euclidean directions, or approximate costly inner computations. This thesis analyzes the objective, assumptions, update, and computation defined by these procedures, rather than those of simplified proxies. For submodel training, we show that Independent Subnetwork Training can converge to a shifted fixed point because sketching before differentiation biases the update for the original objective. Model-Agnostic Sparsified Training instead incorporates the sketch distribution into its objective, which makes sketched gradients unbiased and admits convergence guarantees. For private optimization, we show that adaptive quantile clipping retains bias at any fixed quantile, while suitable quantile schedules recover near-optimal nonconvex rates under heavy-tailed noise. We then combine smoothed normalization with error compensation and obtain distributed private convergence guarantees under standard assumptions, without assuming bounded gradients. For optimizers based on linear minimization oracles, we develop and validate a layer-wise generalized smoothness model and build on it the Gluon framework for Muon- and Scion-style methods. We also show that the approximate orthogonalization in Muon matters: less precise orthogonalization calls for smaller stepsizes, so its precision should be tuned together with the learning rate. Overall, practical methods are better understood when theory retains the features that determine their optimization behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解机器学习中的实用优化
现代机器学习依赖实用优化方法，这些方法往往不同于经典分析中所研究的算法：它们训练子模型、裁剪或归一化梯度、使用逐层非欧几里得方向，或近似代价高昂的内部计算。本论文分析这些过程所定义的目标、假设、更新和计算，而非简化代理的目标、假设、更新和计算。对于子模型训练，我们表明独立子网络训练可能收敛到一个偏移的不动点，因为微分之前的草图化会使原目标的更新产生偏差。模型无关稀疏化训练则将草图分布纳入其目标，使草图梯度无偏，并具有收敛保证。对于隐私优化，我们表明自适应分位数裁剪在任何固定分位数下都会保留偏差，而合适的分位数调度可在重尾噪声下恢复接近最优的非凸速率。随后，我们将平滑归一化与误差补偿相结合，在标准假设下获得分布式隐私收敛保证，且不假设梯度有界。对于基于线性最小化预言机的优化器，我们开发并验证了一个逐层广义平滑性模型，并在此基础上构建了用于 Muon 和 Scion 风格方法的 Gluon 框架。我们还表明，Muon 中的近似正交化很重要：正交化越不精确，所需步长越小，因此其精度应与学习率一起调节。总体而言，当理论保留决定实用方法优化行为的特征时，这些方法才能得到更好的理解。
Summary / 总结
This PhD thesis argues that practical machine-learning optimizers should be analyzed as they are actually implemented rather than through simplified proxies, since procedures such as submodel training, gradient clipping and normalization, layer-wise non-Euclidean directions, and approximate inner computations determine their optimization behavior. The work analyzes the objective, assumptions, update, and computation of these procedures, showing that Independent Subnetwork Training can converge to a shifted fixed point because sketching before differentiation biases the update, whereas Model-Agnostic Sparsified Training incorporates the sketch distribution into its objective to obtain unbiased sketched gradients and convergence guarantees; for private optimization it shows that adaptive quantile clipping retains bias at any fixed quantile while suitable quantile schedules recover near-optimal nonconvex rates under heavy-tailed noise, and it combines smoothed normalization with error compensation to obtain distributed private convergence guarantees without bounded-gradient assumptions. For linear-minimization-oracle optimizers, the thesis develops and validates a layer-wise generalized smoothness model underlying the Gluon framework for Muon- and Scion-style methods, and shows that the precision of Muon's approximate orthogonalization matters, with less precise orthogonalization requiring smaller stepsizes and thus precision tuned together with the learning rate.
本博士论文主张，应按照实际实现方式来分析机器学习中的实用优化方法，而不是分析简化替代模型，因为子模型训练、梯度裁剪与归一化、逐层非欧几里得方向以及近似内层计算等过程各自定义了不同的目标、假设、更新与计算。论文直接分析这些过程：对于子模型训练，指出 Independent Subnetwork Training 可能收敛到偏移的不动点，原因是先做 sketching 再求导会使针对原目标的更新产生偏差，而 Model-Agnostic Sparsified Training 将 sketch 分布纳入目标，使 sketch 梯度无偏并获得收敛保证；对于隐私优化，证明自适应分位数裁剪在任意固定分位数下仍保留偏差，而合适的分位数调度可在重尾噪声下恢复近最优非凸收敛速率，并将平滑归一化与误差补偿结合，在标准假设下获得分布式隐私收敛保证且不假设梯度有界；对于基于线性最小化 oracle 的优化器，建立并验证了逐层广义光滑性模型，并据此构建了面向 Muon 和 Scion 类方法的 Gluon 框架，同时表明 Muon 中近似正交化的精度会影响优化，精度越低所需步长越小，因此正交化精度应与学习率联合调节。总体结论是，当理论保留决定优化行为的关键特征时，才能更好地理解实用方法。
V$^3$AST-Bench: Verifiable, Valid, and Versatile Safety Evaluation for Coding Agents
Authors: Weitao Feng, Hengheng Xiong, Zihao Zeng, Jun Chen, Jiyang Guan, Xiaofeng Wang, Jialing Tao, Hui Xue, Wei Dong
First: 2026-10-26T10:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T10:44:18+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/Alibaba-AAIG/VAST-Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models increasingly act through code, modifying repositories, executing programs, and interacting with services in agent form. Their actions introduce risks that cannot be assessed from generated responses alone. Safety evaluation must verify actual consequences, distinguish harm avoidance from task incapability, and cover realistic workflows. We introduce V$^3$AST-Bench, a benchmark for Verifiable results, Valid interpretation, and Versatile risk coverage of Agent Safety. It comprises 212 risk tasks spanning 18 categories. Dedicated runtime environments verify harm and task outcomes through executable checks over states, records, and artifact behavior, while paired capability twin tasks verify workflow competence. Our experiments span 51 combinations of 11 models and five harnesses, spanning 86,496 trials and over 9 billion tokens of model interaction. Results show that V$^3$AST-Bench remains challenging even for frontier coding agents, as Claude Opus 4.8 with Claude Code still causes verified harm on 20.9% of tasks. Across model-harness combinations, agents with comparable competence exhibit markedly different safety outcomes, showing that task capability alone does not guarantee safe execution. More importantly, even explicit recognition of risks does not reliably prevent harmful actions, revealing a critical gap between risk awareness and safe execution. These findings highlight the need to move beyond risk recognition toward enforcing safety throughout execution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：V$^3$AST-Bench：面向编码智能体的可验证、有效且多用途的安全评估
大语言模型越来越多地以智能体形式通过代码行动，修改代码仓库、执行程序并与服务交互。其行为带来的风险无法仅凭生成的回复加以评估。安全评估必须验证实际后果，区分避免伤害与任务能力不足，并覆盖真实工作流程。我们提出 V$^3$AST-Bench，这是一个面向智能体安全的可验证结果、有效解读和多用途风险覆盖的基准。它包含 212 个风险任务，涵盖 18 个类别。专用运行时环境通过对状态、记录和产物行为的可执行检查来验证伤害与任务结果，而成对的能力孪生任务则验证工作流能力。我们的实验涵盖 11 个模型与五种运行框架的 51 种组合，共 86,496 次试验、超过 90 亿 token 的模型交互。结果表明，即使对前沿编码智能体而言，V$^3$AST-Bench 仍具挑战性：Claude Opus 4.8 配合 Claude Code 仍在 20.9% 的任务中造成经核实的伤害。在不同模型-框架组合中，能力相当的智能体表现出显著不同的安全结果，表明仅具备任务能力并不能保证安全执行。更重要的是，即使明确识别出风险，也不能可靠地阻止有害行为，揭示了风险意识与安全执行之间的关键差距。这些发现凸显了从风险识别转向在执行全过程中落实安全的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Safety evaluation of coding agents is difficult because their risks arise from executed actions rather than generated text, so assessment must verify real consequences, separate harm avoidance from task incapability, and cover realistic workflows. The authors introduce V$^3$AST-Bench, a benchmark of 212 risk tasks across 18 categories, with dedicated runtime environments that verify harm and task outcomes through executable checks on states, records, and artifact behavior, plus paired capability twin tasks that verify workflow competence. Experiments covering 51 model-harness combinations (11 models, five harnesses), 86,496 trials, and over 9 billion tokens of interaction show the benchmark remains challenging for frontier agents, with Claude Opus 4.8 under Claude Code still causing verified harm on 20.9% of tasks; agents of comparable competence show markedly different safety outcomes, and explicit risk recognition does not reliably prevent harmful actions, indicating a gap between risk awareness and safe execution.
针对编码智能体的安全评估需要验证实际后果而非仅看生成回复，区分规避伤害与任务能力不足，并覆盖真实工作流。作者提出 V$^3$AST-Bench，包含 18 个类别共 212 个风险任务，配有专用运行时环境，通过对状态、记录和产物行为的可执行检查来验证伤害与任务结果，并设置配对的能力孪生任务以验证工作流胜任力。实验覆盖 11 个模型与五种执行框架的 51 种组合，共 86,496 次试验、超过 90 亿 token 的模型交互，结果显示该基准对前沿编码智能体仍具挑战性，Claude Opus 4.8 配合 Claude Code 仍在 20.9% 的任务中造成经核实的伤害；能力相当的智能体在安全结果上差异显著，且即使明确识别风险也不能可靠阻止有害行为，揭示了风险认知与安全执行之间的关键差距。
AutoEnergyDesign: An LLM-Driven Agent for Automatic Energy System Design Optimization
Authors: Amedeo Buonanno, Salvatore Fabozzi, Maria Valenti
First: 2026-10-26T10:25:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T10:25:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing building energy systems is a high-dimensional, mixed-integer search problem: when each design is evaluated by a year-long simulation, black-box optimizers typically need hundreds of evaluations. This paper presents AutoEnergyDesign, a framework in which a Large Language Model (LLM) agent reasons over previous simulation results, proposes configuration changes and learns in context from each outcome, while a git-based mechanism automatically reverts unsuccessful changes. The agent couples a reasoning model with an explicit memory organisation (procedural, semantic, episodic and reflective), a constrained action interface and an environment-grounded keep/revert signal. The simulator covers eight technologies (photovoltaics, micro-wind, combined heat and power, solar thermal, gas boilers, batteries, heat pumps and inverters) and computes the 25-year Net Present Cost (NPC), including capital and operating costs and salvage value. On a case study with measured building load profiles, the agent, run with Claude Haiku 5.5, Sonnet 5.5 and Opus 5.5 (ten runs of 50 simulations each), is compared with random search, Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) Bayesian optimization and a $(1+1)$ evolution strategy sharing the agent's acceptance rule (300 evaluations, ten seeds). With all three models the agent reaches an NPC of about 3.53 MEUR, 1.20% below the best TPE result and 0.23% below the best evolution-strategy result. It comes within 1% of this design after a median of 18 (Opus) to 27.5 (Haiku) simulations, whereas among the baselines only the evolution strategy does so, in half of its seeds and after a median of 192 evaluations. Opus reaches the best design in all ten runs, Sonnet in six (two runs never come within 1%), and Haiku comes within 1% in every run but reaches it exactly only once. Every decision is recorded in an auditable trace.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoEnergyDesign：一种由LLM驱动的自动能源系统设计优化智能体
建筑能源系统设计是一个高维混合整数搜索问题：当每个设计都需通过为期一年的仿真来评估时，黑盒优化器通常需要数百次评估。本文提出AutoEnergyDesign框架，其中大型语言模型（LLM）智能体基于先前的仿真结果进行推理，提出配置变更，并从每次结果中进行上下文学习，同时基于git的机制会自动回退不成功的变更。该智能体将推理模型与显式记忆组织（程序性、语义性、情景性和反思性）、受约束的动作接口以及基于环境的保留/回退信号相结合。仿真器涵盖八种技术（光伏、微型风电、热电联产、太阳能热利用、燃气锅炉、电池、热泵和逆变器），并计算25年净现值成本（NPC），包括资本成本、运营成本和残值。在一个使用实测建筑负荷曲线的案例研究中，该智能体分别以Claude Haiku 5.5、Sonnet 5.5和Opus 5.5运行（每次十轮、每轮50次仿真），并与随机搜索、树结构Parzen估计器（TPE）贝叶斯优化以及共享智能体接受规则的$(1+1)$进化策略（300次评估、十个随机种子）进行比较。在三种模型下，智能体均达到约3.53 MEUR的NPC，比最佳TPE结果低1.20%，比最佳进化策略结果低0.23%。它在仿真次数中位数18次（Opus）至27.5次（Haiku）后即达到该设计的1%以内，而在基线中只有进化策略能做到这一点，且仅在其一半的随机种子中、在192次评估的中位数后实现。Opus在全部十轮中均达到最佳设计，Sonnet在六轮中达到（有两轮从未进入1%以内），Haiku在每一轮中均进入1%以内，但仅有一次精确达到。每个决策都记录在可审计的轨迹中。
Summary / 总结
Designing building energy systems is a high-dimensional mixed-integer problem in which each candidate requires a year-long simulation, making black-box optimization costly. AutoEnergyDesign addresses this by using an LLM agent that reasons over prior simulation results, proposes configuration changes, and learns in context, supported by an explicit memory organization (procedural, semantic, episodic, reflective), a constrained action interface, and a git-based keep/revert mechanism that automatically undoes unsuccessful changes; the simulator covers eight technologies and computes 25-year Net Present Cost including capital, operating, and salvage costs. In a case study with measured building loads, the agent run with Claude Haiku, Sonnet, and Opus reached an NPC of about 3.53 MEUR, 1.20% below the best TPE result and 0.23% below the best evolution-strategy result, and came within 1% of this design after a median of 18 to 27.5 simulations, whereas only the evolution strategy among baselines did so, in half its seeds and after a median of 192 evaluations; Opus reached the best design in all ten runs, Sonnet in six, and Haiku came within 1% in every run but matched it exactly only once, with all decisions recorded in an auditable trace.
设计建筑能源系统是一个高维混合整数搜索问题，每个设计方案都需要进行为期一年的仿真评估，导致黑盒优化器通常需要数百次评估。AutoEnergyDesign 提出由大语言模型智能体对既往仿真结果进行推理、提出配置变更并在上下文中学习，同时借助基于 git 的机制自动回退不成功的变更；该智能体结合了显式记忆组织（程序性、语义性、情景性和反思性）、受约束的动作接口以及基于环境反馈的保留/回退信号，仿真器涵盖光伏、微型风电、热电联产、太阳能热利用、燃气锅炉、电池、热泵和逆变器八类技术，并计算包含资本成本、运行成本和残值的 25 年净现值成本。在采用实测建筑负荷曲线的案例研究中，使用 Claude Haiku、Sonnet 和 Opus 运行的智能体达到约 3.53 百万欧元的净现值成本，比最佳 TPE 结果低 1.20%，比最佳进化策略结果低 0.23%，并在中位数 18 至 27.5 次仿真后达到该设计 1% 以内，而基线中只有进化策略做到这一点，且仅在一半随机种子中实现、中位数为 192 次评估；Opus 在全部十次运行中均找到最佳设计，Sonnet 为六次，Haiku 每次运行都进入 1% 以内但仅一次精确达到，所有决策均记录于可审计的轨迹中。
LongSearchEval: Evaluating Long-Horizon Web Search Capabilities of Large Language Models via a Trajectory-Aware Agent Framework with Failure-Node Localization
Authors: Jialiang Liu, Shusheng Li, Wenjun Tan
First: 2026-10-26T10:15:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T10:15:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon web search requires LLM-based agents to integrate information across web pages through extended interactions under task-specific constraints. Evaluating this capability is important not only for measuring final answer quality, but also for assessing whether an agent acquires, verifies, and uses evidence reliably over extended interactions. Existing evaluations, however, mainly rely on simulated trajectories or on post-hoc trajectories reconstructed from final answers, rather than on retained real interaction traces, which limits the fidelity of behavioral assessment and weakens support for fine-grained error localization. To address this gap, we introduce LONGSEARCHEVAL, a trajectory-aware agent evaluation framework that combines a unified agent execution protocol with source constrained outcome verification and retrospective failure-node localization. Stage 1 verifies answer groups against agent-cited webpages without human-authored gold answers; Stage 2 audits normalized rounds and links flagged failure nodes to incorrect answer groups. We evaluate 16 models from eight families on 100 tasks, yielding 1,600 retained runs and 6,545 automatically flagged failure nodes. Trajectory collection and evaluation required 1,037 and 318 hours of cumulative runtime, respectively. Human validation yields 94.50% agreement on supplied answer-group labels and 98.11% feature-correspondence precision for audited round-group links. Together, outcome scores and linked trajectory evidence support controlled model comparison and retrospective inspection of answer-related content.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongSearchEval：通过轨迹感知的智能体框架与失败节点定位评估大语言模型的长程网络搜索能力
长程网络搜索要求基于LLM的智能体在任务特定约束下通过扩展交互跨网页整合信息。评估这一能力不仅对衡量最终答案质量很重要，也对评估智能体是否在扩展交互中可靠地获取、验证和使用证据很重要。然而，现有评估主要依赖模拟轨迹或从最终答案事后重建的轨迹，而非保留的真实交互轨迹，这限制了行为评估的保真度，并削弱了对细粒度错误定位的支持。为弥补这一空白，我们提出LONGSEARCHEVAL，一个轨迹感知的智能体评估框架，它将统一的智能体执行协议与来源约束的结果验证及回溯性失败节点定位相结合。阶段1在无人工编写金标准答案的情况下，依据智能体引用的网页验证答案组；阶段2审计归一化轮次，并将标记的失败节点关联到错误答案组。我们在100个任务上评估了来自8个家族的16个模型，得到1,600次保留运行和6,545个自动标记的失败节点。轨迹收集和评估分别需要1,037和318小时的累计运行时间。人工验证在所提供的答案组标签上达到94.50%的一致性，在审计的轮次-组链接上达到98.11%的特征对应精确率。结果分数与关联的轨迹证据共同支持受控的模型比较以及对答案相关内容的事后检查。
Summary / 总结
Long-horizon web search by LLM agents requires integrating evidence across many interactions, but existing evaluations rely on simulated or post-hoc reconstructed trajectories rather than retained real interaction traces, limiting behavioral fidelity and fine-grained error localization. The authors introduce LongSearchEval, a trajectory-aware agent evaluation framework combining a unified execution protocol, source-constrained outcome verification without human-authored gold answers, and retrospective failure-node localization that audits normalized rounds and links flagged nodes to incorrect answer groups. Evaluating 16 models from eight families on 100 tasks produced 1,600 retained runs and 6,545 automatically flagged failure nodes, with trajectory collection and evaluation taking 1,037 and 318 cumulative runtime hours; human validation showed 94.50% agreement on answer-group labels and 98.11% feature-correspondence precision for audited round-group links.
长程网络搜索要求基于大语言模型的智能体在任务约束下通过多轮交互整合网页信息，但现有评测多依赖模拟轨迹或由最终答案事后重建的轨迹，而非保留的真实交互记录，限制了行为评估的保真度和细粒度错误定位能力。为此，作者提出 LongSearchEval，一个轨迹感知的智能体评测框架，将统一的智能体执行协议与来源约束的结果验证及回溯式失败节点定位相结合：第一阶段在无人工标注金标准答案的情况下，依据智能体引用的网页验证答案组；第二阶段审计规范化轮次，并将标记的失败节点与错误答案组关联。研究在100个任务上评测了来自8个模型家族的16个模型，获得1,600条保留运行记录和6,545个自动标记的失败节点，轨迹采集与评测分别累计耗时1,037小时和318小时；人工验证显示，所提供的答案组标签一致率为94.50%，审计轮次与组别关联的特征对应精确率为98.11%，从而支持受控的模型比较和对答案相关内容的回溯检查。
TUON: A Tensor-Aware Optimizer Utilizing Tensor-SVD and Polarization for Large Language Model Pretraining
Authors: Anda Tang, Xuanlin Yang, Junjie Wang, Zhouchen Lin
First: 2026-10-26T10:04:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T10:04:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large scale language model~(LLM) pretraining is increasingly bottlenecked by optimizer efficiency, driving optimizer design from vector-aware methods such as AdamW to matrix-aware methods. Matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon exploit matrix structure but treat layers independently, neglecting cross-layer correlations. Tensor-aware optimizers are therefore needed, yet existing ones rely on a matricization-based tensor algebra and remain restricted to shallow stacking and specific modules. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TUON}, a novel optimizer built on a transform-based tensor algebra derived from the Tensor-SVD (T-SVD). Unlike methods that polarize a globally unfolded matrix, TUON mixes layers via a unitary transform $\boldsymbol{Q}$ and then polarizes each transformed slice, and inverts the transform to recover the update. This mix-then-separate strategy captures cross-layer correlations while preserving per-layer diversity, avoiding the structural dimension imbalance and cross-layer misalignment of unfolding. Thus it successfully scales to more layers and diverse modules beyond QKV. Theoretically, we prove that TUON performs steepest descent under a $\boldsymbol{Q}$-transformed tensor spectral norm, and identify its update as the tensor polar factor of the T-SVD. We further establish transform non-redundancy, norm and smoothness comparisons with Muon and TEON, and a convergence guarantee. Empirically, TUON improves consistently over Muon and TEON on GPT-124M/350M and Qwen-0.6B/4B, with ablations confirming the transformation mechanism, cross-layer structure beyond two-layer stacking, adaptability beyond QKV, and robustness to the polarization method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TUON：一种利用张量SVD与极化的张量感知优化器，用于大语言模型预训练
大规模语言模型（LLM）预训练日益受限于优化器效率，推动优化器设计从AdamW等向量感知方法转向矩阵感知方法。Muon等矩阵感知优化器利用矩阵结构，但独立处理各层，忽略了跨层相关性。因此需要张量感知优化器，然而现有方法依赖基于矩阵化的张量代数，且仍局限于浅层堆叠和特定模块。本文提出TUON，一种基于由张量SVD（T-SVD）导出的变换型张量代数的新型优化器。与对全局展开矩阵进行极化的方法不同，TUON通过酉变换Q混合各层，然后对每个变换后的切片进行极化，并逆变换以恢复更新。这种先混合后分离的策略在捕捉跨层相关性的同时保持各层多样性，避免了展开带来的结构维度不平衡和跨层错位。因此，它能成功扩展到更多层和QKV之外的多样模块。理论上，我们证明TUON在Q变换张量谱范数下执行最速下降，并将其更新识别为T-SVD的张量极因子。我们进一步建立了变换非冗余性、与Muon和TEON的范数和光滑性比较，以及收敛保证。实验上，TUON在GPT-124M/350M和Qwen-0.6B/4B上持续优于Muon和TEON，消融实验证实了变换机制、超越两层堆叠的跨层结构、超越QKV的适应性以及对极化方法的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Large language model pretraining is increasingly limited by optimizer efficiency, motivating a shift from vector-aware optimizers like AdamW to matrix- and tensor-aware methods, but existing tensor-aware optimizers rely on matricization-based tensor algebra and are confined to shallow stacking and specific modules. The paper proposes TUON, an optimizer built on transform-based tensor algebra derived from Tensor-SVD, which mixes layers via a unitary transform Q, polarizes each transformed slice, and inverts the transform to recover the update, thereby capturing cross-layer correlations while preserving per-layer diversity. Theoretically, the authors prove TUON performs steepest descent under a Q-transformed tensor spectral norm, identify its update as the tensor polar factor of the T-SVD, and establish transform non-redundancy, norm and smoothness comparisons with Muon and TEON, and a convergence guarantee. Empirically, TUON consistently improves over Muon and TEON on GPT-124M/350M and Qwen-0.6B/4B, with ablations confirming the transformation mechanism, cross-layer structure beyond two-layer stacking, adaptability beyond QKV, and robustness to the polarization method.
大语言模型预训练日益受限于优化器效率，推动优化器设计从 AdamW 等向量感知方法转向矩阵感知方法，而 Muon 等矩阵感知优化器虽利用矩阵结构却独立处理各层、忽略跨层相关性，现有张量感知优化器又依赖矩阵化张量代数且局限于浅层堆叠和特定模块。作者提出 TUON，一种基于 Tensor-SVD 的变换式张量代数优化器，它通过酉变换混合各层、对每个变换切片做极化、再逆变换恢复更新，从而在捕捉跨层相关性的同时保持各层多样性。理论上，作者证明 TUON 在 Q 变换张量谱范数下执行最速下降，其更新即 T-SVD 的张量极因子，并给出变换非冗余性、与 Muon 和 TEON 的范数与光滑性比较以及收敛保证。实验上，TUON 在 GPT-124M/350M 和 Qwen-0.6B/4B 上稳定优于 Muon 和 TEON，消融实验验证了变换机制、超越两层堆叠的跨层结构、超越 QKV 的适应性以及对极化方法的鲁棒性。
Back-Translation Still Matters: Leveraging Monolingual Data for Low-Resource Machine Translation with LLMs
Authors: Divyanshu Bathla, Parag Singla, Jitin Singla
First: 2026-10-13T17:50:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:27:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-trained Large Language Model (LLMs) are effective on many tasks in zero-shot and few-shot settings. For machine translation of low-resource languages, the obvious options are few-shot prompting and fine-tuning on a small parallel set. We ask: what if moderate amounts of low-resource monolingual data are also available? We argue that, rather than continued pretraining, a more effective use of this data is the pre-LLM era technique of back-translation (BT). The current fine-tuned LLM translates monolingual sentences in each language, and the resulting pairs train it in the reverse direction. Updates on the parallel set are interleaved for stable learning. With 1K parallel pairs on Odia and Swahili, BT beats continued pretraining into the low-resource language by 7.7 to 16.4 XCOMET-XXL points in domain. With beam-decoded intermediate translations, it also beats a model with 10 time more parallel data. In-context examples retrieved from the parallel data further improve BT. We also analyze the source, quality and amount of monolingual text. Across five low-resource languages, with Hindi as a high-resource control, and two 3B backbones, BT improves translation into the low-resource language by 16 to 173% XCOMET-XXL over parallel-only fine-tuning. Blind human evaluation on Odia and Sanskrit agrees with the automatic metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回译仍然重要：利用单语数据结合大语言模型进行低资源机器翻译
预训练大语言模型（LLMs）在零样本和少样本设置下对许多任务有效。对于低资源语言的机器翻译，显而易见的选择是少样本提示和在小规模平行集上微调。我们提出：如果还有适量低资源单语数据可用呢？我们认为，与其继续预训练，更有效地利用这些数据的方式是前LLM时代的回译（BT）技术。当前微调的LLM翻译每种语言的单语句子，得到的句对以相反方向训练它。平行集上的更新交替进行以实现稳定学习。在奥里亚语和斯瓦希里语上使用1K平行句对，BT在领域内比继续预训练到低资源语言高出7.7至16.4个XCOMET-XXL点。使用束搜索解码的中间翻译，它还优于拥有10倍平行数据的模型。从平行数据中检索的上下文示例进一步改进BT。我们还分析了单语文本的来源、数量和质量。在五种低资源语言上，以印地语作为高资源对照，以及两个3B骨干模型，BT相比仅平行微调将翻译到低资源语言的XCOMET-XXL提高了16%至173%。对奥里亚语和梵语的盲人工评估与自动指标一致。
Summary / 总结
This work asks how best to exploit moderate amounts of low-resource monolingual data when fine-tuning LLMs for translation, arguing that back-translation is more effective than continued pretraining. The fine-tuned LLM translates monolingual sentences in each language, the resulting synthetic pairs train it in the reverse direction, and updates on the parallel set are interleaved for stable learning, with in-context examples retrieved from parallel data further improving results. With 1K parallel pairs for Odia and Swahili, back-translation beats continued pretraining by 7.7 to 16.4 XCOMET-XXL points in domain and, using beam-decoded intermediate translations, also outperforms a model with ten times more parallel data; across five low-resource languages with Hindi as a high-resource control and two 3B backbones, it improves translation into the low-resource language by 16 to 173% XCOMET-XXL over parallel-only fine-tuning, and blind human evaluation on Odia and Sanskrit agrees with the automatic metrics.
本文探讨在仅有少量平行语料用于微调大语言模型进行翻译时，如何更有效地利用适量低资源语言单语数据。作者没有采用继续预训练，而是重新启用大模型时代之前的回译技术：由微调后的模型翻译各语言的单语句子，用生成的合成句对训练反方向的翻译，并与平行集上的更新交替进行以保持学习稳定，同时从平行数据中检索上下文示例进一步提升效果。在 Odia 和 Swahili 各 1K 平行句对的条件下，回译在领域内比继续预训练高出 7.7 至 16.4 个 XCOMET-XXL 点，配合束搜索解码的中间译文时还优于使用十倍平行数据训练的模型；在五种低资源语言（以印地语作为高资源对照）和两个 3B 基座模型上，回译相较仅用平行数据微调将译入低资源语言的效果提升 16% 至 173% XCOMET-XXL，Odia 和梵语的盲测人工评估结果与自动指标一致。
Improving VLMs on Scientific Chart Analysis via Autonomous Evolution
Authors: Abhinav Sudhakar Dubey, Vidushi Goel, Kiran Rana, Shikhar Mathur, Vaggos Chatziafratis
First: 2026-10-26T09:22:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:22:52+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables. Code: https://github.com/AbhinavDubey30/VLM-Autonomous-Evolution
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding and operating with scientific charts is a standard approach in data analysis, and while humans are good at interpreting such charts, open vision-language models (VLMs) are known to perform poorly when it comes to question answering based on such data types. Motivated by an initial observation that many of their errors are due to inexact answering, we raise the following natural question: how much accuracy can we recover without changing the model at all? In this paper, we study this question by searching over inference programs and the code around a "frozen" version of the model. Using ideas from autonomous evolution (famously used in AlphaEvolve), we first start from a baseline inference program, and in each round we show an LLM judge the code together with a few failures on training questions, and ask for an improving evolutionary move. As usual in autonomous evolution approaches, the candidate programs are kept in a small pool ranked by training accuracy, and the next edit is performed on top-scorers from this pool. We run this approach on three VLMs and four popular benchmarks on scientific charts, and we measure the fraction of descriptive questions whose answer exactly matches the ground truth. The accuracy improves in all experimental settings. On average, it rises from 16.6% with the starting program to 41.3% with the evolved one, and the largest single increase is 55.2 percentage points. As a side benefit, our evolved program is 3.8x faster than the starting program, because short answers are easier to grade and faster to generate. Our results demonstrate that a large part of the observed errors arise due to the inference pipeline, and that optimizing it can improve the "frozen" VLM without the need to retrain it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自主进化提升VLM在科学图表分析中的表现
理解和操作科学图表是数据分析中的标准方法，虽然人类擅长解读此类图表，但已知开放视觉语言模型（VLM）在基于此类数据类型的问答任务上表现不佳。受初步观察的启发——它们的许多错误源于不精确的回答——我们提出以下自然问题：在不改变模型本身的情况下，我们能恢复多少准确率？本文通过在一个“冻结”版本的模型周围搜索推理程序和代码来研究这一问题。借鉴自主进化的思想（著名应用于AlphaEvolve），我们首先从基线推理程序开始，在每一轮中让LLM评判者查看代码以及在训练问题上的若干失败案例，并要求给出一个改进的进化动作。与自主进化方法的通常做法一样，候选程序保存在一个小型池中并按训练准确率排序，下一次编辑在池中得分最高的程序上进行。我们在三个VLM和四个流行的科学图表基准上运行该方法，并测量答案与真实值完全匹配的描述性问题的比例。在所有实验设置中准确率均有提升。平均而言，它从起始程序的16.6%上升到进化后的41.3%，最大单次提升为55.2个百分点。作为附带好处，我们进化后的程序比起始程序快3.8倍，因为简短答案更容易评分且生成更快。我们的结果表明，观察到的错误中有很大一部分源于推理流水线，优化它可以改进“冻结”的VLM而无需重新训练。
Summary / 总结
Open vision-language models perform poorly on question answering over scientific charts, and an initial observation that many errors stem from inexact answering motivates the question of how much accuracy can be recovered without changing the model at all. The authors address this by searching over inference programs and surrounding code for a frozen model, using autonomous-evolution ideas in which an LLM judge is shown the current code plus a few training failures and asked for an improving edit, with candidate programs kept in a small pool ranked by training accuracy and edits applied to top scorers. Across three VLMs and four scientific-chart benchmarks, exact-match accuracy on descriptive questions improved in every setting, rising on average from 16.6% to 41.3%, with a largest single gain of 55.2 percentage points, and the evolved program was 3.8x faster than the starting one, indicating that much of the error arises from the inference pipeline rather than the model itself.
开放视觉语言模型在科学图表问答上表现较差，作者初步观察到许多错误源于回答不精确，由此提出在不改变模型本身的前提下能恢复多少准确率的问题。为此，作者针对冻结模型搜索推理程序及其周边代码，采用自主进化方法：每轮将当前代码与若干训练问题上的失败案例展示给LLM评判者，请其提出改进性编辑，候选程序保存在按训练准确率排序的小型池中，并在得分最高者基础上继续编辑。在三个VLM和四个科学图表基准上，以描述性问题答案与标准答案完全一致的比例衡量，所有实验设置下准确率均有提升，平均从初始程序的16.6%升至进化后的41.3%，单次最大提升为55.2个百分点，且进化后的程序比初始程序快3.8倍。结果表明，相当一部分错误来自推理流程，无需重新训练模型即可加以改善。
SpatialCLI: Learning to Reason With Spatial Tools, Then Without Them
Authors: Yang Zhou, Zixuan Huang, Sunzhu Li, Zhuo Yang, Chen Zhang, Shunian Chen, Caijun Yan, Jianyao Xu, Shunyu Liu, Weijie Fu, Peiliang Li, Xiaozhi Chen, Yuxiang Cai
First: 2026-07-30T05:39:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:20:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in embodied agents to interpret visual inputs, reason about spatial relationships, and make task-level decisions based on that reasoning. However, a fundamental capability mismatch remains: general VLMs can reason about the overall task but often miss the visual details that determine success, while specialist vision models can capture those details but cannot translate them into task-level decisions. In this work, we propose SpatialCLI, a framework that teaches VLMs to reason with spatial tools and progressively internalize the specialist perceptual capabilities they provide. SpatialCLI proceeds in three stages: (1) Call exposes specialist vision models as spatial tools to augment the VLM's perception; (2) Learn uses Cold-Start SFT and agentic RL to improve tool use; and (3) Internalize verbalizes successful tool-use trajectories to internalize specialist perceptual capabilities. We further introduce SpatialCLI-Bench, a 516-example benchmark for compositional perception across localization, segmentation, depth, and pose. On MindCube, SpatialCLI raises Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 29.3% to 84.6% with tools, surpassing GPT-5.6 Sol with tools (72.1%), while retaining 73.8% without tools after internalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialCLI：学习借助空间工具进行推理，然后脱离工具
视觉语言模型（VLM）越来越多地被用于具身智能体中，以解读视觉输入、推理空间关系，并基于该推理做出任务级决策。然而，一种根本性的能力错配仍然存在：通用VLM能够对整体任务进行推理，但常常遗漏决定成败的视觉细节；而专用视觉模型能够捕捉这些细节，却无法将其转化为任务级决策。在本工作中，我们提出SpatialCLI，一个教VLM借助空间工具进行推理、并逐步内化这些工具所提供的专用感知能力的框架。SpatialCLI分三个阶段进行：（1）Call将专用视觉模型作为空间工具暴露出来，以增强VLM的感知；（2）Learn使用冷启动SFT和智能体RL来改进工具使用；（3）Internalize将成功的工具使用轨迹言语化，以内化专用感知能力。我们进一步引入SpatialCLI-Bench，一个包含516个示例的基准，用于跨定位、分割、深度和位姿的组合感知。在MindCube上，SpatialCLI借助工具将Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct从29.3%提升至84.6%，超过借助工具的GPT-5.6 Sol（72.1%），同时在内化后于无工具情况下保持73.8%。
Summary / 总结
General vision-language models can reason about task-level goals but often miss the fine visual details that determine success, while specialist vision models capture those details but cannot translate them into decisions. SpatialCLI addresses this mismatch by teaching VLMs to reason with spatial tools and then internalize the specialist perceptual capabilities those tools provide, through three stages: exposing specialist vision models as callable spatial tools, improving tool use with Cold-Start SFT and agentic RL, and verbalizing successful tool-use trajectories to internalize perception. The authors also introduce SpatialCLI-Bench, a 516-example benchmark for compositional perception across localization, segmentation, depth, and pose. On MindCube, SpatialCLI raises Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 29.3% to 84.6% with tools, surpassing GPT-5.6 Sol with tools at 72.1%, and retains 73.8% without tools after internalization.
通用视觉语言模型能够对整体具身任务进行推理，但常常忽略决定成败的视觉细节，而专用视觉模型虽能捕捉这些细节，却无法将其转化为任务级决策。SpatialCLI 针对这一能力错配提出三阶段框架：首先将专用视觉模型封装为可调用的空间工具以增强 VLM 感知，然后通过冷启动监督微调与智能体强化学习提升工具使用能力，最后将成功的工具使用轨迹语言化，从而内化专用感知能力。作者还提出了 SpatialCLI-Bench，一个包含 516 个样例、覆盖定位、分割、深度与位姿的组合感知基准。在 MindCube 上，SpatialCLI 使 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 在有工具时从 29.3% 提升至 84.6%，超过有工具的 GPT-5.6 Sol（72.1%），并在内化后于无工具条件下仍保持 73.8%。
A Cross-Modal Prompt Injection Attack against Large Vision-Language Models with Image-Only Perturbation
Authors: Hao Yang, Zhuo Ma, Yang Liu, Yilong Yang, Guancheng Wang, JianFeng Ma
First: 2026-05-15T15:47:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:17:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for multimodal intelligence, but their growing deployment also expands the attack surface of prompt injection. Despite this growing concern, existing attacks still suffer from a critical limitation: the injected prompt for one modality only steers the model's interpretation of that singular input. Alternatively, these attacks remain multimodal but fail to achieve cross-modal prompt perturbation. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel cross-modal prompt injection attack CrossMPI, which can steer the model's interpretation of both textual and visual inputs via image-only prompt injection. Our design is underpinned by the following key breakthroughs. First, we turn the focus of the injected prompt perturbation optimization from the visual embedding space (typically with only $10^5$ parameters) to the model hidden state space (for multimodal information integration and with $10^7$ parameters). Then, two strategies are adopted to mitigate the optimization challenges posed by the larger parameter space. To constrain the optimized model parameter space, we introduce a layer selection strategy that identifies the layers most critical to multimodal integration. Interestingly, deviating from the past experience, our analysis reveals that the optimal layers for LVLM prompt perturbation reside in the middle of the model rather than the last. To constrain the image perturbation space, we propose a new distance-decremental perturbation budget assignment strategy that allocates budgets decrementally as the pixel distance to semantic-critical regions increases. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs and datasets show that our method significantly outperforms baseline approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种针对大型视觉语言模型的仅图像扰动跨模态提示注入攻击
大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）已成为多模态智能的强大范式，但其日益增长的部署也扩大了提示注入的攻击面。尽管这一担忧日益增加，现有攻击仍存在一个关键局限：针对某一模态的注入提示仅能引导模型对该单一输入的解释。或者，这些攻击虽保持多模态，却未能实现跨模态提示扰动。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了一种新颖的跨模态提示注入攻击CrossMPI，它能够通过仅图像提示注入来引导模型对文本和视觉输入的解释。我们的设计基于以下关键突破。首先，我们将注入提示扰动的优化焦点从视觉嵌入空间（通常仅有$10^5$个参数）转向模型隐藏状态空间（用于多模态信息整合，具有$10^7$个参数）。随后，采用两种策略来缓解更大参数空间带来的优化挑战。为约束优化的模型参数空间，我们引入了一种层选择策略，用于识别对多模态整合最为关键的层。有趣的是，与以往经验不同，我们的分析表明，LVLM提示扰动的最优层位于模型中部而非最后。为约束图像扰动空间，我们提出了一种新的距离递减扰动预算分配策略，该策略随着像素到语义关键区域距离的增加而递减分配预算。在多个LVLM和数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法显著优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Existing prompt injection attacks against large vision-language models are limited because an injected prompt in one modality only steers that single input, and multimodal variants fail to achieve genuine cross-modal perturbation. To address this, the authors propose CrossMPI, an image-only prompt injection attack that steers the model's interpretation of both textual and visual inputs by shifting perturbation optimization from the visual embedding space (about 10^5 parameters) to the model hidden state space used for multimodal integration (about 10^7 parameters). Two strategies handle the larger optimization space: a layer selection strategy that identifies the layers most critical to multimodal integration, which analysis shows lie in the middle of the model rather than the last layers, and a distance-decremental perturbation budget assignment that allocates smaller budgets as pixel distance to semantic-critical regions increases. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs and datasets show the method significantly outperforms baseline approaches.
现有针对大型视觉语言模型的提示注入攻击存在明显局限：单一模态的注入提示只能影响该模态输入的解释，而多模态攻击又未能实现真正的跨模态提示扰动。为此，作者提出仅需图像扰动的跨模态提示注入攻击CrossMPI，通过将注入提示扰动的优化从视觉嵌入空间（约10^5参数）转移到负责多模态信息融合的模型隐藏状态空间（约10^7参数），从而同时操控模型对文本和视觉输入的解释；为缓解更大参数空间带来的优化困难，采用层选择策略定位对多模态融合最关键的层，并提出随像素到语义关键区域距离增大而递减的扰动预算分配策略。分析表明，提示扰动的最优层位于模型中部而非最后层，在多个大型视觉语言模型和数据集上的大量实验显示该方法显著优于基线方法。
PAIQ: Patch-Aligned Semantic Injection via Residual Rotation
Authors: Pinze Ren, Yuwei Zhang, Hao Chen, Linghao Meng, Chang Li, Qiankun Li
First: 2026-09-29T14:32:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:14:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language-aligned and self-supervised visual encoders offer complementary strengths in semantic abstraction and spatial detail. Harnessing this complementarity requires enriching local features while retaining distinctions between semantically related patches. We introduce PAIQ, a patch-aligned semantic injection framework that combines content-based cross-encoder matching with orthogonally constrained residual updates. Using DINOv3 patch features as the spatial base, PAIQ aggregates complementary SigLIP features through joint source allocation and injects the aggregate--base differences through a shared orthogonal transformation Q. This rotation adapts update directions while preserving residual norms and pairwise angles. For fixed projected features, we derive conditions for patch separability under similar semantic aggregates and show that rotation adds a nonnegative separation term over direct interpolation when the aggregate is shared. Only the projection and fusion parameters are trained; both visual encoders and the language model remain frozen, and fusion retains 196 visual tokens. Across diverse language backbones, PAIQ yields broad gains in judge-assessed correctness and reductions in hallucination severity over single-encoder interfaces on image description and visual question answering. On the 2B and 9B Qwen backbones, this compact interface outperforms the strongest evaluated fusion or token-compression baselines by about 2.9 correctness points on average.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PAIQ：通过残差旋转的补丁对齐语义注入
语言对齐和自监督视觉编码器在语义抽象和空间细节方面具有互补优势。利用这种互补性需要在丰富局部特征的同时保留语义相关补丁之间的区分度。我们提出PAIQ，一种补丁对齐语义注入框架，将基于内容的跨编码器匹配与正交约束的残差更新相结合。以DINOv3补丁特征作为空间基础，PAIQ通过联合源分配聚合互补的SigLIP特征，并通过共享正交变换Q注入聚合与基础之间的差异。该旋转在保持残差范数和成对角度的同时调整更新方向。对于固定投影特征，我们推导了在相似语义聚合下补丁可分离性的条件，并证明当聚合共享时，旋转相比直接插值增加了非负分离项。仅训练投影和融合参数；两个视觉编码器和语言模型均保持冻结，融合保留196个视觉令牌。在多种语言骨干网络上，PAIQ在图像描述和视觉问答任务中，相比单编码器接口，在评判评估的正确性上取得广泛提升，并降低幻觉严重程度。在2B和9B Qwen骨干网络上，这一紧凑接口在正确性上平均比最强评估融合或令牌压缩基线高出约2.9分。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the need to combine language-aligned and self-supervised visual encoders so that local features are enriched while semantically related patches remain distinguishable. It proposes PAIQ, a patch-aligned semantic injection framework that uses DINOv3 patch features as a spatial base, aggregates complementary SigLIP features via content-based cross-encoder matching and joint source allocation, and injects the aggregate-base differences through a shared orthogonal transformation Q that preserves residual norms and pairwise angles; only projection and fusion parameters are trained while both encoders and the language model stay frozen, and fusion retains 196 visual tokens. The authors derive conditions for patch separability and show rotation adds a nonnegative separation term over direct interpolation when the aggregate is shared. Experiments across language backbones show gains in judge-assessed correctness and reduced hallucination severity over single-encoder interfaces on image description and visual question answering, with the compact interface outperforming the strongest fusion or token-compression baselines by about 2.9 correctness points on average on 2B and 9B Qwen backbones.
该论文针对如何结合语言对齐编码器与自监督视觉编码器的问题，目标是在丰富局部特征的同时保持语义相近图像块之间的可区分性。PAIQ 以 DINOv3 图像块特征为空间基础，通过基于内容的跨编码器匹配与联合来源分配聚合互补的 SigLIP 特征，并借助共享正交变换 Q 注入聚合特征与基础特征之间的差异，该旋转在保留残差范数与成对夹角的同时调整更新方向；训练时仅更新投影与融合参数，两个视觉编码器和语言模型均保持冻结，融合后保留 196 个视觉 token。作者推导了相似语义聚合下图像块可分离性的条件，并证明在聚合特征共享时旋转相较直接插值带来非负的分离增益。在多种语言主干上，PAIQ 在图像描述与视觉问答任务中提升了评判正确率并降低了幻觉严重程度；在 2B 与 9B Qwen 主干上，该紧凑接口平均比最强的融合或 token 压缩基线高出约 2.9 个正确率点。
Beyond Single-Attribute Auditing: A Configurable Multi-Attribute Benchmark for Intersectional Fairness in LLMs
Authors: Zheng Zhang, Yifan Wu, Yuxiang Zhou, Qi Liu, Qingyang Mao, Ning Li, Weibo Gao, Qiyuan Shi, Zhenya Huang, Baosheng Yu
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-10-26T09:14:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:14:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated for fairness, yet most existing benchmarks audit one sensitive attribute at a time. This single-attribute paradigm can miss intersectional harms, where multiple attributes interact non-additively and produce failures that are invisible under marginal evaluation. We introduce ComFair, a configurable benchmark that converts existing fairness data into 479,066 controlled Dual- and Multi-attribute instances and pairs every augmented instance with its task-equivalent source. Across Recognition, Selection, Continuation, and Conversation, this pairing supports a unified audit of performance or bias-score shifts, how often added attributes introduce new errors or mask existing ones, and model-ranking stability. Experiments on ten LLMs show task- and subgroup-dependent sensitivity: discriminative tasks expose both transition directions even when aggregate changes are modest, generative tasks exhibit larger and directionally asymmetric shifts, and rankings vary with attribute composition. ComFair therefore provides a scalable controlled sensitivity audit---rather than a model of the natural joint distribution of identities---for testing the robustness of fairness-benchmark conclusions beyond single-attribute settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越单属性审计：面向LLM交叉公平性的可配置多属性基准
大型语言模型（LLM）的公平性评估日益增多，但现有基准大多一次只审计一个敏感属性。这种单属性范式可能遗漏交叉性伤害，即多个属性以非可加方式相互作用，产生在边际评估下不可见的失败。我们提出ComFair，一个可配置基准，将现有公平性数据转换为479,066个受控的双属性和多属性实例，并将每个增强实例与其任务等价的源实例配对。在识别、选择、续写和对话任务中，这种配对支持统一审计性能或偏差分数的变化、新增属性引入新错误或掩盖已有错误的频率，以及模型排名的稳定性。在十个LLM上的实验显示出任务和子群依赖的敏感性：判别式任务即使在总体变化不大时也会暴露两个方向的转变，生成式任务表现出更大且方向不对称的变化，排名随属性组成而变化。因此，ComFair提供了一个可扩展的受控敏感性审计——而非身份自然联合分布的模型——用于在单属性设置之外检验公平性基准结论的稳健性。
Summary / 总结
Existing fairness benchmarks for large language models typically audit one sensitive attribute at a time, which can miss intersectional harms arising from non-additive interactions among attributes. The authors introduce ComFair, a configurable benchmark that converts existing fairness data into 479,066 controlled dual- and multi-attribute instances, each paired with its task-equivalent source, enabling unified audits of performance and bias-score shifts, new or masked errors from added attributes, and model-ranking stability across Recognition, Selection, Continuation, and Conversation tasks. Experiments on ten LLMs show task- and subgroup-dependent sensitivity: discriminative tasks exhibit both transition directions even when aggregate changes are modest, generative tasks show larger and directionally asymmetric shifts, and rankings vary with attribute composition, supporting a scalable controlled sensitivity audit rather than a model of natural joint identity distributions.
现有大语言模型公平性基准通常一次只审计一个敏感属性，因而可能遗漏由多属性非加性交互产生的交叉性伤害。作者提出 ComFair，一个可配置基准，将现有公平性数据转换为 479,066 个受控的双属性和多属性实例，并为每个增强实例配对其任务等价的源实例，从而在识别、选择、续写和对话任务上统一审计性能或偏差分数的变化、新增属性引入新错误或掩盖已有错误的情况以及模型排名的稳定性。对十个大模型的实验显示，敏感性因任务和子群体而异：判别式任务即使在总体变化不大时也会出现双向转变，生成式任务表现出更大且方向不对称的变化，模型排名随属性组合而变化，表明 ComFair 是一种可扩展的受控敏感性审计工具，而非对身份自然联合分布的建模。
Reinforcement Learning Done Right for Explicit Confidence Targets
Authors: Zefeng He, Yichen He, Yuan Lin, Yang Yang, Yu Cheng
First: 2026-10-26T09:05:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:05:29+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can produce plausible but incorrect answers when they are uncertain, which can mislead users and undermine model reliability. Training models to abstain with reinforcement learning is a natural approach, but most existing methods use a fixed error penalty and therefore cannot adapt their behavior to different confidence requirements at inference time. We study \emph{explicit confidence targets}, where a user specifies a target \(p\) in the prompt and the model directly chooses whether to answer or abstain, with the goal of achieving an accuracy of at least \(p\) among answered questions. However, learning to respond to different confidence targets with a single model has proven difficult, and prior attempts have failed to distinguish different targets. We show that this failure comes from two problems: the reward signal that distinguishes different values of $p$ is often lost during optimization, while the original error penalty grows without bound as $p$ approaches one. We propose RLEC (\textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning with \textbf{E}xplicit \textbf{C}onfidence Targets), which preserves the learning signal needed to distinguish confidence targets, introduces a stable reward family that keeps the desired answer condition unchanged, and uses reward scheduling to balance effective learning and training stability. Together, these designs enable the first successful training of a single LLM for explicit confidence target control. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and model scales show that RLEC largely preserves general capabilities and achieves competitive calibration performance. RLEC also supports fine-grained target control without an external decision system or threshold calibration after training. By making explicit confidence targets part of the trained policy, RLEC opens a new and more direct path toward behavioral calibration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向显式置信度目标的正确强化学习
大型语言模型（LLM）在不确定时可能产生看似合理但错误的答案，这会误导用户并削弱模型可靠性。通过强化学习训练模型弃答是一种自然的方法，但大多数现有方法使用固定的错误惩罚，因此无法在推理时适应不同的置信度要求。我们研究\emph{显式置信度目标}，即用户在提示中指定目标\(p\)，模型直接选择回答或弃答，目标是在已回答问题中达到至少\(p\)的准确率。然而，用单一模型学习响应不同置信度目标已被证明很困难，先前尝试未能区分不同目标。我们表明这一失败源于两个问题：区分不同$p$值的奖励信号在优化过程中常常丢失，而原始错误惩罚在$p$接近1时无界增长。我们提出RLEC（\textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning with \textbf{E}xplicit \textbf{C}onfidence Targets），它保留区分置信度目标所需的学习信号，引入保持期望答案条件不变的稳定奖励族，并使用奖励调度来平衡有效学习与训练稳定性。这些设计共同实现了首次成功训练单一LLM进行显式置信度目标控制。跨多样基准和模型规模的广泛实验表明，RLEC在很大程度上保留了通用能力，并实现了有竞争力的校准性能。RLEC还支持细粒度目标控制，无需外部决策系统或训练后的阈值校准。通过将显式置信度目标纳入训练策略，RLEC为行为校准开辟了一条新的、更直接的路径。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the problem that large language models can give plausible but incorrect answers when uncertain, and that existing reinforcement-learning abstention methods use a fixed error penalty that cannot adapt to different confidence requirements at inference time. The authors study explicit confidence targets, where a user specifies a target p in the prompt and the model decides whether to answer or abstain so that accuracy among answered questions is at least p, and they identify two causes of prior failure: the reward signal distinguishing different p values is often lost during optimization, and the original error penalty grows without bound as p approaches one. They propose RLEC, which preserves the learning signal for distinguishing confidence targets, introduces a stable reward family that keeps the desired answer condition unchanged, and uses reward scheduling to balance learning and stability. Experiments across diverse benchmarks and model scales show that RLEC largely preserves general capabilities, achieves competitive calibration, and supports fine-grained target control without an external decision system or post-training threshold calibration.
该论文针对大语言模型在不确定时可能给出看似合理但错误的回答这一问题，指出现有基于强化学习的弃答方法使用固定错误惩罚，无法在推理时适应不同的置信度要求。作者研究显式置信目标设定，即用户在提示中指定目标 p，模型直接决定回答或弃答，使已回答问题的准确率至少达到 p，并指出以往方法失败的两个原因：区分不同 p 值的奖励信号在优化过程中常被丢失，且原始错误惩罚在 p 接近 1 时无界增长。为此他们提出 RLEC，保留区分置信目标所需的学习信号，引入保持期望回答条件不变的稳定奖励族，并使用奖励调度平衡有效学习与训练稳定性。跨多种基准和模型规模的实验表明，RLEC 基本保持通用能力，取得有竞争力的校准性能，并支持细粒度目标控制，无需外部决策系统或训练后的阈值校准。
LOCUS: Locate Once, Check Until Settled. Draft-and-Verify Visual Grounding with a Box Head and Coordinate-Token Verification
Authors: Muhammad Talha
First: 2026-10-26T09:01:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T09:01:28+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 7 figures, 10 tables. Project page: https://muhammad-talha-ad.github.io/LOCUS/ Code: https://github.com/muhammad-talha-ad/LOCUS Models: https://huggingface.co/MTalha2001/LOCUS-2B and https://huggingface.co/MTalha2001/LOCUS-0.8B
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) ground a referring expression in one of two ways. They decode the box as coordinate tokens, which is precise but takes one sequential step per token, or they regress it from a hidden state with a small head, which takes one step but loses accuracy at high IoU. The two fail differently: a head usually finds the right object but places its edges loosely, while coordinate tokens place edges precisely. We therefore use the regression head as a speculative drafter for the model's own coordinate tokens. Locus writes the head's box as coordinate tokens and checks them with the same backbone by Jacobi iteration over a shared image prefix. The fixed point is the model's greedy coordinate-token answer, reached in about 5.60 sequential passes on average instead of 18.46 decoding steps; head and verifier are one set of weights, trained jointly. On the 8 RefCOCO, RefCOCO+ and RefCOCOg splits, verification raises Locus-2B's Acc@0.9 from 54.87 for its own head to 59.13, and the one-pass head is ahead at Acc@0.5 of every published model with at most 3B parameters except Florence-2-L. Against Jag, the strongest open regression-head model, at its own size and training budget, Locus ties at Acc@0.5 and, with typical acceptance, gains +4.52 points at Acc@0.9, with paired significance tests. Exact answers come 1.5x faster than vLLM greedy-decoding the same coordinate tokens with the same weights; a pure regression head remains faster still.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LOCUS：一次定位，持续校验。基于框头与坐标Token验证的草稿-验证视觉定位
视觉语言模型（VLM）以两种方式之一将指代表达定位：将框解码为坐标Token，精确但每个Token需要一个顺序步骤；或用小型头从隐藏状态回归，一步完成但在高IoU下损失精度。两者失败方式不同：头通常找到正确物体但边缘放置松散，而坐标Token精确放置边缘。因此我们将回归头用作模型自身坐标Token的推测性草稿器。Locus将头的框写为坐标Token，并通过在共享图像前缀上的Jacobi迭代用同一骨干网络校验它们。不动点是模型的贪心坐标Token答案，平均约5.60次顺序传递达到，而非18.46个解码步骤；头和验证器是一组权重，联合训练。在8个RefCOCO、RefCOCO+和RefCOCOg划分上，验证将Locus-2B的Acc@0.9从其自身头的54.87提升至59.13，且单次传递头在Acc@0.5上领先所有已发表的至多3B参数的模型（除Florence-2-L外）。与Jag——最强的开放回归头模型——在其自身规模和训练预算下相比，Locus在Acc@0.5上持平，并在典型接受下于Acc@0.9上获得+4.52分，配有配对显著性检验。精确答案比用相同权重对相同坐标Token进行vLLM贪心解码快1.5倍；纯回归头仍然更快。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models typically ground referring expressions either by decoding coordinate tokens, which is precise but slow, or by regressing a box from a hidden state with a small head, which is fast but loses accuracy at high IoU. LOCUS addresses this trade-off by using the regression head as a speculative drafter for the model's own coordinate tokens: it writes the head's box as coordinate tokens and verifies them with the same backbone via Jacobi iteration over a shared image prefix, with head and verifier trained jointly as one set of weights. On the eight RefCOCO, RefCOCO+ and RefCOCOg splits, verification raises Locus-2B's Acc@0.9 from 54.87 for its own head to 59.13, and the one-pass head exceeds Acc@0.5 of every published model with at most 3B parameters except Florence-2-L; against Jag at its own size and training budget, LOCUS ties at Acc@0.5 and gains +4.52 points at Acc@0.9 with typical acceptance, while exact answers arrive 1.5x faster than vLLM greedy decoding of the same coordinate tokens.
视觉语言模型定位指代表达通常有两种方式：解码坐标 token 精确但每个 token 需一步顺序解码，或用小头从隐藏状态回归边界框，速度快但在高 IoU 下精度下降。LOCUS 将回归头用作模型自身坐标 token 的推测式草稿器：把头的框写成坐标 token，并在共享图像前缀上通过 Jacobi 迭代用同一骨干网络进行校验，头与校验器为同一组权重联合训练。在 RefCOCO、RefCOCO+ 和 RefCOCOg 的八个划分上，校验将 Locus-2B 的 Acc@0.9 从自身头的 54.87 提升到 59.13，单次前向的头在 Acc@0.5 上超过除 Florence-2-L 外所有参数量不超过 3B 的已发表模型；在同等规模和训练预算下与 Jag 相比，LOCUS 在 Acc@0.5 上持平，并在典型接受率下于 Acc@0.9 上提升 4.52 个点，且精确答案的生成速度比用相同权重经 vLLM 贪心解码相同坐标 token 快 1.5 倍。
DriveUniTok: A Unified Multimodal Tokenizer for Driving World-Action Models
Authors: Jingbo Xu, Hanchao Leng, JiaHui Huang, Hanbing Li, Jingwei Zhao, Kuiyuan Yang, Chen Gao, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Yong Li
First: 2026-10-26T08:55:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:55:30+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 16 figures, 8 tables, including appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models are emerging as a fundamental component of policy learning for real-world agents, enabling agents to predict future world states and plan actions within a learned representation of the dynamic environment. Existing approaches, however, often model appearance, metric geometry represented by projected LiDAR depth, and ego motion either partially or independently, overlooking the fact that these modalities are different projections of a shared underlying world state. This separation can lead to information loss and cross-modal inconsistency, ultimately limiting downstream performance. Motivated by this unified view of the world, we propose DriveUniTok, a unified multimodal tokenizer for driving world-action models (WAMs) that encodes these modalities in a single decodable continuous latent. We freeze DriveUniTok and adapt a pretrained diffusion model, DriveUniWAM, to predict future unified latents; the tokenizer then decodes them into future appearance, projected LiDAR depth, and ego trajectories. Experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that our unified design achieves better future prediction and planning performance at lower downstream computational cost than separate-stream alternatives. With reinforcement learning post-training, DriveUniWAM achieves a PDMS of 91.54, reaching state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated WAM-based planners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DriveUniTok：面向驾驶世界-动作模型的统一多模态分词器
世界模型正逐渐成为真实世界智能体策略学习的基础组件，使智能体能够在动态环境的学习表示中预测未来世界状态并规划动作。然而，现有方法通常部分或独立地对表观、由投影LiDAR深度表示的度量几何以及自车运动进行建模，忽视了这些模态是共享底层世界状态的不同投影这一事实。这种分离可能导致信息损失和跨模态不一致，最终限制下游性能。受这种统一世界视角的启发，我们提出DriveUniTok，一种面向驾驶世界-动作模型（WAM）的统一多模态分词器，将这些模态编码为单一可解码的连续潜变量。我们冻结DriveUniTok，并适配预训练扩散模型DriveUniWAM来预测未来统一潜变量；随后分词器将其解码为未来表观、投影LiDAR深度和自车轨迹。在NAVSIM上的实验表明，与分离流替代方案相比，我们的统一设计以更低的下游计算成本实现了更好的未来预测和规划性能。经过强化学习后训练，DriveUniWAM达到91.54的PDMS，在被评估的基于WAM的规划器中达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Existing driving world models typically handle appearance, projected LiDAR depth, and ego motion separately or only partially, even though these are different projections of a shared world state, which can cause information loss and cross-modal inconsistency that limit downstream performance. DriveUniTok addresses this by encoding all three modalities into a single decodable continuous latent, which is frozen and used with an adapted pretrained diffusion model, DriveUniWAM, that predicts future unified latents later decoded into future appearance, projected LiDAR depth, and ego trajectories. On NAVSIM, this unified design yields better future prediction and planning at lower downstream computational cost than separate-stream alternatives, and with reinforcement learning post-training DriveUniWAM reaches a PDMS of 91.54, reported as state-of-the-art among the evaluated WAM-based planners.
现有驾驶世界模型往往只部分建模或将外观、投影LiDAR深度与自车运动作为独立流处理，忽视了这些模态是同一世界状态的不同投影，容易造成信息损失和跨模态不一致。DriveUniTok将这三类模态编码进单一可解码的连续潜变量，冻结该分词器并适配预训练扩散模型DriveUniWAM来预测未来统一潜变量，再解码为未来外观、投影LiDAR深度和自车轨迹。在NAVSIM上的实验表明，该统一设计相比独立流方案在更低的下游计算成本下取得更好的未来预测与规划表现；经强化学习后训练，DriveUniWAM达到91.54的PDMS，在被评估的基于WAM的规划器中报告为最优性能。
Matching Matters: A Fair Quality-Efficiency Benchmark for Command-Line Agents
Authors: Han Chi, Jiaxin Qi, Yan Cui, Baisheng Lai, Jianqiang Huang
First: 2026-06-19T06:26:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:48:24+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures, 12 tables; includes supplementary material
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rapid advances in large language models have improved the task-solving capabilities of command-line-interface (CLI)-based agents, whose CLIs determine how models invoke tools, maintain interaction history, and recover from failures. Consequently, effective matching between CLIs and LLMs has become essential. However, existing agent benchmarks largely emphasize success rate while overlooking practical objectives such as cost and efficiency, as well as the selection of LM-CLI combinations, all of which are critical in real-world deployment. We therefore introduce AgentMeter, a quality-efficiency benchmark with a new metric, the AgentMeter Score (AMS), that jointly characterizes task quality, budget sensitivity, and resource-intensive zero-reward execution, enabling a more complete assessment of deployed LM-CLI pairs. Furthermore, collected task descriptions may inadvertently favor LM-CLI pairs that are particularly compatible with their wording and structure, causing evaluation results to reflect description-specific advantages rather than general task-solving capability. We therefore propose AgentMeter-Opt, a trajectory-grounded optimization framework that constructs pair-adapted, task-preserving description variants to build a fairer evaluation set across LM-CLI pairs. Extensive experiments show that no CLI is universally optimal across language models and that task success, execution cost, and AMS identify different competitive configurations. Results on AgentMeter-Opt further reveal that task-preserving description changes affect LM-CLI pairs unevenly and can alter their relative ordering across valid description conditions. Together, AgentMeter and AgentMeter-Opt provide a practical foundation for fair and deployment-relevant evaluation of command-line agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：匹配至关重要：面向命令行智能体的公平质量-效率基准
大语言模型的快速进展提升了基于命令行界面（CLI）的智能体的任务求解能力，其CLI决定了模型如何调用工具、维护交互历史以及从失败中恢复。因此，CLI与LLM之间的有效匹配变得至关重要。然而，现有智能体基准大多强调成功率，而忽视了成本与效率等实际目标，以及LM-CLI组合的选择，而这些在真实部署中都至关重要。为此，我们提出AgentMeter，一个质量-效率基准，并引入新指标AgentMeter Score（AMS），该指标联合刻画任务质量、预算敏感性和资源密集型的零奖励执行，从而能够对部署的LM-CLI组合进行更完整的评估。此外，收集的任务描述可能无意中偏向于与其措辞和结构特别兼容的LM-CLI组合，导致评估结果反映的是描述特定的优势而非通用任务求解能力。因此，我们提出AgentMeter-Opt，一个基于轨迹的优化框架，通过构建配对适配且保持任务的描述变体，在LM-CLI组合之间构建更公平的评估集。大量实验表明，没有哪个CLI在所有语言模型上普遍最优，且任务成功、执行成本和AMS识别出不同的竞争性配置。AgentMeter-Opt上的结果进一步揭示，保持任务的描述变化对LM-CLI组合的影响并不均衡，并可能改变它们在有效描述条件下的相对排序。AgentMeter与AgentMeter-Opt共同为命令行智能体的公平且与部署相关的评估提供了实用基础。
Summary / 总结
Existing command-line agent benchmarks emphasize task success while overlooking cost, efficiency, and the choice of language-model/CLI pairings that matter in deployment, and collected task descriptions may inadvertently favor pairs compatible with their wording. The authors introduce AgentMeter, a quality-efficiency benchmark with the AgentMeter Score (AMS) that jointly captures task quality, budget sensitivity, and resource-intensive zero-reward execution, plus AgentMeter-Opt, a trajectory-grounded optimization framework that builds pair-adapted, task-preserving description variants for fairer cross-pair evaluation. Extensive experiments show no CLI is universally optimal across language models, that task success, execution cost, and AMS identify different competitive configurations, and that task-preserving description changes affect LM-CLI pairs unevenly and can alter their relative ordering.
现有命令行智能体基准主要关注任务成功率，而忽视了实际部署中至关重要的成本、效率以及语言模型与CLI组合的选择问题，同时收集到的任务描述可能因措辞和结构而无意中偏向某些特定组合。为此，作者提出AgentMeter，这是一个质量-效率基准，并引入AgentMeter Score（AMS）指标，联合刻画任务质量、预算敏感性和资源消耗大却零奖励的执行情况；同时提出AgentMeter-Opt，一个基于轨迹的优化框架，通过构建适配特定组合且保持任务语义的描述变体，实现更公平的跨组合评估。大量实验表明，没有任何CLI在所有语言模型上普遍最优，成功率、执行成本和AMS识别出不同的竞争性配置，而保持任务语义的描述变化对不同LM-CLI组合的影响并不均衡，并可能改变它们之间的相对排序。
Finding Change in Satellite Archives from Text: How to Combine Before-and-After Images Efficiently
Authors: Simon Roy, Mark Bong, Giovanni Beltrame
First: 2026-07-30T17:35:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:48:15+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at the MACLEAN Workshop (ECML PKDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Operational Earth observation increasingly calls for answering queries such as ``find the image pairs where a new building appeared.'' This means searching an archive of before-and-after (bi-temporal) satellite image pairs and ranking each pair by how well it matches a natural-language description of the change. The component that performs this match, the fusion module that combines the ``before'' and ``after'' views, must be run at query time across many candidate pairs, so its speed largely sets the cost of every search. We present a controlled comparison of how to build that module. Using one fixed image encoder (a frozen CLIP model) and one training recipe for all variants, we evaluate eight designs drawn from three families: attention, state-space models (Mamba), and learned compression (our Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF). Each design is tested on two benchmarks (LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC) with ten random seeds, so the reported differences are statistically grounded. We outline three findings: first, a training-free two-stage search (a cheap difference model that shortlists candidates, followed by attention fusion that re-ranks them) matches or exceeds full-fusion recall on LEVIR-CC while cutting query cost $10$-$15\times$, with comparable R@1/R@5 on Dubai-CC; second, the linear-time scan of Mamba, attractive on paper, gives no speed benefit at the patch counts typical of vision transformers ($L{=}196$): the scan is limited by memory bandwidth, whereas attention maps cleanly onto parallel hardware; and third, compressing the fused representation (TBF) reduces parameters by $2.3\times$ and latency by $1.6\times$ for a change-only BLEU-1 cost of $0.007$, although more aggressive compression quietly discards change-relevant detail that aggregate metrics fail to reveal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从文本中查找卫星档案中的变化：如何高效地结合前后图像
业务化地球观测日益需要回答诸如“找出出现新建筑的图像对”之类的查询。这意味着要搜索前后（双时相）卫星图像对的档案，并根据每对图像与自然语言变化描述的匹配程度进行排序。执行这种匹配的组件——即结合“前”和“后”视图的融合模块——必须在查询时对许多候选图像对运行，因此其速度在很大程度上决定了每次搜索的成本。我们对如何构建该模块进行了受控比较。使用一个固定的图像编码器（冻结的CLIP模型）和一套适用于所有变体的训练方案，我们评估了来自三个家族的八种设计：注意力、状态空间模型（Mamba）和学习压缩（我们的时间瓶颈融合，TBF）。每种设计在两个基准（LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC）上以十个随机种子进行测试，因此报告的差异具有统计依据。我们概述了三个发现：第一，免训练的两阶段搜索（先用廉价的差异模型筛选候选，再用注意力融合重新排序）在LEVIR-CC上达到或超过全融合的召回率，同时将查询成本降低10-15倍，在Dubai-CC上R@1/R@5相当；第二，Mamba的线性时间扫描在理论上很有吸引力，但在视觉Transformer典型的patch数量（L=196）下没有速度优势：扫描受内存带宽限制，而注意力能清晰地映射到并行硬件上；第三，压缩融合表示（TBF）将参数减少2.3倍、延迟降低1.6倍，仅变化BLEU-1代价为0.007，尽管更激进的压缩会悄然丢弃聚合指标无法揭示的变化相关细节。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the cost of query-time fusion in searching bi-temporal satellite image archives by natural-language change descriptions, where the module combining before-and-after views largely determines search expense. Using a frozen CLIP encoder and one shared training recipe, the authors compare eight fusion designs from attention, state-space (Mamba), and learned compression families (their Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF) on LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC with ten random seeds. They find that a training-free two-stage search (cheap difference model for shortlisting, attention fusion for re-ranking) matches or exceeds full-fusion recall on LEVIR-CC while cutting query cost 10-15x, with comparable R@1/R@5 on Dubai-CC; that Mamba's linear-time scan yields no speed benefit at typical ViT patch counts (L=196) due to memory-bandwidth limits; and that TBF reduces parameters 2.3x and latency 1.6x at a change-only BLEU-1 cost of 0.007, though aggressive compression discards change-relevant detail not captured by aggregate metrics.
业务化对地观测需要在地表前后双时相卫星影像档案中检索与自然语言变化描述相匹配的影像对，而融合前后两个视图的模块需在查询时对大量候选对运行，其速度在很大程度上决定了每次搜索的成本。作者对来自注意力、Mamba状态空间模型和学习压缩（其提出的时序瓶颈融合TBF）三个家族的八种融合设计进行了受控比较，统一使用冻结的CLIP编码器和同一训练方案，在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC两个基准上以十个随机种子进行评估。结果有三点：无需训练的两阶段搜索（先用廉价差异模型筛选候选，再用注意力融合重排序）在LEVIR-CC上达到或超过全融合的召回率，同时将查询成本降低10至15倍，在Dubai-CC上R@1/R@5相当；Mamba的线性时间扫描在视觉Transformer典型patch数（L=196）下并未带来速度优势，因为其受内存带宽限制，而注意力能良好映射到并行硬件；TBF压缩将参数量减少2.3倍、延迟降低1.6倍，仅以0.007的变化专用BLEU-1为代价，但更激进的压缩会悄然丢弃聚合指标无法揭示的变化相关细节。
PatentAgentBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents with Professional Patent Task Suites
Authors: Yijie Hu, Wei Li, Junjie Zhou, Zhiqi Jiang, Yi Jin, Licong Xu, Xinkai Sun, Kai Duan, Lisha Zhang
First: 2026-10-26T08:44:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:44:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Patent practice lies at the intersection of technical, legal, and linguistic work, making it challenging yet crucial to technological innovation. Recently, rapid advances in large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems have demonstrated the potential to complete realistic business workflows and even patent works. This raises a question: can current AI systems perform patent work reliably? Existing benchmarks cover a limited range of patent tasks and simplify evidence acquisition by providing reference materials or candidate documents. In this paper, we introduce PatentAgentBench, a benchmark comprising 510 instances across eight task suites: novelty search, freedom-to-operate (FTO) search, design FTO, invalidity search, claim charting, patent drafting, office-action response, and patent translation. Curated from real patent records and professional materials, the benchmark combines task-specific metrics with expert-reviewed annotations and rubrics. Retrieval tasks such novelty search, FTO, etc., require agents to search and compare patent collections without a supplied candidate pool. The drafting and OAR tasks assess the quality of agents' work given technical disclosure and office action notice. We evaluate seven LLMs across four agent harnesses on PatentAgentBench, finding that current systems still struggle to identify novelty, assess risk, and understand practitioner intent throughout patent workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PatentAgentBench：以专业专利任务套件对LLM智能体进行基准测试
专利实务处于技术、法律和语言工作的交叉点，使其对技术创新既具挑战性又至关重要。近来，大语言模型（LLM）和智能体系统的快速进展已展现出完成真实业务工作流乃至专利工作的潜力。这引出一个问题：当前AI系统能否可靠地执行专利工作？现有基准覆盖的专利任务范围有限，并通过提供参考资料或候选文档来简化证据获取。本文提出PatentAgentBench，一个包含八个任务套件、共510个实例的基准：新颖性检索、自由实施（FTO）检索、设计FTO、无效检索、权利要求对照表、专利撰写、审查意见答复和专利翻译。该基准从真实专利记录和专业材料中整理而成，结合任务特定指标与专家评审的标注和评分标准。新颖性检索、FTO等检索任务要求智能体在未提供候选池的情况下检索并比较专利集合。撰写和审查意见答复任务评估智能体在给定技术披露和审查意见通知时的作品质量。我们在PatentAgentBench上评估了七种LLM在四种智能体框架下的表现，发现当前系统在整个专利工作流中仍难以识别新颖性、评估风险并理解从业者意图。

专利实践融合技术、法律与语言工作，大语言模型与智能体系统虽已展现出完成真实业务流程乃至专利工作的潜力，但现有基准覆盖的专利任务范围有限，且通过提供参考资料或候选文档简化了证据获取，因此当前AI系统能否可靠完成专利工作仍不明确。作者提出PatentAgentBench，该基准包含八个任务套件共510个实例，涵盖新颖性检索、自由实施检索、外观设计自由实施、无效检索、权利要求对照表、专利撰写、审查意见答复和专利翻译，数据取自真实专利记录与专业材料，采用任务特定指标并结合专家审核的标注与评分细则；其中检索类任务要求智能体在无候选池的情况下检索并比对专利集合，撰写与审查意见答复任务则评估智能体在给定技术披露书或审查意见通知书时的产出质量。作者在四种智能体框架下评测了七个大语言模型，发现当前系统在专利工作流程中仍难以识别新颖性、评估风险并理解从业者意图。
LUMA: Benchmarking Segmentation via a Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter
Authors: Tobias Christian Nauen, Anosh Billimoria, Federico Raue, Stanislav Frolov, Brian B. Moser, Andreas Dengel
Venue: WACV 2027
First: 2026-07-01T09:35:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:40:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted at WACV 2027. v2: Camera-ready version with supplementary material included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Comparing transformer backbones for image segmentation is confounded: each is paired with a different decoder, recipe, and pretraining, so reported differences rarely reflect the backbone itself. We introduce the Lightweight Universal Mask Adapter (LUMA), a lightweight, backbone-agnostic mask-transformer head that treats any backbone as a black-box feature extractor, letting a set of queries read from its features through cheap cross-attention. LUMA matches the accuracy of EoMT, the state-of-the-art efficient ViT-segmenter, at cost parity, while attaching unchanged to hierarchical, convolutional, and mixture-of-experts backbones alike. Holding this head fixed, we benchmark 20 backbones, 11 pretraining schemes and a range of resolutions on ADE20K, COCO, and Cityscapes under one modern recipe. We find that ``efficient'' token mixers fail to deliver efficiency even at the high resolutions that motivate them, with plain ViT holding the throughput Pareto front at every resolution. Additionally, the pretraining objective, not the architecture, the lever the field has tuned hardest, governs segmentation quality. Code: https://github.com/tobna/LUMA
中文标题/摘要
标题：LUMA：通过轻量级通用掩码适配器进行分割基准测试
比较用于图像分割的Transformer骨干网络存在混杂因素：每个骨干网络都搭配不同的解码器、训练配方和预训练方式，因此所报告的差异很少反映骨干网络本身。我们引入轻量级通用掩码适配器（LUMA），这是一种轻量级、与骨干网络无关的掩码Transformer头，它将任意骨干网络视为黑盒特征提取器，让一组查询通过廉价的交叉注意力从其特征中读取信息。LUMA在成本相当的情况下达到最先进高效ViT分割器EoMT的精度，同时可原样附加到分层、卷积和混合专家骨干网络上。在固定该头的情况下，我们在同一现代配方下对ADE20K、COCO和Cityscapes上的20个骨干网络、11种预训练方案以及一系列分辨率进行基准测试。我们发现，“高效”的token混合器即使在促使其出现的高分辨率下也未能实现高效，而普通ViT在每个分辨率下都保持吞吐量帕累托前沿。此外，决定分割质量的是预训练目标而非架构，而后者是该领域调整力度最大的杠杆。 代码：https://github.com/tobna/LUMA
