Comparing transformer backbones for image segmentation is confounded because each backbone is typically paired with a different decoder, recipe, and pretraining, so reported differences rarely reflect the backbone itself. To address this, the authors introduce LUMA, a lightweight, backbone-agnostic mask-transformer head that treats any backbone as a black-box feature extractor and lets a set of queries read from its features through cheap cross-attention. LUMA matches the accuracy of EoMT, the state-of-the-art efficient ViT segmenter, at cost parity, and attaches unchanged to hierarchical, convolutional, and mixture-of-experts backbones. Holding this head fixed, the authors benchmark 20 backbones, 11 pretraining schemes, and a range of resolutions on ADE20K, COCO, and Cityscapes under one modern recipe, finding that so-called efficient token mixers fail to deliver efficiency even at the high resolutions that motivate them, with plain ViT holding the throughput Pareto front at every resolution, and that the pretraining objective, rather than architecture, governs segmentation quality.
图像分割中比较不同Transformer骨干网络时存在混杂因素：每个骨干通常搭配不同的解码器、训练配方和预训练方式，因此报告的差异很少能反映骨干本身。为解决这一问题，作者提出LUMA，一种轻量、与骨干无关的掩码Transformer头，将任意骨干视为黑盒特征提取器，让一组查询通过低成本交叉注意力读取其特征。LUMA在同等成本下达到最先进高效ViT分割器EoMT的精度，并可原样接入分层、卷积和混合专家骨干。在固定该头的前提下，作者在ADE20K、COCO和Cityscapes上以统一现代配方对20个骨干、11种预训练方案及多种分辨率进行了基准测试。结果表明，所谓高效token混合器即使在促使其提出的高分辨率下也未能实现高效，普通ViT在所有分辨率下都占据吞吐量帕累托前沿；此外，决定分割质量的是预训练目标而非架构。
BellowsKV: One Latent for Many Layers, Expanded on Demand
Authors: Jiamu Zhang, Hanjie Chen
First: 2026-10-26T08:35:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:35:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) now serve as the backbone of agents that reason over many turns, call tools, and pursue long-horizon tasks. To avoid recomputation, a model stores the attention keys and values of all past tokens in a key-value (KV) cache. Each layer maintains its own cache, so KV-cache memory can become the main bottleneck for GPU serving. Most compression methods operate within a single layer, by quantizing the cache entries, reducing the channel dimension, or evicting less important tokens. All three lose substantial accuracy at high compression ratios. Cross-layer methods share one compressed cache across adjacent layers to go beyond this limit. However, these methods also degrade sharply at high compression ratios. We find that the KV-cache representations of adjacent layers are quite different, whereas the input hidden states of adjacent layers are highly similar. We also find that, under the same memory budget, reducing the dimension of this shared latent loses far more accuracy than quantizing it. Based on these findings, we propose BellowsKV, a training-free method that maps the hidden states of n adjacent layers to one shared latent per token. At inference time, each layer reads its own keys and values from this latent through a layer-specific projection. We keep the latent at full dimension and compress it in two other ways: BellowsKV-Quant stores the latent at low precision, which is nearly lossless because the latent has no dominant outlier channels; BellowsKV-Evict makes one shared eviction decision per block, because the layers rank token importance very similarly. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B and Qwen2.5-7B, BellowsKV-Quant retains about 99% of full-cache accuracy at 3.6x compression and 97-98% at 13-14x, where the strongest cross-layer method retains at most 84% at 16x. Code: https://github.com/MorrisZJ/BellowsKV
中文标题/摘要
标题：BellowsKV：一个潜变量服务多层，按需展开
大语言模型（LLM）如今已成为智能体的骨干，这些智能体需要进行多轮推理、调用工具并执行长程任务。为避免重复计算，模型将所有历史 token 的注意力键和值存储在键值（KV）缓存中。每一层都维护自己的缓存，因此 KV 缓存内存可能成为 GPU 服务的主要瓶颈。大多数压缩方法在单层内操作，通过量化缓存条目、缩减通道维度或逐出较不重要的 token 来实现。这三种方法在高压缩比下都会损失大量精度。跨层方法在相邻层之间共享一个压缩缓存，以突破这一限制。然而，这些方法在高压缩比下也会急剧退化。我们发现相邻层的 KV 缓存表示差异很大，而相邻层的输入隐藏状态高度相似。我们还发现，在相同内存预算下，缩减该共享潜变量的维度比量化它损失精度大得多。基于这些发现，我们提出 BellowsKV，一种免训练方法，将 n 个相邻层的隐藏状态映射为每个 token 的一个共享潜变量。在推理时，每一层通过层特定的投影从该潜变量中读取自己的键和值。我们保持潜变量为全维度，并以另外两种方式压缩它：BellowsKV-Quant 以低精度存储潜变量，这几乎无损，因为潜变量没有主导的离群通道；BellowsKV-Evict 为每个块做出一个共享的逐出决策，因为各层对 token 重要性的排序非常相似。在 Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上，BellowsKV-Quant 在 3.6 倍压缩下保留了约 99% 的全缓存精度，在 13-14 倍压缩下保留了 97-98%，而最强的跨层方法在 16 倍压缩下最多保留 84%。代码：https://github.com/MorrisZJ/BellowsKV
Summary / 总结
Cross-layer KV-cache compression is needed to relieve GPU memory pressure in long-horizon LLM serving, but existing methods degrade sharply at high compression ratios. The authors observe that adjacent layers have quite different KV-cache representations while their input hidden states are highly similar, and that under a fixed memory budget reducing latent dimension costs far more accuracy than quantizing it. They therefore propose BellowsKV, a training-free method that maps the hidden states of n adjacent layers to one shared full-dimension latent per token, from which each layer reads its own keys and values via a layer-specific projection; BellowsKV-Quant stores this latent at low precision, and BellowsKV-Evict makes one shared eviction decision per block. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B and Qwen2.5-7B, BellowsKV-Quant retains about 99% of full-cache accuracy at 3.6x compression and 97-98% at 13-14x, whereas the strongest cross-layer baseline retains at most 84% at 16x.
为缓解长程LLM服务中的GPU显存压力，需要跨层KV缓存压缩，但现有方法在高压缩比下精度急剧下降，原因是相邻层的KV表示差异较大，而其输入隐藏状态高度相似。BellowsKV是一种免训练方法，将n个相邻层的隐藏状态映射为每个token一个共享的全维潜变量，推理时每层通过层特定投影从该潜变量读取自身的键和值；压缩通过两种方式进行：以低精度存储潜变量（BellowsKV-Quant），或对每个块做一次共享的逐出决策（BellowsKV-Evict），因为该潜变量没有占主导的离群通道，且各层对token重要性的排序高度一致。在Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B和Qwen2.5-7B上，BellowsKV-Quant在3.6倍压缩下保留约99%的全缓存精度，在13-14倍压缩下保留97-98%，而最强的跨层基线在16倍压缩下最多保留84%。
When Pointing Becomes Profiling: Social Bias and Refusal in Text-Steerable Visual Encoders
Authors: Seyedarmin Azizi, Parsa Razmara, Sajjad Ghiasvand, Ardalan Aryashad, Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh
First: 2026-10-26T08:29:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:29:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-steerable visual encoders condition a frozen Vision Transformer on a natural-language prompt and point at the matching image region, producing prompt-conditioned heatmaps and features. We audit the released SteerViT model and find that its steering interface turns language-driven pointing into demographic profiling. On counterfactual face mosaics and real multi-person photographs, "the person" selects a man more often than the base rate, crime words select Black people more often than occupation words do, "the man" among four women selects the Black woman 75.6% of the time on SocialCounterfactuals, and "the monkey" selects Black people. None of them is in the backbone's unsteered saliency, and the crime association is not in the frozen text encoder; the race effects grow with steering strength and enter through the last two cross-attention layers: giving the prompt to those layers alone reproduces them. Four other grounding models, CLIPSeg, Grounding-DINO, SAM 3, and Qwen2.5-VL, show no crime routing and weaker race routing. The model cannot refuse. Its patch softmax must place its mass somewhere, and its heatmap separates a groundable request ("the police officer") from one that is absent ("the pizza") or that would require a stereotyped demographic inference ("the criminal") only at chance (AUROC 0.52). We introduce SafeSteer, which adds a learned abstention output and counterfactual-invariance training on the same 23M steering parameters. SafeSteer separates should-point from should-abstain prompts at AUROC 0.997, generalizes to held-out sensitive and absent words (0.998; at a 0.5 threshold it refuses 99.8% of trained and 62% of held-out sensitive words) with no over-refusal of visible people on the benchmark, and removes most race routing while keeping localization and retrieval within 1 point of the released model. Code, benchmarks, and the curated mosaic sets will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当指向变成画像：文本可操控视觉编码器中的社会偏见与拒绝
文本可操控视觉编码器以自然语言提示为条件，对冻结的视觉Transformer进行条件化，并指向匹配的图像区域，生成以提示为条件的热力图和特征。我们审计了已发布的SteerViT模型，发现其操控接口将语言驱动的指向转变为人口统计画像。在反事实人脸拼图和真实多人照片上，“the person”选择男性的频率高于基准率，犯罪词汇比职业词汇更常选择黑人，在SocialCounterfactuals上，四名女性中的“the man”有75.6%的时间选择黑人女性，而“the monkey”会选择黑人。这些均不在骨干网络未操控的显著性中，犯罪关联也不在冻结的文本编码器中；种族效应随操控强度增强，并通过最后两个交叉注意力层进入：仅将提示给予这些层即可复现这些效应。另外四个定位模型CLIPSeg、Grounding-DINO、SAM 3和Qwen2.5-VL未显示犯罪路由，种族路由也较弱。该模型无法拒绝。其patch softmax必须将质量放在某处，而其热力图仅以随机水平（AUROC 0.52）区分可定位请求（“the police officer”）与不存在的请求（“the pizza”）或需要刻板人口统计推断的请求（“the criminal”）。我们提出SafeSteer，在相同的23M操控参数上添加学习到的弃权输出和反事实不变性训练。SafeSteer以AUROC 0.997区分应指向与应弃权的提示，泛化到留出的敏感词和缺失词（0.998；在0.5阈值下，它拒绝99.8%的训练敏感词和62%的留出敏感词），在基准上对可见人物没有过度拒绝，并消除了大部分种族路由，同时将定位和检索保持在已发布模型1个百分点以内。代码、基准和整理好的拼图集将发布。
Why Are LLMs Confidently Wrong? Correcting Overconfident Errors via Causal Head Intervention
Authors: Jing Ren, Bowen Li, Ziqi Xu, Xuechao Yang, Feng Xia
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-10-26T08:05:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:05:29+00:00
Comments: The paper has been accepted by NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern large language models (LLMs) often exhibit overconfident errors, assigning high confidence to incorrect predictions and thereby undermining reliability in real-world use. Existing calibration methods typically rely on post-hoc adjustment or retraining, operating at the output level without addressing the underlying causes of miscalibration. We propose CoCHI, (Class-oriented Causal Head Intervention), a framework that improves calibration by identifying and intervening on internal components responsible for distinct confidence behaviours. By analysing predictions through correctness-confidence patterns, CoCHI enables targeted intervention on model internals, reducing overconfident errors while preserving correct predictions. Our approach provides a mechanistic perspective on calibration, linking reliability to identifiable structures within the model rather than treating it as an output-level issue. Experiments across multiple LLMs show consistent improvements in calibration metrics, including Expected Calibration Error, Negative Log-Likelihood, and Brier Score, while maintaining accuracy on five multiple-choice question answering benchmarks. These results suggest that effective calibration requires not only adjusting outputs, but also addressing the underlying causal mechanisms within the model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为什么LLM会自信地出错？通过因果头干预纠正过度自信的错误
现代大语言模型（LLM）常表现出过度自信的错误，对错误预测赋予高置信度，从而削弱了实际应用中的可靠性。现有校准方法通常依赖事后调整或重新训练，在输出层面操作，未解决校准失准的根本原因。我们提出CoCHI（面向类别的因果头干预），该框架通过识别并干预负责不同置信行为的内部组件来改进校准。通过基于正确性-置信度模式分析预测，CoCHI能够对模型内部进行针对性干预，在保留正确预测的同时减少过度自信的错误。我们的方法提供了校准的机制性视角，将可靠性关联到模型内可识别的结构，而非将其视为输出层面的问题。在多个LLM上的实验显示，校准指标（包括期望校准误差、负对数似然和Brier分数）持续改善，同时在五个多项选择问答基准上保持准确率。这些结果表明，有效的校准不仅需要调整输出，还需要处理模型内部的潜在因果机制。
Summary / 总结
Large language models frequently make overconfident errors, assigning high confidence to incorrect predictions, and existing calibration methods address this only at the output level through post-hoc adjustment or retraining without tackling underlying causes. The paper proposes CoCHI (Class-oriented Causal Head Intervention), a framework that analyzes predictions through correctness-confidence patterns to identify internal components responsible for distinct confidence behaviors and then applies targeted interventions on model internals, reducing overconfident errors while preserving correct predictions. Experiments across multiple LLMs on five multiple-choice question answering benchmarks show consistent improvements in Expected Calibration Error, Negative Log-Likelihood, and Brier Score while maintaining accuracy, supporting a mechanistic view of calibration as a matter of internal causal structure rather than output-level adjustment.
大语言模型常出现过度自信的错误，即对错误预测赋予高置信度，从而损害实际应用中的可靠性，而现有校准方法多依赖输出层面的后处理调整或重训练，未触及误校准的根本原因。作者提出CoCHI（面向类别的因果头干预）框架，通过正确性—置信度模式分析预测，识别导致不同置信行为的内部组件，并对模型内部实施有针对性的因果干预，在减少过度自信错误的同时保留正确预测。在多个大语言模型和五个多项选择问答基准上的实验表明，该方法在保持准确率的同时，持续改善了期望校准误差、负对数似然和Brier分数等校准指标，说明有效校准需要处理模型内部的因果机制而非仅调整输出。
Reason or Recite: Complementary Specialists with an LLM Judge for Traffic Anomaly Reasoning
Authors: Mayur Kulkarni
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-10-26T08:05:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T08:05:21+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Accepted at the AI City Challenge Workshop, ECCV 2026. 1st place in Track 3 (Traffic Anomaly Reasoning)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Road anomalies such as collisions, violations, and sudden hazards demand more from video understanding than detection: a practical system must verify what happened, describe the scene, and explain the cause. We find these competences pull in opposite directions for vision-language models: closed-form questions reward precise, evidence-grounded reasoning, while open-ended answers are scored by similarity to reference text, where matching the annotation style matters as much as reasoning correctly. We present Reason-or-Recite (RoR), a two-specialist framework for the Traffic Anomaly Reasoning track of the 2026 AI City Challenge. RoR (1) rewrites the official chain-of-thought annotations into a structured, temporally grounded schema and LoRA-tunes a 9B vision-language model on the enhanced traces; (2) fine-tunes a 4-bit-quantized 27B model on answers alone, absorbing the annotation style; and (3) selects between the two candidates per question with a training-free LLM judge. RoR ranks first on the in-domain leaderboard with mean score 0.6788.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理还是背诵：用于交通异常推理的互补专家与LLM评判器
碰撞、违规和突发危险等道路异常对视频理解的要求超越了检测：实用系统必须核实发生了什么、描述场景并解释原因。我们发现这些能力对视觉语言模型而言方向相反：封闭式问题奖励精确、基于证据的推理，而开放式答案按与参考文本的相似度评分，其中匹配标注风格与正确推理同样重要。我们提出Reason-or-Recite（RoR），一个用于2026年AI City Challenge交通异常推理赛道的双专家框架。RoR（1）将官方思维链标注改写为结构化、时间接地的模式，并在增强轨迹上对9B视觉语言模型进行LoRA微调；（2）仅基于答案对4比特量化的27B模型进行微调，以吸收标注风格；（3）通过无需训练的LLM评判器为每个问题在两个候选答案之间进行选择。RoR以平均分0.6788在域内排行榜上排名第一。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the Traffic Anomaly Reasoning track of the 2026 AI City Challenge, where practical video understanding must verify events, describe scenes, and explain causes, yet closed-form questions reward evidence-grounded reasoning while open-ended answers are scored by similarity to reference text, making annotation style as important as correctness. The authors propose Reason-or-Recite (RoR), a two-specialist framework that rewrites official chain-of-thought annotations into a structured, temporally grounded schema and LoRA-tunes a 9B vision-language model on the enhanced traces, fine-tunes a 4-bit-quantized 27B model on answers alone to absorb annotation style, and selects between the two candidates per question using a training-free LLM judge. RoR ranks first on the in-domain leaderboard with a mean score of 0.6788.
交通异常推理要求视觉语言模型既能基于证据核实事件，又能以贴近参考标注的风格描述场景，而这两种能力对模型的要求相互冲突。本文提出 Reason-or-Recite（RoR）双专家框架：将官方思维链标注改写为结构化、具时间依据的模式，并据此对 9B 视觉语言模型进行 LoRA 微调以实现精确推理；同时对 4-bit 量化的 27B 模型仅用答案进行微调以吸收标注风格；最后由无需训练的 LLM 评判器针对每个问题在两个候选答案间进行选择。RoR 在 2026 AI City Challenge 交通异常推理赛道的域内排行榜上以 0.6788 的平均分位列第一。
A systematic evaluation of vision-language models for observational astronomical reasoning tasks
Authors: Wenke Ren, Hengxiao Guo, Wenwen Zuo, Xiaoman Zhang
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-04-27T15:11:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:52:20+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 15 figures, 11 tables. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026, Track on Evaluations and Datasets
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed as general-purpose tools for scientific data interpretation, yet their reliability on real astronomical observations across diverse modalities remains untested. We present AstroVLBench, a benchmark of over 4,100 expert-verified instances across five tasks spanning optical imaging, radio interferometry, multi-wavelength photometry, time-domain light curves, and optical spectroscopy. We systematically evaluate six state-of-the-art frontier models and find that performance is strongly modality-dependent and uncorrelated with general benchmark rankings. Mechanistic cross-model ablations reveal three concrete bottlenecks. First, prompt grounding exhibits marked model and task dependence: physical guidance yields more balanced classifications on optical imaging, whereas phenomenological prompts better navigate undersampled time-domain data. Second, presenting one-dimensional measurements as numerical tables rather than rendered plots consistently improves light-curve classification across all models by up to 23 percentage points, demonstrating that plot rendering can obscure physically relevant signal. Third, an expert audit of optical spectroscopy reveals a notable "right-answer-wrong-reason" phenomenon: across evaluated models, an average of 26% of correct predictions are accompanied by physically inconsistent or invalid reasoning chains, showing that accuracy alone is insufficient for trustworthy scientific deployment. AstroVLBench provides the first systematic, multi-modal baselines for VLMs in observational astronomy and identifies the representation, grounding, and reasoning bottlenecks that must be addressed before VLMs can be trusted as autonomous scientific interpreters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向观测天文推理任务的视觉语言模型系统性评估
视觉语言模型（VLM）日益被提议作为科学数据解释的通用工具，但其在跨多种模态的真实天文观测中的可靠性仍未得到检验。我们提出 AstroVLBench，这是一个包含 4,100 多个经专家验证实例的基准，涵盖五项任务，涉及光学成像、射电干涉测量、多波段测光、时域光变曲线和光学光谱。我们系统评估了六个最先进的前沿模型，发现性能强烈依赖于模态，且与通用基准排名不相关。跨模型机制性消融揭示了三个具体瓶颈。第一，提示接地表现出显著的模型和任务依赖性：物理引导在光学成像上产生更均衡的分类，而现象学提示更能应对欠采样的时域数据。第二，将一维测量以数值表格而非渲染图呈现，可在所有模型上持续提升光变曲线分类性能，最高达 23 个百分点，表明图渲染可能掩盖物理相关信号。第三，对光学光谱的专家审计揭示出显著的“答案正确、理由错误”现象：在受评估模型中，平均 26% 的正确预测伴随物理上不一致或无效的推理链，表明仅凭准确率不足以支撑可信的科学部署。AstroVLBench 为观测天文学中的 VLM 提供了首个系统性多模态基线，并识别出在 VLM 能够被信任为自主科学解释者之前必须解决的表示、接地和推理瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models are increasingly proposed as general-purpose tools for scientific data interpretation, but their reliability on real astronomical observations across diverse modalities remains untested, motivating this work. The authors introduce AstroVLBench, a benchmark of over 4,100 expert-verified instances spanning five tasks: optical imaging, radio interferometry, multi-wavelength photometry, time-domain light curves, and optical spectroscopy, and they systematically evaluate six state-of-the-art frontier models with mechanistic cross-model ablations. 视觉语言模型日益被提议作为科学数据解释的通用工具，但其在跨多种模态的真实天文观测上的可靠性尚未得到检验，这构成了系统评估的动机。作者提出了AstroVLBench，一个包含4100多个专家核验实例的基准，覆盖光学成像、射电干涉测量、多波段测光、时域光变曲线和光学光谱五项任务，并据此评估了六个最先进的前沿模型，同时开展了跨模型的机制性消融分析。结果显示，模型性能强烈依赖于模态，且与通用基准排名不相关，并揭示了三类瓶颈：提示接地效应因模型和任务而异，物理引导在光学成像上带来更均衡的分类，而现象学提示更能应对欠采样的时域数据；将一维测量以数值表格而非渲染图形式呈现，可使所有模型的光变曲线分类提升最多23个百分点；对光学光谱的专家审计发现，平均26%的正确预测伴随物理上不一致或无效的推理链，表明仅凭准确率不足以支撑可信的科学部署。
PlayPolicy: Dexterity from Supervised Play
Authors: Botao He, Yiming Dou, Yiannis Aloimonos, Jitendra Malik, Akash Sharma, Ruoshi Liu
First: 2026-10-15T17:55:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:47:05+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://play-policy.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
How can robots autonomously collect the contact-rich experience needed to learn dexterous manipulation? We present PlayPolicy: Dexterity from supervised play (DSP), a framework in which a robot learns a play policy from minutes of human play through behavior cloning, then deploys it autonomously to collect hours of interaction data to train a world model. During inference, the same play policy then guides model-predictive control (MPC) by proposing candidate action sequences for evaluation under the learned world model. To accelerate inference, we also introduce a method for distilling a closed-loop, goal-conditioned policy through self-play within the learned world model. We evaluate this framework on underactuated pushing and any-to-any in-hand pose reorientation with a multifingered hand, in simulation and real world. In real world experiment, our method outperforms hindsight behavior cloning by 69.6% in success rate and achieves comparable success to reinforcement learning with 490 times less interaction data. These results demonstrate that supervised play can autonomously collect interaction data, guide model-based planning, and enable policy learning without any task-specific demonstrations or sim-to-real transfer. Project page: https://play-policy.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：PlayPolicy：从监督式玩耍中获得灵巧性
机器人如何自主收集学习灵巧操作所需的富含接触的经验？我们提出 PlayPolicy：从监督式玩耍中获得灵巧性（DSP），这是一个框架，机器人通过行为克隆从数分钟的人类玩耍中学习一个玩耍策略，然后自主部署该策略以收集数小时的交互数据来训练世界模型。在推理过程中，同一个玩耍策略随后通过学习到的世界模型评估候选动作序列，从而引导模型预测控制（MPC）。为加速推理，我们还引入了一种方法，通过学习到的世界模型中的自博弈来蒸馏一个闭环、目标条件策略。我们在仿真和真实世界中，以欠驱动推动和多指手任意到任意的手内姿态重定向对该框架进行了评估。在真实世界实验中，我们的方法在成功率上比事后行为克隆高出 69.6%，并以少 490 倍的交互数据达到与强化学习相当的成功率。这些结果表明，监督式玩耍能够自主收集交互数据、引导基于模型的规划，并在无需任何任务特定演示或仿真到现实迁移的情况下实现策略学习。项目页面：https://play-policy.github.io/
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the problem of autonomously collecting contact-rich experience for learning dexterous manipulation. It proposes PlayPolicy, a framework in which a robot learns a play policy from minutes of human play via behavior cloning, then deploys it autonomously to gather hours of interaction data for training a world model; at inference the same play policy guides model-predictive control by proposing candidate action sequences evaluated under the learned world model, and a closed-loop goal-conditioned policy is distilled through self-play within that world model to speed up inference. Evaluated on underactuated pushing and any-to-any in-hand pose reorientation with a multifingered hand in simulation and the real world, the method outperforms hindsight behavior cloning by 69.6% in real-world success rate and matches reinforcement learning with 490 times less interaction data, without task-specific demonstrations or sim-to-real transfer.

The Target Agent Is Its Own Best Critic but a Poor Proposer: Self-Improved Red Teaming
Authors: Mintong Kang, Stephen Rawls, Melissa Kazemi Rad, Jian Ni, Bo Li
First: 2026-10-26T07:34:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:34:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic red teaming increasingly relies on large language models (LLMs) to iteratively diagnose failed attacks and propose improved adversarial prompts or injections. Although prior work strengthens this loop through prompt engineering and search-harness design, the mechanisms underlying its two core roles, criticism and attack proposal, has not been explored. To further investigate the core roles and therefore improve red teaming effectiveness, we disentangle two design choices: whether criticism is generated by the target session itself (self-criticism) or by a separately instantiated critic (external criticism), and whether that criticism is translated into the next attack by the target session (self-proposal) or by a separate attacker (external proposal). Through our analysis, we find that self-criticism is more effective because it is grounded in the target's live execution context, including observations, constraints, and available reasoning; removing reasoning reduces downstream attack success by 6%-17%. In contrast, self-proposal is substantially less effective because awareness of the adversarial objective induces explicit refusal and non-refusal sandbagging. These findings motivate a role-separated red-teaming approach: the target session diagnoses why an attack failed, while a separately instantiated attacker generates the next proposal. On DTAP-BENCH, spanning Telecom, Workflow, CRM, and Code domains, under both direct misuse and indirect threat models, as well as multiple agent frameworks, this configuration achieves 63.2% overall attack success rate (ASR), much higher than baselines. Our results show that effective automatic red teaming depends not only on model capability and search strategy, but also on how execution context and adversarial responsibility are distributed across model sessions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：目标智能体是自己最好的批评者，却是糟糕的提议者：自我改进的红队测试
自动红队测试日益依赖大语言模型（LLM）迭代诊断失败的攻击，并提出改进的对抗性提示或注入。尽管先前工作通过提示工程和搜索框架设计强化了这一循环，但其两个核心角色——批评与攻击提议——背后的机制尚未被探索。为进一步研究这些核心角色并提升红队测试有效性，我们拆解了两个设计选择：批评是由目标会话自身生成（自我批评），还是由单独实例化的批评者生成（外部批评）；以及该批评是由目标会话转化为下一次攻击（自我提议），还是由单独的攻击者转化（外部提议）。通过分析，我们发现自我批评更有效，因为它基于目标的实时执行上下文，包括观察、约束和可用的推理；移除推理会使下游攻击成功率降低6%-17%。相反，自我提议的效果显著较差，因为对对抗目标的意识会引发显式拒绝和非拒绝式消极应付。这些发现促成了角色分离的红队测试方法：目标会话诊断攻击失败的原因，而单独实例化的攻击者生成下一次提议。在涵盖电信、工作流、CRM和代码领域的DTAP-BENCH上，在直接滥用和间接威胁模型下，以及多种智能体框架中，该配置实现了63.2%的总体攻击成功率（ASR），远高于基线。我们的结果表明，有效的自动红队测试不仅取决于模型能力和搜索策略，还取决于执行上下文和对抗责任如何在模型会话之间分配。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the mechanisms underlying the two core roles in automatic LLM-based red teaming, criticism and attack proposal, which prior work has strengthened through prompt engineering and search design without examining them directly. The authors disentangle whether criticism comes from the target session itself or a separate critic, and whether that criticism is converted into the next attack by the target session or a separate attacker. They find that self-criticism is more effective because it is grounded in the target's live execution context, with removing reasoning reducing downstream attack success by 6%-17%, whereas self-proposal is substantially less effective because awareness of the adversarial objective induces explicit refusal and non-refusal sandbagging. These findings motivate a role-separated approach in which the target session diagnoses failures while a separate attacker generates proposals, achieving 63.2% overall attack success rate on DTAP-BENCH across Telecom, Workflow, CRM, and Code domains under both direct misuse and indirect threat models and multiple agent frameworks, much higher than baselines.
以往的自动红队研究通过提示工程和搜索框架设计强化了大语言模型驱动的攻击循环，但尚未考察批评与攻击提议这两个核心角色的机制。本文对这两个角色进行解耦，比较自我批评与外部批评、自我提议与外部提议两种设计选择。分析发现，自我批评更有效，因为它基于目标会话的实时执行上下文，移除推理会使下游攻击成功率下降6%至17%；而自我提议效果明显较差，因为对对抗目标的感知会引发显式拒绝和非拒绝式消极应付。这些发现促成了角色分离的红队方法：由目标会话诊断攻击失败原因，由单独实例化的攻击者生成下一次提议；在覆盖电信、工作流、CRM和代码领域的DTAP-BENCH上，在直接滥用和间接威胁模型以及多种智能体框架下，该配置取得63.2%的总体攻击成功率，远高于基线。
Efficient Generation of Diverse Cooperative Agents with World Models
Authors: Yi Loo, Akshunn Trivedi, Malika Meghjani
First: 2025-06-09T05:52:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:33:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A major bottleneck in the training process for Zero-Shot Coordination (ZSC) agents is the generation of diverse partner agents via Population-based Training (PBT). Prominent PBT methods, such as Cross-play Minimization (XPM), can be very computationally expensive and sample inefficient, as the training objective requires sampling multiple types of trajectories, in particular the need of Mixed-Play (MP) trajectories to mitigate self-sabotage in cross-play. Each partner agent in the population is also trained from scratch, despite all of the partners in the population learning policies of the same coordination task. In this work, we propose that simulated trajectories from the dynamics model of an environment can drastically speed up the training process for XPM methods. We introduce XPM-WM, a framework for generating simulated trajectories for XPM via a learned World Model (WM). We show XPM with simulated trajectories removes the need to sample Mixed-Play trajectories while still mitigating any self-sabotage. The learned WM also bootstraps the training of subsequent agents in the population. We show that these two factors combined gives XPM-WM significantly better sample efficiency as compared to other XPM methods and scales linearly to the size of the population. We show our proposed method can effectively generate partners with diverse conventions that match the performance of previous methods. Finally, we show that our proposed training paradigm of using learned world models to generate diverse population can be extended to other PBT methods beyond XPM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用世界模型高效生成多样化合作智能体
零样本协调（ZSC）智能体训练过程中的一个主要瓶颈是通过基于种群的训练（PBT）生成多样化的伙伴智能体。著名的PBT方法，如交叉对局最小化（XPM），计算开销大且样本效率低，因为训练目标需要采样多种类型的轨迹，特别是需要混合对局（MP）轨迹以缓解交叉对局中的自我破坏。种群中的每个伙伴智能体也是从头开始训练的，尽管种群中的所有伙伴学习的都是同一协调任务的策略。在本工作中，我们提出从环境动力学模型生成的模拟轨迹可以大幅加速XPM方法的训练过程。我们引入XPM-WM，一个通过学习到的世界模型（WM）为XPM生成模拟轨迹的框架。我们展示了使用模拟轨迹的XPM消除了采样混合对局轨迹的需求，同时仍能缓解任何自我破坏。学习到的WM还为种群中后续智能体的训练提供了引导。我们表明，这两个因素相结合使XPM-WM相比其他XPM方法具有显著更好的样本效率，并且随种群规模线性扩展。我们展示了所提出的方法能够有效生成具有多样化约定的伙伴，其性能与先前方法相当。最后，我们表明我们提出的使用学习到的世界模型生成多样化种群的训练范式可以扩展到XPM之外的其他PBT方法。
Summary / 总结
Zero-shot coordination training is bottlenecked by the cost of generating diverse partner agents through population-based training, since methods such as cross-play minimization require sampling multiple trajectory types, including mixed-play trajectories to prevent self-sabotage, and train every partner from scratch. The authors propose XPM-WM, which uses a learned world model of the environment dynamics to generate simulated trajectories for cross-play minimization, removing the need for mixed-play sampling while still mitigating self-sabotage, and bootstrapping the training of subsequent population members. Experiments show that these two factors together yield significantly better sample efficiency than other XPM methods, scale linearly with population size, and produce partners with diverse conventions whose performance matches prior methods, with the paradigm extendable to other population-based training methods beyond XPM.
零样本协作训练的主要瓶颈在于通过基于群体的训练生成多样化伙伴智能体的成本，交叉对局最小化等方法需要采样多种轨迹，尤其是用于缓解自我破坏的混合对局轨迹，且群体中每个伙伴都要从零开始训练。作者提出 XPM-WM，利用学习到的环境动力学世界模型为交叉对局最小化生成模拟轨迹，从而无需采样混合对局轨迹仍能缓解自我破坏，并利用世界模型为群体中后续智能体的训练提供引导。实验表明，这两个因素结合使样本效率显著优于其他交叉对局最小化方法，且随群体规模线性扩展；该方法能生成具有多样约定且性能与先前方法相当的伙伴，同时该训练范式还可推广到交叉对局最小化之外的其他基于群体的训练方法。
C-SafeGen: Certified Safe LLM Generation with Claim-Based Streaming Guardrails
Authors: Mintong Kang, Zhaorun Chen, Bo Li
First: 2026-10-26T07:28:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:28:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the remarkable capabilities of large language models (LLMs) across diverse applications, they remain vulnerable to generating content that violates safety regulations and policies. To mitigate these risks, LLMs undergo safety alignment; however, they can still be effectively jailbroken. Off-the-shelf guardrail models are commonly deployed to monitor generations, but these models primarily focus on detection rather than ensuring safe decoding of LLM outputs. Moreover, existing efforts lack rigorous safety guarantees, which are crucial for the universal deployment of LLMs and certifiable compliance with regulatory standards. In this paper, we propose a Claim-based Stream Decoding (CSD) algorithm coupled with a statistical risk guarantee framework using conformal analysis. Specifically, our CSD algorithm integrates a stream guardrail model to safeguard sequential claims generated by LLMs and incorporates a backtracking mechanism to revise claims flagged with high safety risks. We provide theoretical guarantees demonstrating that the CSD algorithm achieves the desired generation distribution subject to safety constraints. Furthermore, we introduce a generation risk certification framework and derive a high-probability upper bound on the safety risk of the proposed CSD algorithm. We prove that our method can asymptotically control safety risk to any desired level. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the CSD algorithm compared to state-of-the-art safety decoding approaches. Additionally, we validate the soundness and tightness of the derived safety risk upper bound using realistic data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：C-SafeGen：基于声明级流式护栏的经认证安全LLM生成
尽管大语言模型（LLM）在各类应用中展现出卓越能力，它们仍易生成违反安全法规与政策的内容。为缓解这些风险，LLM会进行安全对齐；然而，它们仍可被有效越狱。现成的护栏模型通常被部署用于监控生成内容，但这些模型主要侧重于检测，而非确保LLM输出的安全解码。此外，现有工作缺乏严格的安全保证，而这对于LLM的通用部署以及可认证地符合监管标准至关重要。本文提出一种基于声明的流式解码（CSD）算法，并结合使用共形分析的统计风险保证框架。具体而言，我们的CSD算法集成了一个流式护栏模型，以保护LLM生成的顺序声明，并引入回溯机制来修正被标记为高安全风险的声明。我们提供理论保证，证明CSD算法在安全约束下达到期望的生成分布。此外，我们引入生成风险认证框架，并推导出所提出CSD算法安全风险的高概率上界。我们证明，我们的方法可以将安全风险渐近地控制到任意期望水平。实证评估表明，与最先进的安全解码方法相比，CSD算法具有有效性和高效性。此外，我们使用真实数据验证了所推导安全风险上界的可靠性与紧致性。
Summary / 总结
Large language models remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content even after safety alignment and can be jailbroken, while existing guardrail models mainly detect rather than guarantee safe decoding and lack rigorous safety guarantees. The authors propose Claim-based Stream Decoding (CSD), which integrates a streaming guardrail model to monitor sequential claims generated by the LLM and adds a backtracking mechanism to revise claims flagged as high risk, combined with a conformal-analysis-based statistical risk certification framework. They provide theoretical guarantees that CSD achieves the desired generation distribution under safety constraints and derive a high-probability upper bound on safety risk, proving asymptotic control to any desired level. Empirical evaluations show CSD is effective and efficient compared with state-of-the-art safety decoding approaches, and realistic data validate the soundness and tightness of the derived risk bound.
该论文针对安全对齐后的大语言模型仍易被越狱、而现有护栏模型主要侧重检测而非确保安全解码、且缺乏严格安全保证的问题展开研究。作者提出基于声明的流式解码算法（CSD），结合流式护栏模型对模型依次生成的声明进行监控，并引入回溯机制修正被标记为高风险的声明，同时基于保形分析构建统计风险保证框架。理论分析证明CSD在安全约束下可达到期望的生成分布，并推导出安全风险的高概率上界，证明该方法可将安全风险渐近控制到任意期望水平；实验评估表明其相较先进安全解码方法更有效且高效，真实数据也验证了所推导风险上界的合理性与紧致性。
GUIFlow: Temporal Redundancy-Aware Incremental Inference for GUI Agents
Authors: Ye Li, Haoran Ding, Huanan Liu, Yuan Meng, Yutian Shen, Yuansong Wang, Kangye Ji, Hanyun Cui, Hongbo Duan, Chen Tang, Jiawei Li, Shu-Tao Xia, Zhi Wang
First: 2026-10-26T07:25:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:25:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
GUI agents built on vision-language models interact with continuously evolving interfaces, yet conventional inference repeatedly recomputes representations of largely overlapping observations and histories. This mismatch makes multi-step interaction costly, while changes in context, interface content, and action arguments prevent straightforward cache reuse. We introduce GUIFlow, a framework for full-pipeline incremental inference in multi-image GUI interaction. Our central insight is that advancing a decision need not require rebuilding all supporting representations: each interaction step can instead selectively update computational states maintained across the trajectory. To support new decisions as the context evolves, GUIFlow adapts how past computation is reused: historical KV states are position-aligned and injected into the current context; change signals guide selective updates of current-observation states in the vision encoder and language model; and earlier action responses serve as speculative drafts verified by the target model. Together, these mechanisms enable cross-step reuse throughout visual encoding, prefill, and decoding without additional training, a separate draft model, or changes to the model-visible inputs and action interface. Experiments demonstrate up to 3.39x geometric-mean speedup across offline multi-image benchmarks and up to 3.49x in online, closed-loop interaction on OSWorld, while maintaining task performance broadly comparable to the dense baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GUIFlow：面向GUI智能体的时序冗余感知增量推理
基于视觉语言模型的GUI智能体与持续演化的界面交互，而传统推理会重复计算大量重叠观测与历史表示。这种不匹配使多步交互代价高昂，而上下文、界面内容和动作参数的变化又阻碍了直接的缓存复用。我们提出GUIFlow，一个用于多图像GUI交互全流程增量推理的框架。我们的核心洞见是：推进决策不必重建所有支撑表示：每个交互步骤可改为选择性更新沿轨迹维护的计算状态。为在上下文演化时支持新决策，GUIFlow调整过去计算的复用方式：历史KV状态经位置对齐后注入当前上下文；变化信号引导视觉编码器和语言模型中对当前观测状态的选择性更新；先前的动作响应作为推测草稿，由目标模型验证。这些机制共同实现跨步骤在视觉编码、预填充和解码中的复用，无需额外训练、独立草稿模型，也不改变模型可见输入和动作接口。实验表明，在离线多图像基准上几何平均加速最高达3.39倍，在OSWorld在线闭环交互中最高达3.49倍，同时任务性能与稠密基线大体相当。
Summary / 总结
GUI agents based on vision-language models repeatedly recompute representations of largely overlapping observations and histories during multi-step interaction, making inference costly, while changes in context, interface content, and action arguments hinder naive cache reuse. GUIFlow addresses this with full-pipeline incremental inference, selectively updating computational states across the trajectory: historical KV states are position-aligned and injected into the current context, change signals guide selective updates of current-observation states in the vision encoder and language model, and earlier action responses serve as speculative drafts verified by the target model, all without additional training, a separate draft model, or changes to model-visible inputs. Experiments show up to 3.39x geometric-mean speedup on offline multi-image benchmarks and up to 3.49x in online closed-loop interaction on OSWorld, with task performance broadly comparable to the dense baseline.
基于视觉语言模型的GUI智能体在多步交互中会反复计算高度重叠的观测与历史表示，导致推理成本高昂，而上下文、界面内容和动作参数的变化又使简单的缓存复用难以奏效。GUIFlow针对多图像GUI交互提出全流程增量推理框架，通过选择性更新跨轨迹维护的计算状态来推进决策：将历史KV状态进行位置对齐并注入当前上下文，利用变化信号引导视觉编码器和语言模型对当前观测状态进行选择性更新，并将先前的动作响应作为由目标模型验证的推测草稿，这些机制无需额外训练、独立草稿模型，也不改变模型可见输入和动作接口。实验表明，该方法在离线多图像基准上取得最高3.39倍几何平均加速，在OSWorld在线闭环交互中最高达3.49倍，同时任务性能与稠密基线大体相当。
Code as Worlds: Agentic Discovery of Executable World Representations for Physical Reasoning
Authors: Hanyang Wang, Yimo Cai, Weiliang Chen, Jiawei Chi, Haowen Sun, Qiyu Dai, Yi-Hsin Hung, Xingzhuo Guo, Jinshan Ren, Runmao Yao, Ziwei Liu, Mingsheng Long, Yueqi Duan, Jun Gao, Jiangran Lyu, Fangfu Liu, Jialong Wu
First: 2026-08-27T17:57:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:16:07+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://mirros-lab.github.io/code-as-world
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Physical understanding and reasoning depend on forming compact and generalizable representations of the world. While modern vision-language models can recognize and explain diverse physical events, they often lack explicit representations of the underlying mechanisms-such as object states, physical parameters, and governing dynamics-needed for reliably reasoning how the world evolves and responds to interventions. In this work, we introduce Code-as-World, a paradigm that represents physical worlds through executable world representations. By expressing physical composition, dynamic evolution, and visual appearance as executable code, Code-as-World provides a compact, quantitatively grounded, and controllable abstraction of the physical world. To construct such representations from multimodal observations, such as natural-language descriptions or real-world videos, we develop an agentic discovery loop inspired by abductive reasoning, where an agent proposes, executes, renders, verifies, and iteratively refines executable world hypotheses. As a concrete application, we use verified executable worlds to provide scalable physical supervision for training vision-language models on quantitative physical reasoning. Experiments show that Code-as-World-VL achieves state-of-the-art performance on QuantiPhy and surpasses leading proprietary models, highlighting the potential of executable world representations as a scalable foundation for physical intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代码即世界：面向物理推理的可执行世界表示的智能体发现
物理理解与推理依赖于形成紧凑且可泛化的世界表示。尽管现代视觉语言模型能够识别并解释多样的物理事件，它们往往缺乏对底层机制的显式表示——例如物体状态、物理参数和控制动力学——而这些对于可靠地推理世界如何演化及如何响应干预是必需的。在本工作中，我们提出代码即世界（Code-as-World），一种通过可执行世界表示来表征物理世界的范式。通过将物理组成、动态演化和视觉外观表达为可执行代码，代码即世界提供了物理世界的一种紧凑、定量有据且可控的抽象。为了从多模态观测（如自然语言描述或真实世界视频）中构建此类表示，我们开发了一个受溯因推理启发的智能体发现循环，其中智能体提出、执行、渲染、验证并迭代精炼可执行世界假设。作为一个具体应用，我们使用经过验证的可执行世界为训练视觉语言模型进行定量物理推理提供可扩展的物理监督。实验表明，Code-as-World-VL 在 QuantiPhy 上取得了最先进的性能，并超越了领先的专有模型，突显了可执行世界表示作为物理智能可扩展基础的潜力。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the tendency of vision-language models to recognize and describe physical events without explicit representations of underlying mechanisms such as object states, parameters, and governing dynamics, which limits reliable reasoning about how the world evolves and responds to interventions. It introduces Code-as-World, a paradigm that represents physical worlds as executable code capturing physical composition, dynamic evolution, and visual appearance, and builds these representations from multimodal observations via an agentic discovery loop inspired by abductive reasoning in which an agent proposes, executes, renders, verifies, and iteratively refines executable world hypotheses. Verified executable worlds are then used as scalable physical supervision to train vision-language models for quantitative physical reasoning, and experiments show that Code-as-World-VL achieves state-of-the-art performance on QuantiPhy and surpasses leading proprietary models.
该论文针对视觉语言模型虽能识别和解释物理事件、却缺乏对物体状态、物理参数和支配动力学等底层机制的显式表示、从而难以可靠推理世界演化与干预响应的问题，提出了 Code-as-World 范式，将物理世界表示为可执行代码，以统一刻画物理组成、动态演化和视觉外观，并从自然语言描述或真实视频等多模态观测中，通过受溯因推理启发的智能体发现循环（提议、执行、渲染、验证并迭代改进可执行世界假设）构建此类表示。经验证的可执行世界随后被用作可扩展的物理监督信号，用于训练视觉语言模型进行定量物理推理；实验表明 Code-as-World-VL 在 QuantiPhy 上取得最先进性能，并超过领先的专有模型。
FermatSyn: SAM2-Enhanced Bidirectional Mamba with Isotropic Spiral Scanning for Multi-Modal Medical Image Synthesis
Authors: Feng Yuan, Yifan Gao, Haoyue Li, Xin Gao
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2025-05-12T15:51:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:11:35+00:00
Comments: MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-modal medical image synthesis is pivotal for alleviating clinical data scarcity, yet existing methods fail to reconcile global anatomical consistency with high-fidelity local detail. We propose FermatSyn, which addresses three persistent limitations: (1) SAM2-based Prior Encoder that injects domain-aware anatomical knowledge via LoRA$^{+}$ efficient fine-tuning of a frozen SAM2 Vision Transformer; (2) Hierarchical Residual Downsampling Module (HRDM) coupled with a Cross-scale Integration Network (CIN) that preserves high-frequency lesion details and adaptively fuses global--local representations; and (3) continuity constrained Fermat Spiral Scanning strategy within a Bidirectional Fermat Scan Mamba (BFS-Mamba), constructing an approximately isotropic receptive field that substantially reduces the directional bias of raster or spiral serialization. Experiments on SynthRAD2023, BraTS2019, BraTS-MEN, and BraTS-MET show FermatSyn surpasses state-of-the-art methods in PSNR, SSIM, FID, and 3D structural consistency. Downstream segmentation on synthesized images yields no significant difference from real-image training ($p{>}0.05$), confirming clinical utility. Code is available at https://github.com/exsinger-hub/ FermatSyn.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FermatSyn：SAM2增强的双向Mamba与各向同性螺旋扫描用于多模态医学图像合成
多模态医学图像合成对于缓解临床数据稀缺至关重要，然而现有方法无法兼顾全局解剖一致性与高保真局部细节。我们提出FermatSyn，解决了三个长期存在的局限：(1) 基于SAM2的先验编码器，通过对冻结的SAM2 Vision Transformer进行LoRA$^{+}$高效微调，注入领域感知的解剖知识；(2) 分层残差下采样模块（HRDM）与跨尺度集成网络（CIN）相结合，保留高频病灶细节并自适应融合全局-局部表示；(3) 在双向Fermat扫描Mamba（BFS-Mamba）中引入连续性约束的Fermat螺旋扫描策略，构建近似各向同性的感受野，显著降低光栅或螺旋序列化的方向偏差。在SynthRAD2023、BraTS2019、BraTS-MEN和BraTS-MET上的实验表明，FermatSyn在PSNR、SSIM、FID和3D结构一致性方面优于最先进方法。对合成图像进行下游分割与真实图像训练无显著差异（$p{>}0.05$），证实了临床实用性。代码见https://github.com/exsinger-hub/ FermatSyn。
Summary / 总结
Multi-modal medical image synthesis is important for alleviating clinical data scarcity, but existing methods struggle to reconcile global anatomical consistency with high-fidelity local detail. The authors propose FermatSyn, which combines a SAM2-based prior encoder that injects domain-aware anatomical knowledge through LoRA+ fine-tuning of a frozen SAM2 Vision Transformer, a Hierarchical Residual Downsampling Module with a Cross-scale Integration Network to preserve high-frequency lesion details and fuse global-local representations, and a continuity-constrained Fermat Spiral Scanning strategy within a Bidirectional Fermat Scan Mamba that builds an approximately isotropic receptive field and reduces directional bias. Experiments on SynthRAD2023, BraTS2019, BraTS-MEN, and BraTS-MET show that FermatSyn outperforms state-of-the-art methods on PSNR, SSIM, FID, and 3D structural consistency, and downstream segmentation trained on synthesized images shows no significant difference from training on real images (p>0.05), supporting clinical utility.
多模态医学图像合成对缓解临床数据稀缺具有重要意义，但现有方法难以兼顾全局解剖一致性与高保真局部细节。作者提出 FermatSyn，通过基于 SAM2 的先验编码器对冻结的 SAM2 Vision Transformer 进行 LoRA+ 高效微调以注入解剖知识，结合分层残差下采样模块与跨尺度集成网络保留高频病灶细节并自适应融合全局与局部表示，并在双向 Fermat 扫描 Mamba 中引入连续性约束的 Fermat 螺旋扫描策略，构建近似各向同性的感受野以降低方向偏差。在 SynthRAD2023、BraTS2019、BraTS-MEN 和 BraTS-MET 上的实验表明，FermatSyn 在 PSNR、SSIM、FID 和三维结构一致性上优于现有先进方法，且基于合成图像训练的下游分割与真实图像训练无显著差异（p>0.05），验证了其临床实用性。
MedZERO: Self-Evolving Agents for Open-Ended Medical Reasoning Through Controlled Knowledge Accumulation
Authors: Xilin Dang, Weilin Ruan, Xue Yang, Jinghao Wang, Xiaowei Hu, Jinpeng Li, Pheng-Ann Heng
Venue: NIPS 2026
First: 2026-10-06T13:25:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:07:35+00:00
Comments: accepted by NIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in medical question answering and clinical reasoning, yet their improvement remains constrained by static parametric knowledge and costly expert supervision. Self-evolving agents offer a promising alternative by enabling models to improve through iterative task generation and problem-solving. However, most existing self-evolving methods are designed for easily verifiable domains such as mathematics and coding, where solutions can be checked by exact answers or executable programs. Medical reasoning is fundamentally different: it is open-ended, knowledge-intensive, and often only partially verifiable. We present MedZERO, a self-evolving framework for open-ended medical reasoning. MedZERO couples an Examiner that generates frontier medical question-option pairs with a Reasoner that solves them through evidence-grounded multi-turn reasoning with external knowledge tools. To support reliable, continual improvement, MedZERO adopts controlled knowledge accumulation, which maintains temporary exploratory knowledge and curated persistent knowledge in reasoning. We evaluate MedZERO on five public medical reasoning benchmarks using 4B- and 8B-scale base models under open-ended evaluation. At both model scales, MedZERO achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated methods. Code available at https://github.com/IcecreamArtist/MedZERO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedZERO：通过受控知识积累实现开放式医学推理的自进化智能体
大语言模型（LLM）在医学问答和临床推理中已展现出潜力，但其提升仍受限于静态参数化知识和昂贵的专家监督。自进化智能体通过迭代式任务生成与问题求解使模型得以改进，提供了一种有前景的替代方案。然而，现有大多数自进化方法面向数学、编程等易于验证的领域，其解答可通过精确答案或可执行程序检验。医学推理则根本不同：它是开放式的、知识密集型的，且往往只能部分验证。我们提出 MedZERO，一个面向开放式医学推理的自进化框架。MedZERO 将生成前沿医学问题-选项对的 Examiner 与通过基于证据的多轮推理并借助外部知识工具求解这些问题的 Reasoner 相耦合。为支持可靠、持续的改进，MedZERO 采用受控知识积累，在推理中维护临时探索性知识和经整理的持久性知识。我们在五个公开医学推理基准上，使用 4B 和 8B 规模的基础模型在开放式评估下评测 MedZERO。在两种模型规模下，MedZERO 在所评估方法中均取得最高平均准确率。代码见 https://github.com/IcecreamArtist/MedZERO。
Summary / 总结
Large language models show promise in medical reasoning but are limited by static parametric knowledge and costly expert supervision, and existing self-evolving agents are built for easily verifiable domains like math and coding rather than open-ended, knowledge-intensive medical tasks. The authors propose MedZERO, a self-evolving framework that pairs an Examiner generating frontier medical question-option pairs with a Reasoner that solves them via evidence-grounded multi-turn reasoning using external knowledge tools, supported by controlled knowledge accumulation that separates temporary exploratory knowledge from curated persistent knowledge. Evaluated on five public medical reasoning benchmarks with 4B- and 8B-scale base models under open-ended evaluation, MedZERO achieves the highest average accuracy among the compared methods at both model scales.
该论文针对大语言模型在医学推理上的提升受限于静态参数化知识和昂贵的专家监督，且现有自演化方法多面向数学、编程等易于验证的领域，而非开放式、知识密集的医学推理这一问题。MedZERO 将生成前沿医学问题-选项对的 Examiner 与借助外部知识工具进行有证据支撑的多轮推理的 Reasoner 相结合，并采用受控知识积累，在推理中区分临时探索性知识与经整理的持久知识。在五个公开医学推理基准上，使用 4B 和 8B 规模基础模型进行开放式评估，MedZERO 在两种模型规模下的平均准确率均高于所比较的方法。
PixFoundation: Are We Heading in the Right Direction with Pixel-level Vision Foundation Models?
Authors: Mennatullah Siam
First: 2025-02-06T16:29:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-26T07:05:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted in TMLR 2026, https://openreview.net/forum?id=HNtjTTAac1
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multiple works have emerged to push the boundaries of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) towards pixel-level understanding. The current trend is to train MLLMs with pixel-level grounding supervision in terms of masks on large-scale labelled data and specialized decoders for the segmentation task. However, we show that such MLLMs when evaluated on recent challenging vision-centric benchmarks, exhibit a weak ability in visual question answering (VQA). Surprisingly, some of these methods even downgrade the grounding ability of MLLMs that were never trained with such pixel-level supervision. In this work, we propose two novel challenging benchmarks with paired evaluation for both VQA and grounding. We demonstrate that simple baselines that are not unified achieve performance that matches or surpasses some of the pixel-level MLLMs. Our paired benchmarks and evaluation enable additional analysis on whether the failure stems from VQA and/or grounding abilities. Furthermore, we propose a paired prompt sensitivity analysis on both the language and visual prompts tailored for the grounding task. Our benchmarking pushes pixel-level MLLMs to improve in both accuracy and robustness, without sacrificing language abilities. More importantly, we study the research question of ``When does grounding emerge in MLLMs with respect to the output tokens?'' We propose a post-hoc interpretability tool that can be plugged into any MLLM to study the aforementioned question. We show that grounding does not necessarily coincide with the exact referring expression in the output, but can coincide with the object parts, its location, appearance, context or state. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://msiam.github.io/PixFoundationSeries/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PixFoundation：像素级视觉基础模型是否走在正确方向上？
多项工作致力于将多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的能力边界推向像素级理解。当前趋势是在大规模标注数据上以掩码形式的像素级定位监督训练MLLMs，并为分割任务配备专用解码器。然而，我们表明此类MLLMs在近期具有挑战性的以视觉为中心的基准上评估时，在视觉问答（VQA）方面表现出较弱的能力。令人意外的是，其中一些方法甚至降低了从未接受此类像素级监督训练的MLLMs的定位能力。在本工作中，我们提出了两个新颖且具有挑战性的基准，并对VQA和定位进行配对评估。我们证明，非统一的简单基线可达到与某些像素级MLLMs相当或更优的性能。我们的配对基准与评估支持进一步分析失败究竟源于VQA和/或定位能力。此外，我们针对定位任务提出了对语言提示和视觉提示的配对提示敏感性分析。我们的基准测试推动像素级MLLMs在准确性和鲁棒性上提升，同时不牺牲语言能力。更重要的是，我们研究了“在MLLMs中，定位相对于输出token何时出现？”这一研究问题。我们提出了一种可插入任何MLLM的事后可解释性工具，以研究上述问题。我们表明，定位并不一定与输出中的确切指代表达重合，而可能与物体部件、其位置、外观、上下文或状态重合。代码和数据集已公开于https://msiam.github.io/PixFoundationSeries/。
Summary / 总结
This work questions whether the prevailing approach of training multimodal large language models with pixel-level grounding supervision and specialized segmentation decoders actually advances pixel-level understanding, since such models show weak visual question answering on challenging vision-centric benchmarks and can even degrade the grounding ability of models never trained with such supervision. The authors introduce two new challenging benchmarks with paired evaluation of both VQA and grounding, along with paired prompt sensitivity analysis on language and visual prompts, and a post-hoc interpretability tool for studying when grounding emerges relative to output tokens. Experiments show that simple non-unified baselines match or surpass some pixel-level MLLMs, that grounding does not necessarily coincide with the exact referring expression in the output but can align with object parts, location, appearance, context, or state, and that the paired benchmarking drives improvements in both accuracy and robustness without sacrificing language abilities.
本文质疑当前以像素级定位监督和专用分割解码器训练多模态大语言模型的主流做法是否真正提升了像素级理解能力，发现这类模型在近期具有挑战性的视觉中心VQA基准上表现较弱，部分方法甚至削弱了未经此类监督训练的模型的定位能力。作者提出了两个新的挑战性基准，对VQA与定位进行配对评估，并针对定位任务设计了语言与视觉提示的配对敏感性分析，以及一个可插入任意MLLM的事后可解释性工具，用于研究定位能力相对于输出token何时出现。实验表明，简单的非统一基线即可匹配或超越部分像素级MLLM，配对评估能够判断失败源于VQA还是定位能力，且定位并不一定与输出中的确切指代表达重合，而可能与物体部件、位置、外观、上下文或状态对应。
